      function checkWinner(g) {
        if (g.winner === "P" || g.winner === "A") return;
        actionFaultHit("victory-before");
        let p = aliveUnits(g.teams.P).length,
          a = aliveUnits(g.teams.A).length;
        if (!p || !a) {
          g.winner = p ? "P" : "A";
          actionFaultHit("victory-after");
          actionFaultHit("terminal-pending-after");
          actionFaultHit("terminal-commit-before");
          log(g, g.winner === "P" ? "플레이어 승리!" : "AI 승리!", "turn");
        } else actionFaultHit("victory-after");
      }
      function gatherPotential(u) {
        let max = card(u).maxEnergy || 4;
        if (totalEnergy(u) < max) return 20 - totalEnergy(u) * 2;
        let base = energyValue(u),
          gain = 0,
          store = energyStore(u);
        for (const col of ["R", "W", "B"]) {
          let best = base;
          store.forEach((e, i) => {
            if (e.a < 1) return;
            let pool = store.filter((_, j) => j !== i).map((x) => ({ ...x }));
            pool.push({ c: col, a: 1 });
            best = Math.max(best, energyValue(u, pool));
          });
          gain += Math.max(0, best - base);
        }
        return gain ? gain / 3 : -120;
      }
      const AI_SKILL_PROFILE_SCORERS = Object.freeze({
        mutualEnergyTrade({ g, u, tar, action, profile }) {
          if (!tar) return 0;
          const n = Number(action?.interactionAmount || 0),
            baseDamage = Number(profile?.baseDamage || 0),
            perEnergyDamage = Number(profile?.perEnergyDamage || 0),
            selfDamagePerEnergy = Number(profile?.selfDamagePerEnergy || perEnergyDamage),
            mean = baseDamage * (g.variance ? 0.83 : 1),
            targetTotal = Number(tar.hp || 0) + Number(tar.shield || 0),
            kill = mean + perEnergyDamage * n >= targetTotal,
            selfDies = selfDamagePerEnergy * n >= Number(u.hp || 0),
            trade = n * selfDamagePerEnergy * (u.hp < 150 ? 1.35 : u.hp < 230 ? 0.75 : 0.4);
          let score = mean + n * 14 - trade + (kill ? 190 : 0) - (selfDies && !kill ? 480 : selfDies ? 70 : 0);
          if (!kill && n > 0 && tar.hp > mean + perEnergyDamage * n) score -= n * 12;
          return score;
        },
      });
      function scoreSkillAiProfile(g, u, tar, action, skill) {
        const profile = skill?.aiProfile, scorer = profile && AI_SKILL_PROFILE_SCORERS[profile.kind];
        return typeof scorer === "function" ? Number(scorer({ g, u, tar, action, skill, profile }) || 0) : 0;
      }
      function actionHeuristicBase(g, t, s, a) {
        let u = unitAt(g, t, s),
          stall = g.stats.turns > 240;
        if (a.type === "deploy") {
          let x = g.teams[t].bench.find((v) => v.uid === a.unit),
            trap = g.teams[t].traps[s];
          return 80 + x.hp * 0.15 - (trap && !isStatusImmune(x, trap.status) ? 35 : 0);
        }
        if (a.type === "scheduled" || a.type === "sequence") {
          let k = u.scheduledAction?.kind;
          return k === "bloom" ? 260 : k === "rubyFollow" ? 150 : 120;
        }
        if (a.type === "dualPass") return -8;
        if (a.type === "switch") {
          let x = g.teams[t].bench[a.bench],
            trap = g.teams[t].traps[s];
          return (
            5 +
            (u.hp / u.maxHp < 0.3 ? 28 : 0) +
            x.hp * 0.06 -
            (trap && !isStatusImmune(x, trap.status) ? 30 : 0) -
            (stall ? 90 : 0)
          );
        }
        if (a.type === "gather") {
          let sc = gatherPotential(u);
          if (status(u, "burn")) sc -= 35;
          return sc;
        }
        let sk = card(u).skills.find((x) => x.id === a.skill),
          tar =
            sk?.target === "enemySlot"
              ? null
              : a.target
                ? unitAt(g, a.target.team, a.target.slot)
                : null,
          sc = 10;
        if (!sk) return sc;
        if (sk.target === "enemySlot") {
          let tr = g.teams[other(t)].traps[a.target.slot];
          return tr ? 3 : 30 + (g.teams[other(t)].bench.length ? 18 : 0);
        }
        if (skillDefHasTag(sk, "free")) sc += 45;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "bloomingLife")) sc += u.hp < 70 ? 80 : 210;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "lifeRain"))
          sc +=
            g.teams[t].field.reduce(
              (n, x) => n + (x ? Math.max(0, x.maxHp - x.hp) : 0),
              0,
            ) *
              0.6 +
            55;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "iceRoar")) {
          let frozenEnemies = g.teams[other(t)].field.filter(
              (x) => x && !x.dead && !isStatusImmune(x, "freeze"),
            ).length,
            ally = g.teams[t].field.find((x) => x && x.uid !== u.uid);
          sc +=
            frozenEnemies * 55 -
            (ally && !isStatusImmune(ally, "freeze") && !cardHasCapability(ally, "irumaCleanse")
              ? 45
              : 0);
          if (canPayFrom(u, "WR*", "rider")) sc += 80;
        }
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "rampage"))
          sc +=
            (1 + (a.extra || 0)) * 10 +
            (tar && tar.hp <= (1 + (a.extra || 0)) * 10 ? 120 : 0) -
            (a.extra || 0) * 8;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "shatterLance") && tar)
          sc += 70 + status(tar, "freeze") * 30;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "firestorm")) sc += 150 + (u.hp > 60 ? -25 : 0);
        if (skillDefHasRuntimeKind(sk, "openingGuard")) sc += u.shield ? 45 : 125;
        if (skillDefHasRuntimeKind(sk, "dynamicDefenseSmash"))
          sc += 85 + (6 - (a.costOverride?.length || 0)) * 5;
        if (skillDefHasTag(sk, "copy")) sc += 75;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "holyFlame") && tar) {
          let d = Math.max(0, 100 - 20 * allEnergy(tar));
          sc += d + (100 - d) * 0.9;
        }
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "prawnCharge") && tar) sc += hasNullifyTrait(tar) ? 170 : 125;
        for (const e of sk.effects) {
          if (e.type === "damage" && tar) {
            let r = damageRange(g, u, tar, e, sk),
              hits = e.hits || 1,
              mean = r.mean * hits,
              killProb =
                tar.shield && !e.shieldMelt
                  ? 0
                  : r.min * hits >= tar.hp + (e.shieldMelt ? tar.shield : 0)
                    ? 1
                    : r.max * hits < tar.hp
                      ? 0
                      : (r.max * hits - tar.hp) /
                        Math.max(1, (r.max - r.min) * hits);
            sc += mean + killProb * 120;
            if (e.shieldMelt) sc += Math.min(tar.shield, mean) * 0.5;
            if (card(tar).reflect) {
              let refl = card(tar).reflect * hits;
              if (refl >= u.hp) sc -= 220;
              if (tar.hideHits && mean / hits <= 30) sc -= 65;
            }
          }
          if (e.type === "heal" && tar)
            sc +=
              Math.min(e.amount, tar.maxHp - tar.hp) * 1.3 -
              (tar.hp === tar.maxHp ? 50 : 0) -
              (stall ? 80 : 0);
          if (e.type === "shield" && tar) {
            let useful = Math.min(
              e.amount,
              Math.max(0, (e.max ?? 999) - tar.shield),
            );
            sc +=
              useful * (tar.shield > 45 ? 0.15 : 0.8) - (useful === 0 ? 50 : 0);
          }
          if (e.type === "status" && tar) {
            let irumaChance = g.knowledge?.unknownCandidates?.includes("iruma")
              ? 1 / Math.max(1, g.knowledge.unknownCandidates.length)
              : 0;
            sc +=
              (e.status === "poison"
                ? 35 + allEnergy(tar) * 7
                : e.status === "slow"
                  ? 24
                  : 18) *
              (1 - irumaChance);
          }
          if (e.type === "statusChance" && tar) sc += e.chance * 24;
          if (e.type === "startSequence" && tar) sc += 100;
          if (e.type === "clearFieldEnergy") {
            let enemy = g.teams[other(t)].field.reduce(
                (n, x) => n + (x && !x.dead ? allEnergy(x) : 0),
                0,
              ),
              ally = g.teams[t].field.reduce(
                (n, x) => n + (x && !x.dead ? allEnergy(x) : 0),
                0,
              );
            sc += (enemy - ally) * 22;
          }
        }
        if (status(u, "burn")) sc += 28;
        return sc;
      }

      }

      function execute(g,t,s,a,opt={}) {
        const thisArg=this;
        const base=(...args)=>executeCoreBase.apply(thisArg,args);
        const main=(...args)=>executeTimeStage(base,...args);
        const inner=(...args)=>{
          const invoke=()=>{
            if(window.__megaSpectatorR18?.canExecute?.(...args)===false)return false;
            return main(...args);
          };
          const overflow=window.__megaEnergyOverflowChoice;
          return typeof overflow?.withExecuteContext==="function"
            ? overflow.withExecuteContext(invoke,...args)
            : invoke();
        };
        const middle=(...args)=>{
          const energy=window.EnergyCanonicalizationEngine;
          return typeof energy?.execute==="function" ? energy.execute(inner,...args) : inner(...args);
        };
        return typeof actionTransactionExecuteStage==="function"
          ? actionTransactionExecuteStage.call(thisArg,middle,g,t,s,a,opt)
          : middle(g,t,s,a,opt);
      }
      function endTurn(g, t, s, usedSkill) {
        const __managed = typeof StateFactory!=="undefined" && typeof BattleEventEngine!=="undefined" && typeof TurnEngine!=="undefined";
        let __beforeSerial=Number(g?.turnSerial)||0;
        if (__managed) {
          StateFactory.decorate(g);
          if(g.ended||g.winner)return false;
          const currentPair=g.order?.[g.turn];
          if(currentPair&&(currentPair[0]!==t||String(currentPair[1])!==String(s)))return false;
          const key=`${g.battleId}:${g.turnSerial}:${t}:${s}`;
          if(g._r48LastEndTurnKey===key)return false;
          g._r48LastEndTurnKey=key; g.phase="TURN_END";
          __beforeSerial=Number(g.turnSerial)||0;
        }
        let __result;
        __endTurnCore: {
        let u = unitAt(g, t, s);
        const hadSeal = u ? !!status(u,"traitSeal") : false;
        const summonOwner = !!u && skillsOf(u).some(sk=>skillDefHasTag(sk,"summon"));
        const regen = !!u && cardHasCapability(u,"regenerationTrait") && traitsActive(u) && !status(u,"traitSeal");
        if (regen) for (const st of Object.keys(u.statuses||{})) if (st!=="traitSeal" && u.statuses[st]>1) u.statuses[st]--;
        if (u && !u.dead) {
          if (status(u, "burn") && !usedSkill)
            nonAttackDamage(g, u, 30, "화상");
          for (const st of Object.keys(u.statuses)) {
            if (u.statuses[st] > 0) {
              u.statuses[st]--;
              log(g, `${u.name}: ${STATUS[st]} ${u.statuses[st]}턴 남음.`);
              if (!u.statuses[st]) {
                delete u.statuses[st];
                queueCombatNotice(g, u, `${STATUS[st]} 종료`, "cure");
              }
            }
          }
          for (const sid of Object.keys(u.skillSeals || {})) {
            u.skillSeals[sid]--;
            if (u.skillSeals[sid] <= 0) {
              let skillName = card(u).skills.find((x) => x.id === sid)?.name || "기술";
              delete u.skillSeals[sid];
              queueCombatNotice(g, u, `${skillName} 기술봉인 종료`, "cure");
            }
          }
          u.turns++;
          u.turnSkillCostReduction = 0;
          if (cardHasCapability(u, "horizonFlow") && Number(u.hp) <= Number(u.maxHp) / 2) {
            const units = [...g.teams[t].field, ...g.teams[t].bench].filter(x => x && !x.dead);
            const countColor = color => units.reduce((sum, x) => sum + energyStore(x).reduce((n, entry) => n + (entry.c === color ? Number(entry.a || 0) : 0), 0), 0);
            const white = countColor("W"), black = countColor("B");
            const flowTargets = cardCapability(u, "horizonFlowTargets", {});
            if (white > black && flowTargets.W) FormFamilyOwner.transition(g, u, flowTargets.W, "시간의 흐름");
            else if (black > white && flowTargets.B) FormFamilyOwner.transition(g, u, flowTargets.B, "시간의 흐름");
          }
        }
        g.stats.turns++;
        let dealt = ["P", "A"]
          .flatMap((x) => [
            ...g.teams[x].field,
            ...g.teams[x].bench,
            ...g.teams[x].graveyard,
          ])
          .filter(Boolean)
          .reduce((n, x) => n + x.damageDealt, 0);
        if (dealt > g.lastDamageTotal) {
          g.lastDamageTotal = dealt;
          g.noDamageTurns = 0;
        } else g.noDamageTurns++;
        if (!g.winner && g.noDamageTurns >= 120 && u && !u.dead) {

        }
        if (!g.winner && g.stats.turns >= 800 && u && !u.dead) {

          } else g.circuitChain=null;
        }

        }
        if (!__managed) return __result;
        if((Number(g.turnSerial)||0)===__beforeSerial){delete g._r48LastEndTurnKey;return __result;}
        const skipped=TurnEngine.normalizeDeadEmptyOrderEntries(g); StateFactory.updateActive(g);
        BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"TURN_ENDED",{teamId:t,payload:{slot:s,usedSkill:!!usedSkill,turnSerial:__beforeSerial,skippedEmptySlots:skipped}});
        if(!g.ended)BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"TURN_STARTED",{teamId:g.activeTeamId,sourceEntityId:g.activeEntityId,payload:{turnSerial:g.turnSerial,round:g.round}});
        return __result===undefined?true:__result;
      }
      
      function checkWinner(g) {
        if (g.winner === "P" || g.winner === "A") return;
        actionFaultHit("victory-before");
        let p = aliveUnits(g.teams.P).length,
          a = aliveUnits(g.teams.A).length;
        if (!p || !a) {
          g.winner = p ? "P" : "A";
          actionFaultHit("victory-after");
          actionFaultHit("terminal-pending-after");
          actionFaultHit("terminal-commit-before");
          log(g, g.winner === "P" ? "플레이어 승리!" : "AI 승리!", "turn");
        } else actionFaultHit("victory-after");
      }
      function gatherPotential(u) {
        let max = card(u).maxEnergy || 4;
        if (totalEnergy(u) < max) return 20 - totalEnergy(u) * 2;
        let base = energyValue(u),
          gain = 0,
          store = energyStore(u);
        for (const col of ["R", "W", "B"]) {
          let best = base;
          store.forEach((e, i) => {
            if (e.a < 1) return;
            let pool = store.filter((_, j) => j !== i).map((x) => ({ ...x }));
            pool.push({ c: col, a: 1 });
            best = Math.max(best, energyValue(u, pool));
          });
          gain += Math.max(0, best - base);
        }
        return gain ? gain / 3 : -120;
      }
      const AI_SKILL_PROFILE_SCORERS = Object.freeze({
        mutualEnergyTrade({ g, u, tar, action, profile }) {
          if (!tar) return 0;
          const n = Number(action?.interactionAmount || 0),
            baseDamage = Number(profile?.baseDamage || 0),
            perEnergyDamage = Number(profile?.perEnergyDamage || 0),
            selfDamagePerEnergy = Number(profile?.selfDamagePerEnergy || perEnergyDamage),
            mean = baseDamage * (g.variance ? 0.83 : 1),
            targetTotal = Number(tar.hp || 0) + Number(tar.shield || 0),
            kill = mean + perEnergyDamage * n >= targetTotal,
            selfDies = selfDamagePerEnergy * n >= Number(u.hp || 0),
            trade = n * selfDamagePerEnergy * (u.hp < 150 ? 1.35 : u.hp < 230 ? 0.75 : 0.4);
          let score = mean + n * 14 - trade + (kill ? 190 : 0) - (selfDies && !kill ? 480 : selfDies ? 70 : 0);
          if (!kill && n > 0 && tar.hp > mean + perEnergyDamage * n) score -= n * 12;
          return score;
        },
      });
      function scoreSkillAiProfile(g, u, tar, action, skill) {
        const profile = skill?.aiProfile, scorer = profile && AI_SKILL_PROFILE_SCORERS[profile.kind];
        return typeof scorer === "function" ? Number(scorer({ g, u, tar, action, skill, profile }) || 0) : 0;
      }
      function actionHeuristicBase(g, t, s, a) {
        let u = unitAt(g, t, s),
          stall = g.stats.turns > 240;
        if (a.type === "deploy") {
          let x = g.teams[t].bench.find((v) => v.uid === a.unit),
            trap = g.teams[t].traps[s];
          return 80 + x.hp * 0.15 - (trap && !isStatusImmune(x, trap.status) ? 35 : 0);
        }
        if (a.type === "scheduled" || a.type === "sequence") {
          let k = u.scheduledAction?.kind;
          return k === "bloom" ? 260 : k === "rubyFollow" ? 150 : 120;
        }
        if (a.type === "dualPass") return -8;
        if (a.type === "switch") {
          let x = g.teams[t].bench[a.bench],
            trap = g.teams[t].traps[s];
          return (
            5 +
            (u.hp / u.maxHp < 0.3 ? 28 : 0) +
            x.hp * 0.06 -
            (trap && !isStatusImmune(x, trap.status) ? 30 : 0) -
            (stall ? 90 : 0)
          );
        }
        if (a.type === "gather") {
          let sc = gatherPotential(u);
          if (status(u, "burn")) sc -= 35;
          return sc;
        }
        let sk = card(u).skills.find((x) => x.id === a.skill),
          tar =
            sk?.target === "enemySlot"
              ? null
              : a.target
                ? unitAt(g, a.target.team, a.target.slot)
                : null,
          sc = 10;
        if (!sk) return sc;
        if (sk.target === "enemySlot") {
          let tr = g.teams[other(t)].traps[a.target.slot];
          return tr ? 3 : 30 + (g.teams[other(t)].bench.length ? 18 : 0);
        }
        if (skillDefHasTag(sk, "free")) sc += 45;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "bloomingLife")) sc += u.hp < 70 ? 80 : 210;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "lifeRain"))
          sc +=
            g.teams[t].field.reduce(
              (n, x) => n + (x ? Math.max(0, x.maxHp - x.hp) : 0),
              0,
            ) *
              0.6 +
            55;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "iceRoar")) {
          let frozenEnemies = g.teams[other(t)].field.filter(
              (x) => x && !x.dead && !isStatusImmune(x, "freeze"),
            ).length,
            ally = g.teams[t].field.find((x) => x && x.uid !== u.uid);
          sc +=
            frozenEnemies * 55 -
            (ally && !isStatusImmune(ally, "freeze") && !cardHasCapability(ally, "irumaCleanse")
              ? 45
              : 0);
          if (canPayFrom(u, "WR*", "rider")) sc += 80;
        }
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "rampage"))
          sc +=
            (1 + (a.extra || 0)) * 10 +
            (tar && tar.hp <= (1 + (a.extra || 0)) * 10 ? 120 : 0) -
            (a.extra || 0) * 8;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "shatterLance") && tar)
          sc += 70 + status(tar, "freeze") * 30;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "firestorm")) sc += 150 + (u.hp > 60 ? -25 : 0);
        if (skillDefHasRuntimeKind(sk, "openingGuard")) sc += u.shield ? 45 : 125;
        if (skillDefHasRuntimeKind(sk, "dynamicDefenseSmash"))
          sc += 85 + (6 - (a.costOverride?.length || 0)) * 5;
        if (skillDefHasTag(sk, "copy")) sc += 75;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "holyFlame") && tar) {
          let d = Math.max(0, 100 - 20 * allEnergy(tar));
          sc += d + (100 - d) * 0.9;
        }
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "prawnCharge") && tar) sc += hasNullifyTrait(tar) ? 170 : 125;
        for (const e of sk.effects) {
          if (e.type === "damage" && tar) {
            let r = damageRange(g, u, tar, e, sk),
              hits = e.hits || 1,
              mean = r.mean * hits,
              killProb =
                tar.shield && !e.shieldMelt
                  ? 0
                  : r.min * hits >= tar.hp + (e.shieldMelt ? tar.shield : 0)
                    ? 1
                    : r.max * hits < tar.hp
                      ? 0
                      : (r.max * hits - tar.hp) /
                        Math.max(1, (r.max - r.min) * hits);
            sc += mean + killProb * 120;
            if (e.shieldMelt) sc += Math.min(tar.shield, mean) * 0.5;
            if (card(tar).reflect) {
              let refl = card(tar).reflect * hits;
              if (refl >= u.hp) sc -= 220;
              if (tar.hideHits && mean / hits <= 30) sc -= 65;
            }
          }
          if (e.type === "heal" && tar)
            sc +=
              Math.min(e.amount, tar.maxHp - tar.hp) * 1.3 -
              (tar.hp === tar.maxHp ? 50 : 0) -
              (stall ? 80 : 0);
          if (e.type === "shield" && tar) {
            let useful = Math.min(
              e.amount,
              Math.max(0, (e.max ?? 999) - tar.shield),
            );
            sc +=
              useful * (tar.shield > 45 ? 0.15 : 0.8) - (useful === 0 ? 50 : 0);
          }
          if (e.type === "status" && tar) {
            let irumaChance = g.knowledge?.unknownCandidates?.includes("iruma")
              ? 1 / Math.max(1, g.knowledge.unknownCandidates.length)
              : 0;
            sc +=
              (e.status === "poison"
                ? 35 + allEnergy(tar) * 7
                : e.status === "slow"
                  ? 24
                  : 18) *
              (1 - irumaChance);
          }
          if (e.type === "statusChance" && tar) sc += e.chance * 24;
          if (e.type === "startSequence" && tar) sc += 100;
          if (e.type === "clearFieldEnergy") {
            let enemy = g.teams[other(t)].field.reduce(
                (n, x) => n + (x && !x.dead ? allEnergy(x) : 0),
                0,
              ),
              ally = g.teams[t].field.reduce(
                (n, x) => n + (x && !x.dead ? allEnergy(x) : 0),
                0,
              );
            sc += (enemy - ally) * 22;
          }
        }
        if (status(u, "burn")) sc += 28;
        return sc;
      }

      if(!old.dead&&unit.dead) BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"ENTITY_DIED",{...common,payload:{cardId:unit.card}});
    }
  };

  const DeathEngine = Object.freeze({
    reconcile(g) {
      StateFactory.decorate(g); const deaths=[];
      for (const unit of Object.values(g.entities||{})) if (Number(unit.hp)<=0 && !unit.dead && typeof checkDeath === "function") { checkDeath(g,unit,null); deaths.push(unit.entityId||unit.uid); }
      EntityRegistry.sync(g); return deaths;
    },
    alive:(g,team)=>typeof aliveUnits === "function" ? aliveUnits(g.teams[team]) : []
  });

  const TurnEngine = (() => {
    const hasLiveReplacement = (g,team) => !!g?.teams?.[team]?.bench?.some(unit => unit && !unit.dead);
    const currentOrderEntryCanResolve = g => {
      const [team,slot] = g?.order?.[g?.turn] || [];
      if (team == null || slot == null) return false;
      const unit = typeof unitAt === "function" ? unitAt(g,team,slot) : null;
      return !!(unit && !unit.dead) || hasLiveReplacement(g,team);
    };
    const normalizeDeadEmptyOrderEntries = g => {
      const skipped = [];
      const orderLength = Math.max(0,Number(g?.order?.length || 0));
      if (!orderLength || g?.winner || g?.ended) return skipped;
      for (let inspected = 0; inspected < orderLength && !currentOrderEntryCanResolve(g); inspected++) {
        const [team,slot] = g.order[g.turn] || [];
        const unit = team != null && slot != null && typeof unitAt === "function" ? unitAt(g,team,slot) : null;
        skipped.push(Object.freeze({teamId:team ?? null,slot:slot ?? null,entityId:unit?.entityId || unit?.uid || null,turnSerial:Number(g.turnSerial||0)}));
        BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"EMPTY_SLOT_TURN_SKIPPED",{
          teamId:team ?? null,
          sourceEntityId:unit?.entityId || unit?.uid || null,
          payload:{slot:slot ?? null,turnSerial:Number(g.turnSerial||0),reason:"DEAD_OR_EMPTY_WITHOUT_LIVE_REPLACEMENT"},
        });
        g.turn = (Number(g.turn || 0) + 1) % orderLength;
        g.turnSerial = Number(g.turnSerial || 0) + 1;
        g.preparedKey = null;
        g.dualActorController = null;
        if (g.turn === 0) g.round = Math.max(1,Number(g.round||1)) + 1;
        if (typeof checkWinner === "function") checkWinner(g);
        if (g.winner || g.ended) break;
      }
      return skipped;
    };

    return Object.freeze({
      end:(...args)=>endTurn(...args),
      current:g=>g?.order?.[g.turn] || null,
      phase:g=>g?.phase || "IDLE",
      normalizeDeadEmptyOrderEntries,
      currentOrderEntryCanResolve,
    });
  })();

  // Live authoritative actions use the transaction boundary. Guest request submission and simulation do not mutate authority state.
  const isBossGuestRequestOnlyExecution = (g,t,s,opt={}) => {
    if (opt?.sim || !g || g !== currentGame() || !g?.bossRaid?.online) return false;
    if (p2p?.role !== "guest" || p2p?.applyingRemoteAction || t !== "P") return false;
    const active = g.order?.[g.turn];
    if (!active || active[0] !== "P" || Number(active[1]) !== Number(s)) return false;
    if (!window.__redTimeoraBossRaid?.localControlsCurrent?.()) return false;
    const unit = unitAt(g,t,s);
    return unit ? unit.bossOwner === "guest" : g.bossRaid.slotOwners?.[s] === "guest";
  };
  const classifyR48Execution = (g,t,s,action,opt={}) => {
    const live = !opt?.sim && g && (g === currentGame() || opt?.r48Live === true);
    if (!live) return "simulation";
    const standardGuestRequest = g === currentGame() && g?.mode === "online" && !g?.bossRaid &&
      p2p?.role === "guest" && !p2p?.applyingRemoteAction && t === onlineSide;
    const bossGuestRequest = isBossGuestRequestOnlyExecution(g,t,s,opt);
    return standardGuestRequest || bossGuestRequest ? "request-only" : "authoritative-execution";
  };
  function actionTransactionExecuteStage(next,g,t,s,action,opt={}) {
      const disposition = classifyR48Execution(g,t,s,action,opt);
      if (disposition !== "authoritative-execution") return next(g,t,s,action,opt);
      StateFactory.decorate(g);
      if (g.ended || g.winner) return false;
      if (action && (action.type === "scheduled" || action.type === "sequence")) {
        const scheduled = ensureScheduledActionIdentity(g, unitAt(g, t, s));
        if (scheduled) {
          action.scheduledActionId ||= scheduled.scheduledActionId;
          action.sourceInstanceId ||= scheduled.sourceInstanceId;
          action.actionId ||= scheduledExecutionId(g, scheduled);
        }
      }
      const tx = ActionTransactionManager.begin(g,t,s,action);
      if (tx.duplicate) return false;
      const lock = InputLockManager.lock("action-transaction",tx.transactionId);
      try {
        ActionFaultInjector.hit("validation-after");
        ActionFaultInjector.hit("draft-after");
        if (action?.type === "skill") {
          ActionFaultInjector.hit("payment-before");
          BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"SKILL_SELECTED",{teamId:t,sourceEntityId:g.activeEntityId,payload:{skillId:action.skill,copySkillId:action.copySkill||null}});
          if (action.target) BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"TARGET_SELECTED",{teamId:action.target.team,targetSlotId:IdFactory.slot(action.target.team,"field",action.target.slot),recipientEntityId:action.target.uid||null,payload:{target:action.target}});
          BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"PAYMENT_STARTED",{teamId:t,sourceEntityId:g.activeEntityId,payload:{skillId:action.skill,paymentPlan:action.paymentPlan||null}});
        }
        const result = next(g,t,s,action,opt);
        if (!result) { ActionTransactionManager.rollback(g,tx,"action-rejected"); return false; }
        const deaths = DeathEngine.reconcile(g);
        StateFactory.updateActive(g);
        ActionFaultInjector.hit("event-publication-before");
        const commitEvent = BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"ACTION_EXECUTED",{teamId:t,sourceEntityId:tx.snapshot ? null : g.activeEntityId,payload:{actionId:tx.actionId,actionType:action?.type,deaths}});
        diffUnits(tx.snapshot,g,commitEvent?.eventId||null,tx.eventSequenceAtStart);
        ActionFaultInjector.hit("remote-ack-before");
        ActionTransactionManager.commit(g,tx);
        StateValidator.validate(g);
        return true;
      } catch (error) {
        const isGradeValidation = error?.code === "INVALID_CARD_GRADE";
        if (!isGradeValidation) console.error("[R48 ActionEngine] execution failed",{battleId:g?.battleId,team:t,slot:s,action,error});
        const sourceEntityId = tx.snapshot?.activeEntityId || g.activeEntityId || null;
        ActionTransactionManager.rollback(g,tx,"exception",error);
        if (isGradeValidation) {
          BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"GRADE_VALIDATION_FAILED",{
            teamId:t,
            sourceEntityId,
            recipientEntityId:action?.target?.uid || null,
            payload:{ ...(error.gradeValidationDetail || {}), message:String(error.message || error) },
          });
        }
        throw error;
      } finally { InputLockManager.unlock(lock); }
  }

      close(reason="scope-close"){if(this.closed)return false;this.closed=true;for(const key of [...this.resources.keys()])this.cancelResource(key);for(const row of [...this.promises]){try{row.cancel?.(reason);}catch{}}this.promises.clear();this.transactions.clear();this.abortController.abort(reason);this.record("scope-closed",{reason});return true;}
      debug(){return{generation:this.generation,phase:this.phase,projectionEpoch:this.projectionEpoch,closed:this.closed,resourceCount:this.resources.size,promiseCount:this.promises.size,transactionCount:this.transactions.size,trace:this.trace.slice(-160)};}

      function revealUnit(g, t, u) {
        if (u && !g.revealed[t].includes(u.uid)) {
          g.revealed[t].push(u.uid);
          g.publicHistory.push({
            type: "reveal",
            team: t,
            uid: u.uid,

          });

      function deployCharacter(g, t, s, uid) {
        const resonancePending = !!g?.teams?.[t]?.pendingElixianResonance?.[s];
        const tm = g?.teams?.[t];
        if (
          !g ||
          !tm ||
          !Array.isArray(tm.field) ||
          !Array.isArray(tm.bench) ||
          !Array.isArray(tm.graveyard) ||
          !Number.isInteger(s) ||
          s < 0 ||
          s >= tm.field.length ||
          typeof uid !== "string"
        ) return false;
        const current = g.order?.[g.turn];
        if (!current || current[0] !== t || current[1] !== s) return false;
        const matches = tm.bench
          .map((unit, index) => unit?.uid === uid ? { unit, index } : null)
          .filter(Boolean);
        if (matches.length !== 1) return false;
        const { unit: incoming, index } = matches[0];
        if (!incoming) return false;
        for (const teamId of Object.keys(g.teams || {})) {
          const field = g.teams[teamId]?.field;
          if (Array.isArray(field) && field.some((unit) => unit?.uid === uid)) return false;
        }
        const occupant = tm.field[s];
        if (occupant && !occupant.dead) return false;
        if (g.bossRaid?.online && t === "P") {
          const slotOwner = g.bossRaid.slotOwners?.[s];
          if (!slotOwner || incoming.bossOwner !== slotOwner) return false;
        }
        if (occupant?.dead) tm.graveyard.push(occupant);
        const u = tm.bench.splice(index, 1)[0];
        tm.field[s] = u;
        initializeDeployment(g, u);
        revealUnit(g, t, u);
        log(
          g,
          `${t === "P" ? "플레이어" : "AI"} 슬롯 ${s + 1}: ${u.name} 출전.`,
        );
        let trap = tm.traps[s];
        if (trap) {
          applyStatus(
            g,
            u,
            trap.status,
            trap.duration,
            findUnit(g, trap.owner),
          );
          tm.traps[s] = null;
          log(g, `전기태클 발동 후 소멸.`);
        }
        const deployed = unitAt(g,t,s);
        if (resonancePending && deployed && !deployed.dead) {
          deployed.elixianResonance = true;
          g.teams[t].pendingElixianResonance[s] = false;
          log(g, `${deployed.name}: 엘릭시안의 잔향을 이어받아 자기 차례 시작마다 HP 10을 영구 회복한다.`);
          queueVisual(g,{type:"elixianPulseFx",uid:deployed.uid,resonance:true});
        }
        syncAromaCombatFlags(g);
        return true;
      }
      function switchCharacter(g, t, s, bi) {
        let tm = g.teams[t],
          out = tm.field[s],
          incoming = tm.bench[bi];
        if (!incoming) return;
        clearOwnedField(g, out, "후퇴");
        if (cardHasCapability(out, "eclipse")) out.eclipseEligible = false;
        if (out.sequence || out.scheduledAction) {
          out.sequence = null;
          out.scheduledAction = null;
          log(g, `${out.name}: 필드를 떠나 예약 효과 취소.`);
        }
        tm.bench[bi] = out;
        tm.field[s] = incoming;
        initializeDeployment(g, incoming);
        revealUnit(g, t, incoming);
        out.switches++;
        out.lastTarget = null;
        g.dualActorController = null;

          applyStatus(
            g,
            incoming,
            trap.status,
            trap.duration,
            findUnit(g, trap.owner),
          );
          tm.traps[s] = null;
          log(g, `전기태클 발동 후 소멸.`);
        }
        syncAromaCombatFlags(g);
      }
      function recordPaidSkill(g, t, u, paid) {
        if (timeFeatureReady && u) u._timePaidRecord={turnSerial:g.turnSerial,amount:(paid||[]).reduce((n,e)=>n+Number(e?.a||0),0),paid:(paid||[]).map(e=>({...e}))};
        if (!paid.length) return;
        for (const aw of g.teams[other(t)].field)
          if (
            aw &&
            !aw.dead &&
            aw.scheduledAction?.kind === "bloom"
          )
            aw.scheduledAction.evaded[u.uid] = { paid: true };
      }
      function advanceDualOrEnd(g, t, s, u, usedSkill, switched = false) {
        if (g?._timeDeferTurnAdvance && !g?._timeCommittingTurnAdvance) {
          g.pendingClockTurnAdvance = {
            teamId: t,
            slotIndex: s,
            actorEntityId: u?.entityId || u?.uid || null,
            usedSkill: !!usedSkill,
            switched: !!switched,
            requestedAt: performance.now(),
            turnSerial: Number(g.turnSerial || 0),
          };
          return;
        }
        if (
          cardHasCapability(u, "dualActor") &&
          !switched &&
          dualActorController.current(g, u) === "beast" &&
          !u.dead &&
          unitAt(g, t, s)?.uid === u.uid
        ) {
          u.dualUsedSkill = !!(u.dualUsedSkill || usedSkill);
          dualActorController.toRider(g, u);
          log(g, `${u.name}: 빙마 행동 종료 → 기수 행동 시작.`, "turn");
          return;
        }
        let used = !!(usedSkill || u.dualUsedSkill);
        u.dualUsedSkill = false;
        endTurn(g, t, s, used);
      }
      function executeCoreBase(g, t, s, a, { sim = false } = {}) {
        if (a?.type === "deploy") {
          const current = g.order?.[g.turn];
          if (!current || current[0] !== t || current[1] !== s || typeof a.unit !== "string") return false;
          const deployed = deployCharacter(g, t, s, a.unit);
          if (!deployed) return false;
          endTurn(g, t, s, false);
          return true;
        }
        const preUnit = unitAt(g, t, s);
        if (preUnit && !preUnit.dead && preUnit.speedDelayTurns > 0 && a?.type !== "speedDelay") return false;
        if (a?.type === "speedDelay") {
          if (!preUnit || preUnit.dead || preUnit.speedDelayTurns <= 0) return false;
          preUnit.speedDelayTurns--;
          preUnit.lastAction = "speedDelay";
          log(g, `${preUnit.name}: Speed ${card(preUnit).speed} 출전 지연으로 행동을 넘김.`, "turn");
          endTurn(g, t, s, false);
          return true;
        }
        if (a?.type === "switch") {
          if (!preUnit || preUnit.dead || !canSwitch(g, t, preUnit) || !g.teams[t].bench[a.bench]) return false;
          const cost = switchCost(preUnit), actor = cardHasCapability(preUnit, "dualActor") ? "beast" : null, plan = a.switchPaymentPlan;
          const currentPlan = plan ? Math.abs(plan.R + plan.W + plan.B - cost) < 0.001 && reqAcceptsCounts(parseCost("*".repeat(cost)), plan) && canPayReq(preUnit, { R:plan.R, W:plan.W, B:plan.B, any:0, wb:0 }, actor) : null;
          if (plan && !currentPlan) return false;
          const paid = plan ? spendPaymentPlan(preUnit, plan, actor) : spendFrom(preUnit, "*".repeat(cost), [], actor);
          g.teams[t].switchUses = (g.teams[t].switchUses || 0) + 1;
          log(g, `${preUnit.name}: 교체 코스트 ${cost ? `${cost}E` : "무료"} 지불, 진영 교체 ${g.teams[t].switchUses}/3.`);
          a = { ...a, switchPaid: paid };
        }
        prepareCurrentTurn(g);
        let u = unitAt(g, t, s);
        if (!a) return false;
        if (!u || u.dead) return false;
        if (a.type === "scheduled" || a.type === "sequence") {
          const scheduledExecution = u.scheduledAction
            ? beginScheduledActionExecution(g, u)
            : null;
          if (scheduledExecution?.duplicate) {
            endTurn(g, t, s, true);
            return true;
          }
          const executed = executeScheduled(g, t, s, u, sim);
          if (!executed) return false;
          if (scheduledExecution) completeScheduledActionExecution(g, scheduledExecution);
          endTurn(g, t, s, true);
          return true;
        }
        let usedSkill = false,
          switched = false;
        if (a.type === "dualPass") {
          u.lastAction = "pass";
          log(
            g,
            `${u.name}: ${a.actor === "rider" ? "기수" : "빙마"} 행동을 넘김.`,
          );
        } else if (a.type === "gather") {
          let actor = a.actor || null,
            store = energyStore(u, actor),
            amt = status(u, "slow") ? 0.5 : 1,
            colors = naturalColors(g, t, u, amt, actor, "natural-gather"),
            existingHalf = amt === 0.5 ? store.find((e) => e.a === 0.5) : null,
            col = a.gatherProposal
              ? consumeNaturalGatherProposal(g, t, s, u, amt, actor, a.gatherProposal, "natural-gather")
              : existingHalf
                ? existingHalf.c

                  ? a.color
                  : g.rng.pick(colors),
            max = card(u).maxEnergy || 4;

          if (existingHalf)

            );
          }

        } else if (a.type === "switch") {
          switchCharacter(g, t, s, a.bench);
          u.lastAction = "switch";
          switched = true;
        } else if (a.type === "skill") {

          if (plan) {

            );

          } else if (!canPayAction(g, t, u, cost, actor)) return false;

      function execute(g,t,s,a,opt={}) {
        const thisArg=this;
        const base=(...args)=>executeCoreBase.apply(thisArg,args);
        const main=(...args)=>executeTimeStage(base,...args);
        const inner=(...args)=>{
          const invoke=()=>{
            if(window.__megaSpectatorR18?.canExecute?.(...args)===false)return false;
            return main(...args);
          };
          const overflow=window.__megaEnergyOverflowChoice;
          return typeof overflow?.withExecuteContext==="function"
            ? overflow.withExecuteContext(invoke,...args)
            : invoke();
        };
        const middle=(...args)=>{
          const energy=window.EnergyCanonicalizationEngine;
          return typeof energy?.execute==="function" ? energy.execute(inner,...args) : inner(...args);
        };

          ? actionTransactionExecuteStage.call(thisArg,middle,g,t,s,a,opt)
          : middle(g,t,s,a,opt);

          /*
           * 드래곤메모리는 껍데기 기술이 아니라 실제로 복사해 실행한 기술을
           * 기준으로 딥마인드를 판정한다. 공격기를 복사한 경우에는 발동하지 않는다.
           */
          if (isNonAttackSkill(sk))
            triggerDeepMind(g, t, u, target || u, a, sk);
          if (cardHasCapability(u, "selectiveEnergyTrait") && traitsActive(u))
            requestTraitEnergy(g, u, "R", 1, "기술 사용");
        }
        advanceDualOrEnd(g, t, s, u, usedSkill, switched);
        return true;
      }
      function execute(g,t,s,a,opt={}) {
        const thisArg=this;
        const base=(...args)=>executeCoreBase.apply(thisArg,args);
        const main=(...args)=>executeTimeStage(base,...args);
        const inner=(...args)=>{
          const invoke=()=>{
            if(window.__megaSpectatorR18?.canExecute?.(...args)===false)return false;
            return main(...args);
          };
          const overflow=window.__megaEnergyOverflowChoice;
          return typeof overflow?.withExecuteContext==="function"
            ? overflow.withExecuteContext(invoke,...args)
            : invoke();
        };
        const middle=(...args)=>{
          const energy=window.EnergyCanonicalizationEngine;
          return typeof energy?.execute==="function" ? energy.execute(inner,...args) : inner(...args);
        };
        return typeof actionTransactionExecuteStage==="function"
          ? actionTransactionExecuteStage.call(thisArg,middle,g,t,s,a,opt)
          : middle(g,t,s,a,opt);
      }
      function endTurn(g, t, s, usedSkill) {
        const __managed = typeof StateFactory!=="undefined" && typeof BattleEventEngine!=="undefined" && typeof TurnEngine!=="undefined";
        let __beforeSerial=Number(g?.turnSerial)||0;
        if (__managed) {
          StateFactory.decorate(g);
          if(g.ended||g.winner)return false;
          const currentPair=g.order?.[g.turn];
          if(currentPair&&(currentPair[0]!==t||String(currentPair[1])!==String(s)))return false;
          const key=`${g.battleId}:${g.turnSerial}:${t}:${s}`;
          if(g._r48LastEndTurnKey===key)return false;
          g._r48LastEndTurnKey=key; g.phase="TURN_END";
          __beforeSerial=Number(g.turnSerial)||0;
        }
        let __result;
        __endTurnCore: {
        let u = unitAt(g, t, s);
        const hadSeal = u ? !!status(u,"traitSeal") : false;
        const summonOwner = !!u && skillsOf(u).some(sk=>skillDefHasTag(sk,"summon"));
        const regen = !!u && cardHasCapability(u,"regenerationTrait") && traitsActive(u) && !status(u,"traitSeal");
        if (regen) for (const st of Object.keys(u.statuses||{})) if (st!=="traitSeal" && u.statuses[st]>1) u.statuses[st]--;
        if (u && !u.dead) {
          if (status(u, "burn") && !usedSkill)
            nonAttackDamage(g, u, 30, "화상");
          for (const st of Object.keys(u.statuses)) {
            if (u.statuses[st] > 0) {
              u.statuses[st]--;
              log(g, `${u.name}: ${STATUS[st]} ${u.statuses[st]}턴 남음.`);
              if (!u.statuses[st]) {
                delete u.statuses[st];
                queueCombatNotice(g, u, `${STATUS[st]} 종료`, "cure");
              }
            }
          }
          for (const sid of Object.keys(u.skillSeals || {})) {
            u.skillSeals[sid]--;
            if (u.skillSeals[sid] <= 0) {
              let skillName = card(u).skills.find((x) => x.id === sid)?.name || "기술";
              delete u.skillSeals[sid];
              queueCombatNotice(g, u, `${skillName} 기술봉인 종료`, "cure");
            }
          }
          u.turns++;
          u.turnSkillCostReduction = 0;
          if (cardHasCapability(u, "horizonFlow") && Number(u.hp) <= Number(u.maxHp) / 2) {
            const units = [...g.teams[t].field, ...g.teams[t].bench].filter(x => x && !x.dead);
            const countColor = color => units.reduce((sum, x) => sum + energyStore(x).reduce((n, entry) => n + (entry.c === color ? Number(entry.a || 0) : 0), 0), 0);
            const white = countColor("W"), black = countColor("B");
            const flowTargets = cardCapability(u, "horizonFlowTargets", {});
            if (white > black && flowTargets.W) FormFamilyOwner.transition(g, u, flowTargets.W, "시간의 흐름");
            else if (black > white && flowTargets.B) FormFamilyOwner.transition(g, u, flowTargets.B, "시간의 흐름");
          }
        }
        g.stats.turns++;
        let dealt = ["P", "A"]
          .flatMap((x) => [
            ...g.teams[x].field,
            ...g.teams[x].bench,
            ...g.teams[x].graveyard,
          ])
          .filter(Boolean)
          .reduce((n, x) => n + x.damageDealt, 0);
        if (dealt > g.lastDamageTotal) {
          g.lastDamageTotal = dealt;
          g.noDamageTurns = 0;
        } else g.noDamageTurns++;
        if (!g.winner && g.noDamageTurns >= 120 && u && !u.dead) {
          let raw = 10 + Math.floor((g.noDamageTurns - 120) / 8) * 10,
            streak = g.noDamageTurns;
          nonAttackDamage(g, u, raw, "교착 압박");
          g.noDamageTurns = streak;
        }
        if (!g.winner && g.stats.turns >= 800 && u && !u.dead) {
          let raw = 40 + Math.floor((g.stats.turns - 800) / 16) * 40,
            streak = g.noDamageTurns;
          nonAttackDamage(g, u, raw, "결판 압박");
          g.noDamageTurns = streak;
        }
        g.turn = (g.turn + 1) % 4;
        g.turnSerial++;
        g.preparedKey = null;
        g.dualActorController = null;
        if (g.turn === 0) g.round++;
        checkWinner(g);

        if (summonOwner && u && !u.dead) processJunglesSummons(g,t,u);
        if (g.circuitChain?.uid === u?.uid) {
          if (!u || u.dead || unitAt(g,t,s)?.uid !== u.uid) g.circuitChain=null;
          else if (g.circuitChain.remaining>0) {
            g.circuitChain.remaining--;
            g.turn=g.order.findIndex(x=>x[0]===t&&x[1]===s);
            g.preparedKey=null;
            log(g,`${u.name}: 전류가속 추가 차례 시작 (${g.circuitChain.remaining}회 더 남음).`,"turn");
          } else g.circuitChain=null;
        }
        if (reactiveEnergyRule(u) && hadSeal !== !!status(u,"traitSeal")) syncReactiveEnergyMaxHp(g,u,"특성봉인 종료",true);
      
        }
        if (!__managed) return __result;
        if((Number(g.turnSerial)||0)===__beforeSerial){delete g._r48LastEndTurnKey;return __result;}
        const skipped=TurnEngine.normalizeDeadEmptyOrderEntries(g); StateFactory.updateActive(g);
        BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"TURN_ENDED",{teamId:t,payload:{slot:s,usedSkill:!!usedSkill,turnSerial:__beforeSerial,skippedEmptySlots:skipped}});
        if(!g.ended)BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"TURN_STARTED",{teamId:g.activeTeamId,sourceEntityId:g.activeEntityId,payload:{turnSerial:g.turnSerial,round:g.round}});
        return __result===undefined?true:__result;
      }
      
      function checkWinner(g) {
        if (g.winner === "P" || g.winner === "A") return;
        actionFaultHit("victory-before");
        let p = aliveUnits(g.teams.P).length,
          a = aliveUnits(g.teams.A).length;
        if (!p || !a) {
          g.winner = p ? "P" : "A";
          actionFaultHit("victory-after");
          actionFaultHit("terminal-pending-after");
          actionFaultHit("terminal-commit-before");
          log(g, g.winner === "P" ? "플레이어 승리!" : "AI 승리!", "turn");
        } else actionFaultHit("victory-after");
      }
      function gatherPotential(u) {
        let max = card(u).maxEnergy || 4;
        if (totalEnergy(u) < max) return 20 - totalEnergy(u) * 2;
        let base = energyValue(u),
          gain = 0,
          store = energyStore(u);
        for (const col of ["R", "W", "B"]) {
          let best = base;
          store.forEach((e, i) => {
            if (e.a < 1) return;
            let pool = store.filter((_, j) => j !== i).map((x) => ({ ...x }));
            pool.push({ c: col, a: 1 });
            best = Math.max(best, energyValue(u, pool));
          });
          gain += Math.max(0, best - base);
        }
        return gain ? gain / 3 : -120;
      }
      const AI_SKILL_PROFILE_SCORERS = Object.freeze({
        mutualEnergyTrade({ g, u, tar, action, profile }) {
          if (!tar) return 0;
          const n = Number(action?.interactionAmount || 0),
            baseDamage = Number(profile?.baseDamage || 0),
            perEnergyDamage = Number(profile?.perEnergyDamage || 0),
            selfDamagePerEnergy = Number(profile?.selfDamagePerEnergy || perEnergyDamage),
            mean = baseDamage * (g.variance ? 0.83 : 1),
            targetTotal = Number(tar.hp || 0) + Number(tar.shield || 0),
            kill = mean + perEnergyDamage * n >= targetTotal,
            selfDies = selfDamagePerEnergy * n >= Number(u.hp || 0),
            trade = n * selfDamagePerEnergy * (u.hp < 150 ? 1.35 : u.hp < 230 ? 0.75 : 0.4);
          let score = mean + n * 14 - trade + (kill ? 190 : 0) - (selfDies && !kill ? 480 : selfDies ? 70 : 0);
          if (!kill && n > 0 && tar.hp > mean + perEnergyDamage * n) score -= n * 12;
          return score;
        },
      });
      function scoreSkillAiProfile(g, u, tar, action, skill) {
        const profile = skill?.aiProfile, scorer = profile && AI_SKILL_PROFILE_SCORERS[profile.kind];
        return typeof scorer === "function" ? Number(scorer({ g, u, tar, action, skill, profile }) || 0) : 0;
      }
      function actionHeuristicBase(g, t, s, a) {
        let u = unitAt(g, t, s),
          stall = g.stats.turns > 240;
        if (a.type === "deploy") {
          let x = g.teams[t].bench.find((v) => v.uid === a.unit),
            trap = g.teams[t].traps[s];
          return 80 + x.hp * 0.15 - (trap && !isStatusImmune(x, trap.status) ? 35 : 0);
        }
        if (a.type === "scheduled" || a.type === "sequence") {
          let k = u.scheduledAction?.kind;
          return k === "bloom" ? 260 : k === "rubyFollow" ? 150 : 120;
        }
        if (a.type === "dualPass") return -8;
        if (a.type === "switch") {
          let x = g.teams[t].bench[a.bench],
            trap = g.teams[t].traps[s];
          return (
            5 +
            (u.hp / u.maxHp < 0.3 ? 28 : 0) +
            x.hp * 0.06 -
            (trap && !isStatusImmune(x, trap.status) ? 30 : 0) -
            (stall ? 90 : 0)
          );
        }
        if (a.type === "gather") {
          let sc = gatherPotential(u);
          if (status(u, "burn")) sc -= 35;
          return sc;
        }
        let sk = card(u).skills.find((x) => x.id === a.skill),
          tar =
            sk?.target === "enemySlot"
              ? null
              : a.target
                ? unitAt(g, a.target.team, a.target.slot)
                : null,
          sc = 10;
        if (!sk) return sc;
        if (sk.target === "enemySlot") {
          let tr = g.teams[other(t)].traps[a.target.slot];
          return tr ? 3 : 30 + (g.teams[other(t)].bench.length ? 18 : 0);
        }
        if (skillDefHasTag(sk, "free")) sc += 45;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "bloomingLife")) sc += u.hp < 70 ? 80 : 210;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "lifeRain"))
          sc +=
            g.teams[t].field.reduce(
              (n, x) => n + (x ? Math.max(0, x.maxHp - x.hp) : 0),
              0,
            ) *
              0.6 +
            55;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "iceRoar")) {
          let frozenEnemies = g.teams[other(t)].field.filter(
              (x) => x && !x.dead && !isStatusImmune(x, "freeze"),
            ).length,
            ally = g.teams[t].field.find((x) => x && x.uid !== u.uid);
          sc +=
            frozenEnemies * 55 -
            (ally && !isStatusImmune(ally, "freeze") && !cardHasCapability(ally, "irumaCleanse")
              ? 45
              : 0);
          if (canPayFrom(u, "WR*", "rider")) sc += 80;
        }
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "rampage"))
          sc +=
            (1 + (a.extra || 0)) * 10 +
            (tar && tar.hp <= (1 + (a.extra || 0)) * 10 ? 120 : 0) -
            (a.extra || 0) * 8;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "shatterLance") && tar)
          sc += 70 + status(tar, "freeze") * 30;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "firestorm")) sc += 150 + (u.hp > 60 ? -25 : 0);
        if (skillDefHasRuntimeKind(sk, "openingGuard")) sc += u.shield ? 45 : 125;
        if (skillDefHasRuntimeKind(sk, "dynamicDefenseSmash"))
          sc += 85 + (6 - (a.costOverride?.length || 0)) * 5;
        if (skillDefHasTag(sk, "copy")) sc += 75;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "holyFlame") && tar) {
          let d = Math.max(0, 100 - 20 * allEnergy(tar));
          sc += d + (100 - d) * 0.9;
        }
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "prawnCharge") && tar) sc += hasNullifyTrait(tar) ? 170 : 125;
        for (const e of sk.effects) {
          if (e.type === "damage" && tar) {
            let r = damageRange(g, u, tar, e, sk),
              hits = e.hits || 1,
              mean = r.mean * hits,
              killProb =
                tar.shield && !e.shieldMelt
                  ? 0
                  : r.min * hits >= tar.hp + (e.shieldMelt ? tar.shield : 0)
                    ? 1
                    : r.max * hits < tar.hp
                      ? 0
                      : (r.max * hits - tar.hp) /
                        Math.max(1, (r.max - r.min) * hits);
            sc += mean + killProb * 120;
            if (e.shieldMelt) sc += Math.min(tar.shield, mean) * 0.5;
            if (card(tar).reflect) {
              let refl = card(tar).reflect * hits;
              if (refl >= u.hp) sc -= 220;
              if (tar.hideHits && mean / hits <= 30) sc -= 65;
            }
          }
          if (e.type === "heal" && tar)
            sc +=
              Math.min(e.amount, tar.maxHp - tar.hp) * 1.3 -
              (tar.hp === tar.maxHp ? 50 : 0) -
              (stall ? 80 : 0);
          if (e.type === "shield" && tar) {
            let useful = Math.min(
              e.amount,
              Math.max(0, (e.max ?? 999) - tar.shield),
            );
            sc +=
              useful * (tar.shield > 45 ? 0.15 : 0.8) - (useful === 0 ? 50 : 0);
          }
          if (e.type === "status" && tar) {
            let irumaChance = g.knowledge?.unknownCandidates?.includes("iruma")
              ? 1 / Math.max(1, g.knowledge.unknownCandidates.length)
              : 0;
            sc +=
              (e.status === "poison"
                ? 35 + allEnergy(tar) * 7
                : e.status === "slow"
                  ? 24
                  : 18) *
              (1 - irumaChance);
          }
          if (e.type === "statusChance" && tar) sc += e.chance * 24;
          if (e.type === "startSequence" && tar) sc += 100;
          if (e.type === "clearFieldEnergy") {
            let enemy = g.teams[other(t)].field.reduce(
                (n, x) => n + (x && !x.dead ? allEnergy(x) : 0),
                0,
              ),
              ally = g.teams[t].field.reduce(
                (n, x) => n + (x && !x.dead ? allEnergy(x) : 0),
                0,
              );
            sc += (enemy - ally) * 22;
          }
        }
        if (status(u, "burn")) sc += 28;
        return sc;
      }
      function pendingBloom(g, t) {
        return (
          g.teams[other(t)].field.find(
            (x) =>
              x &&
              !x.dead &&
              x.scheduledAction?.kind === "bloom",
          ) || null
        );
      }
      function actionPaysEnergy(u, a) {
        if (a.type !== "skill") return false;
        let sk = card(u).skills.find((x) => x.id === a.skill),
          cost = a.costOverride ?? sk?.cost ?? "";
        return costTokens(cost).length + (status(u, "p
===== executeTimeStage count=2 =====
--- executeTimeStage #1 offset=503792 ---
: effectiveSkillCost(g, t, u, baseSk, baseSk?.cost),
            actor = a.actor || null;
          if (!baseSk || u.skillSeals?.[baseSk.id] || !skillRequirementsMet(u, baseSk)) return false;
          if (a.copySkill) {
            let source = CARDS.find((c) => c.id === a.copyCard);
            sk = source?.skills.find((x) => x.id === a.copySkill);
            if (!sk) return false;
          }
          let plan = a.paymentPlan;
          if (plan) {
            let current = findPaymentPlan(
              g,
              t,
              u,
              skillDefHasEffect(baseSk, "hurricaneDive") ? "W*" : cost,
              actor,
              (c) =>
                Math.abs(c.R - plan.R) < 0.001 &&
                Math.abs(c.W - plan.W) < 0.001 &&
                Math.abs(c.B - plan.B) < 0.001,
            );
            if (!current) return false;
          } else if (!canPayAction(g, t, u, cost, actor)) return false;
          let noTarget = ["field"].includes(baseSk.target),
            target =
              baseSk.target === "enemySlot" || noTarget
                ? null
                : unitAt(g, a.target.team, a.target.slot);
          if (
            !noTarget &&
            baseSk.target !== "enemySlot" &&
            (!target || target.dead)
          )
            return false;
          if (skillDefHasRuntimeKind(baseSk, "openingGuard")) {
            if (u.firstGuardUsesRemaining <= 0) return false;
            u.firstGuardUsesRemaining = 0;
            log(g, `${u.name}: 퍼스트가드 사용 횟수 소모(0/1).`);
          }
          let paid = plan
            ? spendPaymentPlan(u, plan, actor)
            : spendAction(g, t, u, cost, a.prefer, actor);
          if (skillDefHasEffect(baseSk, "hurricaneDive") && a.extraW) {
            let extra = spendReq(
              u,
              { R: 0, W: a.extraW, B: 0, any: 0, wb: 0 },
              [],
              actor,
            );
            paid.push(...extra);
          }
          log(
            g,
            `${u.name}: ${a.copySkill ? `드래곤메모리로 ${sk.name}` : baseSk.name} 사용 (실제 코스트 ${paid.map((x) => x.c + (x.a === 0.5 ? "½" : "")).join("") || "없음"}).`,
          );
          const effectiveActionTargets = (() => {
            const requested = (a.targetUids || []).map(uid => findUnit(g, uid)).filter(unit => unit && !unit.dead);
            if (requested.length) return requested;
            if (effectiveSkillTarget(u, baseSk) === "enemyField") return g.teams[other(t)].field.filter(unit => unit && !unit.dead);
            return [target].filter(unit => unit && !unit.dead);
          })();
          const effectiveActionSpec = a.effectiveActionSpec || PlusStarModifierOwner.actionSpec(g, t, s, u, baseSk, a, effectiveActionTargets.map(unit => ({...targetRef(g, other(t), unit), uid:unit.uid})));
          a.effectiveActionSpec = effectiveActionSpec;
          queueSkillVisual(g, u, target, baseSk, a.copySkill ? sk.effects : baseSk.effects);
          if (skillDefHasEffect(baseSk, "startSequence")) {
            const actionIdentity = effectiveActionSpec.actionId;
            queueVisual(g, {
              type:"sugarSwamp", uid:u.uid, targetUids:effectiveActionTargets.map(x => x.uid),
              sourceInstanceId:u.uid, sourceTeam:t, targetTeam:other(t), sourceSlot:s,
              skillId:baseSk.id, actionId:actionIdentity,
              transactionId:effectiveActionSpec.transactionId, vfxSequenceId:`${actionIdentity}:sugar-swamp:initial`, stageIndex:0,
            });
          }
          if (card(u).skillHeal) heal(g, u, card(u).skillHeal, u, { reason:"skill-passive-heal", effectId:baseSk.id, sim });
          if (skillDefHasRuntimeKind(baseSk, "openingGuard")) u.usedOnce[baseSk.id] = true;
          let effects = a.copySkill ? sk.effects : baseSk.effects,
            healTarget = a.healTargetUid ? findUnit(g, a.healTargetUid) : null,
            damageBatch = DamageBatchOwner.begin(g, `${u.uid}:${baseSk.id}:${g.turnSerial}`);
          try {
          for (const e of effects) {
            const context = {
              team:t, slot:a.target?.slot ?? s, paid, sim, extra:a.extra,
              extraEnergyPlan:a.extraEnergyPlan, extraW:a.extraW || 0, split:a.split,
              healTarget, sealedSkillId:a.sealedSkillId, action:a, effectiveActionSpec,
            };
            if (skillDefHasEffect(baseSk, "startSequence") && effectiveActionSpec.effectiveTargetShape === "enemyField") {
              if (e.type === "startSequence") {
                effectApply(g, u, effectiveActionTargets[0] || target || u, e, sk, context);
                continue;
              }
              for (const batchTarget of effectiveActionTargets) {
                if (!batchTarget?.dead || ["clearFieldEnergy"].includes(e.type)) effectApply(g, u, batchTarget, e, sk, context);
              }
              continue;
            }
            if (target?.dead && !["clearFieldEnergy", "startSequence"].includes(e.type)) continue;
            effectApply(g, u, target || u, e, sk, context);
          }
          } finally { DamageBatchOwner.end(g); }
          u.skillUses++;
          if (skillDefHasTag(baseSk, "copy")) u.memoryUses = (u.memoryUses || 0) + 1;
          u.lastAction = "skill";
          u.lastSkill = a.copySkill || baseSk.id;
          if (
            target &&
            effects.some((e) =>
              [
                "damage",
                "proportionalDamage",
                "firstBurst",
                "rampage",
                "shatterLance",
                "firestorm",

                "holyFlame",
                "lightCandelabra",
                "grandField",
                "hurricaneDive",
              ].includes(e.type),
            )
          )
            u.lastTarget = target.uid;
          usedSkill = true;
          recordPaidSkill(g, t, u, paid);
          /*
           * 드래곤메모리는 껍데기 기술이 아니라 실제로 복사해 실행한 기술을
           * 기준으로 딥마인드를 판정한다. 공격기를 복사한 경우에는 발동하지 않는다.
           */
          if (isNonAttackSkill(sk))
            triggerDeepMind(g, t, u, target || u, a, sk);
          if (cardHasCapability(u, "selectiveEnergyTrait") && traitsActive(u))
            requestTraitEnergy(g, u, "R", 1, "기술 사용");
        }
        advanceDualOrEnd(g, t, s, u, usedSkill, switched);
        return true;
      }
      function execute(g,t,s,a,opt={}) {
        const thisArg=this;
        const base=(...args)=>executeCoreBase.apply(thisArg,args);
        const main=(...args)=>executeTimeStage(base,...args);
        const inner=(...args)=>{
          const invoke=()=>{
            if(window.__megaSpectatorR18?.canExecute?.(...args)===false)return false;
            return main(...args);
          };
          const overflow=window.__megaEnergyOverflowChoice;
          return typeof overflow?.withExecuteContext==="function"
            ? overflow.withExecuteContext(invoke,...args)
            : invoke();
        };
        const middle=(...args)=>{
          const energy=window.EnergyCanonicalizationEngine;
          return typeof energy?.execute==="function" ? energy.execute(inner,...args) : inner(...args);
        };
        return typeof actionTransactionExecuteStage==="function"
          ? actionTransactionExecuteStage.call(thisArg,middle,g,t,s,a,opt)
          : middle(g,t,s,a,opt);
      }
      function endTurn(g, t, s, usedSkill) {
        const __managed = typeof StateFactory!=="undefined" && typeof BattleEventEngine!=="undefined" && typeof TurnEngine!=="undefined";
        let __beforeSerial=Number(g?.turnSerial)||0;
        if (__managed) {
          StateFactory.decorate(g);
          if(g.ended||g.winner)return false;
          const currentPair=g.order?.[g.turn];
          if(currentPair&&(currentPair[0]!==t||String(currentPair[1])!==String(s)))return false;
          const key=`${g.battleId}:${g.turnSerial}:${t}:${s}`;
          if(g._r48LastEndTurnKey===key)return false;
          g._r48LastEndTurnKey=key; g.phase="TURN_END";
          __beforeSerial=Number(g.turnSerial)||0;
        }
        let __result;
        __endTurnCore: {
        let u = unitAt(g, t, s);
        const hadSeal = u ? !!status(u,"traitSeal") : false;
        const summonOwner = !!u && skillsOf(u).some(sk=>skillDefHasTag(sk,"summon"));
        const regen = !!u && cardHasCapability(u,"regenerationTrait") && traitsActive(u) && !status(u,"traitSeal");
        if (regen) for (const st of Object.keys(u.statuses||{})) if (st!=="traitSeal" && u.statuses[st]>1) u.statuses[st]--;
        if (u && !u.dead) {
          if (status(u, "burn") && !usedSkill)
            nonAttackDamage(g, u, 30, "화상");
          for (const st of Object.keys(u.statuses)) {
            if (u.statuses[st] > 0) {
              u.statuses[st]--;
              log(g, `${u.name}: ${STATUS[st]} ${u.statuses[st]}턴 남음.`);
              if (!u.statuses[st]) {
                delete u.statuses[st];
                queueCombatNotice(g, u, `${STATUS[st]} 종료`, "cure");
              }
            }
          }
          for (const sid of Object.keys(u.skillSeals || {})) {
            u.skillSeals[sid]--;
            if (u.skillSeals[sid] <= 0) {
              let skillName = card(u).skills.find((x) => x.id === sid)?.name || "기술";
              delete u.skillSeals[sid];
              queueCombatNotice(g, u, `${skillName} 기술봉인 종료`, "cure");
            }
          }
          u.turns++;
          u.turnSkillCostReduction = 0;
          if (cardHasCapability(u, "horizonFlow") && Number(u.hp) <= Number(u.maxHp) / 2) {
            const units = [...g.teams[t].field, ...g.teams[t].bench].filter(x => x && !x.dead);
            const countColor = color => units.reduce((sum, x) => sum + energyStore(x).reduce((n, entry) => n + (entry.c === color ? Number(entry.a || 0) : 0), 0), 0);
            const white = countColor("W"), black = countColor("B");
            const flowTargets = cardCapability(u, "horizonFlowTargets", {});
            if (white > black && flowTargets.W) FormFamilyOwner.transition(g, u, flowTargets.W, "시간의 흐름");
            else if (black > white && flowTargets.B) FormFamilyOwner.transition(g, u, flowTargets.B, "시간의 흐름");
          }
        }
        g.stats.turns++;
        let dealt = ["P", "A"]
          .flatMap((x) => [
            ...g.teams[x].field,
            ...g.teams[x].bench,
            ...g.teams[x].graveyard,
          ])
          .filter(Boolean)
          .reduce((n, x) => n + x.damageDealt, 0);
        if (dealt > g.lastDamageTotal) {
          g.lastDamageTotal = dealt;
          g.noDamageTurns = 0;
        } else g.noDamageTurns++;
        if (!g.winner && g.noDamageTurns >= 120 && u && !u.dead) {
          let raw = 10 + Math.floor((g.noDamageTurns - 120) / 8) * 10,
            streak = g.noDamageTurns;
          nonAttackDamage(g, u, raw, "교착 압박");
          g.noDamageTurns = streak;
        }
        if (!g.winner && g.stats.turns >= 800 && u && !u.dead) {
          let raw = 40 + Math.floor((g.stats.turns - 800) / 16) * 40,
            streak = g.noDamageTurns;
          nonAttackDamage(g, u, raw, "결판 압박");
          g.noDamageTurns = streak;
        }
        g.turn = (g.turn + 1) % 4;
        g.turnSerial++;
        g.preparedKey = null;
        g.dualActorController = null;
        if (g.turn === 0) g.round++;
        checkWinner(g);

        if (summonOwner && u && !u.dead) processJunglesSummons(g,t,u);
        if (g.circuitChain?.uid === u?.uid) {
          if (!u || u.dead || unitAt(g,t,s)?.uid !== u.uid) g.circuitChain=null;
          else if (g.circuitChain.remaining>0) {
            g.circuitChain.remaining--;
            g.turn=g.order.findIndex(x=>x[0]===t&&x[1]===s);
            g.preparedKey=null;
            log(g,`${u.name}: 전류가속 추가 차례 시작 (${g.circuitChain.remaining}회 더 남음).`,"turn");
          } else g.circuitChain=null;
        }
        if (reactiveEnergyRule(u) && hadSeal !== !!status(u,"traitSeal")) syncReactiveEnergyMaxHp(g,u,"특성봉인 종료",true);
      
        }
        if (!__managed) return __result;
        if((Number(g.turnSerial)||0)===__beforeSerial){delete g._r48LastEndTurnKey;return __result;}
        const skipped=TurnEngine.normalizeDeadEmptyOrderEntries(g); StateFactory.updateActive(g);
        BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"TURN_ENDED",{teamId:t,payload:{slot:s,usedSkill:!!usedSkill,turnSerial:__beforeSerial,skippedEmptySlots:skipped}});
        if(!g.ended)BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"TURN_STARTED",{teamId:g.activeTeamId,sourceEntityId:g.activeEntityId,payload:{turnSerial:g.turnSerial,round:g.round}});
        return __result===undefined?true:__result;
      }
      
      function checkWinner(g) {
        if (g.winner === "P" || g.winner === "A") return;
        actionFaultHit("victory-before");
        let p = aliveUnits(g.teams.P).length,
          a = aliveUnits(g.teams.A).length;
        if (!p || !a) {
          g.winner = p ? "P" : "A";
          actionFaultHit("victory-after");
          actionFaultHit("terminal-pending-after");
          actionFaultHit("terminal-commit-before");
          log(g, g.winner === "P" ? "플레이어 승리!" : "AI 승리!", "turn");
        } else actionFaultHit("victory-after");
      }
      function gatherPotential(u) {
        let max = card(u).maxEnergy || 4;
        if (totalEnergy(u) < max) return 20 - totalEnergy(u) * 2;
        let base = energyValue(u),
          gain = 0,
          store = energyStore(u);
        for (const col of ["R", "W", "B"]) {
          let best = base;
          store.forEach((e, i) => {
            if (e.a < 1) return;
            let pool = store.filter((_, j) => j !== i).map((x) => ({ ...x }));
            pool.push({ c: col, a: 1 });
            best = Math.max(best, energyValue(u, pool));
          });
          gain += Math.max(0, best - base);
        }
        return gain ? gain / 3 : -120;
      }
      const AI_SKILL_PROFILE_SCORERS = Object.freeze({
        mutualEnergyTrade({ g, u, tar, action, profile }) {
          if (!tar) return 0;
          const n = Number(action?.interactionAmount || 0),
            baseDamage = Number(profile?.baseDamage || 0),
            perEnergyDamage = Number(profile?.perEnergyDamage || 0),
            selfDamagePerEnergy = Number(profile?.selfDamagePerEnergy || perEnergyDamage),
            mean = baseDamage * (g.variance ? 0.83 : 1),
            targetTotal = Number(tar.hp || 0) + Number(tar.shield || 0),
            kill = mean + perEnergyDamage * n >= targetTotal,
            selfDies = selfDamagePerEnergy * n >= Number(u.hp || 0),
            trade = n * selfDamagePerEnergy * (u.hp < 150 ? 1.35 : u.hp < 230 ? 0.75 : 0.4);
          let score = mean + n * 14 - trade + (kill ? 190 : 0) - (selfDies && !kill ? 480 : selfDies ? 70 : 0);
          if (!kill && n > 0 && tar.hp > mean + perEnergyDamage * n) score -= n * 12;
          return score;
        },
      });
      function scoreSkillAiProfile(g, u, tar, action, skill) {
        const profile = skill?.aiProfile, scorer = profile && AI_SKILL_PROFILE_SCORERS[profile.kind];
        return typeof scorer === "function" ? Number(scorer({ g, u, tar, action, skill, profile }) || 0) : 0;
      }
      function actionHeuristicBase(g, t, s, a) {
        let u = unitAt(g, t, s),
          stall = g.stats.turns > 240;
        if (a.type === "deploy") {
          let x = g.teams[t].bench.find((v) => v.uid === a.unit),
            trap = g.teams[t].traps[s];
          return 80 + x.hp * 0.15 - (trap && !isStatusImmune(x, trap.status) ? 35 : 0);
        }
        if (a.type === "scheduled" || a.type === "sequence") {
          let k = u.scheduledAction?.kind;
          return k === "bloom" ? 260 : k === "rubyFollow" ? 150 : 120;
        }
        if (a.type === "dualPass") return -8;
        if (a.type === "switch") {
          let x = g.teams[t].bench[a.bench],
            trap = g.teams[t].traps[s];
          return (
            5 +
            (u.hp / u.maxHp < 0.3 ? 28 : 0) +
            x.hp * 0.06 -
            (trap && !isStatusImmune(x, trap.status) ? 30 : 0) -
            (stall ? 90 : 0)
          );
        }
        if (a.type === "gather") {
          let sc = gatherPotential(u);
          if (status(u, "burn")) sc -= 35;
          return sc;
        }
        let sk = card(u).skills.find((x) => x.id === a.skill),
          tar =
            sk?.target === "enemySlot"
              ? null
              : a.target
                ? unitAt(g, a.target.team, a.target.slot)
                : null,
          sc = 10;
        if (!sk) return sc;
        if (sk.target === "enemySlot") {
          let tr = g.teams[other(t)].traps[a.target.slot];
          return tr ? 3 : 30 + (g.teams[other(t)].bench.length ? 18 : 0);
        }
        if (skillDefHasTag(sk, "free")) sc += 45;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "bloomingLife")) sc += u.hp < 70 ? 80 : 210;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "lifeRain"))
          sc +=
            g.teams[t].field.reduce(
              (n, x) => n + (x ? Math.max(0, x.maxHp - x.hp) : 0),
              0,
            ) *
              0.6 +
            55;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "iceRoar")) {
          let frozenEnemies = g.teams[other(t)].field.filter(
              (x) => x && !x.dead && !isStatusImmune(x, "freeze"),
            ).length,
            ally = g.teams[t].field.find((x) => x && x.uid !== u.uid);
          sc +=
            frozenEnemies * 55 -
            (ally && !isStatusImmune(ally, "freeze") && !cardHasCapability(ally, "irumaCleanse")
              ? 45
              : 0);
          if (canPayFrom(u, "WR*", "rider")) sc += 80;
        }
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "rampage"))
          sc +=
            (1 + (a.extra || 0)) * 10 +
            (tar && tar.hp <= (1 + (a.extra || 0)) * 10 ? 120 : 0) -
            (a.extra || 0) * 8;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "shatterLance") && tar)
          sc += 70 + status(tar, "freeze") * 30;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "firestorm")) sc += 150 + (u.hp > 60 ? -25 : 0);
        if (skillDefHasRuntimeKind(sk, "openingGuard")) sc += u.shield ? 45 : 125;
        if (skillDefHasRuntimeKind(sk, "dynamicDefenseSmash"))
          sc += 85 + (6 - (a.costOverride?.length || 0)) * 5;
        if (skillDefHasTag(sk, "copy")) sc += 75;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "holyFlame") && tar) {
          let d = Math.max(0, 100 - 20 * allEnergy(tar));
          sc += d + (100 - d) * 0.9;
        }
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "prawnCharge") && tar) sc += hasNullifyTrait(tar) ? 170 : 125;
        for (const e of sk.effects) {
          if (e.type === "damage" && tar) {
            let r = damageRange(g, u, tar, e, sk),
              hits = e.hits || 1,
              mean = r.mean * hits,
              killProb =
                tar.shield && !e.shieldMelt
                  ? 0
                  : r.min * hits >= tar.hp + (e.shieldMelt ? tar.shield : 0)
                    ? 1
                    : r.max * hits < tar.hp
                      ? 0
                      : (r.max * hits - tar.hp) /
                        Math.max(1, (r.max - r.min) * hits);
            sc += mean + killProb * 120;
            if (e.shieldMelt) sc += Math.min(tar.shield, mean) * 0.5;
            if (card(tar).reflect) {
              let refl = card(tar).reflect * hits;
              if (refl >= u.hp) sc -= 220;
              if (tar.hideHits && mean / hits <= 30) sc -= 65;
            }
          }
          if (e.type === "heal" && tar)
            sc +=
              Math.min(e.amount, tar.maxHp - tar.hp) * 1.3 -
              (tar.hp === tar.maxHp ? 50 : 0) -
              (stall ? 80 : 0);
          if (e.type === "shield" && tar) {
            let useful = Math.min(
              e.amount,
              Math.max(0, (e.max ?? 999) - tar.shield),
            );
            sc +=
              useful * (tar.shield > 45 ? 0.15 : 0.8) - (useful === 0 ? 50 : 0);
          }
          if (e.type === "status" && tar) {
            let irumaChance = g.knowledge?.unknownCandidates?.includes("iruma")
              ? 1 / Math.max(1, g.knowledge.unknownCandidates.length)
              : 0;
            sc +=
              (e.status === "poison"
                ? 35 + allEnergy(tar) * 7
                : e.status === "slow"
                  ? 24
                  : 18) *
              (1 - irumaChance);
          }
          if (e.type === "statusChance" && tar) sc += e.chance * 24;
          if (e.type === "startSequence" && tar) sc += 100;
          if (e.type === "clearFieldEnergy") {
            let enemy = g.teams[other(t)].field.reduce(
                (n, x) => n + (x && !x.dead ? allEnergy(x) : 0),
                0,
              ),
              ally = g.teams[t].field.reduce(
                (n, x) => n + (x && !x.dead ? allEnergy(x) : 0),
                0,
              );
            sc += (enemy - ally) * 22;
          }
        }
        if (status(u, "burn")) sc += 28;
        return sc;
      }

        );

          cost = a.costOverride ?? sk?.cost ?? "";
        return costTokens(cost).length + (status(u, "poison") ? 1 : 0) > 0;
      }

      function timeSetInputLock(on, holdBusy=true) {
        const manager = window.InputLockManager;
        if (manager?.setClockLock) return manager.setClockLock(!!on,{holdBusy,owner:"timeSetInputLock"});
        // Bootstrap fallback: this path only exists while the final owner has not loaded yet.
        timeInputLocked = !!on;
        document.body.classList.toggle("time-input-locked",timeInputLocked);
        for (const root of [document.querySelector(".app"), document.querySelector("#modal")]) {
          if (!root) continue;
          try { root.inert = timeInputLocked; } catch (error) { window.__megaRecordRecoveredError(error, "timeSetInputLock:bootstrap"); }
          root.setAttribute("aria-hidden", timeInputLocked ? "true" : "false");
        }
        if (on && holdBusy && typeof busy !== "undefined") {
          if (!busy) timeInputLockBusyOwned = true;
          busy = true;
        }
        if (!on && timeInputLockBusyOwned) {
          timeInputLockBusyOwned = false;
          if (!(typeof onlinePendingCommand !== "undefined" && onlinePendingCommand)) busy = false;
        }
        return timeInputLocked;
      }
