      function timeExecuteLayerBody(next,g,t,s,a,opt={}) {
        const actor = unitAt(g,t,s);
        const isGuestRequest = g===game && g?.mode==="online" && p2p?.role==="guest" && !p2p.applyingRemoteAction && t===onlineSide;
        if (isGuestRequest) return executeSupportLayer(next,g,t,s,a,opt);

        if (a?.type === "gather" && cardHasCapability(actor, "timeControl")) {
          const accelerationActive = timeAccelerationIsActive(g,actor);
          /* 로컬 미리보기와 권위 상태가 어긋나도 기본 1E로 조용히 행동을 소비하지 않는다. */
          if (a.timeAccelerationChoice && !accelerationActive) return false;
          if (accelerationActive) return timeExecuteTimeoraGather(g,t,s,actor,a,opt);
        }
        if (a?.type === "skill" && actionSkillHasTag(a, "copy") && actor?.memoryUsed) return false;
        if (a?.type === "skill" && actionSkillHasTag(a, "copy") && a.copySourceUid) {
          const source = (g.teams[t]?.bench||[]).find(x=>x?.uid===a.copySourceUid&&!x.dead);
          if (!source) return false;
        }
        if (a?.type === "skill" && actionSkillHasEffect(a, "timeRecall") && cardHasCapability(actor, "timeControl") && actor.usedOnce?.[skillIdByEffect(actor, "timeRecall")]) return false;

        if (g?._timeAwaitingNextAiAction && !opt.sim) {
          timeMarkTimeline(g, "aiActionSubmitted", { actorEntityId:actor?.entityId || actor?.uid || null, actionType:a?.type || null });
          g._timeAwaitingNextAiAction = false;
        }
        const isSkill = a?.type === "skill";
        if (!isSkill) return executeSupportLayer(next,g,t,s,a,opt);
        if (cardHasCapability(actor, "timeControl") && actionSkillHasEffect(a, "hyperClock")) {
          const reason = timeHyperReason(actor);
          a = {...a, timeHyperReason:reason, costOverride:status(actor,"traitSeal")?"***":(a.costOverride||"WWorB*")};
          actor._timeHyperReason = reason;
        }
        const oldGame = timeTraitGame;
        timeTraitGame = g;
        const paidGuess = a.paymentPlan ? Number(a.paymentPlan.R||0)+Number(a.paymentPlan.W||0)+Number(a.paymentPlan.B||0) : transformedSkillReq(g,t,actor,a.costOverride ?? skillOf(actor, a.skill)?.cost ?? "").tokens.length;
        const willReact = !!actor && timePotentialReactors(g,actor,paidGuess,t,s).length>0;
        const liveClockBarrier = willReact && !opt.sim && g===game;
        if (liveClockBarrier) {
          g._timeDeferTurnAdvance = { teamId:t, slotIndex:s, actorEntityId:actor?.entityId || actor?.uid || null };
          timeMarkTimeline(g, "clockLockAcquired", { actorEntityId:actor?.entityId || actor?.uid || null });
          timeSetInputLock(true,true);
        }
        let result = false;
        try {
          result = executeSupportLayer(next,g,t,s,a,opt);
          if (!result) {
            if (liveClockBarrier) {
              delete g._timeDeferTurnAdvance;
              delete g.pendingClockTurnAdvance;
              timeSetInputLock(false);
              timeMarkTimeline(g, "clockLockReleased", { reason:"action-rejected" });
            }
            return result;
          }
          if (cardHasCapability(actor, "timeControl") && actionSkillHasEffect(a, "timeRecall")) actor.usedOnce[skillIdByEffect(actor, "timeRecall")] = true;
          if (!!firstSkillByTag(actor, "copy") && actionSkillHasTag(a, "copy") && a.copySkill) {
            actor.memoryUsed = true; actor.memoryUses = 1;
            log(g, `${actor.name}: 드래곤메모리로 ${CARDS.find(c=>c.id===a.copyCard)?.skills.find(sk=>sk.id===a.copySkill)?.name||a.copySkill} 복사 · 드래곤메모리 1회 사용 소비됨.`,"turn");
          }
          const paid = timePaidAmountFromAction(g,t,actor,a);
          const rotatedClocks = timeRotateClocks(g,t,s,actor,paid,!!opt.sim);
          /* 원본 execute가 턴을 넘긴 뒤 시계가 회전하므로, 연속형 특성 수치도 여기서
             즉시 다시 계산해야 한 턴 늦게 반영되는 현상이 생기지 않는다. */
          if (rotatedClocks.length && typeof timeSyncContinuousTraitStates === "function")
            timeSyncContinuousTraitStates(g,"운명의 시계 회전");
          if (liveClockBarrier && !rotatedClocks.length) {
            timeSetInputLock(false);
            timeMarkTimeline(g, "clockLockReleased", { reason:"no-clock-events" });
            timeCommitPendingTurnAdvance(g);
          }
          return result;
        } finally {
          delete actor?._timeHyperReason;
          delete actor?._timePaidRecord;
          timeTraitGame = oldGame;
        }
      }

        return false;
      }
      function timeHumanControlsTeam(g,t) {
        if (g?.mode === "human") return t === "P";
        if (g?.mode === "online") return t === onlineSide;
        if (g?.mode === "bossSolo" || g?.mode === "bossOnline") return t === "P";
        return false;
      }
      function timeAccelerationIsActive(g,u) {
        if (!g || !u || u.dead || !cardHasCapability(u, "timeControl")) return false;
        const oldGame = timeTraitGame;
        timeTraitGame = g;
        try { return !!traitsActive(u); }
        finally { timeTraitGame = oldGame; }
      }
      function timeAccelerationDiscardCandidates(u) {
        const store=energyStore(u);
        return store.map((e,index)=>({e,index})).filter(({e})=>{
          const amount=Number(e?.a||0);
          return amount===1 || amount===.5;
        });
      }
      function timeFitAccelerationGains(store,gains,max) {
        const accepted=[],dropped=[];
        let total=(store||[]).reduce((n,e)=>n+Number(e?.a||0),0);
        for(const gain of gains||[]){
          const amount=Number(gain?.a||0);
          if(total+amount<=max+.001){accepted.push({...gain});total+=amount;}
          else dropped.push({...gain});
        }
        return {accepted,dropped,total};
      }
      function timeBestAccelerationDiscardIndex(u,gains) {
        const store=energyStore(u),max=card(u).maxEnergy||4;
        let best=null;
        for(const {index,e} of timeAccelerationDiscardCandidates(u)){
          const pool=store.filter((_,i)=>i!==index).map(x=>({...x}));
          const fit=timeFitAccelerationGains(pool,gains,max);
          const gained=fit.accepted.reduce((n,x)=>n+Number(x.a||0),0);
          if(gained<=0)continue;
          const value=energyValue(u,pool.concat(fit.accepted.map(x=>({c:x.c,a:x.a}))));
          const score=gained*1000+value-Number(e.a||0)*.01;
          if(!best||score>best.score)best={index,score};
        }
        return best?.index??null;
      }
      function timeExecuteTimeoraGather(g,t,s,u,a,opt={}) {
        if(!timeAccelerationIsActive(g,u))return timeExecuteBefore(g,t,s,a,opt);
        const store=energyStore(u),baseStore=store.map(e=>({...e})),amt=status(u,"slow")?.5:1,colors=naturalColors(g,t,u,amt,null,"time-acceleration-execute");
        const stateBefore=Number(g?.rng?.s ?? 0);
        const firstDraw=timeDrawNaturalColorFrom(g.rng,colors,baseStore,amt),first=firstDraw.color;
        let choice=a.timeAccelerationChoice||"auto";
        if(choice==="auto")choice=timeAIShouldReroll(u,first)?"reroll":"keep";
        if(choice!=="keep"&&choice!=="reroll")return false;
        let gains=[];
        if(choice==="reroll"){
          const secondDraw=timeDrawNaturalColorFrom(g.rng,colors,baseStore,amt),chosen=secondDraw.color;
          gains=[{c:chosen,a:amt,role:"reroll"}];
                    log(g,`${u.name} 특성 · 시간 가속: 에너지를 재추첨함.`,"turn");
        }else{
          const afterFirst=timeStoreAfterGain(baseStore,first,amt),bonusColor=timeDrawNaturalColorFrom(g.rng,colors,afterFirst,.5).color;
          gains=[{c:first,a:amt,role:"first"},{c:bonusColor,a:.5,role:"bonus"}];
        }
        // UI에서 미리 본 난수 상태와 실제 실행 상태가 다르면 실제 권위 상태를 사용하되 기록한다.
        if(a.timePreviewState!=null&&Number(a.timePreviewState)!==stateBefore&&!opt.sim&&g===game){
          log(g,`${u.name}: 시간 가속 상태를 최신 전투 상태로 다시 계산함.`,"turn");
        }
        const explicitKeep=Array.isArray(a.timeKeepGainIndices);
        if(explicitKeep){
          const keepIndices=[...new Set(a.timeKeepGainIndices.map(Number))];
          if(!keepIndices.length||keepIndices.some(index=>!Number.isInteger(index)||!gains[index]))return false;
          gains=keepIndices.map(index=>gains[index]);
        }
        const max=card(u).maxEnergy||4,amount=gains.reduce((n,e)=>n+Number(e.a||0),0);
        let replace=a.timeReplacementIndex??a.timeReplacementIndices??a.replaceIndex??a.replaceIndices;
        if(totalEnergy(u)+amount>max+.001&&replace==null){
          if(a.timeDeclineOverflow){log(g,`${u.name}: 최대 에너지로 시간 가속 수급을 포기.`);u.lastAction="gather";advanceDualOrEnd(g,t,s,u,false,false);return true;}
          if(!timeHumanControlsTeam(g,t))replace=timeBestAccelerationDiscardIndex(u,gains);
          else return false;
        }
        let fit;
        if(replace!=null){
          const indices=[...new Set(Array.isArray(replace)?replace:[replace])].filter(i=>Number.isInteger(i)&&store[i]);
          if(!indices.length)return false;
          const allowed=new Set(timeAccelerationDiscardCandidates(u).map(x=>x.index));
          if(indices.some(index=>!allowed.has(index)))return false;
          if(!explicitKeep&&Array.isArray(a.timeSwapPairs)){
            const gainIndices=new Set(),storeIndices=new Set();
            for(const pair of a.timeSwapPairs){
              const gainIndex=Number(pair?.gainIndex),storeIndex=Number(pair?.storeIndex);
              if(!Number.isInteger(gainIndex)||!Number.isInteger(storeIndex)||!gains[gainIndex]||!store[storeIndex])return false;
              if(gainIndices.has(gainIndex)||storeIndices.has(storeIndex))return false;
              if(Math.abs(Number(gains[gainIndex].a||0)-Number(store[storeIndex].a||0))>.001)return false;
              gainIndices.add(gainIndex);storeIndices.add(storeIndex);
            }
            if(storeIndices.size!==indices.length||indices.some(index=>!storeIndices.has(index)))return false;
          }else if(!explicitKeep&&indices.length!==1)return false;
          const removedSet=new Set(indices);
          const pool=store.filter((_,index)=>!removedSet.has(index)).map(e=>({...e}));
          fit=timeFitAccelerationGains(pool,gains,max);
          if(explicitKeep&&(fit.dropped.length||fit.accepted.length!==gains.length))return false;
          if(!explicitKeep&&!fit.accepted.some(e=>e.role!=="bonus"))return false;
          const removed=indices.slice().sort((a,b)=>b-a).map(index=>store.splice(index,1)[0]).filter(Boolean);
          log(g,`${u.name}: 기존 ${removed.map(e=>`${e.c}${e.a===.5?"½":""}`).join(" + ")} 에너지를 새 에너지와 교환.`);
        }
        fit=timeFitAccelerationGains(store,gains,max);
        // 직접 선택한 경우에는 선택한 새 에너지가 전부 들어가야 하며, 반에너지만 보관하는 선택도 허용한다.
        if(explicitKeep){
          if(!fit.accepted.length||fit.dropped.length||fit.accepted.length!==gains.length)return false;
        }else if(!fit.accepted.some(e=>e.role!=="bonus")){
          // 자동/구형 행동은 첫 수급조차 들어가지 않는 상태에서 행동을 소비하지 않는다.
          return false;
        }
        for(const e of fit.accepted)store.push({c:e.c,a:e.a});
        normalizeStore(store);
        triggerEnergyGainResult(g,u,null);
                if(choice==="keep")log(g,`${u.name} 특성 · 시간 가속: 총 ${energyAmountText(fit.accepted.reduce((n,e)=>n+Number(e.a||0),0))}E 수급.`,"turn");
        if(fit.dropped.some(e=>e.role==="bonus"))log(g,`${u.name}: 보관 공간 부족으로 시간 가속의 추가 0.5E는 획득하지 못함.`,"turn");
        log(g,`${u.name}: ${fit.accepted.map(e=>`${e.c}${e.a===.5?"½":""}`).join(" + ")} 에너지 획득.`);
        u.lastAction="gather";advanceDualOrEnd(g,t,s,u,false,false);return true;
      }

      function timeOpenGatherOverflow(a,choice,peek) {
        const [t,s]=game.order[game.turn],u=unitAt(game,t,s),
          gains=choice==="keep"?[{c:peek.first,a:peek.amt,role:"first"},{c:peek.bonusColor,a:.5,role:"bonus"}]:[{c:peek.second,a:peek.amt,role:"reroll"}],

      function execute(g,t,s,a,opt={}) {
        const thisArg=this;
        const base=(...args)=>executeCoreBase.apply(thisArg,args);
        const main=(...args)=>executeTimeStage(base,...args);
        const inner=(...args)=>{
          const invoke=()=>{
            if(window.__megaSpectatorR18?.canExecute?.(...args)===false)return false;
            return main(...args);
          };
          const overflow=window.__megaEnergyOverflowChoice;
          return typeof overflow?.withExecuteContext==="function"
            ? overflow.withExecuteContext(invoke,...args)
            : invoke();
        };
        const middle=(...args)=>{
          const energy=window.EnergyCanonicalizationEngine;
          return typeof energy?.execute==="function" ? energy.execute(inner,...args) : inner(...args);
        };
        return typeof actionTransactionExecuteStage==="function"
          ? actionTransactionExecuteStage.call(thisArg,middle,g,t,s,a,opt)
          : middle(g,t,s,a,opt);
      }
      function endTurn(g, t, s, usedSkill) {
        const __managed = typeof StateFactory!=="undefined" && typeof BattleEventEngine!=="undefined" && typeof TurnEngine!=="undefined";
        let __beforeSerial=Number(g?.turnSerial)||0;
        if (__managed) {
          StateFactory.decorate(g);
          if(g.ended||g.winner)return false;
          const currentPair=g.order?.[g.turn];
          if(currentPair&&(currentPair[0]!==t||String(currentPair[1])!==String(s)))return false;
          const key=`${g.battleId}:${g.turnSerial}:${t}:${s}`;
          if(g._r48LastEndTurnKey===key)return false;
          g._r48LastEndTurnKey=key; g.phase="TURN_END";
          __beforeSerial=Number(g.turnSerial)||0;
        }
        let __result;
        __endTurnCore: {
        let u = unitAt(g, t, s);
        const hadSeal = u ? !!status(u,"traitSeal") : false;
        const summonOwner = !!u && skillsOf(u).some(sk=>skillDefHasTag(sk,"summon"));
        const regen = !!u && cardHasCapability(u,"regenerationTrait") && traitsActive(u) && !status(u,"traitSeal");
        if (regen) for (const st of Object.keys(u.statuses||{})) if (st!=="traitSeal" && u.statuses[st]>1) u.statuses[st]--;
        if (u && !u.dead) {
          if (status(u, "burn") && !usedSkill)
            nonAttackDamage(g, u, 30, "화상");
          for (const st of Object.keys(u.statuses)) {
            if (u.statuses[st] > 0) {
              u.statuses[st]--;
              log(g, `${u.name}: ${STATUS[st]} ${u.statuses[st]}턴 남음.`);
              if (!u.statuses[st]) {
                delete u.statuses[st];
                queueCombatNotice(g, u, `${STATUS[st]} 종료`, "cure");
              }
            }
          }
          for (const sid of Object.keys(u.skillSeals || {})) {
            u.skillSeals[sid]--;
            if (u.skillSeals[sid] <= 0) {
              let skillName = card(u).skills.find((x) => x.id === sid)?.name || "기술";
              delete u.skillSeals[sid];
              queueCombatNotice(g, u, `${skillName} 기술봉인 종료`, "cure");
            }
          }
          u.turns++;
          u.turnSkillCostReduction = 0;
          if (cardHasCapability(u, "horizonFlow") && Number(u.hp) <= Number(u.maxHp) / 2) {
            const units = [...g.teams[t].field, ...g.teams[t].bench].filter(x => x && !x.dead);
            const countColor = color => units.reduce((sum, x) => sum + energyStore(x).reduce((n, entry) => n + (entry.c === color ? Number(entry.a || 0) : 0), 0), 0);
            const white = countColor("W"), black = countColor("B");
            const flowTargets = cardCapability(u, "horizonFlowTargets", {});
            if (white > black && flowTargets.W) FormFamilyOwner.transition(g, u, flowTargets.W, "시간의 흐름");
            else if (black > white && flowTargets.B) FormFamilyOwner.transition(g, u, flowTargets.B, "시간의 흐름");
          }
        }
        g.stats.turns++;
        let dealt = ["P", "A"]
          .flatMap((x) => [
            ...g.teams[x].field,
            ...g.teams[x].bench,
            ...g.teams[x].graveyard,
          ])
          .filter(Boolean)
          .reduce((n, x) => n + x.damageDealt, 0);
        if (dealt > g.lastDamageTotal) {
          g.lastDamageTotal = dealt;
          g.noDamageTurns = 0;
        } else g.noDamageTurns++;
        if (!g.winner && g.noDamageTurns >= 120 && u && !u.dead) {
          let raw = 10 + Math.floor((g.noDamageTurns - 120) / 8) * 10,
            streak = g.noDamageTurns;
          nonAttackDamage(g, u, raw, "교착 압박");
          g.noDamageTurns = streak;
        }
        if (!g.winner && g.stats.turns >= 800 && u && !u.dead) {
          let raw = 40 + Math.floor((g.stats.turns - 800) / 16) * 40,
            streak = g.noDamageTurns;
          nonAttackDamage(g, u, raw, "결판 압박");
          g.noDamageTurns = streak;
        }
        g.turn = (g.turn + 1) % 4;
        g.turnSerial++;
        g.preparedKey = null;
        g.dualActorController = null;
        if (g.turn === 0) g.round++;
        checkWinner(g);

        if (summonOwner && u && !u.dead) processJunglesSummons(g,t,u);
        if (g.circuitChain?.uid === u?.uid) {
          if (!u || u.dead || unitAt(g,t,s)?.uid !== u.uid) g.circuitChain=null;
          else if (g.circuitChain.remaining>0) {
            g.circuitChain.remaining--;
            g.turn=g.order.findIndex(x=>x[0]===t&&x[1]===s);
            g.preparedKey=null;
            log(g,`${u.name}: 전류가속 추가 차례 시작 (${g.circuitChain.remaining}회 더 남음).`,"turn");
          } else g.circuitChain=null;
        }
        if (reactiveEnergyRule(u) && hadSeal !== !!status(u,"traitSeal")) syncReactiveEnergyMaxHp(g,u,"특성봉인 종료",true);
      
        }
        if (!__managed) return __result;
        if((Number(g.turnSerial)||0)===__beforeSerial){delete g._r48LastEndTurnKey;return __result;}
        const skipped=TurnEngine.normalizeDeadEmptyOrderEntries(g); StateFactory.updateActive(g);
        BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"TURN_ENDED",{teamId:t,payload:{slot:s,usedSkill:!!usedSkill,turnSerial:__beforeSerial,skippedEmptySlots:skipped}});
        if(!g.ended)BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"TURN_STARTED",{teamId:g.activeTeamId,sourceEntityId:g.activeEntityId,payload:{turnSerial:g.turnSerial,round:g.round}});
        return __result===undefined?true:__result;
      }
      
      function checkWinner(g) {
        if (g.winner === "P" || g.winner === "A") return;
        actionFaultHit("victory-before");
        let p = aliveUnits(g.teams.P).length,
          a = aliveUnits(g.teams.A).length;
        if (!p || !a) {
          g.winner = p ? "P" : "A";
          actionFaultHit("victory-after");
          actionFaultHit("terminal-pending-after");
          actionFaultHit("terminal-commit-before");
          log(g, g.winner === "P" ? "플레이어 승리!" : "AI 승리!", "turn");
        } else actionFaultHit("victory-after");
      }
      function gatherPotential(u) {
        let max = card(u).maxEnergy || 4;
        if (totalEnergy(u) < max) return 20 - totalEnergy(u) * 2;
        let base = energyValue(u),
          gain = 0,
          store = energyStore(u);
        for (const col of ["R", "W", "B"]) {
          let best = base;
          store.forEach((e, i) => {
            if (e.a < 1) return;
            let pool = store.filter((_, j) => j !== i).map((x) => ({ ...x }));
            pool.push({ c: col, a: 1 });
            best = Math.max(best, energyValue(u, pool));
          });
          gain += Math.max(0, best - base);
        }
        return gain ? gain / 3 : -120;
      }
      const AI_SKILL_PROFILE_SCORERS = Object.freeze({
        mutualEnergyTrade({ g, u, tar, action, profile }) {
          if (!tar) return 0;
          const n = Number(action?.interactionAmount || 0),
            baseDamage = Number(profile?.baseDamage || 0),
            perEnergyDamage = Number(profile?.perEnergyDamage || 0),
            selfDamagePerEnergy = Number(profile?.selfDamagePerEnergy || perEnergyDamage),
            mean = baseDamage * (g.variance ? 0.83 : 1),
            targetTotal = Number(tar.hp || 0) + Number(tar.shield || 0),
            kill = mean + perEnergyDamage * n >= targetTotal,
            selfDies = selfDamagePerEnergy * n >= Number(u.hp || 0),
            trade = n * selfDamagePerEnergy * (u.hp < 150 ? 1.35 : u.hp < 230 ? 0.75 : 0.4);
          let score = mean + n * 14 - trade + (kill ? 190 : 0) - (selfDies && !kill ? 480 : selfDies ? 70 : 0);
          if (!kill && n > 0 && tar.hp > mean + perEnergyDamage * n) score -= n * 12;
          return score;
        },
      });
      function scoreSkillAiProfile(g, u, tar, action, skill) {
        const profile = skill?.aiProfile, scorer = profile && AI_SKILL_PROFILE_SCORERS[profile.kind];
        return typeof scorer === "function" ? Number(scorer({ g, u, tar, action, skill, profile }) || 0) : 0;
      }
      function actionHeuristicBase(g, t, s, a) {
        let u = unitAt(g, t, s),
          stall = g.stats.turns > 240;
        if (a.type === "deploy") {
          let x = g.teams[t].bench.find((v) => v.uid === a.unit),
            trap = g.teams[t].traps[s];
          return 80 + x.hp * 0.15 - (trap && !isStatusImmune(x, trap.status) ? 35 : 0);
        }
        if (a.type === "scheduled" || a.type === "sequence") {
          let k = u.scheduledAction?.kind;
          return k === "bloom" ? 260 : k === "rubyFollow" ? 150 : 120;
        }
        if (a.type === "dualPass") return -8;
        if (a.type === "switch") {
          let x = g.teams[t].bench[a.bench],
            trap = g.teams[t].traps[s];
          return (
            5 +
            (u.hp / u.maxHp < 0.3 ? 28 : 0) +
            x.hp * 0.06 -
            (trap && !isStatusImmune(x, trap.status) ? 30 : 0) -
            (stall ? 90 : 0)
          );
        }
        if (a.type === "gather") {
          let sc = gatherPotential(u);
          if (status(u, "burn")) sc -= 35;
          return sc;
        }
        let sk = card(u).skills.find((x) => x.id === a.skill),
          tar =
            sk?.target === "enemySlot"
              ? null
              : a.target
                ? unitAt(g, a.target.team, a.target.slot)
                : null,
          sc = 10;
        if (!sk) return sc;
        if (sk.target === "enemySlot") {
          let tr = g.teams[other(t)].traps[a.target.slot];
          return tr ? 3 : 30 + (g.teams[other(t)].bench.length ? 18 : 0);
        }
        if (skillDefHasTag(sk, "free")) sc += 45;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "bloomingLife")) sc += u.hp < 70 ? 80 : 210;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "lifeRain"))
          sc +=
            g.teams[t].field.reduce(
              (n, x) => n + (x ? Math.max(0, x.maxHp - x.hp) : 0),
              0,
            ) *
              0.6 +
            55;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "iceRoar")) {
          let frozenEnemies = g.teams[other(t)].field.filter(
              (x) => x && !x.dead && !isStatusImmune(x, "freeze"),
            ).length,
            ally = g.teams[t].field.find((x) => x && x.uid !== u.uid);
          sc +=
            frozenEnemies * 55 -
            (ally && !isStatusImmune(ally, "freeze") && !cardHasCapability(ally, "irumaCleanse")
              ? 45
              : 0);
          if (canPayFrom(u, "WR*", "rider")) sc += 80;
        }
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "rampage"))
          sc +=
            (1 + (a.extra || 0)) * 10 +
            (tar && tar.hp <= (1 + (a.extra || 0)) * 10 ? 120 : 0) -
            (a.extra || 0) * 8;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "shatterLance") && tar)
          sc += 70 + status(tar, "freeze") * 30;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "firestorm")) sc += 150 + (u.hp > 60 ? -25 : 0);
        if (skillDefHasRuntimeKind(sk, "openingGuard")) sc += u.shield ? 45 : 125;
        if (skillDefHasRuntimeKind(sk, "dynamicDefenseSmash"))
          sc += 85 + (6 - (a.costOverride?.length || 0)) * 5;
        if (skillDefHasTag(sk, "copy")) sc += 75;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "holyFlame") && tar) {
          let d = Math.max(0, 100 - 20 * allEnergy(tar));
          sc += d + (100 - d) * 0.9;
        }
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "prawnCharge") && tar) sc += hasNullifyTrait(tar) ? 170 : 125;
        for (const e of sk.effects) {
          if (e.type === "damage" && tar) {
            let r = damageRange(g, u, tar, e, sk),
              hits = e.hits || 1,
              mean = r.mean * hits,
              killProb =
                tar.shield && !e.shieldMelt
                  ? 0
                  : r.min * hits >= tar.hp + (e.shieldMelt ? tar.shield : 0)
                    ? 1
                    : r.max * hits < tar.hp
                      ? 0
                      : (r.max * hits - tar.hp) /
                        Math.max(1, (r.max - r.min) * hits);
            sc += mean + killProb * 120;
            if (e.shieldMelt) sc += Math.min(tar.shield, mean) * 0.5;
            if (card(tar).reflect) {
              let refl = card(tar).reflect * hits;
              if (refl >= u.hp) sc -= 220;
              if (tar.hideHits && mean / hits <= 30) sc -= 65;
            }
          }
          if (e.type === "heal" && tar)
            sc +=
              Math.min(e.amount, tar.maxHp - tar.hp) * 1.3 -
              (tar.hp === tar.maxHp ? 50 : 0) -
              (stall ? 80 : 0);
          if (e.type === "shield" && tar) {
            let useful = Math.min(
              e.amount,
              Math.max(0, (e.max ?? 999) - tar.shield),
            );
            sc +=
              useful * (tar.shield > 45 ? 0.15 : 0.8) - (useful === 0 ? 50 : 0);
          }
          if (e.type === "status" && tar) {
            let irumaChance = g.knowledge?.unknownCandidates?.includes("iruma")
              ? 1 / Math.max(1, g.knowledge.unknownCandidates.length)
              : 0;
            sc +=
              (e.status === "poison"
                ? 35 + allEnergy(tar) * 7
                : e.status === "slow"
                  ? 24
                  : 18) *
              (1 - irumaChance);
          }
          if (e.type === "statusChance" && tar) sc += e.chance * 24;
          if (e.type === "startSequence" && tar) sc += 100;
          if (e.type === "clearFieldEnergy") {
            let enemy = g.teams[other(t)].field.reduce(
                (n, x) => n + (x && !x.dead ? allEnergy(x) : 0),
                0,
              ),
              ally = g.teams[t].field.reduce(
                (n, x) => n + (x && !x.dead ? allEnergy(x) : 0),
                0,
              );
            sc += (enemy - ally) * 22;
          }
        }
        if (status(u, "burn")) sc += 28;
        return sc;
      }
      function pendingBloom(g, t) {
        return (
          g.teams[other(t)].field.find(
            (x) =>
              x &&
              !x.dead &&
              x.scheduledAction?.kind === "bloom",
          ) || null
        );
      }
      function actionPaysEnergy(u, a) {
        if (a.type !== "skill") return false;
        let sk = card(u).skills.find((x) => x.id === a.skill),
          cost = a.costOverride ?? sk?.cost ?? "";
        return costTokens(cost).length + (status(u, "poison") ? 1 : 0) > 0;
      }
      function sequenceThreatening(g, t, u) {
        let slot = g.teams[t].field.findIndex((x) => x?.uid === u.uid);
        return (
          g.teams[other(t)].field.find(
            (x) =>
              x &&
              !x.dead &&
              cardHasCapability(x, "sweetna") &&
              x.scheduledAction?.kind === "sweet" &&
              x.scheduledAction.targetTeam === t &&
              x.scheduledAction.targetSlot === slot,
          ) || null
        );
      }

<script>
(() => {
  "use strict";
  const HARD_LEAVE_IDS = new Set([
    "onlineRoomLeave", "p2pDisconnect", "matchCancel", "matchmakingCancel",
    "bossMatchCancel", "bossOnlineLeave", "onlineRoomBack"
  ]);

  document.addEventListener("click", event => {
    const button = event.target?.closest?.("button,[role=button]");
    if (!button) return;
    const hard = HARD_LEAVE_IDS.has(button.id) ||
      /연결 끊기|온라인.*나가기|매칭 취소/.test(String(button.textContent || "").trim());
    if (!hard) return;
    try { window.__megaOnlineBattleChat?.clear?.(); } catch (error) { window.__megaRecordRecoveredError(error, "fullFileAuditR29:hard-leave-chat-clear"); }
  }, true);

  function normalizeVisibleMiddleDots(root = document.body) {
    try { scrubUserFacingUndefined(root); } catch (error) { window.__megaRecordRecoveredError(error, "fullFileAuditR29:normalizeVisibleMiddleDots"); }
  }

  function canonicalizeEnergyStore(unit) {
    if (!unit || typeof energyStore !== "function") return;
    const store = energyStore(unit);
    if (!Array.isArray(store)) return;
    for (const e of store) {
      if (!e || !["R", "W", "B"].includes(e.c)) continue;
      const amount = Number(e.a);
      if (Number.isFinite(amount)) e.a = Math.max(0, Math.round(amount * 2) / 2);
    }
    try { normalizeStore(store); } catch (error) { window.__megaRecordRecoveredError(error, "inline-165:L35"); }
  }

  function currentGame() {
    try { return typeof game !== "undefined" ? game : window.game; } catch { return window.game; }
  }

  function canonicalizeGameEnergy(g = currentGame()) {
    if (!g?.teams) return;
    for (const team of ["P", "A"]) {
      for (const unit of [...(g.teams[team]?.field || []), ...(g.teams[team]?.bench || [])]) {
        canonicalizeEnergyStore(unit);
      }
    }
  }

  function canonicalEnergyExecuteStage(next,g,t,s,action,opt={}) {
    const actor = (() => { try { return unitAt(g,t,s); } catch { return null; } })();
    const onlineTimeKeep = !!(
      g === currentGame() && cardHasCapability(actor,"timeControl") && action?.type === "gather" &&
      action?.timeAccelerationChoice === "keep" && !Array.isArray(action?.timeKeepGainIndices) &&
      (g?.mode === "online" || g?.mode === "bossOnline" || g?.bossRaid?.online)
    );
    const beforeTotal = onlineTimeKeep ? Number(totalEnergy(actor) || 0) : 0;
    const max = onlineTimeKeep ? Number(card(actor)?.maxEnergy || 4) : 0;
    const slowed = onlineTimeKeep && !!status(actor,"slow");
    const expected = slowed ? 1 : 1.5;
    const result = next(g,t,s,action,opt);
    if (result && onlineTimeKeep && beforeTotal + expected <= max + 0.001) {
      const delta = Number(totalEnergy(actor) || 0) - beforeTotal;
      if (delta < expected - 0.001) {
        const missing = Math.round((expected - delta) * 2) / 2;
        const color = ["R","W","B"].includes(action?.timeBonusColor) ? action.timeBonusColor :
          (["R","W","B"].includes(action?.timeFirstColor) ? action.timeFirstColor : "W");
        const store = energyStore(actor);
        store.push({c:color,a:missing});
        normalizeStore(store);
        try { triggerEnergyGainResult(g,actor,null); }
        catch (error) { window.__megaRecordRecoveredError(error,"inline-165:L83"); }
      }
    }
    canonicalizeGameEnergy(g);
    return result;
  }

  window.EnergyCanonicalizationEngine = Object.freeze({
    VERSION:"mega-energy-canonicalization-r29",
    canonicalizeGameState:canonicalizeGameEnergy,
    execute:canonicalEnergyExecuteStage
  });

  function cleanTransientDom() {
    const seen = new Set();
    for (const el of document.querySelectorAll("[id]")) {
      if (!seen.has(el.id)) {
        seen.add(el.id);
        continue;
      }
      if (/^(battleChatPopupStack|time.*Layer|online.*Overlay)/i.test(el.id)) el.remove();
    }
    for (const el of document.querySelectorAll(".time-hyper-clock,.time-hyper-clock-overlay,.time-clock-effect-layer")) {
      if (!el.isConnected || (
        !document.getElementById("game")?.classList.contains("active") &&
        getComputedStyle(el).position === "fixed"
      )) el.remove();
    }
    normalizeVisibleMiddleDots(document.body);
    canonicalizeGameEnergy();
    try { window.__megaSyncOnlineChatUI?.(); } catch (error) { window.__megaRecordRecoveredError(error, "inline-165:L112"); }
  }

  let scheduled = false;
  const scheduleClean = () => {
    if (scheduled) return;
    scheduled = true;
    window.TimerManager.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      scheduled = false;
      cleanTransientDom();
    });
  };

  if (document.readyState === "loading") {
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", scheduleClean, { once: true });
  } else {
    scheduleClean();
  }

})();

</script>

  const W = {...BASE_MODEL.weights};
  const mod12 = v => { const n=Math.round(Number(v)||0)%12; return n<0?n+12:n; };
  const alignedStep = s => { s=mod12(s); return s===0||s===6; };
  const phaseDistance = s => { s=mod12(s); return Math.min(s,12-s,Math.abs(s-6)); };
  const bossUnit = (g,u,t=null) => !!(g?.bossRaid && u && !u.dead && (u.bossRed || ((t||teamOf(g,u))==="A" && cardHasCapability(u,"fateClock"))));
  const living = tm => [...(tm?.field||[]),...(tm?.bench||[])].filter(u=>u&&!u.dead);
  const barrier = u => (u?.hpProtectionBarriers||[]).reduce((n,b)=>n+Math.max(0,Number(b?.amount)||0),0);

  const teamEhp = tm => living(tm).reduce((n,u)=>n+ehp(u),0);
  const energy = u => { try{return Number(totalEnergy(u)||0);}catch{return (u?.energy||[]).reduce((n,e)=>n+Number(e?.a||0),0);} };
  const st = (u,k) => { try{return Number(status(u,k)||0);}catch{return Number(u?.statuses?.[k]||u?.skillSeals?.[k]||0);} };
  const statusLoad = u => st(u,"burn")*1.35+st(u,"poison")*1.5+st(u,"paralyze")*1.65+st(u,"slow")*.82+st(u,"freeze")*2.2+st(u,"traitSeal")*2.65+st(u,"skillSeal")*2.05;

  };
  function copyGame(g){ const c=clone(g); if(typeof RNG==="function"){const r=new RNG(1);r.s=Number(g?.rng?.s||1)||1;c.rng=r;} c.logs=[]; return c; }
  function sim(g,t,s,a){ const c=copyGame(g); try{return execute(c,t,s,{...a},{sim:true})?c:null;}catch{return null;} }

  function featureVector(g){
    const A=living(g?.teams?.A),P=living(g?.teams?.P),ph=clockInfo(g);
    let recall=0,scheduled=0,low=0;
    for(const u of A){

      if(u.scheduledAction)scheduled++;
      if((Number(u.hp)||0)<=80)low++;
    }
    return {
      bias:1,bossHp:teamEhp(g?.teams?.A)/100,playerHp:teamEhp(g?.teams?.P)/100,
      bossAlive:A.length,playerAlive:P.length,bossEnergy:A.reduce((n,u)=>n+energy(u),0),playerEnergy:P.reduce((n,u)=>n+energy(u),0),

  }
  function dot(f,w=W){ let v=0; for(const [k,x] of Object.entries(f))v+=(Number(w[k])||0)*(Number(x)||0); return v; }
  function baseValue(g){
    if(g?.winner)return g.winner==="A"?1e6:g.winner==="P"?-1e6:0;

  }

  function actionKey(a){return [a?.type,a?.skill,a?.costOverride,a?.target?.team,a?.target?.slot,a?.target?.uid,a?.extraW,JSON.stringify(a?.paymentPlan||null),JSON.stringify(a?.split||null)].join("|");}
  function paidUnits(g,t,u,a){
    if(a?.type!=="skill")return 0;

  }
  function expanded(g,t,s){
    const u=unitAt(g,t,s),raw=legalActions(g,t,s)||[],out=[];

    const seen=new Set();return out.filter(a=>{const k=actionKey(a);if(seen.has(k))return false;seen.add(k);return true;});
  }
  function transitionDanger(before,after){
    const bA=living(before.teams.A),aA=living(after.teams.A);
    const hpLoss=Math.max(0,teamEhp(before.teams.A)-teamEhp(after.teams.A));
    const deaths=Math.max(0,bA.length-aA.length);
    const statusGain=Math.max(0,aA.reduce((n,u)=>n+statusLoad(u),0)-bA.reduce((n,u)=>n+statusLoad(u),0));
    const energyLoss=Math.max(0,bA.reduce((n,u)=>n+energy(u),0)-aA.reduce((n,u)=>n+energy(u),0));
    const playerGain=Math.max(0,teamEhp(after.teams.P)-teamEhp(before.teams.P));
    return hpLoss*1.05+deaths*520+statusGain*46+energyLoss*18+playerGain*.42;
  }
  function nextPlayerThreats(g){
    const turn=g?.order?.[g.turn]; if(!turn||turn[0]!=="P")return {skills:[],none:true};
    const [t,s]=turn,u=unitAt(g,t,s);if(!u||u.dead)return {skills:[],none:true};
    const skills=[];
    for(const a of expanded(g,t,s)){
      if(a?.type!=="skill")continue;
      const after=sim(g,t,s,a);if(!after)continue;
      let likelihood=0;try{likelihood=Number(actionHeuristic(g,t,s,a)||0);}catch (error) { window.__megaRecordRecoveredError(error, "bossTimeoraRLPlannerR32:L128"); }
      const danger=transitionDanger(g,after),units=paidUnits(g,t,u,a),aligned=clockInfo(after).aligned;
      skills.push({action:a,danger,likelihood,units,aligned,score:danger+Math.max(-80,Math.min(220,likelihood))*.18});
    }
    skills.sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score);
    return {skills,none:skills.length===0};
  }
  function trapScore(afterBoss){
    const q=nextPlayerThreats(afterBoss);
    if(q.none){
      const ph=clockInfo(afterBoss);
      return {score:(ph.aligned?260:0)-ph.distance*28,none:true,target:null,covered:false};
    }
    const top=q.skills.slice(0,Math.min(6,q.skills.length));
    let total=0,best=top[0],covered=false;
    for(let i=0;i<top.length;i++){
      const x=top[i],weight=1/(1+i*.55);
      if(x.aligned){total+=x.score*2.35*weight;if(x===best)covered=true;}
      else total-=x.score*(x===best?1.25:.38)*weight;
    }
    return {score:total,none:false,target:best,covered};
  }
  function immediate(g,t,s,a){
    const child=sim(g,t,s,a);if(!child)return -Infinity;
    let score=baseValue(child)-baseValue(g);
    if(t==="A")score+=trapScore(child).score;
    if(actionSkillHasEffect(a, "bossTimeStrike"))score+=Number(a.bossTimeStrikeDamage||0)*.28;
    if(actionSkillHasEffect(a, "hyperClock"))score+=clockInfo(g).aligned?4.8:1.5;
    return score;
  }
  function ordered(g,t,s,limit){
    const arr=expanded(g,t,s).map(a=>({a,v:immediate(g,t,s,a)})).filter(x=>Number.isFinite(x.v));
    arr.sort((x,y)=>t==="A"?y.v-x.v:x.v-y.v);
    return arr.slice(0,limit);
  }
  function stateKey(g,d){

    return `${d}|${g.turn}|${g.turnSerial||0}|${c.hourDeg||0},${c.minuteDeg||0}|${g.rng?.s||0}|${us}`;
  }
  function search(g,d,alpha,beta,deadline,tt){
    if(d<=0||g?.winner||performance.now()>=deadline)return baseValue(g);
    const turn=g?.order?.[g.turn];if(!turn)return baseValue(g);
    const [t,s]=turn,key=stateKey(g,d);if(tt.has(key))return tt.get(key);
    const max=t==="A",list=ordered(g,t,s,max?8:7);
    if(!list.length){const c=copyGame(g);try{endTurn(c,t,s,false);}catch (error) { window.__megaRecordRecoveredError(error, "bossTimeoraRLPlannerR32:L173"); }const v=search(c,d-1,alpha,beta,deadline,tt);tt.set(key,v);return v;}
    let best=max?-Infinity:Infinity;
    for(const x of list){if(performance.now()>=deadline)break;const c=sim(g,t,s,x.a);if(!c)continue;let v=search(c,d-1,alpha,beta,deadline,tt);if(max)v+=trapScore(c).score*.08;
      if(max){best=Math.max(best,v);alpha=Math.max(alpha,best);}else{best=Math.min(best,v);beta=Math.min(beta,best);}if(beta<=alpha)break;}
    if(!Number.isFinite(best))best=baseValue(g);tt.set(key,best);return best;
  }

function bossSearchChooseR32(g,t,s,quick=false){
    const actor=unitAt(g,t,s);if(!bossUnit(g,actor,t))return null;
    if(actor?.scheduledAction)return {type:"scheduled",reason:"예약된 시간 공격을 실행한다.",bossSearchVersion:VERSION,bossSearchDepth:0};
    const start=performance.now();
    const localBudget=quick?180:DECISION_BUDGET_MS;
    const sharedDeadline=Number(globalThis.__bossTimeoraTurnDeadlineR39||0);
    const deadline=sharedDeadline>start
      ? Math.min(start+localBudget,sharedDeadline)
      : start+localBudget;
    const roots=ordered(g,t,s,quick?8:16);
    if(!roots.length)return null;
    let best={a:roots[0].a,v:-Infinity,depth:0,trap:null};const tt=new Map();
    for(let depth=1;depth<=9&&performance.now()<deadline;depth++){
      let round=null,complete=true;
      for(const x of roots){if(performance.now()>=deadline){complete=false;break;}const c=sim(g,t,s,x.a);if(!c)continue;const trap=trapScore(c);let v=search(c,depth-1,-Infinity,Infinity,deadline,tt)+trap.score*.34+x.v*.12;
        if(!round||v>round.v)round={a:x.a,v,depth,trap};}
      if(round&&(complete||depth===1))best=round;
    }
    const chosen={...best.a},elapsed=Math.min(3000,performance.now()-start),tr=best.trap||{score:0,none:true,target:null,covered:false};
    if(tr.none)chosen.reason=`상대의 사용 가능한 기술이 없어 공용 시계를 자유롭게 조정하는 ${best.depth}수 경로를 선택한다.`;
    else if(tr.covered)chosen.reason=`예상 최고위험 기술(${tr.target?.action?.skill||"기술"}, ${tr.target?.units||0}E) 사용 직후 시계가 정렬되도록 배치한다.`;
    else chosen.reason=`3초 탐색에서 피해·상태이상 기대손실을 최소화하는 공용 시계 경로를 선택한다.`;
    chosen.bossSearchVersion=VERSION;chosen.bossSearchDepth=best.depth;chosen.bossDecisionMs=Math.round(elapsed);chosen.bossThreatSkill=tr.target?.action?.skill||null;chosen.bossThreatPaidUnits=tr.target?.units||0;
    return chosen;
  }
  if(window.__redTimeoraBossRaid) window.__redTimeoraBossRaid.searchChooseR32=bossSearchChooseR32;

})();

  const TurnEngine = (() => {
    const hasLiveReplacement = (g,team) => !!g?.teams?.[team]?.bench?.some(unit => unit && !unit.dead);
    const currentOrderEntryCanResolve = g => {
      const [team,slot] = g?.order?.[g?.turn] || [];
      if (team == null || slot == null) return false;
      const unit = typeof unitAt === "function" ? unitAt(g,team,slot) : null;
      return !!(unit && !unit.dead) || hasLiveReplacement(g,team);
    };
    const normalizeDeadEmptyOrderEntries = g => {
      const skipped = [];
      const orderLength = Math.max(0,Number(g?.order?.length || 0));
      if (!orderLength || g?.winner || g?.ended) return skipped;
      for (let inspected = 0; inspected < orderLength && !currentOrderEntryCanResolve(g); inspected++) {
        const [team,slot] = g.order[g.turn] || [];
        const unit = team != null && slot != null && typeof unitAt === "function" ? unitAt(g,team,slot) : null;
        skipped.push(Object.freeze({teamId:team ?? null,slot:slot ?? null,entityId:unit?.entityId || unit?.uid || null,turnSerial:Number(g.turnSerial||0)}));
        BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"EMPTY_SLOT_TURN_SKIPPED",{
          teamId:team ?? null,
          sourceEntityId:unit?.entityId || unit?.uid || null,
          payload:{slot:slot ?? null,turnSerial:Number(g.turnSerial||0),reason:"DEAD_OR_EMPTY_WITHOUT_LIVE_REPLACEMENT"},
        });
        g.turn = (Number(g.turn || 0) + 1) % orderLength;
        g.turnSerial = Number(g.turnSerial || 0) + 1;
        g.preparedKey = null;
        g.dualActorController = null;
        if (g.turn === 0) g.round = Math.max(1,Number(g.round||1)) + 1;
        if (typeof checkWinner === "function") checkWinner(g);
        if (g.winner || g.ended) break;
      }
      return skipped;
    };

    return Object.freeze({
      end:(...args)=>endTurn(...args),
      current:g=>g?.order?.[g.turn] || null,
      phase:g=>g?.phase || "IDLE",
      normalizeDeadEmptyOrderEntries,
      currentOrderEntryCanResolve,
    });
  })();

    let armed=null, hits=[];
    return Object.freeze({
      phases:()=>[...ACTION_FAULT_PHASES],
      arm:phase=>{if(!ACTION_FAULT_PHASES.includes(phase))throw new Error(`unknown fault phase ${phase}`);armed=phase;hits=[];},
      clear:()=>{armed=null;hits=[];},
      hit(phase){hits.push(phase);if(armed===phase){armed=null;const error=new Error(`CP32_FAULT:${phase}`);error.code="CP32_FAULT_INJECTED";error.phase=phase;throw error;}},
      debug:()=>({armed,hits:[...hits]}),
    });
  })();
  Object.defineProperty(window,"ActionFaultInjector",{value:ActionFaultInjector,writable:false,configurable:false});

  const PresentationFailureChannel = (() => {
    const events = [];
    const emit = record => {
      const frozen = Object.freeze({ presentationSequence:events.length+1, timestamp:Date.now(), ...record });
      events.push(frozen);
      if(events.length>512)events.splice(0,events.length-512);
      return frozen;
    };
    const snapshot = () => events.map(x=>({...x}));
    const clear = () => { events.length=0; };
    return Object.freeze({emit,snapshot,clear});
  })();
  Object.defineProperty(window,"PresentationFailureChannel",{value:PresentationFailureChannel,writable:false,configurable:false});

  const TransactionAuditChannel = (() => {
    const events = [];
    const emit = record => {
      const frozen = Object.freeze({ auditSequence: events.length + 1, timestamp: Date.now(), ...record });
      events.push(frozen);
      if (events.length > 2048) events.splice(0, events.length - 2048);
      return frozen;
    };
    const snapshot = () => events.map(x => ({ ...x }));
    const forAction = actionId => events.filter(x => x.actionId === actionId).map(x => ({ ...x }));
    const clear = () => { events.length = 0; };
    return Object.freeze({ emit, snapshot, forAction, clear });
  })();
  Object.defineProperty(window,"TransactionAuditChannel",{value:TransactionAuditChannel,writable:false,configurable:false});
  const ActionTransactionManager = (() => {
    const committed = new Map(), failed = new Map();
    const begin = (g,t,s,action) => {
      StateFactory.decorate(g);
      const actionId = action?.actionId || action?._r48ActionId || IdFactory.transaction(g.battleId);
      if (action && typeof action === "object" && !action._r48ActionId) Object.defineProperty(action,"_r48ActionId",{value:actionId,configurable:true});
      const seen = committed.get(g.battleId) || new Set(); committed.set(g.battleId,seen);
      const rejected = failed.get(g.battleId) || new Set(); failed.set(g.battleId,rejected);
      if (seen.has(actionId) || rejected.has(actionId)) return { duplicate:true, actionId, terminalStatus:seen.has(actionId)?"committed":"failed" };
      const transaction = { transactionId:IdFactory.transaction(g.battleId), actionId, teamId:t, slot:s, action, startedAt:now(), eventSequenceAtStart:Number(g.eventSequence||0), snapshot:snapshotState(g) };
      g.pendingTransaction = transaction; g.pendingAction = action; g.phase = "EXECUTING_ACTION";
      BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"ACTION_TRANSACTION_STARTED",{teamId:t,sourceEntityId:g.activeEntityId,payload:{transactionId:transaction.transactionId,actionId,actionType:action?.type}});
      return transaction;
    };
    const commit = (g,tx) => {
      const seen = committed.get(g.battleId) || new Set(); seen.add(tx.actionId); committed.set(g.battleId,seen);
      g.pendingTransaction=null; g.pendingAction=null; g.pendingTargetSelection=null; g.pendingPayment=null;
      g.phase = g.winner ? "BATTLE_ENDED" : "WAITING_FOR_ACTION";
      BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"ACTION_TRANSACTION_COMMITTED",{teamId:tx.teamId,payload:{transactionId:tx.transactionId,actionId:tx.actionId,durationMs:now()-tx.startedAt}});
    };
    const rollback = (g,tx,reason,error=null) => {
      restoreState(g,tx.snapshot);
      InputLockManager.unlock("payment"); InputLockManager.unlock("target-selection"); InputLockManager.unlock("action-transaction");
      const rejected = failed.get(g.battleId) || new Set(); rejected.add(tx.actionId); failed.set(g.battleId,rejected);
      const rollbackEvent = TransactionAuditChannel.emit({type:"ACTION_TRANSACTION_ROLLED_BACK",battleId:g.battleId,teamId:tx.teamId,transactionId:tx.transactionId,actionId:tx.actionId,reason,error:error?String(error):null});
      TransactionAuditChannel.emit({type:reason === "action-rejected" ? "ACTION_COMMAND_REJECTED" : "ACTION_COMMAND_FAILED",battleId:g.battleId,teamId:tx.teamId,transactionId:tx.transactionId,actionId:tx.actionId,reason,error:error?String(error):null});
      try { if (typeof p2p !== "undefined") { p2p.remotePending = null; p2p.terminalPending = null; } } catch {}
      return rollbackEvent;
    };
    const clearBattle = id => {
      committed.delete(id); failed.delete(id);
      const g = currentGame();
      if (g?.battleId === id) {
        g.pendingTransaction = null;
        g.pendingAction = null;
        g.pendingTargetSelection = null;
        g.pendingPayment = null;
        g.pendingAnimation = null;
        if (!g.winner) g.phase = "WAITING_FOR_ACTION";
      }
      return true;
    };
    return Object.freeze({ begin, commit, rollback, clearBattle });
  })();

  const diffUnits = (before, g, causedByEventId=null, eventSequenceAtStart=0) => {
    EntityRegistry.sync(g);
    const previous = new Map();
    try {
      const raw = JSON.parse(before.raw);
      for (const team of ["P","A"]) for (const zone of ["field","bench","graveyard","summons"]) for (const unit of raw.teams?.[team]?.[zone] || []) if (unit) previous.set(unit.entityId || unit.uid,unit);
    } catch (error) { console.error("[R48] diff snapshot parse failed",error); }
    for (const unit of Object.values(g.entities || {})) {
      const id = unit.entityId || unit.uid, old = previous.get(id); if (!old) continue;
      const common = { recipientEntityId:id, teamId:typeof teamOf === "function" ? teamOf(g,unit) : null, causedByEventId };
      const hasDetailedDamage = (g.eventLog || []).some(ev =>
        Number(ev.sequence || 0) > Number(eventSequenceAtStart || 0) &&
        ev.type === "DAMAGE_APPLIED" &&
        ev.recipientEntityId === id &&
        ev.payload?.detailed === true
      );
      const hasDetailedHeal = (g.eventLog || []).some(ev =>
        Number(ev.sequence || 0) > Number(eventSequenceAtStart || 0) &&
        ["HEAL_APPLIED","HEALING_APPLIED"].includes(ev.type) &&
        ev.recipientEntityId === id &&
        ev.payload?.detailed === true
      );
      if (Number(unit.hp)!==Number(old.hp) && !((unit.hp<old.hp&&hasDetailedDamage)||(unit.hp>old.hp&&hasDetailedHeal))) {
        if (unit.hp < old.hp) emitDamageAppliedEvent(g, null, unit, {
          damageType:"stateDiff", before:{hp:old.hp,shield:old.shield}, after:{hp:unit.hp,shield:unit.shield},
          amount:Math.abs(unit.hp-old.hp), actionId:g?.pendingTransaction?.actionId || null,
        });
        else if(g?.pendingTransaction&&g.phase==="EXECUTING_ACTION") GenericBattleStatisticsOwner.recordHealing(g,{source:null,target:unit,requestedAmount:Math.abs(unit.hp-old.hp),actualAppliedAmount:Math.abs(unit.hp-old.hp),reason:"action-state-diff",effectId:"actionStateDiff",actionId:g.pendingTransaction.actionId||null,transactionId:g.pendingTransaction.transactionId||null});
      }
      if (Number(unit.shield)!==Number(old.shield)) BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"SHIELD_CHANGED",{...common,payload:{before:old.shield,after:unit.shield}});
      const oldStatus=stableText(old.statuses||{}),newStatus=stableText(unit.statuses||{});
      if(oldStatus!==newStatus) BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"STATUS_CHANGED",{...common,payload:{before:old.statuses||{},after:unit.statuses||{}}});
      const oldEnergy=(old.energy||[]).map(e=>({energyId:e.energyId,c:e.c,a:e.a})),newEnergy=EnergyEngine.snapshot(unit);
      if(stableText(oldEnergy)!==stableText(newEnergy)) BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"ENERGY_CHANGED",{...common,payload:{before:oldEnergy,after:newEnergy}});
      if(!old.dead&&unit.dead) BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"ENTITY_DIED",{...common,payload:{cardId:unit.card}});
    }
  };

  const DeathEngine = Object.freeze({
    reconcile(g) {
      StateFactory.decorate(g); const deaths=[];
      for (const unit of Object.values(g.entities||{})) if (Number(unit.hp)<=0 && !unit.dead && typeof checkDeath === "function") { checkDeath(g,unit,null); deaths.push(unit.entityId||unit.uid); }
      EntityRegistry.sync(g); return deaths;
    },
    alive:(g,team)=>typeof aliveUnits === "function" ? aliveUnits(g.teams[team]) : []
  });

  const TurnEngine = (() => {
    const hasLiveReplacement = (g,team) => !!g?.teams?.[team]?.bench?.some(unit => unit && !unit.dead);
    const currentOrderEntryCanResolve = g => {
      const [team,slot] = g?.order?.[g?.turn] || [];
      if (team == null || slot == null) return false;
      const unit = typeof unitAt === "function" ? unitAt(g,team,slot) : null;
      return !!(unit && !unit.dead) || hasLiveReplacement(g,team);
    };
    const normalizeDeadEmptyOrderEntries = g => {
      const skipped = [];
      const orderLength = Math.max(0,Number(g?.order?.length || 0));
      if (!orderLength || g?.winner || g?.ended) return skipped;
      for (let inspected = 0; inspected < orderLength && !currentOrderEntryCanResolve(g); inspected++) {
        const [team,slot] = g.order[g.turn] || [];
        const unit = team != null && slot != null && typeof unitAt === "function" ? unitAt(g,team,slot) : null;
        skipped.push(Object.freeze({teamId:team ?? null,slot:slot ?? null,entityId:unit?.entityId || unit?.uid || null,turnSerial:Number(g.turnSerial||0)}));
        BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"EMPTY_SLOT_TURN_SKIPPED",{
          teamId:team ?? null,
          sourceEntityId:unit?.entityId || unit?.uid || null,
          payload:{slot:slot ?? null,turnSerial:Number(g.turnSerial||0),reason:"DEAD_OR_EMPTY_WITHOUT_LIVE_REPLACEMENT"},
        });
        g.turn = (Number(g.turn || 0) + 1) % orderLength;
        g.turnSerial = Number(g.turnSerial || 0) + 1;
        g.preparedKey = null;
        g.dualActorController = null;
        if (g.turn === 0) g.round = Math.max(1,Number(g.round||1)) + 1;
        if (typeof checkWinner === "function") checkWinner(g);
        if (g.winner || g.ended) break;
      }
      return skipped;
    };

    return Object.freeze({
      end:(...args)=>endTurn(...args),
      current:g=>g?.order?.[g.turn] || null,
      phase:g=>g?.phase || "IDLE",
      normalizeDeadEmptyOrderEntries,
      currentOrderEntryCanResolve,
    });
  })();

  // Live authoritative actions use the transaction boundary. Guest request submission and simulation do not mutate authority state.
  const isBossGuestRequestOnlyExecution = (g,t,s,opt={}) => {
    if (opt?.sim || !g || g !== currentGame() || !g?.bossRaid?.online) return false;
    if (p2p?.role !== "guest" || p2p?.applyingRemoteAction || t !== "P") return false;
    const active = g.order?.[g.turn];
    if (!active || active[0] !== "P" || Number(active[1]) !== Number(s)) return false;
    if (!window.__redTimeoraBossRaid?.localControlsCurrent?.()) return false;
    const unit = unitAt(g,t,s);
    return unit ? unit.bossOwner === "guest" : g.bossRaid.slotOwners?.[s] === "guest";
  };
  const classifyR48Execution = (g,t,s,action,opt={}) => {
    const live = !opt?.sim && g && (g === currentGame() || opt?.r48Live === true);
    if (!live) return "simulation";
    const standardGuestRequest = g === currentGame() && g?.mode === "online" && !g?.bossRaid &&
      p2p?.role === "guest" && !p2p?.applyingRemoteAction && t === onlineSide;
    const bossGuestRequest = isBossGuestRequestOnlyExecution(g,t,s,opt);
    return standardGuestRequest || bossGuestRequest ? "request-only" : "authoritative-execution";
  };

      function execute(g,t,s,a,opt={}) {
        const thisArg=this;
        const base=(...args)=>executeCoreBase.apply(thisArg,args);
        const main=(...args)=>executeTimeStage(base,...args);
        const inner=(...args)=>{
          const invoke=()=>{
            if(window.__megaSpectatorR18?.canExecute?.(...args)===false)return false;
            return main(...args);
          };
          const overflow=window.__megaEnergyOverflowChoice;
          return typeof overflow?.withExecuteContext==="function"
            ? overflow.withExecuteContext(invoke,...args)
            : invoke();
        };
        const middle=(...args)=>{
          const energy=window.EnergyCanonicalizationEngine;
          return typeof energy?.execute==="function" ? energy.execute(inner,...args) : inner(...args);
        };
        return typeof actionTransactionExecuteStage==="function"
          ? actionTransactionExecuteStage.call(thisArg,middle,g,t,s,a,opt)
          : middle(g,t,s,a,opt);
      }
      function endTurn(g, t, s, usedSkill) {
        const __managed = typeof StateFactory!=="undefined" && typeof BattleEventEngine!=="undefined" && typeof TurnEngine!=="undefined";
        let __beforeSerial=Number(g?.turnSerial)||0;
        if (__managed) {
          StateFactory.decorate(g);
          if(g.ended||g.winner)return false;
          const currentPair=g.order?.[g.turn];
          if(currentPair&&(currentPair[0]!==t||String(currentPair[1])!==String(s)))return false;
          const key=`${g.battleId}:${g.turnSerial}:${t}:${s}`;
          if(g._r48LastEndTurnKey===key)return false;
          g._r48LastEndTurnKey=key; g.phase="TURN_END";
          __beforeSerial=Number(g.turnSerial)||0;
        }
        let __result;
        __endTurnCore: {
        let u = unitAt(g, t, s);
        const hadSeal = u ? !!status(u,"traitSeal") : false;
        const summonOwner = !!u && skillsOf(u).some(sk=>skillDefHasTag(sk,"summon"));
        const regen = !!u && cardHasCapability(u,"regenerationTrait") && traitsActive(u) && !status(u,"traitSeal");
        if (regen) for (const st of Object.keys(u.statuses||{})) if (st!=="traitSeal" && u.statuses[st]>1) u.statuses[st]--;
        if (u && !u.dead) {
          if (status(u, "burn") && !usedSkill)
            nonAttackDamage(g, u, 30, "화상");
          for (const st of Object.keys(u.statuses)) {
            if (u.statuses[st] > 0) {
              u.statuses[st]--;
              log(g, `${u.name}: ${STATUS[st]} ${u.statuses[st]}턴 남음.`);
              if (!u.statuses[st]) {
                delete u.statuses[st];
                queueCombatNotice(g, u, `${STATUS[st]} 종료`, "cure");
              }
            }
          }
          for (const sid of Object.keys(u.skillSeals || {})) {
            u.skillSeals[sid]--;
            if (u.skillSeals[sid] <= 0) {
              let skillName = card(u).skills.find((x) => x.id === sid)?.name || "기술";
              delete u.skillSeals[sid];
              queueCombatNotice(g, u, `${skillName} 기술봉인 종료`, "cure");
            }
          }
          u.turns++;
          u.turnSkillCostReduction = 0;
          if (cardHasCapability(u, "horizonFlow") && Number(u.hp) <= Number(u.maxHp) / 2) {
            const units = [...g.teams[t].field, ...g.teams[t].bench].filter(x => x && !x.dead);
            const countColor = color => units.reduce((sum, x) => sum + energyStore(x).reduce((n, entry) => n + (entry.c === color ? Number(entry.a || 0) : 0), 0), 0);
            const white = countColor("W"), black = countColor("B");
            const flowTargets = cardCapability(u, "horizonFlowTargets", {});
            if (white > black && flowTargets.W) FormFamilyOwner.transition(g, u, flowTargets.W, "시간의 흐름");
            else if (black > white && flowTargets.B) FormFamilyOwner.transition(g, u, flowTargets.B, "시간의 흐름");
          }
        }
        g.stats.turns++;
        let dealt = ["P", "A"]
          .flatMap((x) => [
            ...g.teams[x].field,
            ...g.teams[x].bench,
            ...g.teams[x].graveyard,
          ])
          .filter(Boolean)
          .reduce((n, x) => n + x.damageDealt, 0);
        if (dealt > g.lastDamageTotal) {
          g.lastDamageTotal = dealt;
          g.noDamageTurns = 0;
        } else g.noDamageTurns++;
        if (!g.winner && g.noDamageTurns >= 120 && u && !u.dead) {
          let raw = 10 + Math.floor((g.noDamageTurns - 120) / 8) * 10,
            streak = g.noDamageTurns;
          nonAttackDamage(g, u, raw, "교착 압박");
          g.noDamageTurns = streak;
        }
        if (!g.winner && g.stats.turns >= 800 && u && !u.dead) {
          let raw = 40 + Math.floor((g.stats.turns - 800) / 16) * 40,
            streak = g.noDamageTurns;
          nonAttackDamage(g, u, raw, "결판 압박");
          g.noDamageTurns = streak;
        }
        g.turn = (g.turn + 1) % 4;
        g.turnSerial++;
        g.preparedKey = null;
        g.dualActorController = null;
        if (g.turn === 0) g.round++;
        checkWinner(g);

        if (summonOwner && u && !u.dead) processJunglesSummons(g,t,u);
        if (g.circuitChain?.uid === u?.uid) {
          if (!u || u.dead || unitAt(g,t,s)?.uid !== u.uid) g.circuitChain=null;
          else if (g.circuitChain.remaining>0) {
            g.circuitChain.remaining--;
            g.turn=g.order.findIndex(x=>x[0]===t&&x[1]===s);
            g.preparedKey=null;
            log(g,`${u.name}: 전류가속 추가 차례 시작 (${g.circuitChain.remaining}회 더 남음).`,"turn");
          } else g.circuitChain=null;
        }
        if (reactiveEnergyRule(u) && hadSeal !== !!status(u,"traitSeal")) syncReactiveEnergyMaxHp(g,u,"특성봉인 종료",true);
      
        }
        if (!__managed) return __result;
        if((Number(g.turnSerial)||0)===__beforeSerial){delete g._r48LastEndTurnKey;return __result;}
        const skipped=TurnEngine.normalizeDeadEmptyOrderEntries(g); StateFactory.updateActive(g);
        BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"TURN_ENDED",{teamId:t,payload:{slot:s,usedSkill:!!usedSkill,turnSerial:__beforeSerial,skippedEmptySlots:skipped}});
        if(!g.ended)BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"TURN_STARTED",{teamId:g.activeTeamId,sourceEntityId:g.activeEntityId,payload:{turnSerial:g.turnSerial,round:g.round}});
        return __result===undefined?true:__result;
      }
      
      function checkWinner(g) {
        if (g.winner === "P" || g.winner === "A") return;
        actionFaultHit("victory-before");
        let p = aliveUnits(g.teams.P).length,
          a = aliveUnits(g.teams.A).length;
        if (!p || !a) {
          g.winner = p ? "P" : "A";
          actionFaultHit("victory-after");
          actionFaultHit("terminal-pending-after");
          actionFaultHit("terminal-commit-before");
          log(g, g.winner === "P" ? "플레이어 승리!" : "AI 승리!", "turn");
        } else actionFaultHit("victory-after");
      }
      function gatherPotential(u) {
        let max = card(u).maxEnergy || 4;
        if (totalEnergy(u) < max) return 20 - totalEnergy(u) * 2;
        let base = energyValue(u),
          gain = 0,
          store = energyStore(u);
        for (const col of ["R", "W", "B"]) {
          let best = base;
          store.forEach((e, i) => {
            if (e.a < 1) return;
            let pool = store.filter((_, j) => j !== i).map((x) => ({ ...x }));
            pool.push({ c: col, a: 1 });
            best = Math.max(best, energyValue(u, pool));
          });
          gain += Math.max(0, best - base);
        }
        return gain ? gain / 3 : -120;
      }
      const AI_SKILL_PROFILE_SCORERS = Object.freeze({
        mutualEnergyTrade({ g, u, tar, action, profile }) {
          if (!tar) return 0;
          const n = Number(action?.interactionAmount || 0),
            baseDamage = Number(profile?.baseDamage || 0),
            perEnergyDamage = Number(profile?.perEnergyDamage || 0),
            selfDamagePerEnergy = Number(profile?.selfDamagePerEnergy || perEnergyDamage),
            mean = baseDamage * (g.variance ? 0.83 : 1),
            targetTotal = Number(tar.hp || 0) + Number(tar.shield || 0),
            kill = mean + perEnergyDamage * n >= targetTotal,
            selfDies = selfDamagePerEnergy * n >= Number(u.hp || 0),
            trade = n * selfDamagePerEnergy * (u.hp < 150 ? 1.35 : u.hp < 230 ? 0.75 : 0.4);
          let score = mean + n * 14 - trade + (kill ? 190 : 0) - (selfDies && !kill ? 480 : selfDies ? 70 : 0);
          if (!kill && n > 0 && tar.hp > mean + perEnergyDamage * n) score -= n * 12;
          return score;
        },
      });
      function scoreSkillAiProfile(g, u, tar, action, skill) {
        const profile = skill?.aiProfile, scorer = profile && AI_SKILL_PROFILE_SCORERS[profile.kind];
        return typeof scorer === "function" ? Number(scorer({ g, u, tar, action, skill, profile }) || 0) : 0;
      }
      function actionHeuristicBase(g, t, s, a) {
        let u = unitAt(g, t, s),
          stall = g.stats.turns > 240;
        if (a.type === "deploy") {
          let x = g.teams[t].bench.find((v) => v.uid === a.unit),
            trap = g.teams[t].traps[s];
          return 80 + x.hp * 0.15 - (trap && !isStatusImmune(x, trap.status) ? 35 : 0);
        }
        if (a.type === "scheduled" || a.type === "sequence") {
          let k = u.scheduledAction?.kind;
          return k === "bloom" ? 260 : k === "rubyFollow" ? 150 : 120;
        }
        if (a.type === "dualPass") return -8;
        if (a.type === "switch") {
          let x = g.teams[t].bench[a.bench],
            trap = g.teams[t].traps[s];
          return (
            5 +
            (u.hp / u.maxHp < 0.3 ? 28 : 0) +
            x.hp * 0.06 -
            (trap && !isStatusImmune(x, trap.status) ? 30 : 0) -
            (stall ? 90 : 0)
          );
        }
        if (a.type === "gather") {
          let sc = gatherPotential(u);
          if (status(u, "burn")) sc -= 35;
          return sc;
        }
        let sk = card(u).skills.find((x) => x.id === a.skill),
          tar =
            sk?.target === "enemySlot"
              ? null
              : a.target
                ? unitAt(g, a.target.team, a.target.slot)
                : null,
          sc = 10;
        if (!sk) return sc;
        if (sk.target === "enemySlot") {
          let tr = g.teams[other(t)].traps[a.target.slot];
          return tr ? 3 : 30 + (g.teams[other(t)].bench.length ? 18 : 0);
        }
        if (skillDefHasTag(sk, "free")) sc += 45;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "bloomingLife")) sc += u.hp < 70 ? 80 : 210;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "lifeRain"))
          sc +=
            g.teams[t].field.reduce(
              (n, x) => n + (x ? Math.max(0, x.maxHp - x.hp) : 0),
              0,
            ) *
              0.6 +
            55;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "iceRoar")) {
          let frozenEnemies = g.teams[other(t)].field.filter(
              (x) => x && !x.dead && !isStatusImmune(x, "freeze"),
            ).length,
            ally = g.teams[t].field.find((x) => x && x.uid !== u.uid);
          sc +=
            frozenEnemies * 55 -
            (ally && !isStatusImmune(ally, "freeze") && !cardHasCapability(ally, "irumaCleanse")
              ? 45
              : 0);
          if (canPayFrom(u, "WR*", "rider")) sc += 80;
        }
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "rampage"))
          sc +=
            (1 + (a.extra || 0)) * 10 +
            (tar && tar.hp <= (1 + (a.extra || 0)) * 10 ? 120 : 0) -
            (a.extra || 0) * 8;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "shatterLance") && tar)
          sc += 70 + status(tar, "freeze") * 30;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "firestorm")) sc += 150 + (u.hp > 60 ? -25 : 0);
        if (skillDefHasRuntimeKind(sk, "openingGuard")) sc += u.shield ? 45 : 125;
        if (skillDefHasRuntimeKind(sk, "dynamicDefenseSmash"))
          sc += 85 + (6 - (a.costOverride?.length || 0)) * 5;
        if (skillDefHasTag(sk, "copy")) sc += 75;
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "holyFlame") && tar) {
          let d = Math.max(0, 100 - 20 * allEnergy(tar));
          sc += d + (100 - d) * 0.9;
        }
        if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "prawnCharge") && tar) sc += hasNullifyTrait(tar) ? 170 : 125;
        for (const e of sk.effects) {
          if (e.type === "damage" && tar) {
            let r = damageRange(g, u, tar, e, sk),
              hits = e.hits || 1,
              mean = r.mean * hits,
              killProb =
                tar.shield && !e.shieldMelt
                  ? 0
                  : r.min * hits >= tar.hp + (e.shieldMelt ? tar.shield : 0)
                    ? 1
                    : r.max * hits < tar.hp
                      ? 0
                      : (r.max * hits - tar.hp) /
                        Math.max(1, (r.max - r.min) * hits);
            sc += mean + killProb * 120;
            if (e.shieldMelt) sc += Math.min(tar.shield, mean) * 0.5;
            if (card(tar).reflect) {
              let refl = card(tar).reflect * hits;
              if (refl >= u.hp) sc -= 220;
              if (tar.hideHits && mean / hits <= 30) sc -= 65;
            }
          }
          if (e.type === "heal" && tar)
            sc +=
              Math.min(e.amount, tar.maxHp - tar.hp) * 1.3 -
              (tar.hp === tar.maxHp ? 50 : 0) -
              (stall ? 80 : 0);
          if (e.type === "shield" && tar) {
            let useful = Math.min(
              e.amount,
              Math.max(0, (e.max ?? 999) - tar.shield),
            );
            sc +=
              useful * (tar.shield > 45 ? 0.15 : 0.8) - (useful === 0 ? 50 : 0);
          }
          if (e.type === "status" && tar) {
            let irumaChance = g.knowledge?.unknownCandidates?.includes("iruma")
              ? 1 / Math.max(1, g.knowledge.unknownCandidates.length)
              : 0;
            sc +=
              (e.status === "poison"
                ? 35 + allEnergy(tar) * 7
                : e.status === "slow"
                  ? 24
                  : 18) *
              (1 - irumaChance);
          }
          if (e.type === "statusChance" && tar) sc += e.chance * 24;
          if (e.type === "startSequence" && tar) sc += 100;
          if (e.type === "clearFieldEnergy") {
            let enemy = g.teams[other(t)].field.reduce(
                (n, x) => n + (x && !x.dead ? allEnergy(x) : 0),
                0,
              ),
              ally = g.teams[t].field.reduce(
                (n, x) => n + (x && !x.dead ? allEnergy(x) : 0),
                0,
              );
            sc += (enemy - ally) * 22;
          }
        }
        if (status(u, "burn")) sc += 28;
        return sc;
      }

      }

      function specApplySnapshot(data) {
        const first = !document.body.classList.contains("spectator-mode") || game?.mode !== "spectator";
        try {
          const revived = data.bossRaid && typeof window.__redTimeoraBossRaid?.revive === "function"

            : window.OnlineSnapshotEngine.restoreState(data.state,{source:"spectator",rules:data.rules || ONLINE_COMMON_RULES});
          revived.mode = "spectator";
          revived.spectatorBossRaid = !!data.bossRaid;
          revived.matchId = String(data.matchId || "");
          game = revived;
          onlineSide = spectatorState.viewSide === "A" ? "A" : "P";
          busy = false;
          pendingSkill = null;
          if (first) {
            lastLog = 0;
            visualEvents = [];
            document.getElementById("log").innerHTML = "";
          }
          if (data.visualPacketId && data.visualPacketId !== spectatorState.lastVisualPacketId) {
            spectatorState.lastVisualPacketId = data.visualPacketId;

          } else visualEvents = [];

        }
      }
      function specLeaveWatching() {
        const w = spectatorState.watching;
        if (w) {
          const rec = spectatorState.matches.get(w.matchId) || w.record;
          for (const [transportName,peerId] of Object.entries(rec?.sources || {})) {
            const transport = spectatorState.transports.get(transportName);
            if (transport) specSafeSend(transport.leave,{protocol:SPEC_VERSION,compat:SPEC_COMPAT,matchId:w.matchId,spectatorId},peerId);
          }
        }
        window.TimerManager.clear(spectatorState.requestTimer); spectatorState.requestTimer = null;
        spectatorState.watching = null;
        spectatorState.mode = "idle";
        spectatorState.lastVisualPacketId = null;
        spectatorState.viewSide = "P";
        spectatorState.chatSeen.clear();
        document.body.classList.remove("spectator-mode");
        const bar = document.getElementById("spectatorBar"); if (bar) bar.hidden = true;
        game = null;
        pendingSkill = null;
        busy = false;
        NavigationApplication.request("setup");
        specChatApi()?.sync?.();
        specReturnToSourceLobby();
      }
