      function buildAIDeck(seed, league = "mega", composition = comprehensiveComposition) {
        league = LeagueFormatOwner.normalizeLeague(league);
        composition = LeagueFormatOwner.normalizeComposition(composition);
        let rng = new RNG(seed + "-ai-deck"), deck = [], pool = [...poolForLeague(league, composition)];
        while (deck.length < 4) {
          const ranked = pool
            .filter(id => LeagueFormatOwner.partialValid([...deck, id], league, composition))
            .map(id => ({ id, score: deckScore([...deck, id]) + rng.next() * 4 }))
            .sort((a,b) => b.score - a.score);
          if (!ranked.length) throw new Error(`AI deck composition exhausted: ${league}/${composition}/${deck.join(",")}`);
          const pick = ranked[0].id;
          deck.push(pick);
          pool = pool.filter(id => id !== pick);
        }
        if (!LeagueFormatOwner.completeValid(deck, league, composition)) throw new Error(`AI deck composition invalid: ${league}/${composition}`);
        return league === "mega" ? arrangeAIDeck(deck) : deck;
      }
      function localTeamFor(g) {
        return (g?.mode === "online" || g?.mode === "spectator") ? onlineSide : "P";
      }
      function displaySideFor(g, t) {
        return t === localTeamFor(g) ? "player" : "ai";
      }
      const LocalInputEligibility = (() => {
        let providers = Object.freeze({
          locks: () => [],
          clock: () => ({ locked: false }),
          online: () => ({}),
          bossControls: () => true,
        });
        const bindProviders = next => {
          providers = Object.freeze({ ...providers, ...(next || {}) });
          return true;
        };
        const evaluate = (g, context = null) => {
          const reasons = [];
          const surfaceKind = context?.surfaceKind || (g?.bossRaid ? "boss-raid" : "base");
          const pair = context?.actorTeam != null
            ? [context.actorTeam, context.actorSlot ?? null]
            : (g?.order?.[g?.turn] || []);
          const currentActorCanonicalTeam = pair[0] ?? null;
          const currentActorSlot = pair[1] == null ? null : pair[1];
          const actor = context?.actor || (currentActorCanonicalTeam != null && currentActorSlot != null
            ? unitAt(g, currentActorCanonicalTeam, currentActorSlot)
            : null);
          const currentActorUid = context?.actorUid || actor?.entityId || actor?.uid || null;
          const canonicalLocalTeam = context?.canonicalLocalTeam || (g ? localTeamFor(g) : null);
          const displayLocalSide = canonicalLocalTeam ? displaySideFor(g, canonicalLocalTeam) : null;
          const standardOnline = context?.standardOnline ?? (g?.mode === "online" && !g?.bossRaid?.online);
          const online = providers.online?.(g) || {};
          const runtime = online.runtime || {};
          const locks = [...(providers.locks?.() || [])];
          const clock = providers.clock?.() || {};
          let actions = [];
          try {
            if (Array.isArray(context?.actions)) actions = [...context.actions];
            else if (g && currentActorCanonicalTeam != null && currentActorSlot != null) actions = legalActions(g, currentActorCanonicalTeam, currentActorSlot) || [];
          }
          catch (error) { reasons.push("LEGAL_ACTION_EVALUATION_FAILED"); }
          const replacementRequired = (!actor || actor.dead) && actions.some(action => action.type === "deploy");
          const mandatoryAutomaticAction = actions.some(action => action.type === "scheduled" || action.type === "speedDelay");
          const manualActions = actions.filter(action => action.type !== "scheduled" && action.type !== "speedDelay");

          if (!g) reasons.push("NO_GAME");
          else {
            if (g.ended || g.winner) reasons.push("BATTLE_ENDED");
            if (!pair.length) reasons.push("CURRENT_ACTOR_MISSING");
            if ((!actor || actor.dead) && !replacementRequired) reasons.push("CURRENT_ACTOR_NOT_ALIVE");
            if (!context?.skipActiveIdentityChecks && g.activeTeamId && g.activeTeamId !== currentActorCanonicalTeam) reasons.push("ACTIVE_TEAM_MISMATCH");
            if (!context?.skipActiveIdentityChecks && g.activeEntityId && currentActorUid && g.activeEntityId !== currentActorUid) reasons.push("ACTIVE_ENTITY_MISMATCH");
            if (g.mode === "spectator") reasons.push("SPECTATOR_READ_ONLY");
            if (g.mode === "aivai" || g.mode === "ai-ai") reasons.push("AI_CONTROLLED_MODE");
            if (currentActorCanonicalTeam !== canonicalLocalTeam) reasons.push("REMOTE_ACTOR_TURN");
            if (standardOnline) {
              const expectedTeam = online.role === "host" ? "P" : online.role === "guest" ? "A" : null;
              if (!online.connected) reasons.push("TRANSPORT_NOT_CONNECTED");
              if (!online.battleStarted) reasons.push("BATTLE_START_NOT_COMMITTED");
              if (expectedTeam && canonicalLocalTeam !== expectedTeam) reasons.push("ROLE_CANONICAL_TEAM_MISMATCH");
              if (runtime.generation > 0 && !runtime.committed) reasons.push("SESSION_NOT_COMMITTED");
              if (runtime.generation > 0 && runtime.state !== "IN_BATTLE") reasons.push(`SESSION_${runtime.state || "UNKNOWN"}`);
              if (online.reconnecting) reasons.push("RECONNECTING");
            }
            if (g?.bossRaid?.online) {
              if (!online.connected) reasons.push("TRANSPORT_NOT_CONNECTED");
              if (!online.battleStarted) reasons.push("BATTLE_START_NOT_COMMITTED");
              if (runtime.generation > 0 && (!runtime.committed || runtime.state !== "IN_BATTLE")) reasons.push(`SESSION_${runtime.state || "NOT_COMMITTED"}`);
              if (online.reconnecting) reasons.push("RECONNECTING");
            }
            if (g?.bossRaid?.online && !providers.bossControls?.(g)) reasons.push("BOSS_SLOT_NOT_LOCAL");
            if (locks.length) reasons.push("ACTIVE_INPUT_LOCK");
            if (clock.locked) reasons.push("CLOCK_INPUT_LOCK");
            if (g.pendingTransaction || g.pendingAction || g.pendingPayment || context?.pendingSubmitted) reasons.push("PENDING_AUTHORITATIVE_ACTION");
            if (online.pendingActionId || context?.pendingActionId) reasons.push("PENDING_ONLINE_ACTION");
            if (online.busy) reasons.push("APPLICATION_BUSY");
            if (!actions.length) reasons.push("NO_LEGAL_ACTIONS");
            if (mandatoryAutomaticAction) reasons.push("MANDATORY_AUTOMATIC_ACTION");
          }
          const uniqueReasons = [...new Set(reasons)];
          const ownsCurrentActor = !!g && currentActorCanonicalTeam === canonicalLocalTeam && !["SPECTATOR_READ_ONLY", "AI_CONTROLLED_MODE"].some(reason => uniqueReasons.includes(reason));
          const canAct = ownsCurrentActor && uniqueReasons.length === 0 && manualActions.length > 0;
          return Object.freeze({
            canAct, reasons: uniqueReasons, ownsCurrentActor,
            canonicalLocalTeam, displayLocalSide,
            currentActorCanonicalTeam, currentActorSlot, currentActorUid,
            currentActorCharacterId: actor?.card || null,
            activeEntityId: g?.activeEntityId || null,
            actorAlive: !!actor && !actor.dead, replacementRequired,
            mandatoryAutomaticAction,
            legalActionCount: actions.length,
            manualLegalActionCount: manualActions.length,
            gatherLegal: actions.some(action => action.type === "gather"),
            actions,
            activeLocks: locks.map(lock => ({ ...lock })),
            pendingActionId: online.pendingActionId || g?.pendingAction?.actionId || null,
            authoritativeRevision: Number(online.authoritativeRevision || 0),
            localAppliedRevision: Number(g?.networkRevision || 0),
            sessionState: runtime.state || null,
            reconnectState: online.reconnecting ? "RECONNECTING" : "CONNECTED",
            surfaceKind,
          });
        };
        return Object.freeze({ evaluate, canAct: g => evaluate(g).canAct, bindProviders });
      })();
      function grandVisualOwner(g) {
        if (g?.fieldEffect?.type !== "grand") return null;
        return g.fieldEffect.team === localTeamFor(g) ? "P" : "A";
      }
      function renderCore() {
        if (typeof window.ActionFaultInjector === "object") {
          try { window.ActionFaultInjector.hit("render-before"); }
          catch (error) {
            window.PresentationFailureChannel?.emit?.({surface:"render",phase:"render-before",error:String(error),battleId:game?.battleId||game?.matchId||null,revision:Number(game?.networkRevision||0)});
            queueMicrotask(()=>window.LifecycleManager?.continuity?.authoritativeSettled?.({reason:"presentation-failure",surface:"render"}));
            throw error;
          }
        }
        if (!game) return;
        prepareCurrentTurn(game);
        const inputProjection = LocalInputEligibility.evaluate(game);
        let localSide = inputProjection.canonicalLocalTeam;
        document.querySelector(".player-side .side-head b").textContent =
          game.mode === "online" ? "내 진영" : "플레이어 진영";
        document.querySelector(".ai-side .side-head b").textContent =
          game.mode === "online" ? "상대 진영" : "AI 진영";
        for (const t of ["A", "P"]) {
          let tm = game.teams[t],
            isLocal = t === localSide,
            pre = displaySideFor(game, t),
            displayTeam = isLocal ? "P" : "A";
          $(`#${pre}Slots`).innerHTML = tm.field
            .map(
              (u, i) =>
                `<div class="slot" data-slotteam="${t}" data-slotindex="${i}">${tm.traps[i] ? '<span class="trap">⚡ 전기태클</span>' : ""}${unitHTML(u, t, i, false, displayTeam)}</div>`,
            )
            .join("");
          if (t !== localSide) {
            let known = tm.bench.filter(
                (u) => u && game.revealed[t].includes(u.uid),
              ),
              hidden = tm.bench.filter(
                (u) => u && !game.revealed[t].includes(u.uid),
              ).length,
              parts = known.map((u, i) => unitHTML(u, t, i, true, "A"));
            if (hidden)
              parts.push(
                `<div class="unknown-bench"><b>정체불명 후열</b><span>남은 카드 ${hidden}장</span></div>`,
              );
            $(`#${pre}Bench`).innerHTML =
              parts.join("") || '<span class="muted">후열 없음</span>';
          } else
            $(`#${pre}Bench`).innerHTML =
              tm.bench.map((u, i) => unitHTML(u, t, i, true, "P")).join("") ||
              '<span class="muted">후열 없음</span>';
          $(`#${pre}Alive`).textContent =
            `생존 ${aliveUnits(tm).length}/4 · 교체 ${tm.switchUses || 0}/3`;
        }
        let turns = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
          let oi = (game.turn + i) % 4,
            [t, s] = game.order[oi],
            u = unitAt(game, t, s);
          turns.push(
            `<span class="turntoken ${i ? "" : "now"}">${i ? "다음" : "현재"} ${t === localSide ? "내" : game.mode === "online" ? "상대" : "AI"}-${s + 1}: ${u && !u.dead ? u.name : "빈 슬롯"}</span>`,
          );
        }
        $("#turnStrip").innerHTML = turns.join("");
        $$(".card").forEach((x) =>
          x.classList.remove("active-turn", "targetable"),
        );
        let [ct, cs] = game.order[game.turn],
          cu = unitAt(game, ct, cs),
          activeEntityId = inputProjection.currentActorUid || cu?.entityId || cu?.uid || null,
          el = activeEntityId ? battlefieldCardForUid(game, activeEntityId) : null;
        document.querySelector(".arena")?.setAttribute("data-active-entity-id", activeEntityId || "");
        document.querySelector(".arena")?.setAttribute("data-active-canonical-team", inputProjection.currentActorCanonicalTeam || "");
        if (el?.isConnected) el.classList.add("active-turn");
        if (pendingSkill) {
          for (const tar of pendingSkill.targets) {
            let q = pendingSkill.slotTarget
              ? `.slot[data-slotteam="${tar.team}"][data-slotindex="${tar.slot}"]`
              : `.card[data-zone="field"][data-team="${tar.team}"][data-slot="${tar.slot}"]`;
            let e = $(q);
            if (e) {
              e.classList.add("targetable");
              e.onclick = () => playerChooseTarget(tar);
            }
          }
        }
        renderLog();
        renderActions();
        playVisuals();
        refreshCardTooltip();
        if (game.winner) showEnd();
      }
      function render(...args){ return RenderLayerOwner.run(renderCore,this,args); }
      function syncGrandFieldBackground(g) {
        let arena = document.querySelector(".arena");
        if (!arena) return;
        arena.classList.remove("grand-P", "grand-A");
        document.querySelector(".player-side")?.classList.remove("grand-benefit", "grand-penalty");
        document.querySelector(".ai-side")?.classList.remove("grand-benefit", "grand-penalty");
        arena.classList.toggle("no-field-motion", false);
        if (g?.fieldEffect?.type === "grand") {
          let visualOwner = grandVisualOwner(g);
          arena.classList.add(`grand-${visualOwner}`);
          let good = visualOwner === "P" ? ".player-side" : ".ai-side",
            bad = visualOwner === "P" ? ".ai-side" : ".player-side";
          document.querySelector(good)?.classList.add("grand-benefit");
          document.querySelector(bad)?.classList.add("grand-penalty");
        }
      }

      function renderLog() {
        let box = $("#log");
        const distanceFromBottom = box.scrollHeight - box.scrollTop - box.clientHeight;
        const followLatest = lastLog === 0 || distanceFromBottom <= 28;
        const previousTop = box.scrollTop;
        let mobileClose = box.querySelector(".mobile-log-close");
        if (!mobileClose) {
          mobileClose = document.createElement("button");
          mobileClose.type = "button";
          mobileClose.className = "mobile-log-close";
          mobileClose.textContent = "로그 닫기";
          mobileClose.setAttribute("aria-label", "전투 로그 닫기");
          mobileClose.onclick = (event) => {
            event.preventDefault();
            event.stopPropagation();
            setBattleLogOpen(false);
          };
          box.prepend(mobileClose);
        }
        for (; lastLog < game.logs.length; lastLog++) {
          const l = game.logs[lastLog] || {};
          const message = normalizeGameNumberText(l.msg);
          if (!message) continue;
          const d = document.createElement("div");
          d.className = l.cls || "";
          d.textContent = message;
          box.appendChild(d);
        }
        if (followLatest) box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
        else box.scrollTop = previousTop;
      }
      function actionButton(box, label, fn, cls = "") {
        let b = document.createElement("button");
        b.textContent = normalizeGameNumberText(label) || "행동";
        if (cls) b.className = cls;
        b.onclick = fn;
        box.appendChild(b);
        return b;
      }
      function paymentCostForAction(u, a) {
        let sk = card(u).skills.find((x) => x.id === a.skill);
        return effectiveSkillCost(game, game?.order?.[game.turn]?.[0] || "P", u, sk, a.costOverride ?? (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "hurricaneDive") ? "W*" : sk?.cost || ""));
      }
      function residualEnergyEntries(u, actor, plan) {
        let left = energyStore(u, actor).map((e, index) => ({ ...e, index }));
        for (const c of "RWB") {
          let need = plan?.[c] || 0;
          for (let i = left.length - 1; i >= 0 && need > 0.001; i--) {
            if (left[i].c !== c) continue;
            let take = Math.min(left[i].a, need);
            left[i].a -= take; need -= take;
          }
        }
        return left.filter((e) => e.a > 0.001);
      }
      function residualDualEnergyEntries(u, baseActor, plan) {
        let out = [];
        for (const who of ["beast", "rider"]) {
          let left = energyStore(u, who).map((e, index) => ({ ...e, index, actor: who }));
          if (who === baseActor) {
            for (const c of "RWB") {
              let need = plan?.[c] || 0;
              for (let i = left.length - 1; i >= 0 && need > 0.001; i--) {
                if (left[i].c !== c) continue;
                let take = Math.min(left[i].a, need);
                left[i].a -= take;
                need -= take;
              }
            }
          }
          out.push(...left.filter((e) => e.a > 0.001));
        }
        return out;
      }
      function openManualPayment(actions, done, title = "기술 코스트 선택") {
        if (!game || !Array.isArray(actions) || !actions.length) return;
        const [t, s] = game.order[game.turn], u = unitAt(game, t, s), a0 = actions[0], actor = a0.actor || null;
        if (!u) return;
        const store = energyStore(u, actor), picked = new Set();
        const requirements = actions.map(a => {
          const cost = paymentCostForAction(u, a);
          const transformed = transformedSkillReq(game, t, u, cost);
          const need = Object.values(transformed.req).reduce((n, x) => n + Number(x || 0), 0);
          return { action:a, cost, transformed, need };
        });
        const uniqueLabels = [...new Set(requirements.map(x => x.transformed.tokens.join(" ") || "없음"))];
        $("#modalContent").innerHTML = `<h2>${title}</h2><p>필요 코스트: <b>${uniqueLabels.join(" 또는 ")}</b>. 실제로 지불할 에너지를 선택하세요.</p><div class="energy manual-payment-energy"></div><div id="manualPaymentPreview" class="effect-box"></div><div class="choice-list"><button id="manualPaymentCancel">취소</button><button id="manualPaymentConfirm" class="primary" disabled>이 에너지로 지불</button></div>`;
        const box = $("#modalContent .manual-payment-energy"), preview = $("#manualPaymentPreview"), confirm = $("#manualPaymentConfirm");
        const counts = () => {
          const c = {R:0,W:0,B:0};
          for (const i of picked) {
            const e = store[i];
            if (e) c[e.c] += Number(e.a || 0);
          }
          return c;
        };
        const planMatches = (reqInfo, c) => {
          const total = c.R + c.W + c.B;
          if (Math.abs(total - reqInfo.need) > .001) return false;
          if (!reqAcceptsCounts(reqInfo.transformed.req, c)) return false;
          const p = reqInfo.action.paymentPlan;
          if (p && (Math.abs(Number(p.R||0)-c.R)>.001 || Math.abs(Number(p.W||0)-c.W)>.001 || Math.abs(Number(p.B||0)-c.B)>.001)) return false;
          return true;
        };
        const refresh = () => {
          const c = counts(), total = c.R + c.W + c.B;
          const matchingInfo = requirements.filter(x => planMatches(x,c));
          const matching = matchingInfo.map(x => x.action);
          const targetNeeds = [...new Set(requirements.map(x => energyAmountText(x.need)))].join(" 또는 ");
          const abyssInfo = actionSkillHasEffect(a0, "abyssCannon")
            ? ` · 예상 기본 피해 ${Math.max(0, 100 - 40 * (4 - (Number(a0.baseCostCount) || 0)))}D · 독 조건 ${c.R > 0 && c.W > 0 && c.B > 0 ? "충족" : "불충족"}`
            : "";
          preview.textContent = `선택 ${c.R ? `R${energyAmountText(c.R)} ` : ""}${c.W ? `W${energyAmountText(c.W)} ` : ""}${c.B ? `B${energyAmountText(c.B)}` : ""} · ${matching.length ? "지불 가능" : `총 ${energyAmountText(total)}/${targetNeeds}, 코스트 조건 불충족`}${abyssInfo}`;
          confirm.disabled = !matching.length;
          return {c, matching};
        };
        store.forEach((e,i) => {
          const b = document.createElement("button");
          b.className = `e ${e.c}`;
          b.textContent = `${e.c}${Number(e.a)===.5?"½":""}`;
          b.title = `${e.c} 에너지 ${energyAmountText(e.a)}`;
          b.onclick = () => {
            picked.has(i) ? picked.delete(i) : picked.add(i);
            b.classList.toggle("chosen", picked.has(i));
            refresh();
          };
          box.appendChild(b);
        });
        $("#manualPaymentCancel").onclick = () => $("#modal").classList.remove("open");
        confirm.onclick = () => {
          const {c, matching} = refresh();
          if (!matching.length) return;
          done(matching.map(a => ({...a, paymentPlan:{...c}})), {...c});
        };
        refresh();
        $("#modal").classList.add("open");
      }

      const ActionRenderEngine = (() => {
        const routes = new Map(), decorators = new Map();
        let renderSerial = 0, lastRender = null;
        const ordered = map => [...map.values()].sort((a,b)=>b.priority-a.priority||a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
        const registerRoute = (id,priority,when,render) => {
          if(!id||typeof when!=="function"||typeof render!=="function")throw new TypeError("invalid action render route");
          routes.set(String(id),{id:String(id),priority:Number(priority)||0,when,render});
          return ()=>routes.delete(String(id));
        };
        const registerDecorator = (id,priority,decorate,when=()=>true) => {
          if(!id||typeof decorate!=="function"||typeof when!=="function")throw new TypeError("invalid action render decorator");
          decorators.set(String(id),{id:String(id),priority:Number(priority)||0,decorate,when});
          return ()=>decorators.delete(String(id));
        };
        const context = viewModel => {
          const pair=game?.order?.[game.turn]||[];
          return {game,team:pair[0],slot:pair[1],unit:pair.length?unitAt(game,pair[0],pair[1]):null,box:document.getElementById("actionButtons"),viewModel};
        };
        const render = (viewModel = null, requestReason = "unspecified") => {
          const panelView = viewModel || ActionPanelViewModelOwner.build(game);
          let ctx=context(panelView),result;
          const route=ordered(routes).find(entry=>entry.when(ctx));
          result=route?route.render(ctx):renderBaseActions(panelView);
          ctx=context(panelView);
          for(const entry of ordered(decorators))if(entry.when(ctx))entry.decorate(ctx,result);
          const projection=panelView.projection;
          const buttons=[...(ctx.box?.querySelectorAll("button")||[])];
          const actionButtons=buttons.filter(button=>!button.dataset.actionPanelMeta);
          const fingerprint=actionButtons.map(button=>`${button.disabled?"0":"1"}:${button.textContent?.replace(/\s+/g," ").trim()||""}`).join("|");
          lastRender=Object.freeze({
            renderSerial:++renderSerial,
            renderedAt:Date.now(),
            requestReason:String(requestReason||"unspecified"),
            routeId:route?.id||"base",
            battleId:panelView.battleId,
            sessionGeneration:Number(window.OnlineRuntime?.debug?.().generation||0),
            networkRevision:panelView.canonicalRevision,
            authoritativeRevision:Number(projection.authoritativeRevision||0),
            actionPanelViewModelRevision:panelView.canonicalRevision,
            renderedDomRevision:panelView.canonicalRevision,
            viewModelSignature:panelView.signature,
            turnSerial:panelView.turnSerial,
            actorUid:projection.currentActorUid||null,
            actorCanonicalTeam:projection.currentActorCanonicalTeam||null,
            localCanonicalTeam:projection.canonicalLocalTeam||null,
            canAct:projection.canAct,
            reasons:[...projection.reasons],
            legalActionCount:Number(projection.legalActionCount||0),
            manualLegalActionCount:Number(projection.manualLegalActionCount||0),
            buttonCount:buttons.length,
            actionButtonCount:actionButtons.length,
            enabledButtonCount:actionButtons.filter(button=>!button.disabled).length,
            buttonFingerprint:fingerprint,
            gatherRendered:actionButtons.some(button=>button.textContent?.includes("에너지 모으기")),
          });
          return result;
        };
        return Object.freeze({
          registerRoute,registerDecorator,render,base:()=>ActionPanelConvergenceOwner.request("action-render-base-api",{force:true}),
          reset:(reason="session-boundary")=>{
            renderSerial=0; lastRender=null;
            const box=document.getElementById("actionButtons"); if(box)box.innerHTML="";
            return Object.freeze({reason:String(reason),renderSerial,lastRender:null});
          },
          snapshot:()=>({
            routes:ordered(routes).map(x=>({id:x.id,priority:x.priority})),
            decorators:ordered(decorators).map(x=>({id:x.id,priority:x.priority})),
            renderSerial,
            lastRender:lastRender?{...lastRender,reasons:[...lastRender.reasons]}:null,
          }),
        });
      })();
      const ActionPanelConvergenceOwner = (() => {
        let requestSerial=0,commitSerial=0,skippedDuplicateCount=0,lastRequest=null;
        const domState = () => {
          const root=document.getElementById("actionButtons");
          const buttons=[...(root?.querySelectorAll("button")||[])];
          const actionButtons=buttons.filter(button=>!button.dataset.actionPanelMeta);
          return Object.freeze({
            rootConnected:!!root?.isConnected,
            buttonCount:buttons.length,
            actionButtonCount:actionButtons.length,
            enabledActionButtonCount:actionButtons.filter(button=>!button.disabled).length,
            fingerprint:actionButtons.map(button=>`${button.disabled?"0":"1"}:${button.textContent?.replace(/\s+/g," ").trim()||""}`).join("|"),
          });
        };
        const mustBeEmpty = view => {
          const reasons=view.projection.reasons||[];
          return view.terminal || reasons.some(reason=>[
            "REMOTE_ACTOR_TURN","SPECTATOR_READ_ONLY","AI_CONTROLLED_MODE","PENDING_AUTHORITATIVE_ACTION",
            "PENDING_ONLINE_ACTION","APPLICATION_BUSY","TRANSPORT_NOT_CONNECTED","BATTLE_START_NOT_COMMITTED",
            "RECONNECTING","CLOCK_INPUT_LOCK","ACTIVE_INPUT_LOCK"
          ].includes(reason));
        };
        const matches = (view,renderState=ActionRenderEngine.snapshot(),dom=domState()) => {
          const prior=renderState.lastRender;
          if(!prior||prior.viewModelSignature!==view.signature)return false;
          if(Number(prior.actionPanelViewModelRevision||0)!==Number(view.canonicalRevision||0))return false;
          if(Number(prior.renderedDomRevision||0)!==Number(view.canonicalRevision||0))return false;
          if(prior.actorUid!==(view.projection.currentActorUid||null))return false;
          if(prior.localCanonicalTeam!==(view.projection.canonicalLocalTeam||null))return false;
          if(prior.buttonFingerprint!==dom.fingerprint)return false;
          if(mustBeEmpty(view))return dom.actionButtonCount===0;
          if(view.projection.canAct&&view.projection.manualLegalActionCount>0)return dom.enabledActionButtonCount>0;
          return true;
        };
        const suspend = (reason="action-panel-suspend") => {
          const root=document.getElementById("actionButtons"),buttons=[...(root?.querySelectorAll("button")||[])];
          if(root)root.dataset.actionPanelSuspended=String(reason||"suspended");
          for(const button of buttons){if(button.dataset.actionPanelMeta==="terminal")continue;button.disabled=true;button.setAttribute("aria-disabled","true");}
          const state=domState();lastRequest=Object.freeze({requestId:++requestSerial,reason:String(reason),committed:true,coherent:true,suspended:true,signature:null,canonicalRevision:Number(game?.networkRevision||0),turnSerial:Number(game?.turnSerial||0),actorUid:null,dom:state,at:Date.now()});
          return lastRequest;
        };
        const request = (reason="action-panel-convergence", options={}) => {
          const view=ActionPanelViewModelOwner.build(game);
          const root=document.getElementById("actionButtons");if(root)delete root.dataset.actionPanelSuspended;
          const before=ActionRenderEngine.snapshot(),beforeDom=domState();
          const coherent=!options.force&&matches(view,before,beforeDom);
          const requestId=++requestSerial;
          if(coherent){
            skippedDuplicateCount++;
            lastRequest=Object.freeze({requestId,reason:String(reason),committed:false,coherent:true,signature:view.signature,canonicalRevision:view.canonicalRevision,turnSerial:view.turnSerial,actorUid:view.projection.currentActorUid||null,dom:beforeDom,at:Date.now()});

            return lastRequest;
          }
          ActionRenderEngine.render(view,reason);
          commitSerial++;
          const after=ActionRenderEngine.snapshot(),afterDom=domState();
          const converged=matches(view,after,afterDom);
          lastRequest=Object.freeze({requestId,reason:String(reason),committed:true,coherent:converged,signature:view.signature,canonicalRevision:view.canonicalRevision,turnSerial:view.turnSerial,actorUid:view.projection.currentActorUid||null,dom:afterDom,at:Date.now()});

          return lastRequest;
        };
        return Object.freeze({
          request,suspend,
          matches,
          reset:(reason="session-boundary")=>{
            requestSerial=0; commitSerial=0; skippedDuplicateCount=0; lastRequest=null;
            ActionRenderEngine.reset(reason);
            return Object.freeze({reason:String(reason),requestSerial,commitSerial,lastRequest:null,render:ActionRenderEngine.snapshot(),dom:{...domState()}});
          },
          snapshot:()=>({requestSerial,commitSerial,skippedDuplicateCount,lastRequest:lastRequest?{...lastRequest,dom:{...lastRequest.dom}}:null,render:ActionRenderEngine.snapshot(),dom:{...domState()}}),
        });
      })();
      function renderActions(reason="legacy-render-actions"){ return ActionPanelConvergenceOwner.request(reason); }

      function playerGather(a) {
        const [t, s] = game.order[game.turn],
          u = unitAt(game, t, s),
          actor = a.actor || null;
        if (u && timeAccelerationIsActive(game,u)) return openTimeAccelerationGather(a,t,s,u);
        if (u && (cardHasCapability(u,"jungleMud") || cardHasCapability(u,"alternatingCharge"))) return openCustomNaturalGather(a,t,s,u);
        const store = energyStore(u, actor),
          amt = status(u, "slow") ? 0.5 : 1,
          existingHalf = amt === 0.5 ? store.find((e) => e.a === 0.5) : null,
          proposal = previewNaturalGatherProposal(game, t, s, u, amt, actor, "natural-gather"),
          col = proposal?.color,
          max = card(u).maxEnergy || 4;
        if (!proposal || !col) return;
        a.gatherProposal = proposal;
        if (existingHalf) log(game, `${u.name}: 기존 ${col} 반에너지와 합치기 위해 자연 수급 색이 ${col}로 고정.`);
        if (totalEnergy(u, actor) + amt <= max + .001) {
          const executed = execute(game, t, s, a);
          if (executed) afterAction();
          return;
        }
        const overflow = totalEnergy(u, actor) + amt - max,
          root = $("#modalContent"), seen = new Set(), items = [];
        store.forEach((e, i) => {
          if (Number(e.a || 0) + .001 < overflow) return;
          const key = `${e.c}:${e.a}`;
          if (seen.has(key)) return;
          seen.add(key);
          items.push({i, e});
        });
        root.innerHTML = `<h2>에너지 최대</h2><p>새 ${col}${amt === 0.5 ? "½" : ""}을 보관하려면 기존 에너지 <b>하나만</b> 버리세요.${Math.abs(totalEnergy(u, actor)-3.5)<.001 ? " 현재 3.5E이므로 반에너지 하나도 버릴 수 있습니다." : ""}</p><div class="choice-list energy-single-discard"></div><div class="choice-list"><button id="discardNew">새 에너지 버리기</button><button id="gatherCancel">취소</button></div>`;
        const list = root.querySelector('.energy-single-discard');
        for (const {i,e} of items) {
          const b = document.createElement('button');
          b.textContent = `${e.c}${e.a === .5 ? '½' : ''} 하나 버리고 새 ${col}${amt === .5 ? '½' : ''} 보관`;
          b.onclick = () => {

            a.replaceIndex = i;
            delete a.replaceIndices;
            delete a.discardNew;
            if (execute(game, t, s, a)) afterAction();
          };
          list.appendChild(b);
        }
        if (!items.length) list.innerHTML = `<p class="muted">에너지 하나만 버려서는 새 에너지를 보관할 수 없습니다.</p>`;
        root.querySelector("#discardNew").onclick = () => {
          $("#modal").classList.remove("open");
          a.discardNew = true;
          delete a.replaceIndex; delete a.replaceIndices;
          if (execute(game, t, s, a)) afterAction();
        };
        root.querySelector("#gatherCancel").onclick = () => $("#modal").classList.remove("open");
        $("#modal").classList.add("open");
      }

      function openMysticExtras(actions, plan) {
        let [t, s] = game.order[game.turn], u = unitAt(game, t, s),
          entries = residualEnergyEntries(u, actions[0]?.actor || null, plan)
            .filter((e) => e.a >= 1 - 1e-6),

n validFormDeck(ids) {
        let groups = ids
          .map((id) => CARDS.find((c) => c.id === id)?.formGroup)
          .filter(Boolean);
        return new Set(groups).size === groups.length &&
          new Set(ids).size === ids.length &&
          (ids || []).filter(isInvaderCardId).length <= 1;
      }
      function buildAIDeck(seed, league = "mega", composition = comprehensiveComposition) {
        league = LeagueFormatOwner.normalizeLeague(league);
        composition = LeagueFormatOwner.normalizeComposition(composition);
        let rng = new RNG(seed + "-ai-deck"), deck = [], pool = [...poolForLeague(league, composition)];
        while (deck.length < 4) {
          const ranked = pool
            .filter(id => LeagueFormatOwner.partialValid([...deck, id], league, composition))
            .map(id => ({ id, score: deckScore([...deck, id]) + rng.next() * 4 }))
            .sort((a,b) => b.score - a.score);
          if (!ranked.length) throw new Error(`AI deck composition exhausted: ${league}/${composition}/${deck.join(",")}`);
          const pick = ranked[0].id;
          deck.push(pick);
          pool = pool.filter(id => id !== pick);
        }
        if (!LeagueFormatOwner.completeValid(deck, league, composition)) throw new Error(`AI deck composition invalid: ${league}/${composition}`);
        return league === "mega" ? arrangeAIDeck(deck) : deck;
      }
      function localTeamFor(g) {
        return (g?.mode === "online" || g?.mode === "spectator") ? onlineSide : "P";
      }
      function displaySideFor(g, t) {
        return t === localTeamFor(g) ? "player" : "ai";
      }
      const LocalInputEligibility = (() => {
        let providers = Object.freeze({
          locks: () => [],
          clock: () => ({ locked: false }),
          online: () => ({}),
          bossControls: () => true,
        });
        const bindProviders = next => {
          providers = Object.freeze({ ...providers, ...(next || {}) });
          return true;
        };
        const evaluate = (g, context = null) => {
          const reasons = [];
          const surfaceKind = context?.surfaceKind || (g?.bossRaid ? "boss-raid" : "base");
          const pair = context?.actorTeam != null
            ? [context.actorTeam, context.actorSlot ?? null]
            : (g?.order?.[g?.turn] || []);
          const currentActorCanonicalTeam = pair[0] ?? null;
          const currentActorSlot = pair[1] == null ? null : pair[1];
          const actor = context?.actor || (currentActorCanonicalTeam != null && currentActorSlot != null
            ? unitAt(g, currentActorCanonicalTeam, currentActorSlot)
            : null);
          const currentActorUid = context?.actorUid || actor?.entityId || actor?.uid || null;
          const canonicalLocalTeam = context?.canonicalLocalTeam || (g ? localTeamFor(g) : null);
          const displayLocalSide = canonicalLocalTeam ? displaySideFor(g, canonicalLocalTeam) : null;
          const standardOnline = context?.standardOnline ?? (g?.mode === "online" && !g?.bossRaid?.online);
          const online = providers.online?.(g) || {};
          const runtime = online.runtime || {};
          const locks = [...(providers.locks?.() || [])];
          const clock = providers.clock?.() || {};
          let actions = [];
          try {
            if (Array.isArray(context?.actions)) actions = [...context.actions];
            else if (g && currentActorCanonicalTeam != null && currentActorSlot != null) actions = legalActions(g, currentActorCanonicalTeam, currentActorSlot) || [];
          }
          catch (error) { reasons.push("LEGAL_ACTION_EVALUATION_FAILED"); }
          const replacementRequired = (!actor || actor.dead) && actions.some(action => action.type === "deploy");
          const mandatoryAutomaticAction = actions.some(action => action.type === "scheduled" || action.type === "speedDelay");
          const manualActions = actions.filter(action => action.type !== "scheduled" && action.type !== "speedDelay");

          if (!g) reasons.push("NO_GAME");
          else {
            if (g.ended || g.winner) reasons.push("BATTLE_ENDED");
            if (!pair.length) reasons.push("CURRENT_ACTOR_MISSING");
            if ((!actor || actor.dead) && !replacementRequired) reasons.push("CURRENT_ACTOR_NOT_ALIVE");
            if (!context?.skipActiveIdentityChecks && g.activeTeamId && g.activeTeamId !== currentActorCanonicalTeam) reasons.push("ACTIVE_TEAM_MISMATCH");
            if (!context?.skipActiveIdentityChecks && g.activeEntityId && currentActorUid && g.activeEntityId !== currentActorUid) reasons.push("ACTIVE_ENTITY_MISMATCH");
            if (g.mode === "spectator") reasons.push("SPECTATOR_READ_ONLY");
            if (g.mode === "aivai" || g.mode === "ai-ai") reasons.push("AI_CONTROLLED_MODE");
            if (currentActorCanonicalTeam !== canonicalLocalTeam) reasons.push("REMOTE_ACTOR_TURN");
            if (standardOnline) {
              const expectedTeam = online.role === "host" ? "P" : online.role === "guest" ? "A" : null;
              if (!online.connected) reasons.push("TRANSPORT_NOT_CONNECTED");
              if (!online.battleStarted) reasons.push("BATTLE_START_NOT_COMMITTED");
              if (expectedTeam && canonicalLocalTeam !== expectedTeam) reasons.push("ROLE_CANONICAL_TEAM_MISMATCH");
              if (runtime.generation > 0 && !runtime.committed) reasons.push("SESSION_NOT_COMMITTED");
              if (runtime.generation > 0 && runtime.state !== "IN_BATTLE") reasons.push(`SESSION_${runtime.state || "UNKNOWN"}`);
              if (online.reconnecting) reasons.push("RECONNECTING");
            }
            if (g?.bossRaid?.online) {
              if (!online.connected) reasons.push("TRANSPORT_NOT_CONNECTED");
              if (!online.battleStarted) reasons.push("BATTLE_START_NOT_COMMITTED");
              if (runtime.generation > 0 && (!runtime.committed || runtime.state !== "IN_BATTLE")) reasons.push(`SESSION_${runtime.state || "NOT_COMMITTED"}`);
              if (online.reconnecting) reasons.push("RECONNECTING");
            }
            if (g?.bossRaid?.online && !providers.bossControls?.(g)) reasons.push("BOSS_SLOT_NOT_LOCAL");
            if (locks.length) reasons.push("ACTIVE_INPUT_LOCK");
            if (clock.locked) reasons.push("CLOCK_INPUT_LOCK");
            if (g.pendingTransaction || g.pendingAction || g.pendingPayment || context?.pendingSubmitted) reasons.push("PENDING_AUTHORITATIVE_ACTION");
            if (online.pendingActionId || context?.pendingActionId) reasons.push("PENDING_ONLINE_ACTION");
            if (online.busy) reasons.push("APPLICATION_BUSY");
            if (!actions.length) reasons.push("NO_LEGAL_ACTIONS");
            if (mandatoryAutomaticAction) reasons.push("MANDATORY_AUTOMATIC_ACTION");
          }
          const uniqueReasons = [...new Set(reasons)];
          const ownsCurrentActor = !!g && currentActorCanonicalTeam === canonicalLocalTeam && !["SPECTATOR_READ_ONLY", "AI_CONTROLLED_MODE"].some(reason => uniqueReasons.includes(reason));
          const canAct = ownsCurrentActor && uniqueReasons.length === 0 && manualActions.length > 0;
          return Object.freeze({
            canAct, reasons: uniqueReasons, ownsCurrentActor,
            canonicalLocalTeam, displayLocalSide,
            currentActorCanonicalTeam, currentActorSlot, currentActorUid,
            currentActorCharacterId: actor?.card || null,
            activeEntityId: g?.activeEntityId || null,
            actorAlive: !!actor && !actor.dead, replacementRequired,
            mandatoryAutomaticAction,
            legalActionCount: actions.length,
            manualLegalActionCount: manualActions.length,
            gatherLegal: actions.some(action => action.type === "gather"),
            actions,
            activeLocks: locks.map(lock => ({ ...lock })),
            pendingActionId: online.pendingActionId || g?.pendingAction?.actionId || null,
            authoritativeRevision: Number(online.authoritativeRevision || 0),
            localAppliedRevision: Number(g?.networkRevision || 0),
            sessionState: runtime.state || null,
            reconnectState: online.reconnecting ? "RECONNECTING" : "CONNECTED",
            surfaceKind,
          });
        };
        return Object.freeze({ evaluate, canAct: g => evaluate(g).canAct, bindProviders });
      })();
      function grandVisualOwner(g) {
        if (g?.fieldEffect?.type !== "grand") return null;

      }
      function renderCore() {

            queueMicrotask(()=>window.LifecycleManager?.continuity?.authoritativeSettled?.({reason:"presentation-failure",surface:"render"}));
            throw error;
          }
        }
        if (!game) return;
        prepareCurrentTurn(game);
        const inputProjection = LocalInputEligibility.evaluate(game);
        let localSide = inputProjection.canonicalLocalTeam;
        document.querySelector(".player-side .side-head b").textContent =
          game.mode === "online" ? "내 진영" : "플레이어 진영";
        document.querySelector(".ai-side .side-head b").textContent =
          game.mode === "online" ? "상대 진영" : "AI 진영";
        for (const t of ["A", "P"]) {
          let tm = game.teams[t],
            isLocal = t === localSide,
            pre = displaySideFor(game, t),
            displayTeam = isLocal ? "P" : "A";
          $(`#${pre}Slots`).innerHTML = tm.field
            .map(

            )
            .join("");
          if (t !== localSide) {
            let known = tm.bench.filter(
                (u) => u && game.revealed[t].includes(u.uid),
              ),
              hidden = tm.bench.filter(
                (u) => u && !game.revealed[t].includes(u.uid),
              ).length,
              parts = known.map((u, i) => unitHTML(u, t, i, true, "A"));
            if (hidden)
              parts.push(
                `<div class="unknown-bench"><b>정체불명 후열</b><span>남은 카드 ${hidden}장</span></div>`,
              );
            $(`#${pre}Bench`).innerHTML =
              parts.join("") || '<span class="muted">후열 없음</span>';
          } else
            $(`#${pre}Bench`).innerHTML =
              tm.bench.map((u, i) => unitHTML(u, t, i, true, "P")).join("") ||

          $(`#${pre}Alive`).textContent =

        }
        let turns = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
          let oi = (game.turn + i) % 4,
            [t, s] = game.order[oi],
            u = unitAt(game, t, s);
          turns.push(
            `<span class="turntoken ${i ? "" : "now"}">${i ? "다음" : "현재"} ${t === localSide ? "내" : game.mode === "online" ? "상대" : "AI"}-${s + 1}: ${u && !u.dead ? u.name : "빈 슬롯"}</span>`,
          );
        }

        $$(".card").forEach((x) =>

      function renderLog() {

      function paymentCostForAction(u, a) {
        let sk = card(u).skills.find((x) => x.id === a.skill);
        return effectiveSkillCost(game, game?.order?.[game.turn]?.[0] || "P", u, sk, a.costOverride ?? (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "hurricaneDive") ? "W*" : sk?.cost || ""));
      }
      function residualEnergyEntries(u, actor, plan) {
        let left = energyStore(u, actor).map((e, index) => ({ ...e, index }));
        for (const c of "RWB") {
          let need = plan?.[c] || 0;
          for (let i = left.length - 1; i >= 0 && need > 0.001; i--) {
            if (left[i].c !== c) continue;
            let take = Math.min(left[i].a, need);
            left[i].a -= take; need -= take;
          }
        }
        return left.filter((e) => e.a > 0.001);
      }
      function residualDualEnergyEntries(u, baseActor, plan) {
        let out = [];
        for (const who of ["beast", "rider"]) {
          let left = energyStore(u, who).map((e, index) => ({ ...e, index, actor: who }));
          if (who === baseActor) {
            for (const c of "RWB") {
              let need = plan?.[c] || 0;
              for (let i = left.length - 1; i >= 0 && need > 0.001; i--) {
                if (left[i].c !== c) continue;
                let take = Math.min(left[i].a, need);
                left[i].a -= take;
                need -= take;
              }
            }
          }
          out.push(...left.filter((e) => e.a > 0.001));
        }
        return out;
      }
      function openManualPayment(actions, done, title = "기술 코스트 선택") {
        if (!game || !Array.isArray(actions) || !actions.length) return;
        const [t, s] = game.order[game.turn], u = unitAt(game, t, s), a0 = actions[0], actor = a0.actor || null;
        if (!u) return;
        const store = energyStore(u, actor), picked = new Set();
        const requirements = actions.map(a => {
          const cost = paymentCostForAction(u, a);
          const transformed = transformedSkillReq(game, t, u, cost);
          const need = Object.values(transformed.req).reduce((n, x) => n + Number(x || 0), 0);
          return { action:a, cost, transformed, need };
        });
        const uniqueLabels = [...new Set(requirements.map(x => x.transformed.tokens.join(" ") || "없음"))];
        $("#modalContent").innerHTML = `<h2>${title}</h2><p>필요 코스트: <b>${uniqueLabels.join(" 또는 ")}</b>. 실제로 지불할 에너지를 선택하세요.</p><div class="energy manual-payment-energy"></div><div id="manualPaymentPreview" class="effect-box"></div><div class="choice-list"><button id="manualPaymentCancel">취소</button><button id="manualPaymentConfirm" class="primary" disabled>이 에너지로 지불</button></div>`;
        const box = $("#modalContent .manual-payment-energy"), preview = $("#manualPaymentPreview"), confirm = $("#manualPaymentConfirm");
        const counts = () => {
          const c = {R:0,W:0,B:0};
          for (const i of picked) {
            const e = store[i];
            if (e) c[e.c] += Number(e.a || 0);
          }
          return c;
        };
        const planMatches = (reqInfo, c) => {
          const total = c.R + c.W + c.B;
          if (Math.abs(total - reqInfo.need) > .001) return false;
          if (!reqAcceptsCounts(reqInfo.transformed.req, c)) return false;
          const p = reqInfo.action.paymentPlan;
          if (p && (Math.abs(Number(p.R||0)-c.R)>.001 || Math.abs(Number(p.W||0)-c.W)>.001 || Math.abs(Number(p.B||0)-c.B)>.001)) return false;
          return true;
        };
        const refresh = () => {
          const c = counts(), total = c.R + c.W + c.B;
          const matchingInfo = requirements.filter(x => planMatches(x,c));
          const matching = matchingInfo.map(x => x.action);

      function buildAIDeck(seed, league = "mega", composition = comprehensiveComposition) {
        league = LeagueFormatOwner.normalizeLeague(league);
        composition = LeagueFormatOwner.normalizeComposition(composition);
        let rng = new RNG(seed + "-ai-deck"), deck = [], pool = [...poolForLeague(league, composition)];
        while (deck.length < 4) {
          const ranked = pool
            .filter(id => LeagueFormatOwner.partialValid([...deck, id], league, composition))
            .map(id => ({ id, score: deckScore([...deck, id]) + rng.next() * 4 }))
            .sort((a,b) => b.score - a.score);
          if (!ranked.length) throw new Error(`AI deck composition exhausted: ${league}/${composition}/${deck.join(",")}`);
          const pick = ranked[0].id;
          deck.push(pick);
          pool = pool.filter(id => id !== pick);
        }
        if (!LeagueFormatOwner.completeValid(deck, league, composition)) throw new Error(`AI deck composition invalid: ${league}/${composition}`);
        return league === "mega" ? arrangeAIDeck(deck) : deck;
      }

      const LocalInputEligibility = (() => {
        let providers = Object.freeze({
          locks: () => [],
          clock: () => ({ locked: false }),
          online: () => ({}),
          bossControls: () => true,
        });
        const bindProviders = next => {
          providers = Object.freeze({ ...providers, ...(next || {}) });
          return true;
        };
        const evaluate = (g, context = null) => {
          const reasons = [];
          const surfaceKind = context?.surfaceKind || (g?.bossRaid ? "boss-raid" : "base");
          const pair = context?.actorTeam != null
            ? [context.actorTeam, context.actorSlot ?? null]
            : (g?.order?.[g?.turn] || []);
          const currentActorCanonicalTeam = pair[0] ?? null;
          const currentActorSlot = pair[1] == null ? null : pair[1];
          const actor = context?.actor || (currentActorCanonicalTeam != null && currentActorSlot != null
            ? unitAt(g, currentActorCanonicalTeam, currentActorSlot)
            : null);
          const currentActorUid = context?.actorUid || actor?.entityId || actor?.uid || null;
          const canonicalLocalTeam = context?.canonicalLocalTeam || (g ? localTeamFor(g) : null);
          const displayLocalSide = canonicalLocalTeam ? displaySideFor(g, canonicalLocalTeam) : null;
          const standardOnline = context?.standardOnline ?? (g?.mode === "online" && !g?.bossRaid?.online);
          const online = providers.online?.(g) || {};
          const runtime = online.runtime || {};
          const locks = [...(providers.locks?.() || [])];
          const clock = providers.clock?.() || {};
          let actions = [];
          try {
            if (Array.isArray(context?.actions)) actions = [...context.actions];
            else if (g && currentActorCanonicalTeam != null && currentActorSlot != null) actions = legalActions(g, currentActorCanonicalTeam, currentActorSlot) || [];
          }
          catch (error) { reasons.push("LEGAL_ACTION_EVALUATION_FAILED"); }
          const replacementRequired = (!actor || actor.dead) && actions.some(action => action.type === "deploy");
          const mandatoryAutomaticAction = actions.some(action => action.type === "scheduled" || action.type === "speedDelay");
          const manualActions = actions.filter(action => action.type !== "scheduled" && action.type !== "speedDelay");

          if (!g) reasons.push("NO_GAME");
          else {
            if (g.ended || g.winner) reasons.push("BATTLE_ENDED");
            if (!pair.length) reasons.push("CURRENT_ACTOR_MISSING");
            if ((!actor || actor.dead) && !replacementRequired) reasons.push("CURRENT_ACTOR_NOT_ALIVE");
            if (!context?.skipActiveIdentityChecks && g.activeTeamId && g.activeTeamId !== currentActorCanonicalTeam) reasons.push("ACTIVE_TEAM_MISMATCH");
            if (!context?.skipActiveIdentityChecks && g.activeEntityId && currentActorUid && g.activeEntityId !== currentActorUid) reasons.push("ACTIVE_ENTITY_MISMATCH");
            if (g.mode === "spectator") reasons.push("SPECTATOR_READ_ONLY");
            if (g.mode === "aivai" || g.mode === "ai-ai") reasons.push("AI_CONTROLLED_MODE");
            if (currentActorCanonicalTeam !== canonicalLocalTeam) reasons.push("REMOTE_ACTOR_TURN");
            if (standardOnline) {
              const expectedTeam = online.role === "host" ? "P" : online.role === "guest" ? "A" : null;
              if (!online.connected) reasons.push("TRANSPORT_NOT_CONNECTED");
              if (!online.battleStarted) reasons.push("BATTLE_START_NOT_COMMITTED");
              if (expectedTeam && canonicalLocalTeam !== expectedTeam) reasons.push("ROLE_CANONICAL_TEAM_MISMATCH");
              if (runtime.generation > 0 && !runtime.committed) reasons.push("SESSION_NOT_COMMITTED");
              if (runtime.generation > 0 && runtime.state !== "IN_BATTLE") reasons.push(`SESSION_${runtime.state || "UNKNOWN"}`);
              if (online.reconnecting) reasons.push("RECONNECTING");
            }
            if (g?.bossRaid?.online) {
              if (!online.connected) reasons.push("TRANSPORT_NOT_CONNECTED");
              if (!online.battleStarted) reasons.push("BATTLE_START_NOT_COMMITTED");
              if (runtime.generation > 0 && (!runtime.committed || runtime.state !== "IN_BATTLE")) reasons.push(`SESSION_${runtime.state || "NOT_COMMITTED"}`);
              if (online.reconnecting) reasons.push("RECONNECTING");
            }
            if (g?.bossRaid?.online && !providers.bossControls?.(g)) reasons.push("BOSS_SLOT_NOT_LOCAL");
            if (locks.length) reasons.push("ACTIVE_INPUT_LOCK");
            if (clock.locked) reasons.push("CLOCK_INPUT_LOCK");
            if (g.pendingTransaction || g.pendingAction || g.pendingPayment || context?.pendingSubmitted) reasons.push("PENDING_AUTHORITATIVE_ACTION");
            if (online.pendingActionId || context?.pendingActionId) reasons.push("PENDING_ONLINE_ACTION");
            if (online.busy) reasons.push("APPLICATION_BUSY");
            if (!actions.length) reasons.push("NO_LEGAL_ACTIONS");
            if (mandatoryAutomaticAction) reasons.push("MANDATORY_AUTOMATIC_ACTION");
          }
          const uniqueReasons = [...new Set(reasons)];
          const ownsCurrentActor = !!g && currentActorCanonicalTeam === canonicalLocalTeam && !["SPECTATOR_READ_ONLY", "AI_CONTROLLED_MODE"].some(reason => uniqueReasons.includes(reason));
          const canAct = ownsCurrentActor && uniqueReasons.length === 0 && manualActions.length > 0;
          return Object.freeze({
            canAct, reasons: uniqueReasons, ownsCurrentActor,
            canonicalLocalTeam, displayLocalSide,
            currentActorCanonicalTeam, currentActorSlot, currentActorUid,
            currentActorCharacterId: actor?.card || null,
            activeEntityId: g?.activeEntityId || null,
            actorAlive: !!actor && !actor.dead, replacementRequired,
            mandatoryAutomaticAction,
            legalActionCount: actions.length,
            manualLegalActionCount: manualActions.length,
            gatherLegal: actions.some(action => action.type === "gather"),
            actions,
            activeLocks: locks.map(lock => ({ ...lock })),
            pendingActionId: online.pendingActionId || g?.pendingAction?.actionId || null,
            authoritativeRevision: Number(online.authoritativeRevision || 0),
            localAppliedRevision: Number(g?.networkRevision || 0),
            sessionState: runtime.state || null,
            reconnectState: online.reconnecting ? "RECONNECTING" : "CONNECTED",
            surfaceKind,
          });
        };
        return Object.freeze({ evaluate, canAct: g => evaluate(g).canAct, bindProviders });
      })();

      function paymentCostForAction(u, a) {
        let sk = card(u).skills.find((x) => x.id === a.skill);
        return effectiveSkillCost(game, game?.order?.[game.turn]?.[0] || "P", u, sk, a.costOverride ?? (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "hurricaneDive") ? "W*" : sk?.cost || ""));

      function residualEnergyEntries(u, actor, plan) {
        let left = energyStore(u, actor).map((e, index) => ({ ...e, index }));
        for (const c of "RWB") {
          let need = plan?.[c] || 0;
          for (let i = left.length - 1; i >= 0 && need > 0.001; i--) {
            if (left[i].c !== c) continue;
            let take = Math.min(left[i].a, need);
            left[i].a -= take; need -= take;
          }
        }
        return left.filter((e) => e.a > 0.001);
      }
      function residualDualEnergyEntries(u, baseActor, plan) {
        let out = [];
        for (const who of ["beast", "rider"]) {
          let left = energyStore(u, who).map((e, index) => ({ ...e, index, actor: who }));
          if (who === baseActor) {
            for (const c of "RWB") {
              let need = plan?.[c] || 0;
              for (let i = left.length - 1; i >= 0 && need > 0.001; i--) {
                if (left[i].c !== c) continue;
                let take = Math.min(left[i].a, need);
                left[i].a -= take;
                need -= take;
              }
            }
          }
          out.push(...left.filter((e) => e.a > 0.001));
        }
        return out;
      }

        const bindProviders = next => {
          providers = Object.freeze({ ...providers, ...(next || {}) });
          return true;
        };
        const evaluate = (g, context = null) => {
          const reasons = [];
          const surfaceKind = context?.surfaceKind || (g?.bossRaid ? "boss-raid" : "base");
          const pair = context?.actorTeam != null
            ? [context.actorTeam, context.actorSlot ?? null]
            : (g?.order?.[g?.turn] || []);
          const currentActorCanonicalTeam = pair[0] ?? null;
          const currentActorSlot = pair[1] == null ? null : pair[1];
          const actor = context?.actor || (currentActorCanonicalTeam != null && currentActorSlot != null
            ? unitAt(g, currentActorCanonicalTeam, currentActorSlot)
            : null);
          const currentActorUid = context?.actorUid || actor?.entityId || actor?.uid || null;
          const canonicalLocalTeam = context?.canonicalLocalTeam || (g ? localTeamFor(g) : null);
          const displayLocalSide = canonicalLocalTeam ? displaySideFor(g, canonicalLocalTeam) : null;
          const standardOnline = context?.standardOnline ?? (g?.mode === "online" && !g?.bossRaid?.online);
          const online = providers.online?.(g) || {};
          const runtime = online.runtime || {};
          const locks = [...(providers.locks?.() || [])];
          const clock = providers.clock?.() || {};
          let actions = [];
          try {
            if (Array.isArray(context?.actions)) actions = [...context.actions];
            else if (g && currentActorCanonicalTeam != null && currentActorSlot != null) actions = legalActions(g, currentActorCanonicalTeam, currentActorSlot) || [];
          }
          catch (error) { reasons.push("LEGAL_ACTION_EVALUATION_FAILED"); }
          const replacementRequired = (!actor || actor.dead) && actions.some(action => action.type === "deploy");
          const mandatoryAutomaticAction = actions.some(action => action.type === "scheduled" || action.type === "speedDelay");
          const manualActions = actions.filter(action => action.type !== "scheduled" && action.type !== "speedDelay");

          if (!g) reasons.push("NO_GAME");
          else {
            if (g.ended || g.winner) reasons.push("BATTLE_ENDED");
            if (!pair.length) reasons.push("CURRENT_ACTOR_MISSING");
            if ((!actor || actor.dead) && !replacementRequired) reasons.push("CURRENT_ACTOR_NOT_ALIVE");
            if (!context?.skipActiveIdentityChecks && g.activeTeamId && g.activeTeamId !== currentActorCanonicalTeam) reasons.push("ACTIVE_TEAM_MISMATCH");
            if (!context?.skipActiveIdentityChecks && g.activeEntityId && currentActorUid && g.activeEntityId !== currentActorUid) reasons.push("ACTIVE_ENTITY_MISMATCH");
            if (g.mode === "spectator") reasons.push("SPECTATOR_READ_ONLY");
            if (g.mode === "aivai" || g.mode === "ai-ai") reasons.push("AI_CONTROLLED_MODE");
            if (currentActorCanonicalTeam !== canonicalLocalTeam) reasons.push("REMOTE_ACTOR_TURN");
            if (standardOnline) {
              const expectedTeam = online.role === "host" ? "P" : online.role === "guest" ? "A" : null;
              if (!online.connected) reasons.push("TRANSPORT_NOT_CONNECTED");
              if (!online.battleStarted) reasons.push("BATTLE_START_NOT_COMMITTED");
              if (expectedTeam && canonicalLocalTeam !== expectedTeam) reasons.push("ROLE_CANONICAL_TEAM_MISMATCH");
              if (runtime.generation > 0 && !runtime.committed) reasons.push("SESSION_NOT_COMMITTED");
              if (runtime.generation > 0 && runtime.state !== "IN_BATTLE") reasons.push(`SESSION_${runtime.state || "UNKNOWN"}`);
              if (online.reconnecting) reasons.push("RECONNECTING");
            }
            if (g?.bossRaid?.online) {
              if (!online.connected) reasons.push("TRANSPORT_NOT_CONNECTED");
              if (!online.battleStarted) reasons.push("BATTLE_START_NOT_COMMITTED");
              if (runtime.generation > 0 && (!runtime.committed || runtime.state !== "IN_BATTLE")) reasons.push(`SESSION_${runtime.state || "NOT_COMMITTED"}`);
              if (online.reconnecting) reasons.push("RECONNECTING");
            }
            if (g?.bossRaid?.online && !providers.bossControls?.(g)) reasons.push("BOSS_SLOT_NOT_LOCAL");
            if (locks.length) reasons.push("ACTIVE_INPUT_LOCK");
            if (clock.locked) reasons.push("CLOCK_INPUT_LOCK");
            if (g.pendingTransaction || g.pendingAction || g.pendingPayment || context?.pendingSubmitted) reasons.push("PENDING_AUTHORITATIVE_ACTION");
            if (online.pendingActionId || context?.pendingActionId) reasons.push("PENDING_ONLINE_ACTION");
            if (online.busy) reasons.push("APPLICATION_BUSY");
            if (!actions.length) reasons.push("NO_LEGAL_ACTIONS");
            if (mandatoryAutomaticAction) reasons.push("MANDATORY_AUTOMATIC_ACTION");
          }
          const uniqueReasons = [...new Set(reasons)];
          const ownsCurrentActor = !!g && currentActorCanonicalTeam === canonicalLocalTeam && !["SPECTATOR_READ_ONLY", "AI_CONTROLLED_MODE"].some(reason => uniqueReasons.includes(reason));
          const canAct = ownsCurrentActor && uniqueReasons.length === 0 && manualActions.length > 0;
          return Object.freeze({
            canAct, reasons: uniqueReasons, ownsCurrentActor,
            canonicalLocalTeam, displayLocalSide,
            currentActorCanonicalTeam, currentActorSlot, currentActorUid,
            currentActorCharacterId: actor?.card || null,
            activeEntityId: g?.activeEntityId || null,
            actorAlive: !!actor && !actor.dead, replacementRequired,
            mandatoryAutomaticAction,
            legalActionCount: actions.length,
            manualLegalActionCount: manualActions.length,
            gatherLegal: actions.some(action => action.type === "gather"),
            actions,
            activeLocks: locks.map(lock => ({ ...lock })),
            pendingActionId: online.pendingActionId || g?.pendingAction?.actionId || null,
            authoritativeRevision: Number(online.authoritativeRevision || 0),
            localAppliedRevision: Number(g?.networkRevision || 0),
            sessionState: runtime.state || null,
            reconnectState: online.reconnecting ? "RECONNECTING" : "CONNECTED",
            surfaceKind,
          });
        };
        return Object.freeze({ evaluate, canAct: g => evaluate(g).canAct, bindProviders });
      })();
      function grandVisualOwner(g) {
        if (g?.fieldEffect?.type !== "grand") return null;
        return g.fieldEffect.team === localTeamFor(g) ? "P" : "A";
      }
      function renderCore() {

            throw error;
          }
        }
        if (!game) return;

          game.mode === "online" ? "상대 진영" : "AI 진영";
        for (const t of ["A", "P"]) {

            )
            .join("");
          if (t !== localSide) {

                (u) => u && game.revealed[t].includes(u.uid),

              parts = known.map((u, i) => unitHTML(u, t, i, true, "A"));
            if (hidden)

              );
            $(`#${pre}Bench`).innerHTML =
              parts.join("") || '<span class="muted">후열 없음</span>';
          } else

        }
        let turns = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
          let oi = (game.turn + i) % 4,
            [t, s] = game.order[oi],
            u = unitAt(game, t, s);
          turns.push(
            `<span class="turntoken ${i ? "" : "now"}">${i ? "다음" : "현재"} ${t === localSide ? "내" : game.mode === "online" ? "상대" : "AI"}-${s + 1}: ${u && !u.dead ? u.name : "빈 슬롯"}</span>`,
          );
        }
        $("#turnStrip").innerHTML = turns.join("");
        $$(".card").forEach((x) =>
          x.classList.remove("active-turn", "targetable"),
        );
        let [ct, cs] = game.order[game.turn],
          cu = unitAt(game, ct, cs),

          el = activeEntityId ? battlefieldCardForUid(game, activeEntityId) : null;
        document.querySelector(".arena")?.setAttribute("data-active-entity-id", activeEntityId || "");
        document.querySelector(".arena")?.setAttribute("data-active-canonical-team", inputProjection.currentActorCanonicalTeam || "");
        if (el?.isConnected) el.classList.add("active-turn");
        if (pendingSkill) {
          for (const tar of pendingSkill.targets) {

      function renderLog() {

        const previousTop = box.scrollTop;

          mobileClose.textContent = "로그 닫기";
          mobileClose.setAttribute("aria-label", "전투 로그 닫기");

          box.prepend(mobileClose);
        }

          d.textContent = message;
          box.appendChild(d);
        }
        if (followLatest) box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
        else box.scrollTop = previousTop;
      }

      function paymentCostForAction(u, a) {
        let sk = card(u).skills.find((x) => x.id === a.skill);
        return effectiveSkillCost(game, game?.order?.[game.turn]?.[0] || "P", u, sk, a.costOverride ?? (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "hurricaneDive") ? "W*" : sk?.cost || ""));
      }
      function residualEnergyEntries(u, actor, plan) {
        let left = energyStore(u, actor).map((e, index) => ({ ...e, index }));
        for (const c of "RWB") {
          let need = plan?.[c] || 0;
          for (let i = left.length - 1; i >= 0 && need > 0.001; i--) {
            if (left[i].c !== c) continue;
            let take = Math.min(left[i].a, need);
            left[i].a -= take; need -= take;
          }
        }
        return left.filter((e) => e.a > 0.001);
      }
      function residualDualEnergyEntries(u, baseActor, plan) {
        let out = [];
        for (const who of ["beast", "rider"]) {
          let left = energyStore(u, who).map((e, index) => ({ ...e, index, actor: who }));
          if (who === baseActor) {
            for (const c of "RWB") {
              let need = plan?.[c] || 0;
              for (let i = left.length - 1; i >= 0 && need > 0.001; i--) {
                if (left[i].c !== c) continue;
                let take = Math.min(left[i].a, need);
                left[i].a -= take;
                need -= take;
              }
            }
          }
          out.push(...left.filter((e) => e.a > 0.001));
        }
        return out;
      }
      function openManualPayment(actions, done, title = "기술 코스트 선택") {
        if (!game || !Array.isArray(actions) || !actions.length) return;
        const [t, s] = game.order[game.turn], u = unitAt(game, t, s), a0 = actions[0], actor = a0.actor || null;
        if (!u) return;
        const store = energyStore(u, actor), picked = new Set();
        const requirements = actions.map(a => {
          const cost = paymentCostForAction(u, a);
          const transformed = transformedSkillReq(game, t, u, cost);
          const need = Object.values(transformed.req).reduce((n, x) => n + Number(x || 0), 0);
          return { action:a, cost, transformed, need };
        });
        const uniqueLabels = [...new Set(requirements.map(x => x.transformed.tokens.join(" ") || "없음"))];
        $("#modalContent").innerHTML = `<h2>${title}</h2><p>필요 코스트: <b>${uniqueLabels.join(" 또는 ")}</b>. 실제로 지불할 에너지를 선택하세요.</p><div class="energy manual-payment-energy"></div><div id="manualPaymentPreview" class="effect-box"></div><div class="choice-list"><button id="manualPaymentCancel">취소</button><button id="manualPaymentConfirm" class="primary" disabled>이 에너지로 지불</button></div>`;
        const box = $("#modalContent .manual-payment-energy"), preview = $("#manualPaymentPreview"), confirm = $("#manualPaymentConfirm");
        const counts = () => {
          const c = {R:0,W:0,B:0};
          for (const i of picked) {
            const e = store[i];
            if (e) c[e.c] += Number(e.a || 0);
          }
          return c;
        };
        const planMatches = (reqInfo, c) => {
          const total = c.R + c.W + c.B;
          if (Math.abs(total - reqInfo.need) > .001) return false;
          if (!reqAcceptsCounts(reqInfo.transformed.req, c)) return false;
          const p = reqInfo.action.paymentPlan;
          if (p && (Math.abs(Number(p.R||0)-c.R)>.001 || Math.abs(Number(p.W||0)-c.W)>.001 || Math.abs(Number(p.B||0)-c.B)>.001)) return false;
          return true;
        };
        const refresh = () => {
          const c = counts(), total = c.R + c.W + c.B;
          const matchingInfo = requirements.filter(x => planMatches(x,c));
          const matching = matchingInfo.map(x => x.action);
          const targetNeeds = [...new Set(requirements.map(x => energyAmountText(x.need)))].join(" 또는 ");
          const abyssInfo = actionSkillHasEffect(a0, "abyssCannon")
            ? ` · 예상 기본 피해 ${Math.max(0, 100 - 40 * (4 - (Number(a0.baseCostCount) || 0)))}D · 독 조건 ${c.R > 0 && c.W > 0 && c.B > 0 ? "충족" : "불충족"}`
            : "";
          preview.textContent = `선택 ${c.R ? `R${energyAmountText(c.R)} ` : ""}${c.W ? `W${energyAmountText(c.W)} ` : ""}${c.B ? `B${energyAmountText(c.B)}` : ""} · ${matching.length ? "지불 가능" : `총 ${energyAmountText(total)}/${targetNeeds}, 코스트 조건 불충족`}${abyssInfo}`;
          confirm.disabled = !matching.length;
          return {c, matching};
        };
        store.forEach((e,i) => {
          const b = document.createElement("button");
          b.className = `e ${e.c}`;
          b.textContent = `${e.c}${Number(e.a)===.5?"½":""}`;
          b.title = `${e.c} 에너지 ${energyAmountText(e.a)}`;
          b.onclick = () => {
            picked.has(i) ? picked.delete(i) : picked.add(i);
            b.classList.toggle("chosen", picked.has(i));
            refresh();
          };
          box.appendChild(b);
        });
        $("#manualPaymentCancel").onclick = () => $("#modal").classList.remove("open");
        confirm.onclick = () => {
          const {c, matching} = refresh();
          if (!matching.length) return;
          done(matching.map(a => ({...a, paymentPlan:{...c}})), {...c});
        };
        refresh();
        $("#modal").classList.add("open");
      }

      function openExtraAnyEnergy(actions, plan) {

        };

      }

      const LocalInputEligibility = (() => {
        let providers = Object.freeze({
          locks: () => [],
          clock: () => ({ locked: false }),
          online: () => ({}),
          bossControls: () => true,
        });
        const bindProviders = next => {
          providers = Object.freeze({ ...providers, ...(next || {}) });
          return true;
        };
        const evaluate = (g, context = null) => {

          const pair = context?.actorTeam != null
            ? [context.actorTeam, context.actorSlot ?? null]
            : (g?.order?.[g?.turn] || []);
          const currentActorCanonicalTeam = pair[0] ?? null;
          const currentActorSlot = pair[1] == null ? null : pair[1];
          const actor = context?.actor || (currentActorCanonicalTeam != null && currentActorSlot != null
            ? unitAt(g, currentActorCanonicalTeam, currentActorSlot)

          const displayLocalSide = canonicalLocalTeam ? displaySideFor(g, canonicalLocalTeam) : null;
          const standardOnline = context?.standardOnline ?? (g?.mode === "online" && !g?.bossRaid?.online);
          const online = providers.online?.(g) || {};

          const locks = [...(providers.locks?.() || [])];
          const clock = providers.clock?.() || {};
          let actions = [];
          try {
            if (Array.isArray(context?.actions)) actions = [...context.actions];
            else if (g && currentActorCanonicalTeam != null && currentActorSlot != null) actions = legalActions(g, currentActorCanonicalTeam, currentActorSlot) || [];
          }
          catch (error) { reasons.push("LEGAL_ACTION_EVALUATION_FAILED"); }
          const replacementRequired = (!actor || actor.dead) && actions.some(action => action.type === "deploy");
          const mandatoryAutomaticAction = actions.some(action => action.type === "scheduled" || action.type === "speedDelay");
          const manualActions = actions.filter(action => action.type !== "scheduled" && action.type !== "speedDelay");

          if (!g) reasons.push("NO_GAME");
          else {
            if (g.ended || g.winner) reasons.push("BATTLE_ENDED");
            if (!pair.length) reasons.push("CURRENT_ACTOR_MISSING");
            if ((!actor || actor.dead) && !replacementRequired) reasons.push("CURRENT_ACTOR_NOT_ALIVE");
            if (!context?.skipActiveIdentityChecks && g.activeTeamId && g.activeTeamId !== currentActorCanonicalTeam) reasons.push("ACTIVE_TEAM_MISMATCH");
            if (!context?.skipActiveIdentityChecks && g.activeEntityId && currentActorUid && g.activeEntityId !== currentActorUid) reasons.push("ACTIVE_ENTITY_MISMATCH");
            if (g.mode === "spectator") reasons.push("SPECTATOR_READ_ONLY");

            if (currentActorCanonicalTeam !== canonicalLocalTeam) reasons.push("REMOTE_ACTOR_TURN");
            if (standardOnline) {
              const expectedTeam = online.role === "host" ? "P" : online.role === "guest" ? "A" : null;
