              if (!online.battleStarted) reasons.push("BATTLE_START_NOT_COMMITTED");
              if (expectedTeam && canonicalLocalTeam !== expectedTeam) reasons.push("ROLE_CANONICAL_TEAM_MISMATCH");
              if (runtime.generation > 0 && !runtime.committed) reasons.push("SESSION_NOT_COMMITTED");
              if (runtime.generation > 0 && runtime.state !== "IN_BATTLE") reasons.push(`SESSION_${runtime.state || "UNKNOWN"}`);
              if (online.reconnecting) reasons.push("RECONNECTING");
            }
            if (g?.bossRaid?.online) {
              if (!online.connected) reasons.push("TRANSPORT_NOT_CONNECTED");
              if (!online.battleStarted) reasons.push("BATTLE_START_NOT_COMMITTED");
              if (runtime.generation > 0 && (!runtime.committed || runtime.state !== "IN_BATTLE")) reasons.push(`SESSION_${runtime.state || "NOT_COMMITTED"}`);
              if (online.reconnecting) reasons.push("RECONNECTING");
            }
            if (g?.bossRaid?.online && !providers.bossControls?.(g)) reasons.push("BOSS_SLOT_NOT_LOCAL");
            if (locks.length) reasons.push("ACTIVE_INPUT_LOCK");
            if (clock.locked) reasons.push("CLOCK_INPUT_LOCK");
            if (g.pendingTransaction || g.pendingAction || g.pendingPayment || context?.pendingSubmitted) reasons.push("PENDING_AUTHORITATIVE_ACTION");
            if (online.pendingActionId || context?.pendingActionId) reasons.push("PENDING_ONLINE_ACTION");
            if (online.busy) reasons.push("APPLICATION_BUSY");
            if (!actions.length) reasons.push("NO_LEGAL_ACTIONS");
            if (mandatoryAutomaticAction) reasons.push("MANDATORY_AUTOMATIC_ACTION");
          }
          const uniqueReasons = [...new Set(reasons)];
          const ownsCurrentActor = !!g && currentActorCanonicalTeam === canonicalLocalTeam && !["SPECTATOR_READ_ONLY", "AI_CONTROLLED_MODE"].some(reason => uniqueReasons.includes(reason));
          const canAct = ownsCurrentActor && uniqueReasons.length === 0 && manualActions.length > 0;
          return Object.freeze({
            canAct, reasons: uniqueReasons, ownsCurrentActor,
            canonicalLocalTeam, displayLocalSide,
            currentActorCanonicalTeam, currentActorSlot, currentActorUid,
            currentActorCharacterId: actor?.card || null,
            activeEntityId: g?.activeEntityId || null,
            actorAlive: !!actor && !actor.dead, replacementRequired,
            mandatoryAutomaticAction,
            legalActionCount: actions.length,
            manualLegalActionCount: manualActions.length,
            gatherLegal: actions.some(action => action.type === "gather"),
            actions,
            activeLocks: locks.map(lock => ({ ...lock })),
            pendingActionId: online.pendingActionId || g?.pendingAction?.actionId || null,
            authoritativeRevision: Number(online.authoritativeRevision || 0),
            localAppliedRevision: Number(g?.networkRevision || 0),
            sessionState: runtime.state || null,
            reconnectState: online.reconnecting ? "RECONNECTING" : "CONNECTED",
            surfaceKind,
          });
        };
        return Object.freeze({ evaluate, canAct: g => evaluate(g).canAct, bindProviders });
      })();
      function grandVisualOwner(g) {
        if (g?.fieldEffect?.type !== "grand") return null;
        return g.fieldEffect.team === localTeamFor(g) ? "P" : "A";
      }
      function renderCore() {
        if (typeof window.ActionFaultInjector === "object") {
          try { window.ActionFaultInjector.hit("render-before"); }
          catch (error) {
            window.PresentationFailureChannel?.emit?.({surface:"render",phase:"render-before",error:String(error),battleId:game?.battleId||game?.matchId||null,revision:Number(game?.networkRevision||0)});
            queueMicrotask(()=>window.LifecycleManager?.continuity?.authoritativeSettled?.({reason:"presentation-failure",surface:"render"}));
            throw error;
          }
        }
        if (!game) return;
        prepareCurrentTurn(game);
        const inputProjection = LocalInputEligibility.evaluate(game);
        let localSide = inputProjection.canonicalLocalTeam;
        document.querySelector(".player-side .side-head b").textContent =
          game.mode === "online" ? "내 진영" : "플레이어 진영";
        document.querySelector(".ai-side .side-head b").textContent =
          game.mode === "online" ? "상대 진영" : "AI 진영";
        for (const t of ["A", "P"]) {
          let tm = game.teams[t],
            isLocal = t === localSide,
            pre = displaySideFor(game, t),
            displayTeam = isLocal ? "P" : "A";
          $(`#${pre}Slots`).innerHTML = tm.field
            .map(
              (u, i) =>
                `<div class="slot" data-slotteam="${t}" data-slotindex="${i}">${tm.traps[i] ? '<span class="trap">⚡ 전기태클</span>' : ""}${unitHTML(u, t, i, false, displayTeam)}</div>`,
            )
            .join("");
          if (t !== localSide) {
            let known = tm.bench.filter(
                (u) => u && game.revealed[t].includes(u.uid),
              ),
              hidden = tm.bench.filter(
                (u) => u && !game.revealed[t].includes(u.uid),
              ).length,
              parts = known.map((u, i) => unitHTML(u, t, i, true, "A"));
            if (hidden)
              parts.push(
                `<div class="unknown-bench"><b>정체불명 후열</b><span>남은 카드 ${hidden}장</span></div>`,
              );
            $(`#${pre}Bench`).innerHTML =
              parts.join("") || '<span class="muted">후열 없음</span>';
          } else
            $(`#${pre}Bench`).innerHTML =
              tm.bench.map((u, i) => unitHTML(u, t, i, true, "P")).join("") ||
              '<span class="muted">후열 없음</span>';
          $(`#${pre}Alive`).textContent =
            `생존 ${aliveUnits(tm).length}/4 · 교체 ${tm.switchUses || 0}/3`;
        }
        let turns = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
          let oi = (game.turn + i) % 4,
            [t, s] = game.order[oi],
            u = unitAt(game, t, s);
          turns.push(
            `<span class="turntoken ${i ? "" : "now"}">${i ? "다음" : "현재"} ${t === localSide ? "내" : game.mode === "online" ? "상대" : "AI"}-${s + 1}: ${u && !u.dead ? u.name : "빈 슬롯"}</span>`,
          );
        }
        $("#turnStrip").innerHTML = turns.join("");
        $$(".card").forEach((x) =>
          x.classList.remove("active-turn", "targetable"),
        );
        let [ct, cs] = game.order[game.turn],
          cu = unitAt(game, ct, cs),
          activeEntityId = inputProjection.currentActorUid || cu?.entityId || cu?.uid || null,
          el = activeEntityId ? battlefieldCardForUid(game, activeEntityId) : null;
        document.querySelector(".arena")?.setAttribute("data-active-entity-id", activeEntityId || "");
        document.querySelector(".arena")?.setAttribute("data-active-canonical-team", inputProjection.currentActorCanonicalTeam || "");
        if (el?.isConnected) el.classList.add("active-turn");
        if (pendingSkill) {
          for (const tar of pendingSkill.targets) {
            let q = pendingSkill.slotTarget
              ? `.slot[data-slotteam="${tar.team}"][data-slotindex="${tar.slot}"]`
              : `.card[data-zone="field"][data-team="${tar.team}"][data-slot="${tar.slot}"]`;
            let e = $(q);
            if (e) {
              e.classList.add("targetable");
              e.onclick = () => playerChooseTarget(tar);
            }
          }
        }
        renderLog();
        renderActions();
        playVisuals();
        refreshCardTooltip();
        if (game.winner) showEnd();
      }

      function syncGrandFieldBackground(g) {

            bad = visualOwner === "P" ? ".ai-side" : ".player-side";

          document.querySelector(bad)?.classList.add("grand-penalty");
        }

      function renderLog() {
        let box = $("#log");
        const distanceFromBottom = box.scrollHeight - box.scrollTop - box.clientHeight;
        const followLatest = lastLog === 0 || distanceFromBottom <= 28;
        const previousTop = box.scrollTop;
        let mobileClose = box.querySelector(".mobile-log-close");
        if (!mobileClose) {
          mobileClose = document.createElement("button");
          mobileClose.type = "button";
          mobileClose.className = "mobile-log-close";
          mobileClose.textContent = "로그 닫기";
          mobileClose.setAttribute("aria-label", "전투 로그 닫기");
          mobileClose.onclick = (event) => {
            event.preventDefault();
            event.stopPropagation();
            setBattleLogOpen(false);
          };
          box.prepend(mobileClose);
        }
        for (; lastLog < game.logs.length; lastLog++) {
          const l = game.logs[lastLog] || {};
          const message = normalizeGameNumberText(l.msg);
          if (!message) continue;
          const d = document.createElement("div");
          d.className = l.cls || "";
          d.textContent = message;
          box.appendChild(d);
        }
        if (followLatest) box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
        else box.scrollTop = previousTop;
      }
      function actionButton(box, label, fn, cls = "") {

      function paymentCostForAction(u, a) {
        let sk = card(u).skills.find((x) => x.id === a.skill);
        return effectiveSkillCost(game, game?.order?.[game.turn]?.[0] || "P", u, sk, a.costOverride ?? (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "hurricaneDive") ? "W*" : sk?.cost || ""));
      }
      function residualEnergyEntries(u, actor, plan) {
        let left = energyStore(u, actor).map((e, index) => ({ ...e, index }));
        for (const c of "RWB") {
          let need = plan?.[c] || 0;
          for (let i = left.length - 1; i >= 0 && need > 0.001; i--) {
            if (left[i].c !== c) continue;
            let take = Math.min(left[i].a, need);
            left[i].a -= take; need -= take;
          }
        }
        return left.filter((e) => e.a > 0.001);
      }
      function residualDualEnergyEntries(u, baseActor, plan) {
        let out = [];
        for (const who of ["beast", "rider"]) {
          let left = energyStore(u, who).map((e, index) => ({ ...e, index, actor: who }));
          if (who === baseActor) {
            for (const c of "RWB") {
              let need = plan?.[c] || 0;
              for (let i = left.length - 1; i >= 0 && need > 0.001; i--) {
                if (left[i].c !== c) continue;
                let take = Math.min(left[i].a, need);
                left[i].a -= take;
                need -= take;
              }
            }
          }
          out.push(...left.filter((e) => e.a > 0.001));
        }
        return out;
      }
      function openManualPayment(actions, done, title = "기술 코스트 선택") {
        if (!game || !Array.isArray(actions) || !actions.length) return;
        const [t, s] = game.order[game.turn], u = unitAt(game, t, s), a0 = actions[0], actor = a0.actor || null;
        if (!u) return;
        const store = energyStore(u, actor), picked = new Set();
        const requirements = actions.map(a => {
          const cost = paymentCostForAction(u, a);
          const transformed = transformedSkillReq(game, t, u, cost);
          const need = Object.values(transformed.req).reduce((n, x) => n + Number(x || 0), 0);
          return { action:a, cost, transformed, need };
        });
        const uniqueLabels = [...new Set(requirements.map(x => x.transformed.tokens.join(" ") || "없음"))];
        $("#modalContent").innerHTML = `<h2>${title}</h2><p>필요 코스트: <b>${uniqueLabels.join(" 또는 ")}</b>. 실제로 지불할 에너지를 선택하세요.</p><div class="energy manual-payment-energy"></div><div id="manualPaymentPreview" class="effect-box"></div><div class="choice-list"><button id="manualPaymentCancel">취소</button><button id="manualPaymentConfirm" class="primary" disabled>이 에너지로 지불</button></div>`;
        const box = $("#modalContent .manual-payment-energy"), preview = $("#manualPaymentPreview"), confirm = $("#manualPaymentConfirm");
        const counts = () => {
          const c = {R:0,W:0,B:0};
          for (const i of picked) {
            const e = store[i];
            if (e) c[e.c] += Number(e.a || 0);
          }
          return c;
        };
        const planMatches = (reqInfo, c) => {
          const total = c.R + c.W + c.B;
          if (Math.abs(total - reqInfo.need) > .001) return false;
          if (!reqAcceptsCounts(reqInfo.transformed.req, c)) return false;
          const p = reqInfo.action.paymentPlan;
          if (p && (Math.abs(Number(p.R||0)-c.R)>.001 || Math.abs(Number(p.W||0)-c.W)>.001 || Math.abs(Number(p.B||0)-c.B)>.001)) return false;
          return true;
        };
        const refresh = () => {
          const c = counts(), total = c.R + c.W + c.B;
          const matchingInfo = requirements.filter(x => planMatches(x,c));
          const matching = matchingInfo.map(x => x.action);
          const targetNeeds = [...new Set(requirements.map(x => energyAmountText(x.need)))].join(" 또는 ");
          const abyssInfo = actionSkillHasEffect(a0, "abyssCannon")
            ? ` · 예상 기본 피해 ${Math.max(0, 100 - 40 * (4 - (Number(a0.baseCostCount) || 0)))}D · 독 조건 ${c.R > 0 && c.W > 0 && c.B > 0 ? "충족" : "불충족"}`
            : "";
          preview.textContent = `선택 ${c.R ? `R${energyAmountText(c.R)} ` : ""}${c.W ? `W${energyAmountText(c.W)} ` : ""}${c.B ? `B${energyAmountText(c.B)}` : ""} · ${matching.length ? "지불 가능" : `총 ${energyAmountText(total)}/${targetNeeds}, 코스트 조건 불충족`}${abyssInfo}`;
          confirm.disabled = !matching.length;
          return {c, matching};
        };
        store.forEach((e,i) => {
          const b = document.createElement("button");
          b.className = `e ${e.c}`;
          b.textContent = `${e.c}${Number(e.a)===.5?"½":""}`;
          b.title = `${e.c} 에너지 ${energyAmountText(e.a)}`;
          b.onclick = () => {
            picked.has(i) ? picked.delete(i) : picked.add(i);
            b.classList.toggle("chosen", picked.has(i));
            refresh();
          };
          box.appendChild(b);
        });
        $("#manualPaymentCancel").onclick = () => $("#modal").classList.remove("open");
        confirm.onclick = () => {
          const {c, matching} = refresh();
          if (!matching.length) return;
          done(matching.map(a => ({...a, paymentPlan:{...c}})), {...c});
        };
        refresh();
        $("#modal").classList.add("open");
      }

      function openExtraAnyEnergy(actions, plan) {
        let [t, s] = game.order[game.turn], u = unitAt(game, t, s), base = actions[0],

          b.onclick = () => { picked.has(i) ? picked.delete(i) : picked.add(i); b.classList.toggle("chosen", picked.has(i)); refresh(); };
          box.appendChild(b);
        });
        confirm.onclick = () => {
          const match = refresh(); if (!match) return;

          $("#modal").classList.remove("open");
          executeChosenPlayerAction({ ...match, paymentPlan:plan ? { ...plan } : null, extraEnergyPlan });
        };

      }

 = false;

            action?.target?.team === targetRef.team &&
            action?.target?.slot === targetRef.slot
          );
          if (!matches.length) return false;
          if (matches.some((action) => actionSkillHasTag(action, "copy"))) {
            const live = matches.filter((action) => {
              if (action?.target?.uid != null && targetRef.uid != null && action.target.uid !== targetRef.uid) return false;
              if (action.copyTargetType === "enemySlot") return true;
              const unit = unitAt(game, action.target.team, action.target.slot);
              return !!unit && !unit.dead && unit.uid === action.target.uid;
            });
            if (!live.length) {
              cancel("dragon-memory-target-stale", { dragonMemory: true, releaseBusy: true, closeModal: true });
              return false;
            }
            clearPending({ reason: "dragon-memory-target-selected", restoreActionPhase: false, releaseTargetLock: true });
            if (typeof dmPendingFlow !== "undefined" && dmPendingFlow) dmPendingFlow.stage = "payment";
            dispatchPlayerSkill(live);
            return true;
          }
          clearPending({ reason: "target-selected", restoreActionPhase: false, releaseTargetLock: true });
          dispatchPlayerSkill(matches);
          return true;
        };
        return Object.freeze({ begin, choose, cancel, clearPending, snapshot });
      })();

      function chooseOrExecute(candidates, slotTarget = false) {
        let [t, s] = game.order[game.turn];
        if (
          candidates.length === 1 ||
          !candidates.some((x) => x.target?.team !== t)
        ) {
          dispatchPlayerSkill(candidates);
        } else {
          let seenTargets = new Set(),
            targets = candidates
              .map((x) => x.target)
              .filter((x) => {
                if (!x) return false;
                let key = `${x.team}:${x.slot}`;
                if (seenTargets.has(key)) return false;
                seenTargets.add(key);
                return true;
              });
          TargetSelectionApplication.begin({
            actions: candidates,
            targets,
            slotTarget,
            reason: "skill-target-selection",
          });
        }
      }

      const ActionPanelViewModelOwner = Object.freeze({
        build(g = game, projection = null) {
          const surface = actionPanelSurfaceContext(g);
          const inputProjection = projection || LocalInputEligibility.evaluate(g, surface.eligibilityContext);
          const pair = surface.eligibilityContext?.actorTeam != null
            ? [surface.eligibilityContext.actorTeam, surface.eligibilityContext.actorSlot ?? null]

          const freezeAction = action => Object.freeze({
            ...(action || {}),

              ? Object.freeze(action.paymentPlans.map(plan=>Object.freeze({...plan})))
              : action?.paymentPlans,
            paymentRequirements:action?.paymentRequirements ? Object.freeze({...action.paymentRequirements}) : null,
          });
          const actions = Object.freeze([...(inputProjection.actions || [])].map(freezeAction));
          const actionKeys = Object.freeze(actions.map((action, index) => [
            index,
            action?.type || "",
            action?.skill || "",
            action?.actor || "",
            action?.target?.team || "",
            action?.target?.slot ?? "",
            action?.targetUid || action?.targetInstanceId || "",
            action?.label || "",
            action?.costOverride || action?.displayCost || action?.paymentRequirements?.cost || "",
            action?.actionId || "",
            action?.transactionId || "",
          ].join(":")));
          const pendingInteraction = !!(typeof pendingSkill !== "undefined" && pendingSkill) || !!g?.pendingTargetSelection || !!g?.pendingPayment || !!surface.manualSummon?.pendingSubmitted;
          const canonicalRevision = Number(g?.networkRevision ?? g?.revision ?? 0);
          const authoritativeDigest = String(g?.authoritativeDigest || g?.stateDigest || g?.digest || "");
          const surfaceSignature = surface.manualSummon ? {
            actionId:surface.manualSummon.actionId,
            transactionId:surface.manualSummon.transactionId,
            sourceInstanceId:surface.manualSummon.sourceInstanceId,
            candidateIdentity:surface.manualSummon.candidateIdentity,
            pendingSubmitted:surface.manualSummon.pendingSubmitted,
            submitState:surface.manualSummon.submitState,
          } : surface.boss ? {online:surface.boss.online,localControl:surface.boss.localControl} : null;
          const signature = JSON.stringify({
            battleId:g?.battleId || g?.matchId || null,
            canonicalRevision,
            authoritativeDigest,
            turnSerial:Number(g?.turnSerial || 0),
            surfaceKind:surface.kind,
            surfaceSignature,
            currentActorUid:inputProjection.currentActorUid || null,
            currentActorTeam:inputProjection.currentActorCanonicalTeam || null,
            localTeam:inputProjection.canonicalLocalTeam || null,
            canAct:inputProjection.canAct === true,
            reasons:[...(inputProjection.reasons || [])],
            busy:!!busy,
            pendingInteraction,
            pendingActionId:inputProjection.pendingActionId || surface.manualSummon?.actionId || null,
            terminal:!!(g?.winner || g?.ended),
            actionKeys,
          });
          return Object.freeze({
            game:g,
            projection:inputProjection,
            actions,
            actionKeys,
            signature,
            canonicalRevision,
            authoritativeDigest,
            turnSerial:Number(g?.turnSerial || 0),
            battleId:g?.battleId || g?.matchId || null,
            actor,
            actorTeam:pair[0] ?? null,
            actorSlot:pair[1] ?? null,
            surfaceKind:surface.kind,
            manualSummon:surface.manualSummon,
            boss:surface.boss,
            pendingInteraction,
            terminal:!!(g?.winner || g?.ended),
          });
        },
      });
      function renderBaseActions(viewModel = null) {
        const panelView = viewModel || ActionPanelViewModelOwner.build(game);
        let box = $("#actionButtons"),
          t = panelView.actorTeam,
          s = panelView.actorSlot,
          u = panelView.actor;
        const inputProjection = panelView.projection;
        box.innerHTML = "";
        let dual =

      const ActionRenderEngine = (() => {
        const routes = new Map(), decorators = new Map();
        let renderSerial = 0, lastRender = null;
        const ordered = map => [...map.values()].sort((a,b)=>b.priority-a.priority||a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
        const registerRoute = (id,priority,when,render) => {
          if(!id||typeof when!=="function"||typeof render!=="function")throw new TypeError("invalid action render route");
          routes.set(String(id),{id:String(id),priority:Number(priority)||0,when,render});
          return ()=>routes.delete(String(id));
        };
        const registerDecorator = (id,priority,decorate,when=()=>true) => {
          if(!id||typeof decorate!=="function"||typeof when!=="function")throw new TypeError("invalid action render decorator");
          decorators.set(String(id),{id:String(id),priority:Number(priority)||0,decorate,when});
          return ()=>decorators.delete(String(id));
        };
        const context = viewModel => {
          const pair=game?.order?.[game.turn]||[];
          return {game,team:pair[0],slot:pair[1],unit:pair.length?unitAt(game,pair[0],pair[1]):null,box:document.getElementById("actionButtons"),viewModel};
        };
        const render = (viewModel = null, requestReason = "unspecified") => {
          const panelView = viewModel || ActionPanelViewModelOwner.build(game);
          let ctx=context(panelView),result;
          const route=ordered(routes).find(entry=>entry.when(ctx));
          result=route?route.render(ctx):renderBaseActions(panelView);
          ctx=context(panelView);
          for(const entry of ordered(decorators))if(entry.when(ctx))entry.decorate(ctx,result);
          const projection=panelView.projection;
          const buttons=[...(ctx.box?.querySelectorAll("button")||[])];
          const actionButtons=buttons.filter(button=>!button.dataset.actionPanelMeta);
          const fingerprint=actionButtons.map(button=>`${button.disabled?"0":"1"}:${button.textContent?.replace(/\s+/g," ").trim()||""}`).join("|");
          lastRender=Object.freeze({
            renderSerial:++renderSerial,
            renderedAt:Date.now(),
            requestReason:String(requestReason||"unspecified"),
            routeId:route?.id||"base",
            battleId:panelView.battleId,
            sessionGeneration:Number(window.OnlineRuntime?.debug?.().generation||0),
            networkRevision:panelView.canonicalRevision,
            authoritativeRevision:Number(projection.authoritativeRevision||0),
            actionPanelViewModelRevision:panelView.canonicalRevision,
            renderedDomRevision:panelView.canonicalRevision,
            viewModelSignature:panelView.signature,
            turnSerial:panelView.turnSerial,
            actorUid:projection.currentActorUid||null,
            actorCanonicalTeam:projection.currentActorCanonicalTeam||null,
            localCanonicalTeam:projection.canonicalLocalTeam||null,
            canAct:projection.canAct,
            reasons:[...projection.reasons],
            legalActionCount:Number(projection.legalActionCount||0),
            manualLegalActionCount:Number(projection.manualLegalActionCount||0),
            buttonCount:buttons.length,
            actionButtonCount:actionButtons.length,
            enabledButtonCount:actionButtons.filter(button=>!button.disabled).length,
            buttonFingerprint:fingerprint,
            gatherRendered:actionButtons.some(button=>button.textContent?.includes("에너지 모으기")),
          });
          return result;
        };
        return Object.freeze({
          registerRoute,registerDecorator,render,base:()=>ActionPanelConvergenceOwner.request("action-render-base-api",{force:true}),
          reset:(reason="session-boundary")=>{
            renderSerial=0; lastRender=null;
            const box=document.getElementById("actionButtons"); if(box)box.innerHTML="";
            return Object.freeze({reason:String(reason),renderSerial,lastRender:null});
          },
          snapshot:()=>({
            routes:ordered(routes).map(x=>({id:x.id,priority:x.priority})),
            decorators:ordered(decorators).map(x=>({id:x.id,priority:x.priority})),
            renderSerial,
            lastRender:lastRender?{...lastRender,reasons:[...lastRender.reasons]}:null,
          }),
        });
      })();
      const ActionPanelConvergenceOwner = (() => {
        let requestSerial=0,commitSerial=0,skippedDuplicateCount=0,lastRequest=null;
        const domState = () => {
          const root=document.getElementById("actionButtons");
          const buttons=[...(root?.querySelectorAll("button")||[])];
          const actionButtons=buttons.filter(button=>!button.dataset.actionPanelMeta);
          return Object.freeze({
            rootConnected:!!root?.isConnected,
            buttonCount:buttons.length,
            actionButtonCount:actionButtons.length,
            enabledActionButtonCount:actionButtons.filter(button=>!button.disabled).length,
            fingerprint:actionButtons.map(button=>`${button.disabled?"0":"1"}:${button.textContent?.replace(/\s+/g," ").trim()||""}`).join("|"),
          });
        };
        const mustBeEmpty = view => {
          const reasons=view.projection.reasons||[];
          return view.terminal || reasons.some(reason=>[
            "REMOTE_ACTOR_TURN","SPECTATOR_READ_ONLY","AI_CONTROLLED_MODE","PENDING_AUTHORITATIVE_ACTION",
            "PENDING_ONLINE_ACTION","APPLICATION_BUSY","TRANSPORT_NOT_CONNECTED","BATTLE_START_NOT_COMMITTED",
            "RECONNECTING","CLOCK_INPUT_LOCK","ACTIVE_INPUT_LOCK"
          ].includes(reason));
        };
        const matches = (view,renderState=ActionRenderEngine.snapshot(),dom=domState()) => {
          const prior=renderState.lastRender;
          if(!prior||prior.viewModelSignature!==view.signature)return false;
          if(Number(prior.actionPanelViewModelRevision||0)!==Number(view.canonicalRevision||0))return false;
          if(Number(prior.renderedDomRevision||0)!==Number(view.canonicalRevision||0))return false;
          if(prior.actorUid!==(view.projection.currentActorUid||null))return false;
          if(prior.localCanonicalTeam!==(view.projection.canonicalLocalTeam||null))return false;
          if(prior.buttonFingerprint!==dom.fingerprint)return false;
          if(mustBeEmpty(view))return dom.actionButtonCount===0;
          if(view.projection.canAct&&view.projection.manualLegalActionCount>0)return dom.enabledActionButtonCount>0;
          return true;
        };
        const suspend = (reason="action-panel-suspend") => {
          const root=document.getElementById("actionButtons"),buttons=[...(root?.querySelectorAll("button")||[])];
          if(root)root.dataset.actionPanelSuspended=String(reason||"suspended");
          for(const button of buttons){if(button.dataset.actionPanelMeta==="terminal")continue;button.disabled=true;button.setAttribute("aria-disabled","true");}
          const state=domState();lastRequest=Object.freeze({requestId:++requestSerial,reason:String(reason),committed:true,coherent:true,suspended:true,signature:null,canonicalRevision:Number(game?.networkRevision||0),turnSerial:Number(game?.turnSerial||0),actorUid:null,dom:state
--- pendingActionId #6 offset=6086366 ---
closeModal === true) document.querySelector("#modal")?.classList.remove("open");
          if (options.project !== false && game) render();
          return result;
        };
        const choose = (targetRef) => {
          if (!pendingSkill?.actions?.length || !targetRef) return false;
          const matches = pendingSkill.actions.filter((action) =>
            action?.target?.team === targetRef.team &&
            action?.target?.slot === targetRef.slot
          );
          if (!matches.length) return false;
          if (matches.some((action) => actionSkillHasTag(action, "copy"))) {
            const live = matches.filter((action) => {
              if (action?.target?.uid != null && targetRef.uid != null && action.target.uid !== targetRef.uid) return false;
              if (action.copyTargetType === "enemySlot") return true;
              const unit = unitAt(game, action.target.team, action.target.slot);
              return !!unit && !unit.dead && unit.uid === action.target.uid;
            });
            if (!live.length) {
              cancel("dragon-memory-target-stale", { dragonMemory: true, releaseBusy: true, closeModal: true });
              return false;
            }
            clearPending({ reason: "dragon-memory-target-selected", restoreActionPhase: false, releaseTargetLock: true });
            if (typeof dmPendingFlow !== "undefined" && dmPendingFlow) dmPendingFlow.stage = "payment";
            dispatchPlayerSkill(live);
            return true;
          }
          clearPending({ reason: "target-selected", restoreActionPhase: false, releaseTargetLock: true });
          dispatchPlayerSkill(matches);
          return true;
        };
        return Object.freeze({ begin, choose, cancel, clearPending, snapshot });
      })();

      function chooseOrExecute(candidates, slotTarget = false) {
        let [t, s] = game.order[game.turn];
        if (
          candidates.length === 1 ||
          !candidates.some((x) => x.target?.team !== t)
        ) {
          dispatchPlayerSkill(candidates);
        } else {
          let seenTargets = new Set(),
            targets = candidates
              .map((x) => x.target)
              .filter((x) => {
                if (!x) return false;
                let key = `${x.team}:${x.slot}`;
                if (seenTargets.has(key)) return false;
                seenTargets.add(key);
                return true;
              });
          TargetSelectionApplication.begin({
            actions: candidates,
            targets,
            slotTarget,
            reason: "skill-target-selection",
          });
        }
      }
      function actionPanelSurfaceContext(g) {
        const manual = typeof manualSummonSurfaceContext === "function" ? manualSummonSurfaceContext(g) : null;
        if (manual) return manual;
        return Object.freeze({
          kind:g?.bossRaid ? "boss-raid" : "base",
          eligibilityContext:null,
          manualSummon:null,
          boss:g?.bossRaid ? Object.freeze({online:!!g.bossRaid.online,localControl:typeof bossLocalControlsCurrent!=="function"||!!window.__redTimeoraBossRaid?.localControlsCurrent?.(g)}) : null,
        });
      }
      const ActionPanelViewModelOwner = Object.freeze({
        build(g = game, projection = null) {
          const surface = actionPanelSurfaceContext(g);
          const inputProjection = projection || LocalInputEligibility.evaluate(g, surface.eligibilityContext);
          const pair = surface.eligibilityContext?.actorTeam != null
            ? [surface.eligibilityContext.actorTeam, surface.eligibilityContext.actorSlot ?? null]
            : (g?.order?.[g?.turn] || []);
          const actor = surface.eligibilityContext?.actor || (pair.length ? unitAt(g, pair[0], pair[1]) : null);
          const freezeAction = action => Object.freeze({
            ...(action || {}),
            paymentPlans:Array.isArray(action?.paymentPlans)
              ? Object.freeze(action.paymentPlans.map(plan=>Object.freeze({...plan})))
              : action?.paymentPlans,
            paymentRequirements:action?.paymentRequirements ? Object.freeze({...action.paymentRequirements}) : null,
          });
          const actions = Object.freeze([...(inputProjection.actions || [])].map(freezeAction));
          const actionKeys = Object.freeze(actions.map((action, index) => [
            index,
            action?.type || "",
            action?.skill || "",
            action?.actor || "",
            action?.target?.team || "",
            action?.target?.slot ?? "",
            action?.targetUid || action?.targetInstanceId || "",
            action?.label || "",
            action?.costOverride || action?.displayCost || action?.paymentRequirements?.cost || "",
            action?.actionId || "",
            action?.transactionId || "",
          ].join(":")));
          const pendingInteraction = !!(typeof pendingSkill !== "undefined" && pendingSkill) || !!g?.pendingTargetSelection || !!g?.pendingPayment || !!surface.manualSummon?.pendingSubmitted;
          const canonicalRevision = Number(g?.networkRevision ?? g?.revision ?? 0);
          const authoritativeDigest = String(g?.authoritativeDigest || g?.stateDigest || g?.digest || "");
          const surfaceSignature = surface.manualSummon ? {
            actionId:surface.manualSummon.actionId,
            transactionId:surface.manualSummon.transactionId,
            sourceInstanceId:surface.manualSummon.sourceInstanceId,
            candidateIdentity:surface.manualSummon.candidateIdentity,
            pendingSubmitted:surface.manualSummon.pendingSubmitted,
            submitState:surface.manualSummon.submitState,
          } : surface.boss ? {online:surface.boss.online,localControl:surface.boss.localControl} : null;
          const signature = JSON.stringify({
            battleId:g?.battleId || g?.matchId || null,
            canonicalRevision,
            authoritativeDigest,
            turnSerial:Number(g?.turnSerial || 0),
            surfaceKind:surface.kind,
            surfaceSignature,
            currentActorUid:inputProjection.currentActorUid || null,
            currentActorTeam:inputProjection.currentActorCanonicalTeam || null,
            localTeam:inputProjection.canonicalLocalTeam || null,
            canAct:inputProjection.canAct === true,
            reasons:[...(inputProjection.reasons || [])],
            busy:!!busy,
            pendingInteraction,
            pendingActionId:inputProjection.pendingActionId || surface.manualSummon?.actionId || null,
            terminal:!!(g?.winner || g?.ended),
            actionKeys,
          });
          return Object.freeze({
            game:g,
            projection:inputProjection,
            actions,
            actionKeys,
            signature,
            canonicalRevision,
            authoritativeDigest,
            turnSerial:Number(g?.turnSerial || 0),
            battleId:g?.battleId || g?.matchId || null,
            actor,
            actorTeam:pair[0] ?? null,
            actorSlot:pair[1] ?? null,
            surfaceKind:surface.kind,
            manualSummon:surface.manualSummon,
            boss:surface.boss,
            pendingInteraction,
            terminal:!!(g?.winner || g?.ended),
          });
        },
      });

      const ActionRenderEngine = (() => {
        const routes = new Map(), decorators = new Map();
        let renderSerial = 0, lastRender = null;
        const ordered = map => [...map.values()].sort((a,b)=>b.priority-a.priority||a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
        const registerRoute = (id,priority,when,render) => {
          if(!id||typeof when!=="function"||typeof render!=="function")throw new TypeError("invalid action render route");
          routes.set(String(id),{id:String(id),priority:Number(priority)||0,when,render});
          return ()=>routes.delete(String(id));
        };
        const registerDecorator = (id,priority,decorate,when=()=>true) => {
          if(!id||typeof decorate!=="function"||typeof when!=="function")throw new TypeError("invalid action render decorator");
          decorators.set(String(id),{id:String(id),priority:Number(priority)||0,decorate,when});
          return ()=>decorators.delete(String(id));
        };
        const context = viewModel => {
          const pair=game?.order?.[game.turn]||[];
          return {game,team:pair[0],slot:pair[1],unit:pair.length?unitAt(game,pair[0],pair[1]):null,box:document.getElementById("actionButtons"),viewModel};
        };
        const render = (viewModel = null, requestReason = "unspecified") => {
          const panelView = viewModel || ActionPanelViewModelOwner.build(game);
          let ctx=context(panelView),result;
          const route=ordered(routes).find(entry=>entry.when(ctx));
          result=route?route.render(ctx):renderBaseActions(panelView);
          ctx=context(panelView);
          for(const entry of ordered(decorators))if(entry.when(ctx))entry.decorate(ctx,result);
          const projection=panelView.projection;
          const buttons=[...(ctx.box?.querySelectorAll("button")||[])];
          const actionButtons=buttons.filter(button=>!button.dataset.actionPanelMeta);
          const fingerprint=actionButtons.map(button=>`${button.disabled?"0":"1"}:${button.textContent?.replace(/\s+/g," ").trim()||""}`).join("|");
          lastRender=Object.freeze({
            renderSerial:++renderSerial,
            renderedAt:Date.now(),
            requestReason:String(requestReason||"unspecified"),
            routeId:route?.id||"base",
            battleId:panelView.battleId,
            sessionGeneration:Number(window.OnlineRuntime?.debug?.().generation||0),
            networkRevision:panelView.canonicalRevision,
            authoritativeRevision:Number(projection.authoritativeRevision||0),
            actionPanelViewModelRevision:panelView.canonicalRevision,
            renderedDomRevision:panelView.canonicalRevision,
            viewModelSignature:panelView.signature,
            turnSerial:panelView.turnSerial,
            actorUid:projection.currentActorUid||null,
            actorCanonicalTeam:projection.currentActorCanonicalTeam||null,
            localCanonicalTeam:projection.canonicalLocalTeam||null,
            canAct:projection.canAct,
            reasons:[...projection.reasons],
            legalActionCount:Number(projection.legalActionCount||0),
            manualLegalActionCount:Number(projection.manualLegalActionCount||0),
            buttonCount:buttons.length,
            actionButtonCount:actionButtons.length,
            enabledButtonCount:actionButtons.filter(button=>!button.disabled).length,
            buttonFingerprint:fingerprint,
            gatherRendered:actionButtons.some(button=>button.textContent?.includes("에너지 모으기")),
          });
          return result;
        };
        return Object.freeze({
          registerRoute,registerDecorator,render,base:()=>ActionPanelConvergenceOwner.request("action-render-base-api",{force:true}),
          reset:(reason="session-boundary")=>{
            renderSerial=0; lastRender=null;

          },
          snapshot:()=>({

            decorators:ordered(decorators).map(x=>({id:x.id,priority:x.priority})),

      /* ================= 온라인 턴 권위 동기화·재전송 근본 보강 r7 ================= */
      const ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION = "mega-online-turn-sync-r7";
      let onlinePendingCommand = null;
      let onlineCommandRetryTimer = null;
      let onlineStateProbeTimer = null;
      let onlineAuthoritativeRevision = 0;
      const onlineHostCommandResults = new Map();
      const onlineCommandResolutionTrace = [];

      function onlineSafeSend(packet) {
        try {
          const conn = p2p?.conn;
          if (!conn?.open) return false;
          return conn.send(packet) !== false;
        } catch (error) {
          if (window.DEBUG_BATTLE_EVENTS) console.warn("[online] send failed", packet?.type, error);
          return false;
        }
      }
      function onlineCurrentMatchId() {
        return String(p2p?.matchId || game?.matchId || p2p?.roomCode || "online");
      }
      // 온라인도 로컬과 동일한 리그/등급 구성 owner를 사용한다.

      function stopOnlinePendingCommand({ unlock = true, resolution = "cleared" } = {}) {
        const pending = onlinePendingCommand;
        if (onlineCommandRetryTimer && !window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.cancel?.(onlineCommandRetryTimer)) window.TimerManager.clear(onlineCommandRetryTimer);
        onlineCommandRetryTimer = null;
        onlinePendingCommand = null;
        if (p2p) p2p.pendingActionId = null;
        if (pending) {
          onlineCommandResolutionTrace.push({
            at:Date.now(), actionId:pending.packet?.actionId || null,
            expectedTurnSerial:Number(pending.packet?.expectedTurnSerial ?? -1),
            resolution:String(resolution || "cleared"), attempts:Number(pending.attempts || 0),
          });
          if (onlineCommandResolutionTrace.length > 160) onlineCommandResolutionTrace.splice(0,onlineCommandResolutionTrace.length - 96);
        }
        if (unlock && (game?.mode === "online" || game?.bossRaid?.online) && !game?.paused && !game?.winner) {
          busy = false;
          render();
        }
      }
      function suspendOnlinePendingCommand(reason = "background") {
        if (onlineCommandRetryTimer && !window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.cancel?.(onlineCommandRetryTimer)) window.TimerManager.clear(onlineCommandRetryTimer);
        onlineCommandRetryTimer = null;
        if (onlineStateProbeTimer) window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.cancel?.(onlineStateProbeTimer);
        onlineStateProbeTimer = null;
        if (onlinePendingCommand) {
          onlinePendingCommand.suspendedAt = Date.now();
          onlinePendingCommand.suspendReason = String(reason);
        }
        return !!onlinePendingCommand;
      }
      function requestOnlinePendingResolution(reason = "authoritative-resume") {
        const pending = onlinePendingCommand;
        if (!pending || !p2p?.connected) return false;
        pending.reconciliationRequestedAt = Date.now();
        pending.reconciliationReason = String(reason);
        return !!window.OnlineRuntime?.sendControl?.({
          type:"stateRequest", protocol:ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION, mode:pending.packet.mode || "PVP",
          matchId:pending.packet.matchId || onlineCurrentMatchId(), actionId:pending.packet.actionId,
          knownTurnSerial:Number(game?.turnSerial ?? -1),
          knownRevision:Number(p2p.lastAuthoritativeRevision || game?.networkRevision || 0),
          resumeReason:String(reason),
        });
      }
      function reconcileOnlinePendingCommand(msg) {
        const pending = onlinePendingCommand;
        if (!pending) return Object.freeze({ settled:false, status:"none" });
        const actionId = String(pending.packet?.actionId || "");
        const resolution = msg?.actionResolution || null;
        let status = "pending";
        if (actionId && String(msg?.ackActionId || "") === actionId) status = "accepted";
        else if (actionId && String(resolution?.actionId || "") === actionId && ["accepted","rejected","unknown"].includes(String(resolution?.status || ""))) status = String(resolution.status);
        else if (Number(msg?.turnSerial) !== Number(pending.packet?.expectedTurnSerial)) status = "superseded";
        if (status === "pending") return Object.freeze({ settled:false, status, actionId });
        stopOnlinePendingCommand({ unlock:false, resolution:`authority:${status}` });
        busy = false;
        return Object.freeze({ settled:true, status, actionId });
      }
      function sendOnlinePendingCommand() {
        if (!onlinePendingCommand || !p2p?.connected) return;
        const pending = onlinePendingCommand;
        if (pending.suspendedAt) return;
        pending.attempts++;
        pending.lastSentAt = Date.now();
        if (pending.acked) {
          onlineSafeSend({
            type:"stateRequest", protocol:ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION, mode:pending.packet.mode||"PVP",
            matchId:pending.packet.matchId, actionId:pending.packet.actionId,
            knownTurnSerial:game?.turnSerial ?? -1,
            knownRevision:p2p.lastAuthoritativeRevision || 0
          });
        } else {
          onlineSafeSend(pending.packet);
        }
      }
      function beginOnlinePendingCommand(packet) {
        if (onlinePendingCommand) return false;
        onlinePendingCommand = { packet, attempts:0, acked:false, createdAt:Date.now(), lastSentAt:0, suspendedAt:0 };
        p2p.pendingActionId = packet.actionId;
        busy = true;
        renderActions();
        sendOnlinePendingCommand();
        if (onlineCommandRetryTimer && !window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.cancel?.(onlineCommandRetryTimer)) window.TimerManager.clear(onlineCommandRetryTimer);
        onlineCommandRetryTimer = window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.setInterval?.(sendOnlinePendingCommand,650,"online-command-retry",{phase:"SESSION"}) || window.TimerManager.setInterval(sendOnlinePendingCommand,650);
        return true;
      }

            } finally {

      function sendAuthoritativeOnlineState(visuals = [], ackActionId = null, force = false, actionResolution = null) {
        window.__megaSpectatorR18?.beforeAuthoritativeState?.(visuals);
        const bossOnline=!!game?.bossRaid?.online;
        if (!game || !(game.mode === "online" || bossOnline) || p2p?.role !== "host" || !p2p.connected) return false;
        const packet = cloneOnlineVisualEvents(visuals);
        const normalizedResolution = actionResolution?.actionId && ["accepted","rejected","unknown"].includes(String(actionResolution.status || ""))
          ? { actionId:String(actionResolution.actionId), status:String(actionResolution.status), reason:String(actionResolution.reason || "") }
          : null;
        onlineAuthoritativeRevision=Math.max(Number(onlineAuthoritativeRevision||0),Number(game.networkRevision||0))+1;
        game.networkRevision = onlineAuthoritativeRevision;
        const revision = onlineAuthoritativeRevision;
        const sent = onlineSafeSend({
          type:"authoritativeState", protocol:ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION, mode:bossOnline?"BOSS_COOP":"PVP",
          matchId:onlineCurrentMatchId(), revision,
          turnSerial:game.turnSerial, turn:game.turn,
          state:bossOnline?window.__redTimeoraBossRaid.serialize(game):serializeOnlineGame(), ackActionId:ackActionId || null,
          actionResolution:normalizedResolution,
          visualPacketId:packet.length ? `${game.turnSerial}:${revision}` : null,
          visuals:packet, forced:!!force
        });
        window.LifecycleManager?.continuity?.authoritativeSettled?.({
          source:"host-authoritative-publication", mode:bossOnline?"BOSS_COOP":"PVP", revision,
          turnSerial:Number(game.turnSerial || 0), sent:!!sent, forced:!!force,
        });
        if (p2p?.role === "host" && game?.mode === "online") {
          // Keep the newest canonical snapshot even when the first socket write fails.
          // The reliable retry loop can then publish this exact revision once transport recovers.
          p2p.__reliableStatePacket = {
            type:"authoritativeState", protocol:ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION, mode:"PVP",
            matchId:onlineCurrentMatchId(), revision, turnSerial:game.turnSerial, turn:game.turn,
            state:serializeOnlineGame(), ackActionId:ackActionId || null, actionResolution:normalizedResolution,
            visualPacketId:null, visuals:[], forced:!!force,
          };
          p2p.__reliableStateAck = Math.min(Number(p2p.__reliableStateAck || 0), revision - 1);
          p2p.__reliableStateSentAt = sent ? Date.now() : 0;
        }
        window.__megaSpectatorR18?.afterAuthoritativeState?.(sent);
        return sent;
      }

      function onlineSnapshotEquivalentForInput(currentRaw,incomingRaw){
        try{
          const normalize=raw=>{
            const value=typeof raw==="string"?JSON.parse(raw):JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(raw));
            if(value&&typeof value==="object"){
              delete value.networkRevision;
              delete value.networkSequence;
              delete value.lastAppliedNetworkSequence;
            }
            return JSON.stringify(value);
          };
          return normalize(currentRaw)===normalize(incomingRaw);
        }catch{return false;}
      }

      /* ================= 온라인 턴 권위 동기화·재전송 근본 보강 r7 ================= */
      const ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION = "mega-online-turn-sync-r7";
      let onlinePendingCommand = null;
      let onlineCommandRetryTimer = null;
      let onlineStateProbeTimer = null;
      let onlineAuthoritativeRevision = 0;
      const onlineHostCommandResults = new Map();
      const onlineCommandResolutionTrace = [];

      function onlineSafeSend(packet) {
        try {
          const conn = p2p?.conn;
          if (!conn?.open) return false;
          return conn.send(packet) !== false;
        } catch (error) {
          if (window.DEBUG_BATTLE_EVENTS) console.warn("[online] send failed", packet?.type, error);
          return false;
        }
      }
      function onlineCurrentMatchId() {
        return String(p2p?.matchId || game?.matchId || p2p?.roomCode || "online");
      }
      // 온라인도 로컬과 동일한 리그/등급 구성 owner를 사용한다.

      function stopOnlinePendingCommand({ unlock = true, resolution = "cleared" } = {}) {
        const pending = onlinePendingCommand;
        if (onlineCommandRetryTimer && !window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.cancel?.(onlineCommandRetryTimer)) window.TimerManager.clear(onlineCommandRetryTimer);
        onlineCommandRetryTimer = null;
        onlinePendingCommand = null;
        if (p2p) p2p.pendingActionId = null;
        if (pending) {
          onlineCommandResolutionTrace.push({
            at:Date.now(), actionId:pending.packet?.actionId || null,
            expectedTurnSerial:Number(pending.packet?.expectedTurnSerial ?? -1),
            resolution:String(resolution || "cleared"), attempts:Number(pending.attempts || 0),
          });
          if (onlineCommandResolutionTrace.length > 160) onlineCommandResolutionTrace.splice(0,onlineCommandResolutionTrace.length - 96);
        }
        if (unlock && (game?.mode === "online" || game?.bossRaid?.online) && !game?.paused && !game?.winner) {
          busy = false;
          render();
        }
      }
      function suspendOnlinePendingCommand(reason = "background") {
        if (onlineCommandRetryTimer && !window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.cancel?.(onlineCommandRetryTimer)) window.TimerManager.clear(onlineCommandRetryTimer);
        onlineCommandRetryTimer = null;
        if (onlineStateProbeTimer) window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.cancel?.(onlineStateProbeTimer);
        onlineStateProbeTimer = null;
        if (onlinePendingCommand) {
          onlinePendingCommand.suspendedAt = Date.now();
          onlinePendingCommand.suspendReason = String(reason);
        }
        return !!onlinePendingCommand;
      }
      function requestOnlinePendingResolution(reason = "authoritative-resume") {
        const pending = onlinePendingCommand;
        if (!pending || !p2p?.connected) return false;
        pending.reconciliationRequestedAt = Date.now();
        pending.reconciliationReason = String(reason);
        return !!window.OnlineRuntime?.sendControl?.({
          type:"stateRequest", protocol:ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION, mode:pending.packet.mode || "PVP",
          matchId:pending.packet.matchId || onlineCurrentMatchId(), actionId:pending.packet.actionId,
          knownTurnSerial:Number(game?.turnSerial ?? -1),
          knownRevision:Number(p2p.lastAuthoritativeRevision || game?.networkRevision || 0),
          resumeReason:String(reason),
        });
      }
      function reconcileOnlinePendingCommand(msg) {
        const pending = onlinePendingCommand;
        if (!pending) return Object.freeze({ settled:false, status:"none" });
        const actionId = String(pending.packet?.actionId || "");
        const resolution = msg?.actionResolution || null;
        let status = "pending";
        if (actionId && String(msg?.ackActionId || "") === actionId) status = "accepted";
        else if (actionId && String(resolution?.actionId || "") === actionId && ["accepted","rejected","unknown"].includes(String(resolution?.status || ""))) status = String(resolution.status);
        else if (Number(msg?.turnSerial) !== Number(pending.packet?.expectedTurnSerial)) status = "superseded";
        if (status === "pending") return Object.freeze({ settled:false, status, actionId });
        stopOnlinePendingCommand({ unlock:false, resolution:`authority:${status}` });
        busy = false;
        return Object.freeze({ settled:true, status, actionId });
      }
      function sendOnlinePendingCommand() {
        if (!onlinePendingCommand || !p2p?.connected) return;
        const pending = onlinePendingCommand;
        if (pending.suspendedAt) return;
        pending.attempts++;
        pending.lastSentAt = Date.now();
        if (pending.acked) {
          onlineSafeSend({
            type:"stateRequest", protocol:ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION, mode:pending.packet.mode||"PVP",
            matchId:pending.packet.matchId, actionId:pending.packet.actionId,
            knownTurnSerial:game?.turnSerial ?? -1,
            knownRevision:p2p.lastAuthoritativeRevision || 0
          });
        } else {
          onlineSafeSend(pending.packet);
        }
      }
      function beginOnlinePendingCommand(packet) {
        if (onlinePendingCommand) return false;
        onlinePendingCommand = { packet, attempts:0, acked:false, createdAt:Date.now(), lastSentAt:0, suspendedAt:0 };
        p2p.pendingActionId = packet.actionId;
        busy = true;
        renderActions();
        sendOnlinePendingCommand();
        if (onlineCommandRetryTimer && !window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.cancel?.(onlineCommandRetryTimer)) window.TimerManager.clear(onlineCommandRetryTimer);
        onlineCommandRetryTimer = window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.setInterval?.(sendOnlinePendingCommand,650,"online-command-retry",{phase:"SESSION"}) || window.TimerManager.setInterval(sendOnlinePendingCommand,650);
        return true;
      }

      function commitAuthoritativeStateProjection(detail={}) {

      function applyAuthoritativeOnlineState(msg) {
        if (p2p?.role === "host" || !msg?.state) return false;
        const incomingMatch = String(msg.matchId || "");
        const localMatch = String(p2p?.matchId || game?.matchId || "");
        if (incomingMatch && localMatch && incomingMatch !== localMatch) return false;
        const revision = Number(msg.revision || 0);
        const restorePending=!!window.OnlineRuntime?.restore?.pending?.();
        const appliedRevision=Number(p2p.lastAuthoritativeRevision||0);
        if (revision && (revision<appliedRevision || (revision===appliedRevision&&!restorePending))) return false;
        if (incomingMatch && !p2p.matchId) p2p.matchId = incomingMatch;
        const bossMode=p2p?.sessionMode==="bossRaid"||!!game?.bossRaid?.online,expectedMode=bossMode?"BOSS_COOP":"PVP";
        if(msg.mode!==expectedMode)return false;
        if(p2p.guestStartAwaitingHostCommit&&String(p2p.guestStartAwaitingHostCommit.matchId||"")===String(msg.matchId||p2p.matchId||"")){
          const pendingStart={...p2p.guestStartAwaitingHostCommit};traceOnlineStart("GUEST_START_COMMIT_CONFIRMED_BY_STATE",msg);p2p.guestStartAwaitingHostCommit=null;
          sendOnlineStartCommittedAck(pendingStart.identity,pendingStart.matchId,pendingStart.battleId,"authoritative-state-proves-commit");
        }
        if(bossMode){
          let incomingPreview;
          try{incomingPreview=JSON.parse(msg.state);}catch(error){window.__megaRecordRecoveredError?.(error,"authoritative-state.boss.parse");return false;}
          reconcileOnlinePendingCommand(msg);
          const current=game?.order?.[game?.turn]||[],currentUnit=game?unitAt(game,current[0],current[1]):null;
          const localOwnsTurn=!!game&&current[0]==="P"&&((currentUnit&&currentUnit.bossOwner===p2p.role)||(!currentUnit&&game?.bossRaid?.slotOwners?.[current[1]]===p2p.role));
          const modalDecision=document.getElementById("modal")?.classList.contains("open")&&!game?.winner;
          const localDecisionOpen=localOwnsTurn&&!onlinePendingCommand&&(!!pendingSkill||modalDecision);
          const sameTurnSnapshot=Number(msg.turnSerial)===Number(game?.turnSerial)&&Number(incomingPreview?.turn)===Number(game?.turn);
          const sameDecisionState=sameTurnSnapshot&&!!game&&onlineSnapshotEquivalentForInput(window.__redTimeoraBossRaid.serialize(game),msg.state);

      function handleOnlineActionRequest(msg) {
        const bossMode=!!game?.bossRaid?.online,expectedMode=bossMode?"BOSS_COOP":"PVP";
        if(msg?.mode!==expectedMode)return false;
        if (p2p?.role !== "host" || !game || !(game.mode === "online" || bossMode)) return false;
        const actionId = String(msg?.actionId || "");
        if (!actionId) return false;
        const incomingMatch = String(msg.matchId || ""), localMatch = onlineCurrentMatchId();
        if (incomingMatch && localMatch && incomingMatch !== localMatch) return false;
        const cached = onlineHostCommandResults.get(actionId);
        if (cached) {
          onlineSafeSend({type:"commandAck",protocol:ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION,mode:bossMode?"BOSS_COOP":"PVP",matchId:localMatch,actionId,accepted:cached.accepted,reason:cached.reason||"duplicate",turnSerial:game.turnSerial});
          sendAuthoritativeOnlineState([], cached.accepted ? actionId : null, true, {actionId,status:cached.accepted?"accepted":"rejected",reason:cached.reason||"duplicate"});
          return true;
        }
        const cur = game.order?.[game.turn] || [];
        let valid = Number(msg.expectedTurnSerial) === Number(game.turnSerial) && Number(msg.expectedTurn) === Number(game.turn);
        if(bossMode){
          const currentUnit=unitAt(game,cur[0],cur[1]);
          const guestOwnsSlot=currentUnit?.bossOwner==="guest"||(!currentUnit&&game.bossRaid?.slotOwners?.[cur[1]]==="guest");
          valid=valid&&cur[0]==="P"&&msg.team==="P"&&Number(cur[1])===Number(msg.slot)&&guestOwnsSlot;
        }else if (msg.commandKind === "summon") {
          const pending = normalizePendingSummonTurns(game);
          valid = valid && msg.team === "A" && !!pending && pending.q.team === "A" &&
            pending.u.uid === msg.summonUid && pending.owner.uid === msg.ownerUid &&
            msg.actionId === pending.q.actionId && msg.transactionId === pending.q.transactionId;
          if(valid)pending.q.submitState="COMMITTING";
        } else {
          valid = valid && msg.team === "A" && cur[0] === "A" && Number(cur[1]) === Number(msg.slot);
        }
        if (!valid) {
          onlineHostCommandResults.set(actionId, { accepted:false, reason:"stale-turn" });
          onlineSafeSend({type:"commandAck",protocol:ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION,mode:bossMode?"BOSS_COOP":"PVP",matchId:localMatch,actionId,accepted:false,reason:"stale-turn",turnSerial:game.turnSerial});
          sendAuthoritativeOnlineState([], null, true, {actionId,status:"rejected",reason:"stale-turn"});
          return false;
        }
        const previousApplyingRemoteAction = p2p.applyingRemoteAction;
        p2p.applyingRemoteAction = true;
        let accepted = false;
        try {
          accepted = !bossMode && msg.commandKind === "summon"
            ? !!applyManualSummonAction(game, msg.team, msg.summonUid, msg.action || {})
            : !!execute(game, msg.team, Number(msg.slot), msg.action || {}, { remoteAuthoritative:true });
        } catch (error) {
          accepted = false;
          if (window.DEBUG_BATTLE_EVENTS) console.error("[online] remote action error", error);
        } finally { p2p.applyingRemoteAction = previousApplyingRemoteAction; }
        onlineHostCommandResults.set(actionId, { accepted, reason:accepted ? "" : "invalid-action" });
        if (onlineHostCommandResults.size > 256) onlineHostCommandResults.delete(onlineHostCommandResults.keys().next().value);
        if (accepted) { p2p.__pendingAuthoritativeAck = actionId; afterAction(); }
        else sendAuthoritativeOnlineState([], null, true, {actionId,status:"rejected",reason:"invalid-action"});
        onlineSafeSend({type:"commandAck",protocol:ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION,mode:bossMode?"BOSS_COOP":"PVP",matchId:localMatch,actionId,accepted,reason:accepted?"":"invalid-action",turnSerial:game.turnSerial});
        return accepted;
      }

      function startOnlineStateProbeLoop() {
        if(onlineStateProbeTimer)window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.cancel?.(onlineStateProbeTimer);
        onlineStateProbeTimer=null;
        onlineStateProbeTimer = window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.setInterval?.(() => {
          const bossOnline=!!game?.bossRaid?.online;
          if (!p2p?.connected || !game || !(game.mode === "online" || bossOnline)) return;
          if (p2p.role === "guest") {
            onlineSafeSend({type:"syncProbe",protocol:ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION,mode:bossOnline?"BOSS_COOP":"PVP",matchId:onlineCurrentMatchId(),turnSerial:game.turnSerial,revision:p2p.lastAuthoritativeRevision||0,pendingActionId:onlinePendingCommand?.packet?.actionId||null,awaitingStartCommitIdentity:p2p?.guestStartAwaitingHostCommit?.identity||null});
          }
        }, 2500,"battle-authoritative-state-probe",{phase:"IN_BATTLE"}) || null;
        return !!onlineStateProbeTimer;
      }

      function handleOnlineTurnSyncPacket(msg){
        const expectedMode=(p2p?.sessionMode==="bossRaid"||!!game?.bossRaid?.online)?"BOSS_COOP":"PVP";
        const incomingMatch=String(msg?.matchId||""),localMatch=String(p2p?.matchId||game?.matchId||"");
        if(msg?.protocol!==ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION||msg?.mode!==expectedMode||!incomingMatch||!localMatch||incomingMatch!==localMatch)return false;
        if(msg.type==="actionRequest"){handleOnlineActionRequest(msg);return true;}
        if(msg.type==="commandAck"&&p2p?.role==="guest"){
          const pending=onlinePendingCommand;
          if(!pending||msg.actionId!==pending.packet.actionId)return;
          pending.acked=true;pending.ackAt=Date.now();pending.ackRejected=msg.accepted!==true;
          // Rejection may mean this client is stale. Keep input locked until the host state resolves it.
          sendOnlinePendingCommand();
          return;
        }
        if(msg.type==="authoritativeState")return applyAuthoritativeOnlineState(msg);
        if(msg.type==="stateRequest"&&p2p?.role==="host"){
          const requestedActionId=String(msg.actionId||"");
          const cached=requestedActionId?onlineHostCommandResults.get(requestedActionId):null;
          const resolution=requestedActionId?{actionId:requestedActionId,status:cached?(cached.accepted?"accepted":"rejected"):"unknown",reason:cached?(cached.reason||(cached.accepted?"accepted-by-authority":"rejected-by-authority")):"not-observed-by-authority"}:null;
          sendAuthoritativeOnlineState([],cached?.accepted?requestedActionId:null,true,resolution);return;
        }
        if(msg.type==="syncProbe"&&p2p?.role==="host"&&(game?.mode==="online"||game?.bossRaid?.online)){
          const awaitingIdentity=String(msg.awaitingStartCommitIdentity||"");
          if(awaitingIdentity&&p2p?.startCommitPacket&&onlineStartIdentity(p2p.startCommitPacket)===awaitingIdentity){sendOnlineStartCommitted(p2p.startCommitPacket,"guest-sync-probe-awaiting-commit");scheduleOnlineStartCommittedRetry(p2p.startCommitPacket,1);}
          const differs=Number(msg.turnSerial)!==Number(game.turnSerial)||Number(msg.revision||0)!==Number(onlineAuthoritativeRevision||0)||!!msg.pendingActionId||!!awaitingIdentity;
          if(differs){const requestedActionId=String(msg.pendingActionId||"");const cached=requestedActionId?onlineHostCommandResults.get(requestedActionId):null;const resolution=requestedActionId?{actionId:requestedActionId,status:cached?(cached.accepted?"accepted":"rejected"):"unknown",reason:cached?(cached.reason||(cached.accepted?"accepted-by-authority":"rejected-by-authority")):"not-observed-by-authority"}:null;sendAuthoritativeOnlineState([],cached?.accepted?requestedActionId:null,true,resolution);}
        }
      }
      for(const type of ["actionRequest","commandAck","authoritativeState","stateRequest","syncProbe"])
        registerOnlineConnectionRoute(type,"turn-sync",handleOnlineTurnSyncPacket,70);

      const OnlineTurnSyncEngine = (() => {
        const resumeSession = (reason = "foreground") => {
          const active=!!game&&(game.mode==="online"||!!game?.bossRaid?.online);
          if(!active)return false;
          if(onlinePendingCommand){
            if(onlineCommandRetryTimer&&!window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.cancel?.(onlineCommandRetryTimer))window.TimerManager.clear(onlineCommandRetryTimer);
            onlineCommandRetryTimer=null;

            // an authoritative resolution arrives; a single stateRequest can itself be lost.

            const retryResolution=()=>requestOnlinePendingResolution(`resume-retry:${reason}`);

          }

            onlineAutoCommandOwner=null;onlineAutoCommandActive=false;
            if(same){busy=false;renderActions();scheduleAI();}
          }
          return true;
        };
        const suspendSession = (reason = "background") => suspendOnlinePendingCommand(reason);
        const closeSession = () => {
          if(onlineStateProbeTimer)window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.cancel?.(onlineStateProbeTimer); onlineStateProbeTimer = null;
          stopOnlinePendingCommand({ unlock: false, resolution:"session-close" });

          authoritativeRevision: Number(onlineAuthoritativeRevision || 0),
          resolutionTrace:onlineCommandResolutionTrace.slice(-40),
        });
        return Object.freeze({ closeSession, suspendSession, resumeSession, debug });
      })();

      function timeMarkTimeline(g, type, payload = {}) {
        if (!g) return null;
        g.clockTimeline ||= [];
        const mark = {
          type,
          at: performance.now(),
          turnSerial: Number(g.turnSerial || 0),
          activeEntityId: g.activeEntityId || unitAt(g, ...(g.order?.[g.turn] || []))?.uid || null,
          ...payload,
        };
        g.clockTimeline.push(mark);
        if (g.clockTimeline.length > 160) g.clockTimeline.splice(0, g.clockTimeline.length - 160);
        return mark;
      }
      function timeCommitPendingTurnAdvance(g) {

        delete g._timeDeferTurnAdvance;

      function timeNormDeg(n) { n = Number(n) || 0; return ((n % 360) + 360) % 360; }
      function timeIsAligned(u) {
        if (!u || !cardHasCapability(u, "timeControl")) return false;
        const d = timeNormDeg((u.timeHourDeg || 0) - (u.timeMinuteDeg || 0));
        return Math.abs(d) < .001 || Math.abs(d - 180) < .001;
      }

      class TimeClockOwnershipDomain {
        constructor({ id, team, owners, suppressedUnits = [], shared = false, label = "운명의 시계" }) {
          this.id = String(id || "clock-domain");
          this.team = team || null;
          this.shared = !!shared;
          this.label = label;
          this.owners = (owners || []).filter(Boolean);
          this.ownerUids = new Set(this.owners.map(u => String(u.uid)));
          this.suppressedUnits = (suppressedUnits || []).filter(Boolean);
          this.suppressedUids = new Set(this.suppressedUnits.map(u => String(u.uid)));
        }
        owns(unitOrUid) {
          const uid = typeof unitOrUid === "object" ? unitOrUid?.uid : unitOrUid;
          return uid != null && this.ownerUids.has(String(uid));
        }
        suppresses(unitOrUid) {
          const uid = typeof unitOrUid === "object" ? unitOrUid?.uid : unitOrUid;
          return uid != null && this.suppressedUids.has(String(uid));
        }
        representative() { return this.owners[0] || null; }
      }

      class TimeClockTraitSuppressionPolicy {
        resolve(g) {
          if (!g || g.winner) return null;
          const bossResolver = window.__redTimeoraBossRaid?.resolveSuppression;
          if (g.bossRaid && typeof bossResolver === "function") return bossResolver(g);

          /* 보스 IIFE의 지역 함수에 기대지 않고 직렬화되는 공용 시계 상태로 직접 판정한다. */
          const bossClockState = g?.bossRaid?.sharedClock;
          const bossClockDelta = bossClockState
            ? timeNormDeg(Number(bossClockState.hourDeg || 0) - Number(bossClockState.minuteDeg || 0))
            : null;
          const bossClockIsAligned = bossClockDelta != null && (
            Math.abs(bossClockDelta) < .001 ||
            Math.abs(bossClockDelta - 180) < .001
          );
          if (g?.bossRaid && bossClockIsAligned) {
            const owners = [
              ...(g.teams?.A?.field || []),
              ...(g.teams?.A?.bench || [])
            ].filter(u => u && !u.dead && cardHasCapability(u, "timeControl") && !Number(u?.statuses?.traitSeal || 0));
            if (owners.length) return new TimeClockOwnershipDomain({
              id: `boss-shared-clock:${g.matchId || g.seed || "raid"}`,
              team: "A",
              owners,
              suppressedUnits: (g.teams?.P?.field || []).filter(u => u && !u.dead),
              shared: true,
              label: "붉은 공용 시계",
            });
          }

          const playerOwners = timeFieldTimeoras(g, "P").filter(u => !Number(u?.statuses?.traitSeal || 0) && timeIsAligned(u));
          const enemyOwners = timeFieldTimeoras(g, "A").filter(u => !Number(u?.statuses?.traitSeal || 0) && timeIsAligned(u));
          if (!playerOwners.length && !enemyOwners.length) return null;

          /* 양쪽이 동시에 정렬되면 두 정렬 효과가 서로 상쇄된다. 이때는 어느 쪽도
             상대를 정지시키지 않으며 두 시계 모두 계속 반응할 수 있다. */
          if (playerOwners.length && enemyOwners.length) return null;

          const owners = playerOwners.length ? playerOwners : enemyOwners;
          const ownerTeam = playerOwners.length ? "P" : "A";
          const ownerUids = new Set(owners.map(unit => String(unit.uid)));
          const suppressedUnits = [
            ...(g.teams?.P?.field || []),
            ...(g.teams?.A?.field || []),
          ].filter(unit => unit && !unit.dead && !ownerUids.has(String(unit.uid)) && !!String(card(unit)?.trait || "").trim());
          return new TimeClockOwnershipDomain({
            id: `unit-clock:${owners.map(u => u.uid).sort().join("+")}`,
            team: ownerTeam,
            owners,
            suppressedUnits,
            shared: false,
            label: `${owners[0].name}의 운명의 시계`,
          });
        }
        suppresses(g, unit) {
          const domain = this.resolve(g);
          return !!domain && domain.suppresses(unit);
        }
        traitsActive(g, unit) {
          return !!unit && !status(unit,"traitSeal") && !this.suppresses(g,unit);
        }
      }
