          sendAuthoritativeOnlineState([], cached.accepted ? actionId : null, true, {actionId,status:cached.accepted?"accepted":"rejected",reason:cached.reason||"duplicate"});
          return true;
        }
        const cur = game.order?.[game.turn] || [];
        let valid = Number(msg.expectedTurnSerial) === Number(game.turnSerial) && Number(msg.expectedTurn) === Number(game.turn);
        if(bossMode){
          const currentUnit=unitAt(game,cur[0],cur[1]);
          const guestOwnsSlot=currentUnit?.bossOwner==="guest"||(!currentUnit&&game.bossRaid?.slotOwners?.[cur[1]]==="guest");
          valid=valid&&cur[0]==="P"&&msg.team==="P"&&Number(cur[1])===Number(msg.slot)&&guestOwnsSlot;
        }else if (msg.commandKind === "summon") {
          const pending = normalizePendingSummonTurns(game);
          valid = valid && msg.team === "A" && !!pending && pending.q.team === "A" &&
            pending.u.uid === msg.summonUid && pending.owner.uid === msg.ownerUid &&
            msg.actionId === pending.q.actionId && msg.transactionId === pending.q.transactionId;
          if(valid)pending.q.submitState="COMMITTING";
        } else {
          valid = valid && msg.team === "A" && cur[0] === "A" && Number(cur[1]) === Number(msg.slot);
        }
        if (!valid) {
          onlineHostCommandResults.set(actionId, { accepted:false, reason:"stale-turn" });
          onlineSafeSend({type:"commandAck",protocol:ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION,mode:bossMode?"BOSS_COOP":"PVP",matchId:localMatch,actionId,accepted:false,reason:"stale-turn",turnSerial:game.turnSerial});
          sendAuthoritativeOnlineState([], null, true, {actionId,status:"rejected",reason:"stale-turn"});
          return false;
        }
        const previousApplyingRemoteAction = p2p.applyingRemoteAction;
        p2p.applyingRemoteAction = true;
        let accepted = false;
        try {
          accepted = !bossMode && msg.commandKind === "summon"
            ? !!applyManualSummonAction(game, msg.team, msg.summonUid, msg.action || {})
            : !!execute(game, msg.team, Number(msg.slot), msg.action || {}, { remoteAuthoritative:true });
        } catch (error) {
          accepted = false;
          if (window.DEBUG_BATTLE_EVENTS) console.error("[online] remote action error", error);
        } finally { p2p.applyingRemoteAction = previousApplyingRemoteAction; }
        onlineHostCommandResults.set(actionId, { accepted, reason:accepted ? "" : "invalid-action" });
        if (onlineHostCommandResults.size > 256) onlineHostCommandResults.delete(onlineHostCommandResults.keys().next().value);
        if (accepted) { p2p.__pendingAuthoritativeAck = actionId; afterAction(); }
        else sendAuthoritativeOnlineState([], null, true, {actionId,status:"rejected",reason:"invalid-action"});
        onlineSafeSend({type:"commandAck",protocol:ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION,mode:bossMode?"BOSS_COOP":"PVP",matchId:localMatch,actionId,accepted,reason:accepted?"":"invalid-action",turnSerial:game.turnSerial});
        return accepted;
      }

      function startOnlineStateProbeLoop() {
        if(onlineStateProbeTimer)window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.cancel?.(onlineStateProbeTimer);
        onlineStateProbeTimer=null;
        onlineStateProbeTimer = window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.setInterval?.(() => {
          const bossOnline=!!game?.bossRaid?.online;
          if (!p2p?.connected || !game || !(game.mode === "online" || bossOnline)) return;
          if (p2p.role === "guest") {
            onlineSafeSend({type:"syncProbe",protocol:ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION,mode:bossOnline?"BOSS_COOP":"PVP",matchId:onlineCurrentMatchId(),turnSerial:game.turnSerial,revision:p2p.lastAuthoritativeRevision||0,pendingActionId:onlinePendingCommand?.packet?.actionId||null,awaitingStartCommitIdentity:p2p?.guestStartAwaitingHostCommit?.identity||null});
          }
        }, 2500,"battle-authoritative-state-probe",{phase:"IN_BATTLE"}) || null;
        return !!onlineStateProbeTimer;
      }

      function handleOnlineTurnSyncPacket(msg){
        const expectedMode=(p2p?.sessionMode==="bossRaid"||!!game?.bossRaid?.online)?"BOSS_COOP":"PVP";
        const incomingMatch=String(msg?.matchId||""),localMatch=String(p2p?.matchId||game?.matchId||"");
        if(msg?.protocol!==ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION||msg?.mode!==expectedMode||!incomingMatch||!localMatch||incomingMatch!==localMatch)return false;
        if(msg.type==="actionRequest"){handleOnlineActionRequest(msg);return true;}
        if(msg.type==="commandAck"&&p2p?.role==="guest"){
          const pending=onlinePendingCommand;
          if(!pending||msg.actionId!==pending.packet.actionId)return;
          pending.acked=true;pending.ackAt=Date.now();pending.ackRejected=msg.accepted!==true;
          // Rejection may mean this client is stale. Keep input locked until the host state resolves it.
          sendOnlinePendingCommand();
          return;
        }
        if(msg.type==="authoritativeState")return applyAuthoritativeOnlineState(msg);
        if(msg.type==="stateRequest"&&p2p?.role==="host"){
          const requestedActionId=String(msg.actionId||"");
          const cached=requestedActionId?onlineHostCommandResults.get(requestedActionId):null;
          const resolution=requestedActionId?{actionId:requestedActionId,status:cached?(cached.accepted?"accepted":"rejected"):"unknown",reason:cached?(cached.reason||(cached.accepted?"accepted-by-authority":"rejected-by-authority")):"not-observed-by-authority"}:null;
          sendAuthoritativeOnlineState([],cached?.accepted?requestedActionId:null,true,resolution);return;
        }
        if(msg.type==="syncProbe"&&p2p?.role==="host"&&(game?.mode==="online"||game?.bossRaid?.online)){
          const awaitingIdentity=String(msg.awaitingStartCommitIdentity||"");
          if(awaitingIdentity&&p2p?.startCommitPacket&&onlineStartIdentity(p2p.startCommitPacket)===awaitingIdentity){sendOnlineStartCommitted(p2p.startCommitPacket,"guest-sync-probe-awaiting-commit");scheduleOnlineStartCommittedRetry(p2p.startCommitPacket,1);}
          const differs=Number(msg.turnSerial)!==Number(game.turnSerial)||Number(msg.revision||0)!==Number(onlineAuthoritativeRevision||0)||!!msg.pendingActionId||!!awaitingIdentity;
          if(differs){const requestedActionId=String(msg.pendingActionId||"");const cached=requestedActionId?onlineHostCommandResults.get(requestedActionId):null;const resolution=requestedActionId?{actionId:requestedActionId,status:cached?(cached.accepted?"accepted":"rejected"):"unknown",reason:cached?(cached.reason||(cached.accepted?"accepted-by-authority":"rejected-by-authority")):"not-observed-by-authority"}:null;sendAuthoritativeOnlineState([],cached?.accepted?requestedActionId:null,true,resolution);}
        }
      }
      for(const type of ["actionRequest","commandAck","authoritativeState","stateRequest","syncProbe"])
        registerOnlineConnectionRoute(type,"turn-sync",handleOnlineTurnSyncPacket,70);

      const OnlineTurnSyncEngine = (() => {
        const resumeSession = (reason = "foreground") => {
          const active=!!game&&(game.mode==="online"||!!game?.bossRaid?.online);
          if(!active)return false;
          if(onlinePendingCommand){
            if(onlineCommandRetryTimer&&!window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.cancel?.(onlineCommandRetryTimer))window.TimerManager.clear(onlineCommandRetryTimer);
            onlineCommandRetryTimer=null;
            // After background, never blindly replay the original action. Re-query the host until
            // an authoritative resolution arrives; a single stateRequest can itself be lost.
            onlinePendingCommand.suspendedAt ||= Date.now();
            requestOnlinePendingResolution(`resume:${reason}`);
            const retryResolution=()=>requestOnlinePendingResolution(`resume-retry:${reason}`);
            onlineCommandRetryTimer=window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.setInterval?.(retryResolution,650,"online-command-resolution-retry",{phase:"SESSION"}) || window.TimerManager.setInterval(retryResolution,650);
          }
          if(game.mode==="online"&&p2p?.role==="host"&&onlineAutoCommandOwner){
            const owner=onlineAutoCommandOwner,same=owner.game===game&&owner.token===game.token&&owner.serial===game.turnSerial;
            if(owner.timerId&&!window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.cancel?.(owner.timerId))window.TimerManager.clear(owner.timerId);
            onlineAutoCommandOwner=null;onlineAutoCommandActive=false;
            if(same){busy=false;renderActions();scheduleAI();}
          }
          return true;
        };
        const suspendSession = (reason = "background") => suspendOnlinePendingCommand(reason);
        const closeSession = () => {
          if(onlineStateProbeTimer)window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.cancel?.(onlineStateProbeTimer); onlineStateProbeTimer = null;
          stopOnlinePendingCommand({ unlock: false, resolution:"session-close" });
          onlineHostCommandResults.clear();
          onlineAuthoritativeRevision = 0;
          if (p2p) {
            p2p.lastAuthoritativeRevision = 0;
            p2p.lastVisualPacketId = null;
          }
          return debug();
        };
        const debug = () => ({
          stateProbeTimer: !!onlineStateProbeTimer,
          pendingCommand: !!onlinePendingCommand,
          pendingActionId:onlinePendingCommand?.packet?.actionId || null,
          pendingSuspended:!!onlinePendingCommand?.suspendedAt,
          hostCommandResults: onlineHostCommandResults.size,
          authoritativeRevision: Number(onlineAuthoritativeRevision || 0),
          resolutionTrace:onlineCommandResolutionTrace.slice(-40),
        });
        return Object.freeze({ closeSession, suspendSession, resumeSession, debug });
      })();

      function timeMarkTimeline(g, type, payload = {}) {
        if (!g) return null;
        g.clockTimeline ||= [];
        const mark = {
          type,
          at: performance.now(),
          turnSerial: Number(g.turnSerial || 0),
          activeEntityId: g.activeEntityId || unitAt(g, ...(g.order?.[g.turn] || []))?.uid || null,
          ...payload,
        };
        g.clockTimeline.push(mark);
        if (g.clockTimeline.length > 160) g.clockTimeline.splice(0, g.clockTimeline.length - 160);
        return mark;
      }
      function timeCommitPendingTurnAdvance(g) {
        const pending = g?.pendingClockTurnAdvance;
        if (!pending) return false;
        delete g.pendingClockTurnAdvance;
        delete g._timeDeferTurnAdvance;
        const actor = findUnit(g, pending.actorEntityId) || unitAt(g, pending.teamId, pending.slotIndex);
        g._timeCommittingTurnAdvance = true;

          if (actor) advanceDualOrEnd(g, pending.teamId, pending.slotIndex, actor, pending.usedSkill, pending.switched);
          else endTurn(g, pending.teamId, pending.slotIndex, pending.usedSkill);

        }

        timeMarkTimeline(g, "turnAdvanced", { fromTurnSerial:pending.turnSerial, toTurnSerial:Number(g.turnSerial || 0) });

        );

      function timeNormDeg(n) { n = Number(n) || 0; return ((n % 360) + 360) % 360; }
      function timeIsAligned(u) {
        if (!u || !cardHasCapability(u, "timeControl")) return false;
        const d = timeNormDeg((u.timeHourDeg || 0) - (u.timeMinuteDeg || 0));
        return Math.abs(d) < .001 || Math.abs(d - 180) < .001;
      }
      function timeTeamOf(g, unit) { return unit ? teamOf(g, unit) : null; }
      function timeFieldUnits(g) { return ["P","A"].flatMap(t => (g?.teams?.[t]?.field || []).filter(Boolean)); }
      function timeFieldTimeoras(g, t = null) {
        const teams = t ? [t] : ["P","A"];
        return teams.flatMap(team => (g?.teams?.[team]?.field || []).filter(u => u && !u.dead && cardHasCapability(u, "timeControl")));
      }

      class TimeClockOwnershipDomain {
        constructor({ id, team, owners, suppressedUnits = [], shared = false, label = "운명의 시계" }) {
          this.id = String(id || "clock-domain");
          this.team = team || null;
          this.shared = !!shared;
          this.label = label;
          this.owners = (owners || []).filter(Boolean);
          this.ownerUids = new Set(this.owners.map(u => String(u.uid)));
          this.suppressedUnits = (suppressedUnits || []).filter(Boolean);
          this.suppressedUids = new Set(this.suppressedUnits.map(u => String(u.uid)));
        }
        owns(unitOrUid) {
          const uid = typeof unitOrUid === "object" ? unitOrUid?.uid : unitOrUid;
          return uid != null && this.ownerUids.has(String(uid));
        }
        suppresses(unitOrUid) {
          const uid = typeof unitOrUid === "object" ? unitOrUid?.uid : unitOrUid;
          return uid != null && this.suppressedUids.has(String(uid));
        }
        representative() { return this.owners[0] || null; }
      }

      class TimeClockTraitSuppressionPolicy {
        resolve(g) {
          if (!g || g.winner) return null;
          const bossResolver = window.__redTimeoraBossRaid?.resolveSuppression;
          if (g.bossRaid && typeof bossResolver === "function") return bossResolver(g);

          /* 보스 IIFE의 지역 함수에 기대지 않고 직렬화되는 공용 시계 상태로 직접 판정한다. */
          const bossClockState = g?.bossRaid?.sharedClock;
          const bossClockDelta = bossClockState
            ? timeNormDeg(Number(bossClockState.hourDeg || 0) - Number(bossClockState.minuteDeg || 0))
            : null;
          const bossClockIsAligned = bossClockDelta != null && (
            Math.abs(bossClockDelta) < .001 ||
            Math.abs(bossClockDelta - 180) < .001
          );
          if (g?.bossRaid && bossClockIsAligned) {
            const owners = [
              ...(g.teams?.A?.field || []),
              ...(g.teams?.A?.bench || [])
            ].filter(u => u && !u.dead && cardHasCapability(u, "timeControl") && !Number(u?.statuses?.traitSeal || 0));
            if (owners.length) return new TimeClockOwnershipDomain({
              id: `boss-shared-clock:${g.matchId || g.seed || "raid"}`,
              team: "A",
              owners,
              suppressedUnits: (g.teams?.P?.field || []).filter(u => u && !u.dead),
              shared: true,
              label: "붉은 공용 시계",
            });
          }

          const playerOwners = timeFieldTimeoras(g, "P").filter(u => !Number(u?.statuses?.traitSeal || 0) && timeIsAligned(u));
          const enemyOwners = timeFieldTimeoras(g, "A").filter(u => !Number(u?.statuses?.traitSeal || 0) && timeIsAligned(u));
          if (!playerOwners.length && !enemyOwners.length) return null;

          /* 양쪽이 동시에 정렬되면 두 정렬 효과가 서로 상쇄된다. 이때는 어느 쪽도
             상대를 정지시키지 않으며 두 시계 모두 계속 반응할 수 있다. */
          if (playerOwners.length && enemyOwners.length) return null;

          const owners = playerOwners.length ? playerOwners : enemyOwners;
          const ownerTeam = playerOwners.length ? "P" : "A";
          const ownerUids = new Set(owners.map(unit => String(unit.uid)));
          const suppressedUnits = [
            ...(g.teams?.P?.field || []),
            ...(g.teams?.A?.field || []),
          ].filter(unit => unit && !unit.dead && !ownerUids.has(String(unit.uid)) && !!String(card(unit)?.trait || "").trim());
          return new TimeClockOwnershipDomain({
            id: `unit-clock:${owners.map(u => u.uid).sort().join("+")}`,
            team: ownerTeam,
            owners,
            suppressedUnits,
            shared: false,
            label: `${owners[0].name}의 운명의 시계`,
          });
        }
        suppresses(g, unit) {
          const domain = this.resolve(g);
          return !!domain && domain.suppresses(unit);
        }
        traitsActive(g, unit) {
          return !!unit && !status(unit,"traitSeal") && !this.suppresses(g,unit);
        }
      }

      const timeClockTraitSuppression = new TimeClockTraitSuppressionPolicy();

      function timeStatusClone(obj) {
        const out = {};
        for (const [k,v] of Object.entries(obj || {})) if (Number(v) > 0) out[k] = Number(v);
        return out;

      function timeRecordTurnStart(g, u) {
        if (!u || u.dead) return;
        u.timeRecallHistory ||= [];
        const stamp = `${g.matchId || g.seed}:${g.turnSerial}:${u.uid}`;

        if (u.timeRecallHistory.length > 16) u.timeRecallHistory.splice(0, u.timeRecallHistory.length - 16);
      }
      function timeRecallSnapshot(u) {
        const h = u?.timeRecallHistory || [];

        const index = Math.max(0, h.length - 3);
        return { snapshot:h[index], turnsAgo:Math.min(2, h.length - 1) };
      }

          valid = valid && msg.team === "A" && cur[0] === "A" && Number(cur[1]) === Number(msg.slot);
        }
        if (!valid) {
          onlineHostCommandResults.set(actionId, { accepted:false, reason:"stale-turn" });
          onlineSafeSend({type:"commandAck",protocol:ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION,mode:bossMode?"BOSS_COOP":"PVP",matchId:localMatch,actionId,accepted:false,reason:"stale-turn",turnSerial:game.turnSerial});
          sendAuthoritativeOnlineState([], null, true, {actionId,status:"rejected",reason:"stale-turn"});
          return false;
        }
        const previousApplyingRemoteAction = p2p.applyingRemoteAction;
        p2p.applyingRemoteAction = true;
        let accepted = false;
        try {
          accepted = !bossMode && msg.commandKind === "summon"
            ? !!applyManualSummonAction(game, msg.team, msg.summonUid, msg.action || {})
            : !!execute(game, msg.team, Number(msg.slot), msg.action || {}, { remoteAuthoritative:true });
        } catch (error) {
          accepted = false;
          if (window.DEBUG_BATTLE_EVENTS) console.error("[online] remote action error", error);
        } finally { p2p.applyingRemoteAction = previousApplyingRemoteAction; }
        onlineHostCommandResults.set(actionId, { accepted, reason:accepted ? "" : "invalid-action" });
        if (onlineHostCommandResults.size > 256) onlineHostCommandResults.delete(onlineHostCommandResults.keys().next().value);
        if (accepted) { p2p.__pendingAuthoritativeAck = actionId; afterAction(); }
        else sendAuthoritativeOnlineState([], null, true, {actionId,status:"rejected",reason:"invalid-action"});
        onlineSafeSend({type:"commandAck",protocol:ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION,mode:bossMode?"BOSS_COOP":"PVP",matchId:localMatch,actionId,accepted,reason:accepted?"":"invalid-action",turnSerial:game.turnSerial});
        return accepted;
      }

      function startOnlineStateProbeLoop() {
        if(onlineStateProbeTimer)window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.cancel?.(onlineStateProbeTimer);
        onlineStateProbeTimer=null;
        onlineStateProbeTimer = window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.setInterval?.(() => {
          const bossOnline=!!game?.bossRaid?.online;
          if (!p2p?.connected || !game || !(game.mode === "online" || bossOnline)) return;
          if (p2p.role === "guest") {
            onlineSafeSend({type:"syncProbe",protocol:ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION,mode:bossOnline?"BOSS_COOP":"PVP",matchId:onlineCurrentMatchId(),turnSerial:game.turnSerial,revision:p2p.lastAuthoritativeRevision||0,pendingActionId:onlinePendingCommand?.packet?.actionId||null,awaitingStartCommitIdentity:p2p?.guestStartAwaitingHostCommit?.identity||null});
          }
        }, 2500,"battle-authoritative-state-probe",{phase:"IN_BATTLE"}) || null;
        return !!onlineStateProbeTimer;
      }

      function handleOnlineTurnSyncPacket(msg){
        const expectedMode=(p2p?.sessionMode==="bossRaid"||!!game?.bossRaid?.online)?"BOSS_COOP":"PVP";
        const incomingMatch=String(msg?.matchId||""),localMatch=String(p2p?.matchId||game?.matchId||"");
        if(msg?.protocol!==ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION||msg?.mode!==expectedMode||!incomingMatch||!localMatch||incomingMatch!==localMatch)return false;
        if(msg.type==="actionRequest"){handleOnlineActionRequest(msg);return true;}
        if(msg.type==="commandAck"&&p2p?.role==="guest"){
          const pending=onlinePendingCommand;
          if(!pending||msg.actionId!==pending.packet.actionId)return;
          pending.acked=true;pending.ackAt=Date.now();pending.ackRejected=msg.accepted!==true;
          // Rejection may mean this client is stale. Keep input locked until the host state resolves it.
          sendOnlinePendingCommand();
          return;
        }
        if(msg.type==="authoritativeState")return applyAuthoritativeOnlineState(msg);
        if(msg.type==="stateRequest"&&p2p?.role==="host"){
          const requestedActionId=String(msg.actionId||"");
          const cached=requestedActionId?onlineHostCommandResults.get(requestedActionId):null;
          const resolution=requestedActionId?{actionId:requestedActionId,status:cached?(cached.accepted?"accepted":"rejected"):"unknown",reason:cached?(cached.reason||(cached.accepted?"accepted-by-authority":"rejected-by-authority")):"not-observed-by-authority"}:null;
          sendAuthoritativeOnlineState([],cached?.accepted?requestedActionId:null,true,resolution);return;
        }
        if(msg.type==="syncProbe"&&p2p?.role==="host"&&(game?.mode==="online"||game?.bossRaid?.online)){
          const awaitingIdentity=String(msg.awaitingStartCommitIdentity||"");
          if(awaitingIdentity&&p2p?.startCommitPacket&&onlineStartIdentity(p2p.startCommitPacket)===awaitingIdentity){sendOnlineStartCommitted(p2p.startCommitPacket,"guest-sync-probe-awaiting-commit");scheduleOnlineStartCommittedRetry(p2p.startCommitPacket,1);}
          const differs=Number(msg.turnSerial)!==Number(game.turnSerial)||Number(msg.revision||0)!==Number(onlineAuthoritativeRevision||0)||!!msg.pendingActionId||!!awaitingIdentity;
          if(differs){const requestedActionId=String(msg.pendingActionId||"");const cached=requestedActionId?onlineHostCommandResults.get(requestedActionId):null;const resolution=requestedActionId?{actionId:requestedActionId,status:cached?(cached.accepted?"accepted":"rejected"):"unknown",reason:cached?(cached.reason||(cached.accepted?"accepted-by-authority":"rejected-by-authority")):"not-observed-by-authority"}:null;sendAuthoritativeOnlineState([],cached?.accepted?requestedActionId:null,true,resolution);}
        }
      }
      for(const type of ["actionRequest","commandAck","authoritativeState","stateRequest","syncProbe"])
        registerOnlineConnectionRoute(type,"turn-sync",handleOnlineTurnSyncPacket,70);

      const OnlineTurnSyncEngine = (() => {
        const resumeSession = (reason = "foreground") => {
          const active=!!game&&(game.mode==="online"||!!game?.bossRaid?.online);
          if(!active)return false;
          if(onlinePendingCommand){
            if(onlineCommandRetryTimer&&!window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.cancel?.(onlineCommandRetryTimer))window.TimerManager.clear(onlineCommandRetryTimer);
            onlineCommandRetryTimer=null;
            // After background, never blindly replay the original action. Re-query the host until
            // an authoritative resolution arrives; a single stateRequest can itself be lost.
            onlinePendingCommand.suspendedAt ||= Date.now();
            requestOnlinePendingResolution(`resume:${reason}`);
            const retryResolution=()=>requestOnlinePendingResolution(`resume-retry:${reason}`);
            onlineCommandRetryTimer=window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.setInterval?.(retryResolution,650,"online-command-resolution-retry",{phase:"SESSION"}) || window.TimerManager.setInterval(retryResolution,650);
          }
          if(game.mode==="online"&&p2p?.role==="host"&&onlineAutoCommandOwner){
            const owner=onlineAutoCommandOwner,same=owner.game===game&&owner.token===game.token&&owner.serial===game.turnSerial;
            if(owner.timerId&&!window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.cancel?.(owner.timerId))window.TimerManager.clear(owner.timerId);
            onlineAutoCommandOwner=null;onlineAutoCommandActive=false;
            if(same){busy=false;renderActions();scheduleAI();}
          }
          return true;
        };
        const suspendSession = (reason = "background") => suspendOnlinePendingCommand(reason);
        const closeSession = () => {
          if(onlineStateProbeTimer)window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.cancel?.(onlineStateProbeTimer); onlineStateProbeTimer = null;
          stopOnlinePendingCommand({ unlock: false, resolution:"session-close" });
          onlineHostCommandResults.clear();
          onlineAuthoritativeRevision = 0;
          if (p2p) {
            p2p.lastAuthoritativeRevision = 0;
            p2p.lastVisualPacketId = null;
          }
          return debug();
        };
        const debug = () => ({
          stateProbeTimer: !!onlineStateProbeTimer,
          pendingCommand: !!onlinePendingCommand,
          pendingActionId:onlinePendingCommand?.packet?.actionId || null,
          pendingSuspended:!!onlinePendingCommand?.suspendedAt,
          hostCommandResults: onlineHostCommandResults.size,
          authoritativeRevision: Number(onlineAuthoritativeRevision || 0),
          resolutionTrace:onlineCommandResolutionTrace.slice(-40),
        });
        return Object.freeze({ closeSession, suspendSession, resumeSession, debug });
      })();

      function timeMarkTimeline(g, type, payload = {}) {
        if (!g) return null;
        g.clockTimeline ||= [];
        const mark = {
          type,
          at: performance.now(),
          turnSerial: Number(g.turnSerial || 0),
          activeEntityId: g.activeEntityId || unitAt(g, ...(g.order?.[g.turn] || []))?.uid || null,
          ...payload,
        };
        g.clockTimeline.push(mark);
        if (g.clockTimeline.length > 160) g.clockTimeline.splice(0, g.clockTimeline.length - 160);
        return mark;
      }
      function timeCommitPendingTurnAdvance(g) {
        const pending = g?.pendingClockTurnAdvance;
        if (!pending) return false;
        delete g.pendingClockTurnAdvance;
        delete g._timeDeferTurnAdvance;
        const actor = findUnit(g, pending.actorEntityId) || unitAt(g, pending.teamId, pending.slotIndex);
        g._timeCommittingTurnAdvance = true;
        try {
          if (actor) advanceDualOrEnd(g, pending.teamId, pending.slotIndex, actor, pending.usedSkill, pending.switched);
          else endTurn(g, pending.teamId, pending.slotIndex, pending.usedSkill);
        } finally {
          delete g._timeCommittingTurnAdvance;
        }
        if (window.StateFactory?.updateActive) window.StateFactory.updateActive(g);
        timeMarkTimeline(g, "turnAdvanced", { fromTurnSerial:pending.turnSerial, toTurnSerial:Number(g.turnSerial || 0) });
        timeMarkTimeline(g, "nextActorAssigned", { nextActorEntityId:g.activeEntityId || null, nextTeamId:g.activeTeamId || null });
        g._timeAwaitingNextAiAction = !!(
          !g.winner &&
          ((g.mode === "human" && g.activeTeamId === "A") || g.mode === "aivai" || (g.bossRaid && g.activeTeamId === "A"))
        );
        return true;

      function timeNormDeg(n) { n = Number(n) || 0; return ((n % 360) + 360) % 360; }
      function timeIsAligned(u) {
        if (!u || !cardHasCapability(u, "timeControl")) return false;
        const d = timeNormDeg((u.timeHourDeg || 0) - (u.timeMinuteDeg || 0));
        return Math.abs(d) < .001 || Math.abs(d - 180) < .001;
      }
      function timeTeamOf(g, unit) { return unit ? teamOf(g, unit) : null; }
      function timeFieldUnits(g) { return ["P","A"].flatMap(t => (g?.teams?.[t]?.field || []).filter(Boolean)); }
      function timeFieldTimeoras(g, t = null) {
        const teams = t ? [t] : ["P","A"];
        return teams.flatMap(team => (g?.teams?.[team]?.field || []).filter(u => u && !u.dead && cardHasCapability(u, "timeControl")));
      }

      class TimeClockOwnershipDomain {
        constructor({ id, team, owners, suppressedUnits = [], shared = false, label = "운명의 시계" }) {
          this.id = String(id || "clock-domain");
          this.team = team || null;
          this.shared = !!shared;
          this.label = label;
          this.owners = (owners || []).filter(Boolean);
          this.ownerUids = new Set(this.owners.map(u => String(u.uid)));
          this.suppressedUnits = (suppressedUnits || []).filter(Boolean);
          this.suppressedUids = new Set(this.suppressedUnits.map(u => String(u.uid)));
        }
        owns(unitOrUid) {
          const uid = typeof unitOrUid === "object" ? unitOrUid?.uid : unitOrUid;
          return uid != null && this.ownerUids.has(String(uid));
        }
        suppresses(unitOrUid) {
          const uid = typeof unitOrUid === "object" ? unitOrUid?.uid : unitOrUid;
          return uid != null && this.suppressedUids.has(String(uid));
        }
        representative() { return this.owners[0] || null; }
      }

      class TimeClockTraitSuppressionPolicy {
        resolve(g) {
          if (!g || g.winner) return null;
          const bossResolver = window.__redTimeoraBossRaid?.resolveSuppression;
          if (g.bossRaid && typeof bossResolver === "function") return bossResolver(g);

          /* 보스 IIFE의 지역 함수에 기대지 않고 직렬화되는 공용 시계 상태로 직접 판정한다. */
          const bossClockState = g?.bossRaid?.sharedClock;
          const bossClockDelta = bossClockState
            ? timeNormDeg(Number(bossClockState.hourDeg || 0) - Number(bossClockState.minuteDeg || 0))
            : null;
          const bossClockIsAligned = bossClockDelta != null && (
            Math.abs(bossClockDelta) < .001 ||
            Math.abs(bossClockDelta - 180) < .001
          );
          if (g?.bossRaid && bossClockIsAligned) {
            const owners = [
              ...(g.teams?.A?.field || []),
              ...(g.teams?.A?.bench || [])
            ].filter(u => u && !u.dead && cardHasCapability(u, "timeControl") && !Number(u?.statuses?.traitSeal || 0));
            if (owners.length) return new TimeClockOwnershipDomain({
              id: `boss-shared-clock:${g.matchId || g.seed || "raid"}`,
              team: "A",
              owners,
              suppressedUnits: (g.teams?.P?.field || []).filter(u => u && !u.dead),
              shared: true,
              label: "붉은 공용 시계",
            });
          }

          const playerOwners = timeFieldTimeoras(g, "P").filter(u => !Number(u?.statuses?.traitSeal || 0) && timeIsAligned(u));
          const enemyOwners = timeFieldTimeoras(g, "A").filter(u => !Number(u?.statuses?.traitSeal || 0) && timeIsAligned(u));
          if (!playerOwners.length && !enemyOwners.length) return null;

          /* 양쪽이 동시에 정렬되면 두 정렬 효과가 서로 상쇄된다. 이때는 어느 쪽도
             상대를 정지시키지 않으며 두 시계 모두 계속 반응할 수 있다. */
          if (playerOwners.length && enemyOwners.length) return null;

          const owners = playerOwners.length ? playerOwners : enemyOwners;
          const ownerTeam = playerOwners.length ? "P" : "A";
          const ownerUids = new Set(owners.map(unit => String(unit.uid)));
          const suppressedUnits = [
            ...(g.teams?.P?.field || []),
            ...(g.teams?.A?.field || []),
          ].filter(unit => unit && !unit.dead && !ownerUids.has(String(unit.uid)) && !!String(card(unit)?.trait || "").trim());
          return new TimeClockOwnershipDomain({
            id: `unit-clock:${owners.map(u => u.uid).sort().join("+")}`,
            team: ownerTeam,
            owners,
            suppressedUnits,
            shared: false,
            label: `${owners[0].name}의 운명의 시계`,
          });
        }
        suppresses(g, unit) {
          const domain = this.resolve(g);
          return !!domain && domain.suppresses(unit);
        }
        traitsActive(g, unit) {
          return !!unit && !status(unit,"traitSeal") && !this.suppresses(g,unit);
        }
      }

      const timeClockTraitSuppression = new TimeClockTraitSuppressionPolicy();

      function timeStatusClone(obj) {
        const out = {};
        for (const [k,v] of Object.entries(obj || {})) if (Number(v) > 0) out[k] = Number(v);
        return out;
      }
      function timeRecordTurnStart(g, u) {
        if (!u || u.dead) return;
        u.timeRecallHistory ||= [];
        const stamp = `${g.matchId || g.seed}:${g.turnSerial}:${u.uid}`;
        if (u.timeRecallHistory.at(-1)?.stamp === stamp) return;
        u.timeRecallHistory.push({ stamp, turnSerial:g.turnSerial, ownTurn:u.turns || 0, hp:combatInt(u.hp), statuses:timeStatusClone(u.statuses), skillSeals:timeStatusClone(u.skillSeals) });
        if (u.timeRecallHistory.length > 16) u.timeRecallHistory.splice(0, u.timeRecallHistory.length - 16);
      }
      function timeRecallSnapshot(u) {
        const h = u?.timeRecallHistory || [];
        if (!h.length) return { snapshot:{hp:combatInt(u?.hp||0),statuses:timeStatusClone(u?.statuses),skillSeals:timeStatusClone(u?.skillSeals)}, turnsAgo:0 };
        const index = Math.max(0, h.length - 3);
        return { snapshot:h[index], turnsAgo:Math.min(2, h.length - 1) };
      }

      function timeOrderIndexForSlot(g,t,s) {

      function timeOrderSlotForUnit(g,u) {

      function timeClockReactionDecision(g,timeora,actor,actorTeam=null,actorSlot=null) {
        if(!timeora||timeora.dead||!cardHasCapability(timeora,"timeControl"))return {ok:false,reason:"inactive"};

      function timeMarkTimeline(g, type, payload = {}) {
        if (!g) return null;
        g.clockTimeline ||= [];
        const mark = {
          type,
          at: performance.now(),
          turnSerial: Number(g.turnSerial || 0),
          activeEntityId: g.activeEntityId || unitAt(g, ...(g.order?.[g.turn] || []))?.uid || null,
          ...payload,
        };
        g.clockTimeline.push(mark);
        if (g.clockTimeline.length > 160) g.clockTimeline.splice(0, g.clockTimeline.length - 160);
        return mark;
      }
      function timeCommitPendingTurnAdvance(g) {
        const pending = g?.pendingClockTurnAdvance;
        if (!pending) return false;
        delete g.pendingClockTurnAdvance;
        delete g._timeDeferTurnAdvance;
        const actor = findUnit(g, pending.actorEntityId) || unitAt(g, pending.teamId, pending.slotIndex);
        g._timeCommittingTurnAdvance = true;
        try {
          if (actor) advanceDualOrEnd(g, pending.teamId, pending.slotIndex, actor, pending.usedSkill, pending.switched);
          else endTurn(g, pending.teamId, pending.slotIndex, pending.usedSkill);
        } finally {
          delete g._timeCommittingTurnAdvance;
        }
        if (window.StateFactory?.updateActive) window.StateFactory.updateActive(g);
        timeMarkTimeline(g, "turnAdvanced", { fromTurnSerial:pending.turnSerial, toTurnSerial:Number(g.turnSerial || 0) });
        timeMarkTimeline(g, "nextActorAssigned", { nextActorEntityId:g.activeEntityId || null, nextTeamId:g.activeTeamId || null });
        g._timeAwaitingNextAiAction = !!(
          !g.winner &&
          ((g.mode === "human" && g.activeTeamId === "A") || g.mode === "aivai" || (g.bossRaid && g.activeTeamId === "A"))
        );
        return true;
      }

      function timeNormDeg(n) { n = Number(n) || 0; return ((n % 360) + 360) % 360; }
      function timeIsAligned(u) {
        if (!u || !cardHasCapability(u, "timeControl")) return false;
        const d = timeNormDeg((u.timeHourDeg || 0) - (u.timeMinuteDeg || 0));
        return Math.abs(d) < .001 || Math.abs(d - 180) < .001;
      }
      function timeTeamOf(g, unit) { return unit ? teamOf(g, unit) : null; }
      function timeFieldUnits(g) { return ["P","A"].flatMap(t => (g?.teams?.[t]?.field || []).filter(Boolean)); }
      function timeFieldTimeoras(g, t = null) {
        const teams = t ? [t] : ["P","A"];
        return teams.flatMap(team => (g?.teams?.[team]?.field || []).filter(u => u && !u.dead && cardHasCapability(u, "timeControl")));
      }

      class TimeClockOwnershipDomain {
        constructor({ id, team, owners, suppressedUnits = [], shared = false, label = "운명의 시계" }) {
          this.id = String(id || "clock-domain");
          this.team = team || null;
          this.shared = !!shared;
          this.label = label;
          this.owners = (owners || []).filter(Boolean);
          this.ownerUids = new Set(this.owners.map(u => String(u.uid)));
          this.suppressedUnits = (suppressedUnits || []).filter(Boolean);
          this.suppressedUids = new Set(this.suppressedUnits.map(u => String(u.uid)));
        }
        owns(unitOrUid) {
          const uid = typeof unitOrUid === "object" ? unitOrUid?.uid : unitOrUid;
          return uid != null && this.ownerUids.has(String(uid));
        }
        suppresses(unitOrUid) {
          const uid = typeof unitOrUid === "object" ? unitOrUid?.uid : unitOrUid;
          return uid != null && this.suppressedUids.has(String(uid));
        }
        representative() { return this.owners[0] || null; }
      }

      class TimeClockTraitSuppressionPolicy {
        resolve(g) {
          if (!g || g.winner) return null;
          const bossResolver = window.__redTimeoraBossRaid?.resolveSuppression;
          if (g.bossRaid && typeof bossResolver === "function") return bossResolver(g);

          /* 보스 IIFE의 지역 함수에 기대지 않고 직렬화되는 공용 시계 상태로 직접 판정한다. */
          const bossClockState = g?.bossRaid?.sharedClock;
          const bossClockDelta = bossClockState
            ? timeNormDeg(Number(bossClockState.hourDeg || 0) - Number(bossClockState.minuteDeg || 0))
            : null;
          const bossClockIsAligned = bossClockDelta != null && (
            Math.abs(bossClockDelta) < .001 ||
            Math.abs(bossClockDelta - 180) < .001
          );
          if (g?.bossRaid && bossClockIsAligned) {
            const owners = [
              ...(g.teams?.A?.field || []),
              ...(g.teams?.A?.bench || [])
            ].filter(u => u && !u.dead && cardHasCapability(u, "timeControl") && !Number(u?.statuses?.traitSeal || 0));
            if (owners.length) return new TimeClockOwnershipDomain({
              id: `boss-shared-clock:${g.matchId || g.seed || "raid"}`,
              team: "A",
              owners,
              suppressedUnits: (g.teams?.P?.field || []).filter(u => u && !u.dead),
              shared: true,
              label: "붉은 공용 시계",
            });
          }

          const playerOwners = timeFieldTimeoras(g, "P").filter(u => !Number(u?.statuses?.traitSeal || 0) && timeIsAligned(u));
          const enemyOwners = timeFieldTimeoras(g, "A").filter(u => !Number(u?.statuses?.traitSeal || 0) && timeIsAligned(u));
          if (!playerOwners.length && !enemyOwners.length) return null;

          /* 양쪽이 동시에 정렬되면 두 정렬 효과가 서로 상쇄된다. 이때는 어느 쪽도
             상대를 정지시키지 않으며 두 시계 모두 계속 반응할 수 있다. */
          if (playerOwners.length && enemyOwners.length) return null;

          const owners = playerOwners.length ? playerOwners : enemyOwners;
          const ownerTeam = playerOwners.length ? "P" : "A";
          const ownerUids = new Set(owners.map(unit => String(unit.uid)));
          const suppressedUnits = [
            ...(g.teams?.P?.field || []),
            ...(g.teams?.A?.field || []),
          ].filter(unit => unit && !unit.dead && !ownerUids.has(String(unit.uid)) && !!String(card(unit)?.trait || "").trim());
          return new TimeClockOwnershipDomain({
            id: `unit-clock:${owners.map(u => u.uid).sort().join("+")}`,
            team: ownerTeam,
            owners,
            suppressedUnits,
            shared: false,
            label: `${owners[0].name}의 운명의 시계`,
          });
        }
        suppresses(g, unit) {
          const domain = this.resolve(g);
          return !!domain && domain.suppresses(unit);
        }
        traitsActive(g, unit) {
          return !!unit && !status(unit,"traitSeal") && !this.suppresses(g,unit);
        }
      }

      const timeClockTraitSuppression = new TimeClockTraitSuppressionPolicy();
      function timeCurrentSuppressionDomain(g) { return timeClockTraitSuppression.resolve(g); }
      function timeAlignedOwnersForTeam(g,t) {

      function timeClockAlignmentsCancel(g) {

      function timeClockStopReason(g,u) {
        if (!u || u.dead || !cardHasCapability(u, "timeControl")) return null;
        if (status(u,"traitSeal")) return "traitSeal";
        const domain = timeCurrentSuppressionDomain(g);
        return domain?.suppresses(u) ? "opponentAlignment" : null;
      }

      function timeStatusClone(obj) {
        const out = {};
        for (const [k,v] of Object.entries(obj || {})) if (Number(v) > 0) out[k] = Number(v);
        return out;
      }
      function timeRecordTurnStart(g, u) {
        if (!u || u.dead) return;
        u.timeRecallHistory ||= [];
        const stamp = `${g.matchId || g.seed}:${g.turnSerial}:${u.uid}`;
        if (u.timeRecallHistory.at(-1)?.stamp === stamp) return;
        u.timeRecallHistory.push({ stamp, turnSerial:g.turnSerial, ownTurn:u.turns || 0, hp:combatInt(u.hp), statuses:timeStatusClone(u.statuses), skillSeals:timeStatusClone(u.skillSeals) });
        if (u.timeRecallHistory.length > 16) u.timeRecallHistory.splice(0, u.timeRecallHistory.length - 16);
      }
      function timeRecallSnapshot(u) {
        const h = u?.timeRecallHistory || [];
        if (!h.length) return { snapshot:{hp:combatInt(u?.hp||0),statuses:timeStatusClone(u?.statuses),skillSeals:timeStatusClone(u?.skillSeals)}, turnsAgo:0 };
        const index = Math.max(0, h.length - 3);
        return { snapshot:h[index], turnsAgo:Math.min(2, h.length - 1) };
      }

      function timeOrderIndexForSlot(g,t,s) {
        return g?.order?.findIndex(x => x?.[0] === t && Number(x?.[1]) === Number(s)) ?? -1;
      }
      function timeOrderSlotForUnit(g,u) {
        const t=timeTeamOf(g,u),s=g?.teams?.[t]?.field?.findIndex(x=>x?.uid===u?.uid)??-1;
        return {t,s,index:timeOrderIndexForSlot(g,t,s)};
      }
      function timeClockReactionDecision(g,timeora,actor,actorTeam=null,actorSlot=null) {
        if(!timeora||timeora.dead||!cardHasCapability(timeora,"timeControl"))return {ok:false,reason:"inactive"};
        if(status(timeora,"traitSeal"))return {ok:false,reason:"traitSeal"};
        const suppressionDomain=timeCurrentSuppressionDomain(g);
        if(suppressionDomain&&suppressionDomain.suppresses(timeora))return {ok:false,reason:"clockSuppressed"};
        const timePos=timeOrderSlotForUnit(g,timeora);
        let at=actorTeam,as=actorSlot;
        if(at==null||as==null){const p=timeOrderSlotForUnit(g,actor);at=p.t;as=p.s;}
        const actorIndex=timeOrderIndexForSlot(g,at,as),tt=timeTeamOf(g,timeora);
        if(timePos.index>=0&&actorIndex>=0&&at&&tt&&at!==tt){
          const previousIndex=(timePos.index-1+g.order.length)%g.order.length;
          if(actorIndex===previousIndex && !(g?.bossRaid && timeora?.bossRed))return {ok:false,reason:"previousEnemyTurn"};
        }
        return {ok:true,reason:"react"};
      }
      function timeShouldClockReact(g,timeora,actor,actorTeam=null,actorSlot=null) {
        return timeClockReactionDecision(g,timeora,actor,actorTeam,actorSlot).ok;
      }
      function timePaidAmountFromAction(g,t,u,a) {
        const recorded = u?._timePaidRecord;
        if (recorded && Number(recorded.amount) > 0) return recorded.amount;
        if (a?.paymentPlan) return Number(a.paymentPlan.R||0)+Number(a.paymentPlan.W||0)+Number(a.paymentPlan.B||0)+Number(a.extraW||0);
        const base = card(u)?.skills?.find(x => x.id === a?.skill);
        const cost = a?.costOverride ?? base?.cost ?? "";
        return transformedSkillReq(g,t,u,cost).tokens.length + Number(a?.extraW||0);
      }

      function timeRotateClocks(g,t,s,actor,paidAmount,sim=false) {
        const boss=window.__redTimeoraBossRaid;
        if(g?.bossRaid&&typeof boss?.rotateClocksR36==="function")return boss.rotateClocksR36(g,t,s,actor,paidAmount,sim);
        const n = Math.ceil(Math.max(0,Number(paidAmount)||0) - 1e-9);
        if (!n) return [];
        const clocks = [];

        /* 모든 시계의 반응 가능 여부를 회전 시작 시점에 한 번만 스냅샷한다.
           앞에서 처리된 시계가 정렬을 풀거나 새로 맞추더라도, 같은 행동에 반응 중인
           뒤쪽 시계의 정지 여부가 중간에 바뀌어서는 안 된다. */
        const reactionSnapshot = timeFieldTimeoras(g).map(owner => ({
          owner,
          decision: timeClockReactionDecision(g,owner,actor,t,s),
          beforeHour: timeNormDeg(owner.timeHourDeg),
          beforeMinute: timeNormDeg(owner.timeMinuteDeg),
        }));

        for (const snap of reactionSnapshot) {
          const {owner,decision,beforeHour,beforeMinute}=snap;
          if (!decision.ok) {
            if(decision.reason==="previousEnemyTurn"){
              if(!sim&&typeof game!=="undefined"&&g===game)
                log(g,`${owner.name}의 시계: 행동순서상 바로 이전 적 차례의 기술에는 반응하지 않음.`,"turn");
              clocks.push({ uid:owner.uid, team:timeTeamOf(g,owner), beforeHour, beforeMinute, afterHour:beforeHour, afterMinute:beforeMinute, hourDelta:0, minuteDelta:0, aligned:timeIsAligned(owner), blocked:true, blockReason:decision.reason });
            }
            /* 특성봉인·상대 정렬로 완전히 정지한 시계는 회전 연출 목록에도 넣지 않는다. */
            continue;
          }
          const hourDelta = 30*n, minuteDelta = owner.uid === actor.uid ? 90*n : 0;
          owner.timeHourDeg = timeNormDeg(beforeHour + hourDelta);
          owner.timeMinuteDeg = timeNormDeg(beforeMinute + minuteDelta);

          clocks.push({ uid:owner.uid, team:timeTeamOf(g,owner), beforeHour,beforeMinute, afterHour:owner.timeHourDeg, afterMinute:owner.timeMinuteDeg, hourDelta,minuteDelta, aligned:timeIsAligned(owner) });
          log(g, `${owner.name}의 운명의 시계: ${n}E 반응 · 시침 +${hourDelta}°${minuteDelta?` · 분침 +${minuteDelta}°`:""} → ${owner.timeHourDeg}°/${owner.timeMinuteDeg}°${timeIsAligned(owner)?" · 정렬":""}.`,"turn");
        }
        if (!sim && typeof game !== "undefined" && g === game && clocks.length) {
          visualEvents.push({ type:"timeClockRotationF
