t = listeners.get(type) || new Set(); set.add(listener); listeners.set(type, set);
      return () => set.delete(listener);
    };
    const emit = event => {
      for (const type of [event.type, "*"]) {
        for (const listener of [...(listeners.get(type) || [])]) {
          try { listener(event); }
          catch (error) { console.error(`[R48 EventBus:${type}]`, error); queueMicrotask(() => { throw error; }); }
        }
      }
      return event;
    };
    return Object.freeze({ on, emit, clear(type) { type ? listeners.delete(type) : listeners.clear(); }, listenerCount(type) { return (listeners.get(type) || new Set()).size; } });
  })();

  const TimerManager = window.TimerManager;


  const InputLockManager = (() => {
    const locks = new Map();
    let clockToken = null;
    const roots = () => [document.querySelector(".app"), document.getElementById("modal")].filter(Boolean);
    const applyDomLock = on => {
      document.body.classList.toggle("time-input-locked",!!on);
      for (const root of roots()) {
        try { root.inert = !!on; } catch (error) { window.__megaRecordRecoveredError(error,"InputLockManager.applyDomLock"); }
        root.setAttribute("aria-hidden", on ? "true" : "false");
      }
    };
    const updateDataset = () => {
      if (locks.size) document.documentElement.dataset.inputLocked = "true";
      else delete document.documentElement.dataset.inputLocked;
    };
    const lock = (reason, owner="runtime", options={}) => {
      const normalized = String(reason || "unknown");
      const token = IdFactory.lock(normalized);
      const runtime = window.OnlineRuntime?.debug?.() || {};
      const createdAt = now();
      locks.set(token, {
        token, reason:normalized, owner, createdAt,
        battleId:options.battleId ?? currentGame()?.battleId ?? null,
        sessionGeneration:Number(options.sessionGeneration ?? runtime.generation ?? 0),
        revision:Number(options.revision ?? currentGame()?.networkRevision ?? 0),
        timeoutAt:Number(options.timeoutMs) > 0 ? createdAt + Number(options.timeoutMs) : null,
        cleanupPath:String(options.cleanupPath || "owner-finally-or-battle-cleanup"),
      });
      updateDataset();
      EventBus.emit({ type:"INPUT_LOCKED", token, reason:normalized, owner, createdAt:now() });
      return token;
    };
    const unlock = tokenOrReason => {
      let count = 0;
      if (locks.has(tokenOrReason)) {
        if (tokenOrReason === clockToken) clockToken = null;
        locks.delete(tokenOrReason); count=1;
      } else {
        for (const [token, entry] of [...locks]) if (entry.reason === tokenOrReason) {
          if (token === clockToken) clockToken = null;
          locks.delete(token); count++;
        }
      }
      updateDataset();
      if (count) EventBus.emit({ type:"INPUT_UNLOCKED", tokenOrReason, count, createdAt:now() });
      return count;
    };
    const releaseClockOwner = () => {
      if (clockToken && locks.has(clockToken)) locks.delete(clockToken);
      clockToken = null;
      for (const [token, entry] of [...locks]) if (entry.reason === "clock-animation" && entry.owner === "timeSetInputLock") locks.delete(token);
      updateDataset();
    };
    const setClockLock = (on, options={}) => {
      const holdBusy = options.holdBusy !== false;
      if (on) {
        if (!clockToken || !locks.has(clockToken)) clockToken = lock("clock-animation",options.owner || "timeSetInputLock");
        timeInputLocked = true;
        applyDomLock(true);
        if (holdBusy && typeof busy !== "undefined") {
          if (!busy) timeInputLockBusyOwned = true;
          busy = true;
        }
      } else {
        timeInputLocked = false;
        applyDomLock(false);
        releaseClockOwner();
        if (timeInputLockBusyOwned) {
          timeInputLockBusyOwned = false;
          if (!(typeof onlinePendingCommand !== "undefined" && onlinePendingCommand)) busy = false;
        }
      }
      return timeInputLocked;
    };
    const clockVisualPresent = () => !!document.querySelector(".time-clock-action-overlay,.time-hyper-clock,.time-recall-fx,.time-clock-cinematic,.time-skill-fx");
    const reconcileLegacyClock = (reason="reconcile") => {
      const declared = !!timeInputLocked || document.body.classList.contains("time-input-locked");
      const legitimate = !!currentGame()?.pendingClockTurnAdvance || clockVisualPresent();
      if (declared && !legitimate) {
        setClockLock(false,{holdBusy:false,owner:`reconcile:${reason}`});
        return {changed:true,reason,state:"released-stale-clock"};
      }
      if (declared && legitimate && (!clockToken || !locks.has(clockToken))) {
        setClockLock(true,{holdBusy:false,owner:"timeSetInputLock"});
        return {changed:true,reason,state:"adopted-clock"};
      }
      if (!declared && clockToken) {
        releaseClockOwner();
        return {changed:true,reason,state:"released-orphan-token"};
      }
      applyDomLock(declared);
      return {changed:false,reason,state:declared?"locked":"unlocked"};
    };
    const releaseBattle = battleId => {
      for (const [token, entry] of [...locks]) if (entry.battleId === battleId) {
        if (token === clockToken) clockToken = null;
        locks.delete(token);
      }
      updateDataset();
      if (!clockToken && timeInputLocked && currentGame()?.battleId === battleId) {
        timeInputLocked=false; applyDomLock(false); timeInputLockBusyOwned=false;
      }
    };
    const reasons = () => [...new Set([...locks.values()].map(x=>x.reason))];
    const snapshot = () => [...locks.values()].map(x=>({...x}));
    const clockState = () => ({token:clockToken,locked:!!timeInputLocked||document.body.classList.contains("time-input-locked"),visual:clockVisualPresent(),pending:!!currentGame()?.pendingClockTurnAdvance});
    LocalInputEligibility.bindProviders({
      locks:snapshot,
      clock:clockState,
      online:g=>{const runtime=window.OnlineRuntime?.debug?.()||{};return{
        role:p2p?.role || null,
        connected:!!p2p?.connected && !!p2p?.conn?.open,
        battleStarted:(!!p2p?.battleStarted || !!g?.battleStarted) && !(p2p?.role==="host" && (p2p?.onlineStartPending || p2p?.startCommitPacket) && !p2p?.startCommitAck) && !(p2p?.role==="guest" && p2p?.guestStartAwaitingHostCommit),
        reconnecting:!!p2p?.reconnecting || !!p2p?.connectionInterrupted || runtime.state === "RECONNECTING" || (!!runtime.generation&&runtime.committed&&!runtime.routesReady),
        pendingActionId:(typeof onlinePendingCommand !== "undefined" ? onlinePendingCommand?.packet?.actionId : null) || p2p?.pendingActionId || null,
        authoritativeRevision:Number(p2p?.lastAuthoritativeRevision || (typeof onlineAuthoritativeRevision !== "undefined" ? onlineAuthoritativeRevision : 0) || 0),
        busy:!!busy,
        runtime,
      };},
      bossControls:g=>typeof bossLocalControlsCurrent !== "function" || !!bossLocalControlsCurrent(g),
    });
    const canHumanInput = g => LocalInputEligibility.canAct(g);
    return Object.freeze({ lock, unlock, releaseBattle, setClockLock, reconcileLegacyClock,
      hasLocks:()=>locks.size>0, count:()=>locks.size, reasons, snapshot, canHumanInput,
      evaluate:g=>LocalInputEligibility.evaluate(g), clockState
    });
  })();

  const EnergyEngine = (() => {
    const ensureEntry = (entry, entityId="entity") => {
      if (!entry || typeof entry !== "object") return entry;
      entry.a = Math.max(0, Math.round((Number(entry.a)||0)*2)/2);
      if (!["R","W","B"].includes(entry.c)) entry.c = "W";
      if (!entry.energyId) entry.energyId = IdFactory.energy(entityId);
      return entry;
    };
    const ensureStore = (unit, actor=null, explicitStore=null) => {
      const store = explicitStore || (typeof energyStore === "function" ? energyStore(unit, actor) : unit?.energy);
      if (!Array.isArray(store)) return [];
      const seen = new Set();
      for (const entry of store) {
        ensureEntry(entry, unit?.entityId || unit?.uid || "entity");
        if (seen.has(entry.energyId)) entry.energyId = IdFactory.energy(unit?.entityId || unit?.uid || "entity");
        seen.add(entry.energyId);
      }
      return store;
    };
    const snapshot = unit => {
      const groups = cardHasCapability(unit, "dualActor") ? [["beast",unit.beastEnergy],["rider",unit.riderEnergy]] : [[null,unit?.energy]];
      return groups.flatMap(([actor,store]) => ensureStore(unit,actor,store).map((e,index)=>({ energyId:e.energyId, color:e.c, amount:e.a, actor, index })));
    };
    const validatePayment = (unit, ids, actor=null) => {
      const store = ensureStore(unit,actor), unique = [...new Set(ids || [])];
      if (unique.length !== (ids || []).length) return { ok:false, reason:"duplicate-energy-id" };
      if (!unique.every(id => store.some(e=>e.energyId===id))) return { ok:false, reason:"foreign-or-missing-energy" };
      return { ok:true, entries:unique.map(id=>store.find(e=>e.energyId===id)) };
    };
    return Object.freeze({ ensureEntry, ensureStore, snapshot, validatePayment,
      canPay:(g,t,u,cost,actor=null)=>canPayAction(g,t,u,cost,actor),
      spend:(g,t,u,cost,prefer=[],actor=null)=>spendAction(g,t,u,cost,prefer,actor),
      gain:(g,u,color,amount=1,replace=null,actor=null,opt={})=>gainEnergy(g,u,color,amount,replace,actor,opt)
    });
  })();

  const EntityRegistry = (() => {
    const zonesFor = (g, team) => {
      const tm = g?.teams?.[team] || {};
      return [["field",tm.field||[]],["bench",tm.bench||[]],["graveyard",tm.graveyard||[]],["summon",tm.summons||[]]];
    };
    const sync = g => {
      if (!g?.teams) return { entities:{}, slots:{} };
      const entities = {}, slots = {};
      for (const team of ["P","A"]) for (const [zone,list] of zonesFor(g,team)) {
        list.forEach((unit,index) => {
          if (!unit) return;
          unit.entityId ||= unit.uid || `legacy:${team}:${zone}:${index}`;
          unit.uid ||= unit.entityId;
          EnergyEngine.ensureStore(unit);
          if (cardHasCapability(unit, "dualActor")) { EnergyEngine.ensureStore(unit,"beast",unit.beastEnergy); EnergyEngine.ensureStore(unit,"rider",unit.riderEnergy); }
          entities[unit.entityId] = unit;
          const slotId = IdFactory.slot(team,zone,zone === "summon" ? unit.entityId : index);
          slots[slotId] = { slotId, teamId:team, zone, index, entityId:unit.entityId };
        });
      }
      defineDerived(g,"entities",entities); defineDerived(g,"slots",slots);
      return { entities, slots };
    };
    const get = (g,id) => (g?.entities || sync(g).entities)?.[id] || (typeof findUnit === "function" ? findUnit(g,id) : null);
    const locate = (g,id) => Object.values(g?.slots || sync(g).slots).find(x=>x.entityId===id) || null;
    return Object.freeze({ sync, get, locate, list:g=>Object.values((g?.entities||sync(g).entities)) });
  })();

  const BattleEventEngine = (() => {
    const emit = (g, type, data={}) => {
      if (!g) return null;
      g.eventSequence = Math.max(0, Number(g.eventSequence)||0) + 1;
      g.eventLog ||= [];
      const event = {
        eventId: IdFactory.event(g.battleId || g.matchId || "battle", g.eventSequence),
        battleId: g.battleId || g.matchId || null,
        sequence: g.eventSequence,
        type,
        sourceEntityId: data.sourceEntityId || null,
        recipientEntityId: data.recipientEntityId || null,
        targetSlotId: data.targetSlotId || null,
        teamId: data.teamId || null,
        payload: data.payload || {},
        createdAt: now(),
        causedByEventId: data.causedByEventId || null
      };
      if (!g.appliedEventIds) defineDerived(g,"appliedEventIds",new Set());
      if (g.appliedEventIds.has(event.eventId)) return null;
      g.appliedEventIds.add(event.eventId);
      g.eventLog.push(event);
      if (g.eventLog.length > GameConfig.maxEventLog) g.eventLog.splice(0,g.eventLog.length-GameConfig.maxEventLog);
      EventBus.emit(event);
      return event;
    };
    return Object.freeze({ emit });
  })();

  const StateFactory = (() => {
    const active = g => {
      const [team,slot] = g?.order?.[g.turn] || [];
      const unit = team != null && typeof unitAt === "function" ? unitAt(g,team,slot) : null;
      return { team, slot, unit };
    };
    const decorate = (g, options={}) => {
      if (!g || typeof g !== "object") return g;
      g.battleId ||= g.matchId || IdFactory.battle(g.seed || "battle");
      g.mode ||= options.mode || "human";
      g.phase ||= g.winner ? "BATTLE_ENDED" : "WAITING_FOR_ACTION";
      g.round = Math.max(1, Number(g.round)||1);
      g.turnNumber = Math.max(0, Number(g.turnSerial)||0);
      const cur = active(g);
      g.activeTeamId = cur.team || null;
      g.activeEntityId = cur.unit?.entityId || cur.unit?.uid || null;
      g.actionOwnerId = g.activeEntityId;
      // Input ownership is authoritative battle state, not a client-local viewing side.
      g.inputOwnerId = cur.team || null;
      g.battleStarted ??= !!(g.mode === "online" || g.bossRaid?.online);
      g.pendingAction ??= null; g.pendingTargetSelection ??= null; g.pendingPayment ??= null; g.pendingAnimation ??= null;
      g.pendingTransaction ??= null;
      g.decks ||= {
        P:[...(g.teams?.P?.field||[]),...(g.teams?.P?.bench||[])].map(u=>u?.card).filter(Boolean),
        A:[...(g.teams?.A?.field||[]),...(g.teams?.A?.bench||[])].map(u=>u?.card).filter(Boolean)
      };
      g.discard ||= { P:[], A:[] };
      g.fields ||= { global:g.fieldEffect || null, team:{P:null,A:null} };
      g.globalEffects ||= [];
      g.bossState ||= g.bossRaid ? { active:true, type:g.bossRaid.type || "raid" } : null;
      g.networkRevision = Math.max(Number(g.networkRevision)||0, Number(typeof onlineAuthoritativeRevision !== "undefined" ? onlineAuthoritativeRevision : 0)||0);
      g.eventSequence = Math.max(0, Number(g.eventSequence)||0);
      g.eventLog ||= [];
      g.ended = !!(g.ended || g.winner);
      EntityRegistry.sync(g);
      return g;
    };
    const updateActive = g => {
      if (!g || typeof g !== "object") return g;
      decorate(g);
      const cur = active(g);
      g.activeTeamId = cur.team || null; g.activeEntityId = cur.unit?.entityId || cur.unit?.uid || null;
      g.actionOwnerId = g.activeEntityId; g.inputOwnerId = cur.team || null; g.turnNumber = Math.max(0,Number(g.turnSerial)||0);
      if (g.winner) { g.ended=true; g.phase="BATTLE_ENDED"; }
      return g;
    };
    return Object.freeze({ create:(p,a,opt)=>decorate(newGame(p,a,opt),opt), decorate, updateActive });
  })();

  const StateValidator = (() => {
    const validate = (g, options={}) => {
      const issues = [];
      if (!g) return { ok:false, issues:[{code:"NO_STATE",message:"game state missing"}] };
      StateFactory.decorate(g);
      const entities = Object.values(g.entities || {}), ids = new Set(), slotIds = new Set(), occupied = new Set(), energyIds = new Set();
      for (const unit of entities) {
        const id = unit.entityId || unit.uid;
        if (ids.has(id)) issues.push({code:"DUP_ENTITY_ID",entityId:id}); ids.add(id);
        if (Number(unit.shield||0) < 0) issues.push({code:"NEGATIVE_SHIELD",entityId:id,value:unit.shield});
        if (Number(unit.hp||0) > Number(unit.maxHp||0) + Math.max(0,Number(unit.maxHpOverflowAllowance||0))) issues.push({code:"HP_OVER_MAX",entityId:id,hp:unit.hp,maxHp:unit.maxHp});
        for (const e of EnergyEngine.snapshot(unit)) {
          if (energyIds.has(e.energyId)) issues.push({code:"DUP_ENERGY_ID",energyId:e.energyId}); energyIds.add(e.energyId);
        }
        const max = Number(card(unit)?.maxEnergy || GameConfig.maxEnergyDefault);
        if (typeof totalEnergy === "function" && !cardHasCapability(unit, "dualActor") && totalEnergy(unit) > max + .001) issues.push({code:"ENERGY_OVER_MAX",entityId:id,value:totalEnergy(unit),max});
      }
      for (const slot of Object.values(g.slots || {})) {
        if (slotIds.has(slot.slotId)) issues.push({code:"DUP_SLOT_ID",slotId:slot.slotId}); slotIds.add(slot.slotId);
        if (slot.entityId && slot.zone !== "graveyard") { if (occupied.has(slot.entityId)) issues.push({code:"MULTI_SLOT_ENTITY",entityId:slot.entityId}); occupied.add(slot.entityId); }
      }
      const active = g.activeEntityId ? EntityRegistry.get(g,g.activeEntityId) : null;
      if (g.activeEntityId && !active) issues.push({code:"ACTIVE_ENTITY_MISSING",entityId:g.activeEntityId});
      if (active && g.activeTeamId && typeof teamOf === "function" && teamOf(g,active) !== g.activeTeamId) issues.push({code:"ACTIVE_TEAM_MISMATCH",entityId:g.activeEntityId,activeTeamId:g.activeTeamId});
      if (!g.winner && active?.dead && !g.teams?.[g.activeTeamId]?.bench?.some(unit=>unit&&!unit.dead)) issues.push({code:"DEAD_ACTIVE_WITHOUT_REPLACEMENT",entityId:g.activeEntityId,activeTeamId:g.activeTeamId,turn:g.turn,turnSerial:g.turnSerial});
      if (Number(g.actionPoints||0) < 0) issues.push({code:"NEGATIVE_ACTION_POINTS",value:g.actionPoints});
      if (g.ended && !g.winner) issues.push({code:"ENDED_WITHOUT_WINNER"});
      if (g.winner && !g.ended) issues.push({code:"WINNER_NOT_ENDED"});
      if (g.pendingTransaction && g.pendingAction && g.pendingTransaction.action !== g.pendingAction && g.pendingTransaction.actionId !== g.pendingAction?.actionId) issues.push({code:"PENDING_TRANSACTION_MISMATCH"});
      if (Number(g.networkSequence||0) < Number(g.lastAppliedNetworkSequence||0)) issues.push({code:"NETWORK_SEQUENCE_REVERSED"});
      const duplicateEvents = new Set();
      for (const ev of g.eventLog || []) { if (duplicateEvents.has(ev.eventId)) issues.push({code:"DUP_EVENT",eventId:ev.eventId}); duplicateEvents.add(ev.eventId); }
      const result = { ok:issues.length===0, issues, battleId:g.battleId, checkedAt:now() };
      if (!result.ok) console.error("[R48 StateValidator]", result);
      if (!result.ok && options.throwOnError) throw new Error(`State invariant violation: ${issues.map(x=>x.code).join(",")}`);
      return result;
    };
    return Object.freeze({ validate, assert:g=>validate(g,{throwOnError:true}) });
  })();

  const OnlineSnapshotEngine = (() => {
    const VERSION = "mega-online-snapshot-restore-cp25";
    const SOURCES = new Set(["legacy-start","legacy-state","authoritative-state","authoritative-start","spectator","sync-engine","test"]);
    const RESTORE_STAGES = Object.freeze([
      "parseSnapshot",
      "restoreRng",
      "assignFreshBattleToken",
      "applyOnlineModeDefaults",
      "normalizeGenociderState",
      "canonicalizeEnergyState",
      "decorateBattleState",
      "normalizeNetworkRevision",
      "validateRestoredState"
    ]);
    let restoreCount = 0;
    let lastRestore = null;

    const normalizeContext = context => {
      const source = SOURCES.has(context?.source) ? context.source : "test";
      const rules = context?.rules ?? p2p?.rules ?? ONLINE_COMMON_RULES;
      return Object.freeze({ source, rules });
    };
    const parseSnapshot = rawSnapshot => JSON.parse(rawSnapshot);
    const restoreRng = g => {
      const rng = new RNG(g.seed);
      rng.s = g.rng?.s || rng.s;
      g.rng = rng;
      return g;
    };
    const assignFreshBattleToken = g => { g.token = ++battleToken; return g; };
    const applyOnlineModeDefaults = (g, context) => {
      g.mode = "online";
      g.variance = false;
      g.difficulty = "normal";
      const rules = context.rules;
      if (rules?.league) {
        g.league = LeagueFormatOwner.normalizeLeague(rules.league);
        const sourceCompositions = g.leagueCompositions || rules.compositions || g.leagueComposition || rules.composition || null;
        g.leagueCompositions = LeagueFormatOwner.normalizeTeamCompositions(g.league, sourceCompositions);
        g.leagueComposition = g.leagueCompositions.P;
        g.matchType = LeagueFormatOwner.definition(g.league).label;
      }
      return g;
    };
    const restoredUnits = g => {
      if (!g?.teams) return [];
      const seen = new Set(), units = [];
      for (const team of ["P","A"])
        for (const unit of [...(g.teams[team]?.field || []), ...(g.teams[team]?.bench || []), ...(g.teams[team]?.graveyard || [])])
          if (unit && !seen.has(unit.uid)) { seen.add(unit.uid); units.push(unit); }
      return units;
    };
    const genociderBAmount = unit => {
      if (!unit || !reactiveEnergyRule(unit)) return 0;
      return +(energyStore(unit).reduce((total,entry)=>total+(entry.c === "B" ? Number(entry.a || 0) : 0),0)).toFixed(1);
    };
    const normalizeGenociderState = g => {
      const baseHp = 240;
      for (const unit of restoredUnits(g)) {
        if (!reactiveEnergyRule(unit)) continue;
        unit._genoObservedB = genociderBAmount(unit);
        const activeB = traitsActive(unit) ? genociderBAmount(unit) : 0;
        unit.maxHp = Math.max(baseHp, combatInt(baseHp + activeB * 60));
        if (unit.hp > unit.maxHp) {
          unit.hp = unit.maxHp;

          log(g, `${unit.name}: 최대 HP 변화로 현재 HP가 ${unit.hp}/${unit.maxHp}로 조정됐다.`);
        }
      }
      return g;
    };
    const canonicalizeEnergyState = g => {
      const engine = window.EnergyCanonicalizationEngine;
      if (!engine || typeof engine.canonicalizeGameState !== "function")
        throw new Error("EnergyCanonicalizationEngine unavailable");
      engine.canonicalizeGameState(g);
      return g;
    };
    const decorateBattleState = g => StateFactory.decorate(g,{mode:"online"});
    const
--- pendingActionId #14 offset=6761235 ---
ner);
    };
    const emit = event => {
      for (const type of [event.type, "*"]) {
        for (const listener of [...(listeners.get(type) || [])]) {
          try { listener(event); }
          catch (error) { console.error(`[R48 EventBus:${type}]`, error); queueMicrotask(() => { throw error; }); }
        }
      }
      return event;
    };
    return Object.freeze({ on, emit, clear(type) { type ? listeners.delete(type) : listeners.clear(); }, listenerCount(type) { return (listeners.get(type) || new Set()).size; } });
  })();

  const TimerManager = window.TimerManager;


  const InputLockManager = (() => {
    const locks = new Map();
    let clockToken = null;
    const roots = () => [document.querySelector(".app"), document.getElementById("modal")].filter(Boolean);
    const applyDomLock = on => {
      document.body.classList.toggle("time-input-locked",!!on);
      for (const root of roots()) {
        try { root.inert = !!on; } catch (error) { window.__megaRecordRecoveredError(error,"InputLockManager.applyDomLock"); }
        root.setAttribute("aria-hidden", on ? "true" : "false");
      }
    };
    const updateDataset = () => {
      if (locks.size) document.documentElement.dataset.inputLocked = "true";
      else delete document.documentElement.dataset.inputLocked;
    };
    const lock = (reason, owner="runtime", options={}) => {
      const normalized = String(reason || "unknown");
      const token = IdFactory.lock(normalized);
      const runtime = window.OnlineRuntime?.debug?.() || {};
      const createdAt = now();
      locks.set(token, {
        token, reason:normalized, owner, createdAt,
        battleId:options.battleId ?? currentGame()?.battleId ?? null,
        sessionGeneration:Number(options.sessionGeneration ?? runtime.generation ?? 0),
        revision:Number(options.revision ?? currentGame()?.networkRevision ?? 0),
        timeoutAt:Number(options.timeoutMs) > 0 ? createdAt + Number(options.timeoutMs) : null,
        cleanupPath:String(options.cleanupPath || "owner-finally-or-battle-cleanup"),
      });
      updateDataset();
      EventBus.emit({ type:"INPUT_LOCKED", token, reason:normalized, owner, createdAt:now() });
      return token;
    };
    const unlock = tokenOrReason => {
      let count = 0;
      if (locks.has(tokenOrReason)) {
        if (tokenOrReason === clockToken) clockToken = null;
        locks.delete(tokenOrReason); count=1;
      } else {
        for (const [token, entry] of [...locks]) if (entry.reason === tokenOrReason) {
          if (token === clockToken) clockToken = null;
          locks.delete(token); count++;
        }
      }
      updateDataset();
      if (count) EventBus.emit({ type:"INPUT_UNLOCKED", tokenOrReason, count, createdAt:now() });
      return count;
    };
    const releaseClockOwner = () => {
      if (clockToken && locks.has(clockToken)) locks.delete(clockToken);
      clockToken = null;
      for (const [token, entry] of [...locks]) if (entry.reason === "clock-animation" && entry.owner === "timeSetInputLock") locks.delete(token);
      updateDataset();
    };
    const setClockLock = (on, options={}) => {
      const holdBusy = options.holdBusy !== false;
      if (on) {
        if (!clockToken || !locks.has(clockToken)) clockToken = lock("clock-animation",options.owner || "timeSetInputLock");
        timeInputLocked = true;
        applyDomLock(true);
        if (holdBusy && typeof busy !== "undefined") {
          if (!busy) timeInputLockBusyOwned = true;
          busy = true;
        }
      } else {
        timeInputLocked = false;
        applyDomLock(false);
        releaseClockOwner();
        if (timeInputLockBusyOwned) {
          timeInputLockBusyOwned = false;
          if (!(typeof onlinePendingCommand !== "undefined" && onlinePendingCommand)) busy = false;
        }
      }
      return timeInputLocked;
    };
    const clockVisualPresent = () => !!document.querySelector(".time-clock-action-overlay,.time-hyper-clock,.time-recall-fx,.time-clock-cinematic,.time-skill-fx");
    const reconcileLegacyClock = (reason="reconcile") => {
      const declared = !!timeInputLocked || document.body.classList.contains("time-input-locked");
      const legitimate = !!currentGame()?.pendingClockTurnAdvance || clockVisualPresent();
      if (declared && !legitimate) {
        setClockLock(false,{holdBusy:false,owner:`reconcile:${reason}`});
        return {changed:true,reason,state:"released-stale-clock"};
      }
      if (declared && legitimate && (!clockToken || !locks.has(clockToken))) {
        setClockLock(true,{holdBusy:false,owner:"timeSetInputLock"});
        return {changed:true,reason,state:"adopted-clock"};
      }
      if (!declared && clockToken) {
        releaseClockOwner();
        return {changed:true,reason,state:"released-orphan-token"};
      }
      applyDomLock(declared);
      return {changed:false,reason,state:declared?"locked":"unlocked"};
    };
    const releaseBattle = battleId => {
      for (const [token, entry] of [...locks]) if (entry.battleId === battleId) {
        if (token === clockToken) clockToken = null;
        locks.delete(token);
      }
      updateDataset();
      if (!clockToken && timeInputLocked && currentGame()?.battleId === battleId) {
        timeInputLocked=false; applyDomLock(false); timeInputLockBusyOwned=false;
      }
    };
    const reasons = () => [...new Set([...locks.values()].map(x=>x.reason))];
    const snapshot = () => [...locks.values()].map(x=>({...x}));
    const clockState = () => ({token:clockToken,locked:!!timeInputLocked||document.body.classList.contains("time-input-locked"),visual:clockVisualPresent(),pending:!!currentGame()?.pendingClockTurnAdvance});
    LocalInputEligibility.bindProviders({
      locks:snapshot,
      clock:clockState,
      online:g=>{const runtime=window.OnlineRuntime?.debug?.()||{};return{
        role:p2p?.role || null,
        connected:!!p2p?.connected && !!p2p?.conn?.open,
        battleStarted:(!!p2p?.battleStarted || !!g?.battleStarted) && !(p2p?.role==="host" && (p2p?.onlineStartPending || p2p?.startCommitPacket) && !p2p?.startCommitAck) && !(p2p?.role==="guest" && p2p?.guestStartAwaitingHostCommit),
        reconnecting:!!p2p?.reconnecting || !!p2p?.connectionInterrupted || runtime.state === "RECONNECTING" || (!!runtime.generation&&runtime.committed&&!runtime.routesReady),
        pendingActionId:(typeof onlinePendingCommand !== "undefined" ? onlinePendingCommand?.packet?.actionId : null) || p2p?.pendingActionId || null,
        authoritativeRevision:Number(p2p?.lastAuthoritativeRevision || (typeof onlineAuthoritativeRevision !== "undefined" ? onlineAuthoritativeRevision : 0) || 0),
        busy:!!busy,
        runtime,
      };},
      bossControls:g=>typeof bossLocalControlsCurrent !== "function" || !!bossLocalControlsCurrent(g),
    });
    const canHumanInput = g => LocalInputEligibility.canAct(g);
    return Object.freeze({ lock, unlock, releaseBattle, setClockLock, reconcileLegacyClock,
      hasLocks:()=>locks.size>0, count:()=>locks.size, reasons, snapshot, canHumanInput,
      evaluate:g=>LocalInputEligibility.evaluate(g), clockState
    });
  })();

  const EnergyEngine = (() => {
    const ensureEntry = (entry, entityId="entity") => {
      if (!entry || typeof entry !== "object") return entry;
      entry.a = Math.max(0, Math.round((Number(entry.a)||0)*2)/2);
      if (!["R","W","B"].includes(entry.c)) entry.c = "W";
      if (!entry.energyId) entry.energyId = IdFactory.energy(entityId);
      return entry;
    };
    const ensureStore = (unit, actor=null, explicitStore=null) => {
      const store = explicitStore || (typeof energyStore === "function" ? energyStore(unit, actor) : unit?.energy);
      if (!Array.isArray(store)) return [];
      const seen = new Set();
      for (const entry of store) {
        ensureEntry(entry, unit?.entityId || unit?.uid || "entity");
        if (seen.has(entry.energyId)) entry.energyId = IdFactory.energy(unit?.entityId || unit?.uid || "entity");
        seen.add(entry.energyId);
      }
      return store;
    };
    const snapshot = unit => {
      const groups = cardHasCapability(unit, "dualActor") ? [["beast",unit.beastEnergy],["rider",unit.riderEnergy]] : [[null,unit?.energy]];
      return groups.flatMap(([actor,store]) => ensureStore(unit,actor,store).map((e,index)=>({ energyId:e.energyId, color:e.c, amount:e.a, actor, index })));
    };
    const validatePayment = (unit, ids, actor=null) => {
      const store = ensureStore(unit,actor), unique = [...new Set(ids || [])];
      if (unique.length !== (ids || []).length) return { ok:false, reason:"duplicate-energy-id" };
      if (!unique.every(id => store.some(e=>e.energyId===id))) return { ok:false, reason:"foreign-or-missing-energy" };
      return { ok:true, entries:unique.map(id=>store.find(e=>e.energyId===id)) };
    };
    return Object.freeze({ ensureEntry, ensureStore, snapshot, validatePayment,
      canPay:(g,t,u,cost,actor=null)=>canPayAction(g,t,u,cost,actor),
      spend:(g,t,u,cost,prefer=[],actor=null)=>spendAction(g,t,u,cost,prefer,actor),
      gain:(g,u,color,amount=1,replace=null,actor=null,opt={})=>gainEnergy(g,u,color,amount,replace,actor,opt)
    });
  })();

  const EntityRegistry = (() => {
    const zonesFor = (g, team) => {
      const tm = g?.teams?.[team] || {};
      return [["field",tm.field||[]],["bench",tm.bench||[]],["graveyard",tm.graveyard||[]],["summon",tm.summons||[]]];
    };
    const sync = g => {
      if (!g?.teams) return { entities:{}, slots:{} };
      const entities = {}, slots = {};
      for (const team of ["P","A"]) for (const [zone,list] of zonesFor(g,team)) {
        list.forEach((unit,index) => {
          if (!unit) return;
          unit.entityId ||= unit.uid || `legacy:${team}:${zone}:${index}`;
          unit.uid ||= unit.entityId;
          EnergyEngine.ensureStore(unit);
          if (cardHasCapability(unit, "dualActor")) { EnergyEngine.ensureStore(unit,"beast",unit.beastEnergy); EnergyEngine.ensureStore(unit,"rider",unit.riderEnergy); }
          entities[unit.entityId] = unit;
          const slotId = IdFactory.slot(team,zone,zone === "summon" ? unit.entityId : index);
          slots[slotId] = { slotId, teamId:team, zone, index, entityId:unit.entityId };
        });
      }
      defineDerived(g,"entities",entities); defineDerived(g,"slots",slots);
      return { entities, slots };
    };
    const get = (g,id) => (g?.entities || sync(g).entities)?.[id] || (typeof findUnit === "function" ? findUnit(g,id) : null);
    const locate = (g,id) => Object.values(g?.slots || sync(g).slots).find(x=>x.entityId===id) || null;
    return Object.freeze({ sync, get, locate, list:g=>Object.values((g?.entities||sync(g).entities)) });
  })();

  const BattleEventEngine = (() => {
    const emit = (g, type, data={}) => {
      if (!g) return null;
      g.eventSequence = Math.max(0, Number(g.eventSequence)||0) + 1;
      g.eventLog ||= [];
      const event = {
        eventId: IdFactory.event(g.battleId || g.matchId || "battle", g.eventSequence),
        battleId: g.battleId || g.matchId || null,
        sequence: g.eventSequence,
        type,
        sourceEntityId: data.sourceEntityId || null,
        recipientEntityId: data.recipientEntityId || null,
        targetSlotId: data.targetSlotId || null,
        teamId: data.teamId || null,
        payload: data.payload || {},
        createdAt: now(),
        causedByEventId: data.causedByEventId || null
      };
      if (!g.appliedEventIds) defineDerived(g,"appliedEventIds",new Set());
      if (g.appliedEventIds.has(event.eventId)) return null;
      g.appliedEventIds.add(event.eventId);
      g.eventLog.push(event);
      if (g.eventLog.length > GameConfig.maxEventLog) g.eventLog.splice(0,g.eventLog.length-GameConfig.maxEventLog);
      EventBus.emit(event);
      return event;
    };
    return Object.freeze({ emit });
  })();

  const StateFactory = (() => {
    const active = g => {
      const [team,slot] = g?.order?.[g.turn] || [];
      const unit = team != null && typeof unitAt === "function" ? unitAt(g,team,slot) : null;
      return { team, slot, unit };
    };
    const decorate = (g, options={}) => {
      if (!g || typeof g !== "object") return g;
      g.battleId ||= g.matchId || IdFactory.battle(g.seed || "battle");
      g.mode ||= options.mode || "human";
      g.phase ||= g.winner ? "BATTLE_ENDED" : "WAITING_FOR_ACTION";
      g.round = Math.max(1, Number(g.round)||1);
      g.turnNumber = Math.max(0, Number(g.turnSerial)||0);
      const cur = active(g);
      g.activeTeamId = cur.team || null;
      g.activeEntityId = cur.unit?.entityId || cur.unit?.uid || null;
      g.actionOwnerId = g.activeEntityId;
      // Input ownership is authoritative battle state, not a client-local viewing side.
      g.inputOwnerId = cur.team || null;
      g.battleStarted ??= !!(g.mode === "online" || g.bossRaid?.online);
      g.pendingAction ??= null; g.pendingTargetSelection ??= null; g.pendingPayment ??= null; g.pendingAnimation ??= null;
      g.pendingTransaction ??= null;
      g.decks ||= {
        P:[...(g.teams?.P?.field||[]),...(g.teams?.P?.bench||[])].map(u=>u?.card).filter(Boolean),
        A:[...(g.teams?.A?.field||[]),...(g.teams?.A?.bench||[])].map(u=>u?.card).filter(Boolean)
      };
      g.discard ||= { P:[], A:[] };
      g.fields ||= { global:g.fieldEffect || null, team:{P:null,A:null} };
      g.globalEffects ||= [];
      g.bossState ||= g.bossRaid ? { active:true, type:g.bossRaid.type || "raid" } : null;
      g.networkRevision = Math.max(Number(g.networkRevision)||0, Number(typeof onlineAuthoritativeRevision !== "undefined" ? onlineAuthoritativeRevision : 0)||0);
      g.eventSequence = Math.max(0, Number(g.eventSequence)||0);
      g.eventLog ||= [];
      g.ended = !!(g.ended || g.winner);
      EntityRegistry.sync(g);
      return g;
    };
    const updateActive = g => {
      if (!g || typeof g !== "object") return g;
      decorate(g);
      const cur = active(g);
      g.activeTeamId = cur.team || null; g.activeEntityId = cur.unit?.entityId || cur.unit?.uid || null;
      g.actionOwnerId = g.activeEntityId; g.inputOwnerId = cur.team || null; g.turnNumber = Math.max(0,Number(g.turnSerial)||0);
      if (g.winner) { g.ended=true; g.phase="BATTLE_ENDED"; }
      return g;
    };
    return Object.freeze({ create:(p,a,opt)=>decorate(newGame(p,a,opt),opt), decorate, updateActive });
  })();

  const StateValidator = (() => {
    const validate = (g, options={}) => {
      const issues = [];
      if (!g) return { ok:false, issues:[{code:"NO_STATE",message:"game state missing"}] };
      StateFactory.decorate(g);
      const entities = Object.values(g.entities || {}), ids = new Set(), slotIds = new Set(), occupied = new Set(), energyIds = new Set();
      for (const unit of entities) {
        const id = unit.entityId || unit.uid;
        if (ids.has(id)) issues.push({code:"DUP_ENTITY_ID",entityId:id}); ids.add(id);
        if (Number(unit.shield||0) < 0) issues.push({code:"NEGATIVE_SHIELD",entityId:id,value:unit.shield});
        if (Number(unit.hp||0) > Number(unit.maxHp||0) + Math.max(0,Number(unit.maxHpOverflowAllowance||0))) issues.push({code:"HP_OVER_MAX",entityId:id,hp:unit.hp,maxHp:unit.maxHp});
        for (const e of EnergyEngine.snapshot(unit)) {
          if (energyIds.has(e.energyId)) issues.push({code:"DUP_ENERGY_ID",energyId:e.energyId}); energyIds.add(e.energyId);
        }
        const max = Number(card(unit)?.maxEnergy || GameConfig.maxEnergyDefault);
        if (typeof totalEnergy === "function" && !cardHasCapability(unit, "dualActor") && totalEnergy(unit) > max + .001) issues.push({code:"ENERGY_OVER_MAX",entityId:id,value:totalEnergy(unit),max});
      }
      for (const slot of Object.values(g.slots || {})) {
        if (slotIds.has(slot.slotId)) issues.push({code:"DUP_SLOT_ID",slotId:slot.slotId}); slotIds.add(slot.slotId);
        if (slot.entityId && slot.zone !== "graveyard") { if (occupied.has(slot.entityId)) issues.push({code:"MULTI_SLOT_ENTITY",entityId:slot.entityId}); occupied.add(slot.entityId); }
      }
      const active = g.activeEntityId ? EntityRegistry.get(g,g.activeEntityId) : null;
      if (g.activeEntityId && !active) issues.push({code:"ACTIVE_ENTITY_MISSING",entityId:g.activeEntityId});
      if (active && g.activeTeamId && typeof teamOf === "function" && teamOf(g,active) !== g.activeTeamId) issues.push({code:"ACTIVE_TEAM_MISMATCH",entityId:g.activeEntityId,activeTeamId:g.activeTeamId});
      if (!g.winner && active?.dead && !g.teams?.[g.activeTeamId]?.bench?.some(unit=>unit&&!unit.dead)) issues.push({code:"DEAD_ACTIVE_WITHOUT_REPLACEMENT",entityId:g.activeEntityId,activeTeamId:g.activeTeamId,turn:g.turn,turnSerial:g.turnSerial});
      if (Number(g.actionPoints||0) < 0) issues.push({code:"NEGATIVE_ACTION_POINTS",value:g.actionPoints});
      if (g.ended && !g.winner) issues.push({code:"ENDED_WITHOUT_WINNER"});
      if (g.winner && !g.ended) issues.push({code:"WINNER_NOT_ENDED"});
      if (g.pendingTransaction && g.pendingAction && g.pendingTransaction.action !== g.pendingAction && g.pendingTransaction.actionId !== g.pendingAction?.actionId) issues.push({code:"PENDING_TRANSACTION_MISMATCH"});
      if (Number(g.networkSequence||0) < Number(g.lastAppliedNetworkSequence||0)) issues.push({code:"NETWORK_SEQUENCE_REVERSED"});
      const duplicateEvents = new Set();
      for (const ev of g.eventLog || []) { if (duplicateEvents.has(ev.eventId)) issues.push({code:"DUP_EVENT",eventId:ev.eventId}); duplicateEvents.add(ev.eventId); }
      const result = { ok:issues.length===0, issues, battleId:g.battleId, checkedAt:now() };
      if (!result.ok) console.error("[R48 StateValidator]", result);
      if (!result.ok && options.throwOnError) throw new Error(`State invariant violation: ${issues.map(x=>x.code).join(",")}`);
      return result;
    };
    return Object.freeze({ validate, assert:g=>validate(g,{throwOnError:true}) });
  })();

  const OnlineSnapshotEngine = (() => {
    const VERSION = "mega-online-snapshot-restore-cp25";
    const SOURCES = new Set(["legacy-start","legacy-state","authoritative-state","authoritative-start","spectator","sync-engine","test"]);
    const RESTORE_STAGES = Object.freeze([
      "parseSnapshot",
      "restoreRng",
      "assignFreshBattleToken",
      "applyOnlineModeDefaults",
      "normalizeGenociderState",
      "canonicalizeEnergyState",
      "decorateBattleState",
      "normalizeNetworkRevision",
      "validateRestoredState"
    ]);
    let restoreCount = 0;
    let lastRestore = null;

    const normalizeContext = context => {
      const source = SOURCES.has(context?.source) ? context.source : "test";
      const rules = context?.rules ?? p2p?.rules ?? ONLINE_COMMON_RULES;
      return Object.freeze({ source, rules });
    };
    const parseSnapshot = rawSnapshot => JSON.parse(rawSnapshot);
    const restoreRng = g => {
      const rng = new RNG(g.seed);
      rng.s = g.rng?.s || rng.s;
      g.rng = rng;
      return g;
    };
    const assignFreshBattleToken = g => { g.token = ++battleToken; return g; };
    const applyOnlineModeDefaults = (g, context) => {
      g.mode = "online";
      g.variance = false;
      g.difficulty = "normal";
      const rules = context.rules;
      if (rules?.league) {
        g.league = LeagueFormatOwner.normalizeLeague(rules.league);
        const sourceCompositions = g.leagueCompositions || rules.compositions || g.leagueComposition || rules.composition || null;
        g.leagueCompositions = LeagueFormatOwner.normalizeTeamCompositions(g.league, sourceCompositions);
        g.leagueComposition = g.leagueCompositions.P;
        g.matchType = LeagueFormatOwner.definition(g.league).label;
      }
      return g;
    };
    const restoredUnits = g => {
      if (!g?.teams) return [];
      const seen = new Set(), units = [];
      for (const team of ["P","A"])
        for (const unit of [...(g.teams[team]?.field || []), ...(g.teams[team]?.bench || []), ...(g.teams[team]?.graveyard || [])])
          if (unit && !seen.has(unit.uid)) { seen.add(unit.uid); units.push(unit); }
      return units;
    };
    const genociderBAmount = unit => {
      if (!unit || !reactiveEnergyRule(unit)) return 0;
      return +(energyStore(unit).reduce((total,entry)=>total+(entry.c === "B" ? Number(entry.a || 0) : 0),0)).toFixed(1);
    };
    const normalizeGenociderState = g => {
      const baseHp = 240;
      for (const unit of restoredUnits(g)) {
        if (!reactiveEnergyRule(unit)) continue;
        unit._genoObservedB = genociderBAmount(unit);
        const activeB = traitsActive(unit) ? genociderBAmount(unit) : 0;
        unit.maxHp = Math.max(baseHp, combatInt(baseHp + activeB * 60));
        if (unit.hp > unit.maxHp) {
          unit.hp = unit.maxHp;

          log(g, `${unit.name}: 최대 HP 변화로 현재 HP가 ${unit.hp}/${unit.maxHp}로 조정됐다.`);
        }
      }
      return g;
    };
    const canonicalizeEnergyState = g => {
      const engine = window.EnergyCanonicalizationEngine;
      if (!engine || typeof engine.canonicalizeGameState !== "function")
        throw new Error("EnergyCanonicalizationEngine unavailable");
      engine.canonicalizeGameState(g);
      return g;
    };
    const decorateBattleState = g => StateFactory.decorate(g,{mode:"online"});
    const normalizeNetworkRevision = g => {
      g.networkRevision = Math.max(g.networkRevision || 0, Number(g.revision || 0))
===== REMOTE_ACTOR_TURN count=2 =====
--- REMOTE_ACTOR_TURN #1 offset=6039803 ---
arseCost(s.cost).R +
                  parseCost(s.cost).W +
                  parseCost(s.cost).B >=
                3,
            ).length,
          support = cs.filter((c) =>
            c.skills.some((s) =>
              s.tags.some((t) => ["heal", "shield", "support"].includes(t)),
            ),
          ).length,
          tanks = cs.filter((c) => c.role.includes("탱커")).length,
          cheap = cs.filter((c) =>
            c.skills.some(
              (s) =>
                s.tags.includes("damage") &&
                costGap(makeUnit(c.id, "score"), s.cost) <= 1,
            ),
          ).length;
        return (
          roles * 10 +
          Math.min(1, support) * 12 +
          Math.min(1, tanks) * 12 +
          Math.min(2, cheap) * 5 +
          (expensive ? support * 6 : 0) +
          deckConflictPenalty(ids)
        );
      }
      function arrangeAIDeck(ids) {
        let d = [...ids],
          memoryIndex = d.findIndex((id) =>
            CARDS.find((c) => c.id === id)?.skills.some(
              (s) => skillDefHasTag(s, "copy"),
            ),
          );
        if (memoryIndex < 0) return d;
        let memoryId = d[memoryIndex],
          dragonIndex = d.findIndex(
            (id, i) =>
              i !== memoryIndex &&
              CARDS.find((c) => c.id === id)?.tags.includes("dragon"),
          );
        if (dragonIndex < 0) return d;
        let dragonId = d[dragonIndex],
          rest = d.filter((_, i) => i !== memoryIndex && i !== dragonIndex);
        return [memoryId, ...rest.slice(0, 1), dragonId, ...rest.slice(1)];
      }
      function validFormDeck(ids) {
        let groups = ids
          .map((id) => CARDS.find((c) => c.id === id)?.formGroup)
          .filter(Boolean);
        return new Set(groups).size === groups.length &&
          new Set(ids).size === ids.length &&
          (ids || []).filter(isInvaderCardId).length <= 1;
      }
      function buildAIDeck(seed, league = "mega", composition = comprehensiveComposition) {
        league = LeagueFormatOwner.normalizeLeague(league);
        composition = LeagueFormatOwner.normalizeComposition(composition);
        let rng = new RNG(seed + "-ai-deck"), deck = [], pool = [...poolForLeague(league, composition)];
        while (deck.length < 4) {
          const ranked = pool
            .filter(id => LeagueFormatOwner.partialValid([...deck, id], league, composition))
            .map(id => ({ id, score: deckScore([...deck, id]) + rng.next() * 4 }))
            .sort((a,b) => b.score - a.score);
          if (!ranked.length) throw new Error(`AI deck composition exhausted: ${league}/${composition}/${deck.join(",")}`);
          const pick = ranked[0].id;
          deck.push(pick);
          pool = pool.filter(id => id !== pick);
        }
        if (!LeagueFormatOwner.completeValid(deck, league, composition)) throw new Error(`AI deck composition invalid: ${league}/${composition}`);
        return league === "mega" ? arrangeAIDeck(deck) : deck;
      }
      function localTeamFor(g) {
        return (g?.mode === "online" || g?.mode === "spectator") ? onlineSide : "P";
      }
      function displaySideFor(g, t) {
        return t === localTeamFor(g) ? "player" : "ai";
      }
      const LocalInputEligibility = (() => {
        let providers = Object.freeze({
          locks: () => [],
          clock: () => ({ locked: false }),
          online: () => ({}),
          bossControls: () => true,
        });
        const bindProviders = next => {
          providers = Object.freeze({ ...providers, ...(next || {}) });
          return true;
        };
        const evaluate = (g, context = null) => {
          const reasons = [];
          const surfaceKind = context?.surfaceKind || (g?.bossRaid ? "boss-raid" : "base");
          const pair = context?.actorTeam != null
            ? [context.actorTeam, context.actorSlot ?? null]
            : (g?.order?.[g?.turn] || []);
          const currentActorCanonicalTeam = pair[0] ?? null;
          const currentActorSlot = pair[1] == null ? null : pair[1];
          const actor = context?.actor || (currentActorCanonicalTeam != null && currentActorSlot != null
            ? unitAt(g, currentActorCanonicalTeam, currentActorSlot)
            : null);
          const currentActorUid = context?.actorUid || actor?.entityId || actor?.uid || null;
          const canonicalLocalTeam = context?.canonicalLocalTeam || (g ? localTeamFor(g) : null);
          const displayLocalSide = canonicalLocalTeam ? displaySideFor(g, canonicalLocalTeam) : null;
          const standardOnline = context?.standardOnline ?? (g?.mode === "online" && !g?.bossRaid?.online);
          const online = providers.online?.(g) || {};
          const runtime = online.runtime || {};
          const locks = [...(providers.locks?.() || [])];
          const clock = providers.clock?.() || {};
          let actions = [];
          try {
            if (Array.isArray(context?.actions)) actions = [...context.actions];
            else if (g && currentActorCanonicalTeam != null && currentActorSlot != null) actions = legalActions(g, currentActorCanonicalTeam, currentActorSlot) || [];
          }
          catch (error) { reasons.push("LEGAL_ACTION_EVALUATION_FAILED"); }
          const replacementRequired = (!actor || actor.dead) && actions.some(action => action.type === "deploy");
          const mandatoryAutomaticAction = actions.some(action => action.type === "scheduled" || action.type === "speedDelay");
          const manualActions = actions.filter(action => action.type !== "scheduled" && action.type !== "speedDelay");

          if (!g) reasons.push("NO_GAME");
          else {
            if (g.ended || g.winner) reasons.push("BATTLE_ENDED");
            if (!pair.length) reasons.push("CURRENT_ACTOR_MISSING");
            if ((!actor || actor.dead) && !replacementRequired) reasons.push("CURRENT_ACTOR_NOT_ALIVE");
            if (!context?.skipActiveIdentityChecks && g.activeTeamId && g.activeTeamId !== currentActorCanonicalTeam) reasons.push("ACTIVE_TEAM_MISMATCH");
            if (!context?.skipActiveIdentityChecks && g.activeEntityId && currentActorUid && g.activeEntityId !== currentActorUid) reasons.push("ACTIVE_ENTITY_MISMATCH");
            if (g.mode === "spectator") reasons.push("SPECTATOR_READ_ONLY");
            if (g.mode === "aivai" || g.mode === "ai-ai") reasons.push("AI_CONTROLLED_MODE");
            if (currentActorCanonicalTeam !== canonicalLocalTeam) reasons.push("REMOTE_ACTOR_TURN");
            if (standardOnline) {
              const expectedTeam = online.role === "host" ? "P" : online.role === "guest" ? "A" : null;
              if (!online.connected) reasons.push("TRANSPORT_NOT_CONNECTED");
              if (!online.battleStarted) reasons.push("BATTLE_START_NOT_COMMITTED");
              if (expectedTeam && canonicalLocalTeam !== expectedTeam) reasons.push("ROLE_CANONICAL_TEAM_MISMATCH");
              if (runtime.generation > 0 && !runtime.committed) reasons.push("SESSION_NOT_COMMITTED");
              if (runtime.generation > 0 && runtime.state !== "IN_BATTLE") reasons.push(`SESSION_${runtime.state || "UNKNOWN"}`);
              if (online.reconnecting) reasons.push("RECONNECTING");
            }
            if (g?.bossRaid?.online) {
              if (!online.connected) reasons.push("TRANSPORT_NOT_CONNECTED");
              if (!online.battleStarted) reasons.push("BATTLE_START_NOT_COMMITTED");
              if (runtime.generation > 0 && (!runtime.committed || runtime.state !== "IN_BATTLE")) reasons.push(`SESSION_${runtime.state || "NOT_COMMITTED"}`);
              if (online.reconnecting) reasons.push("RECONNECTING");
            }
            if (g?.bossRaid?.online && !providers.bossControls?.(g)) reasons.push("BOSS_SLOT_NOT_LOCAL");
            if (locks.length) reasons.push("ACTIVE_INPUT_LOCK");
            if (clock.locked) reasons.push("CLOCK_INPUT_LOCK");
            if (g.pendingTransaction || g.pendingAction || g.pendingPayment || context?.pendingSubmitted) reasons.push("PENDING_AUTHORITATIVE_ACTION");
            if (online.pendingActionId || context?.pendingActionId) reasons.push("PENDING_ONLINE_ACTION");
            if (online.busy) reasons.push("APPLICATION_BUSY");
            if (!actions.length) reasons.push("NO_LEGAL_ACTIONS");
            if (mandatoryAutomaticAction) reasons.push("MANDATORY_AUTOMATIC_ACTION");
          }
          const uniqueReasons = [...new Set(reasons)];
          const ownsCurrentActor = !!g && currentActorCanonicalTeam === canonicalLocalTeam && !["SPECTATOR_READ_ONLY", "AI_CONTROLLED_MODE"].some(reason => uniqueReasons.includes(reason));
          const canAct = ownsCurrentActor && uniqueReasons.length === 0 && manualActions.length > 0;
          return Object.freeze({
            canAct, reasons: uniqueReasons, ownsCurrentActor,
            canonicalLocalTeam, displayLocalSide,
            currentActorCanonicalTeam, currentActorSlot, currentActorUid,
            currentActorCharacterId: actor?.card || null,
            activeEntityId: g?.activeEntityId || null,
            actorAlive: !!actor && !actor.dead, replacementRequired,
            mandatoryAutomaticAction,
            legalActionCount: actions.length,
            manualLegalActionCount: manualActions.length,
            gatherLegal: actions.some(action => action.type === "gather"),
            actions,
            activeLocks: locks.map(lock => ({ ...lock })),
            pendingActionId: online.pendingActionId || g?.pendingAction?.actionId || null,
            authoritativeRevision: Number(online.authoritativeRevision || 0),
            localAppliedRevision: Number(g?.networkRevision || 0),
            sessionState: runtime.state || null,
            reconnectState: online.reconnecting ? "RECONNECTING" : "CONNECTED",
            surfaceKind,
          });
        };
        return Object.freeze({ evaluate, canAct: g => evaluate(g).canAct, bindProviders });
      })();
      function grandVisualOwner(g) {
        if (g?.fieldEffect?.type !== "grand") return null;
        return g.fieldEffect.team === localTeamFor(g) ? "P" : "A";
      }
      function renderCore() {
        if (typeof window.ActionFaultInjector === "object") {
          try { window.ActionFaultInjector.hit("render-before"); }
          catch (error) {
            window.PresentationFailureChannel?.emit?.({surface:"render",phase:"render-before",error:String(error),battleId:game?.battleId||game?.matchId||null,revision:Number(game?.networkRevision||0)});
            queueMicrotask(()=>window.LifecycleManager?.continuity?.authoritativeSettled?.({reason:"presentation-failure",surface:"render"}));
            throw error;
          }
        }
        if (!game) return;
        prepareCurrentTurn(game);
        const inputProjection = LocalInputEligibility.evaluate(game);
        let localSide = inputProjection.canonicalLocalTeam;
        document.querySelector(".player-side .side-head b").textContent =
          game.mode === "online" ? "내 진영" : "플레이어 진영";
        document.querySelector(".ai-side .side-head b").textContent =
          game.mode === "online" ? "상대 진영" : "AI 진영";
        for (const t of ["A", "P"]) {
          let tm = game.teams[t],
            isLocal = t === localSide,
            pre = displaySideFor(game, t),
            displayTeam = isLocal ? "P" : "A";
          $(`#${pre}Slots`).innerHTML = tm.field
            .map(
              (u, i) =>
                `<div class="slot" data-slotteam="${t}" data-slotindex="${i}">${tm.traps[i] ? '<span class="trap">⚡ 전기태클</span>' : ""}${unitHTML(u, t, i, false, displayTeam)}</div>`,
            )
            .join("");
          if (t !== localSide) {
            let known = tm.bench.filter(
                (u) => u && game.revealed[t].includes(u.uid),
              ),
              hidden = tm.bench.filter(
                (u) => u && !game.revealed[t].includes(u.uid),
              ).length,
              parts = known.map((u, i) => unitHTML(u, t, i, true, "A"));
            if (hidden)
              parts.push(
                `<div class="unknown-bench"><b>정체불명 후열</b><span>남은 카드 ${hidden}장</span></div>`,
              );
            $(`#${pre}Bench`).innerHTML =
              parts.join("") || '<span class="muted">후열 없음</span>';
          } else
            $(`#${pre}Bench`).innerHTML =
              tm.bench.map((u, i) => unitHTML(u, t, i, true, "P")).join("") ||
              '<span class="muted">후열 없음</span>';
          $(`#${pre}Alive`).textContent =
            `생존 ${aliveUnits(tm).length}/4 · 교체 ${tm.switchUses || 0}/3`;
        }
        let turns = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
          let oi = (game.turn + i) % 4,
            [t, s] = game.order[oi],
            u = unitAt(game, t, s);
          turns.push(
            `<span class="turntoken ${i ? "" : "now"}">${i ? "다음" : "현재"} ${t === localSide ? "내" : game.mode === "online" ? "상대" : "AI"}-${s + 1}: ${u && !u.dead ? u.name : "빈 슬롯"}</span>`,
          );
        }
        $("#turnStrip").innerHTML = turns.join("");
        $$(".card").forEach((x) =>
          x.classList.remove("active-turn", "targetable"),
        );
        let [ct, cs] = game.order[game.turn],
          cu = unitAt(game, ct, cs),
          activeEntityId = inputProjection.currentActorUid || cu?.entityId || cu?.uid || null,
          el = activeEntityId ? battlefieldCardForUid(game, activeEntityId) : null;
        document.querySelector(".arena")?.setAttribute("data-active-entity-id", activeEntityId || "");
        document.querySelector(".arena")?.setAttribute("data-active-canonical-team", inputProjection.currentActorCanonicalTeam || "");
        if (el?.isConnected) el.classList.add("active-turn");
        if (pendingSkill) {
          for (const tar of pendingSkill.targets) {
            let q = pendingSkill.slotTarget
              ? `.slot[data-slotteam="${tar.team}"][data-slotindex="${tar.slot}"]`
              : `.card[data-zone="field"][data-team="${tar.team}"][data-slot="${tar.slot}"]`;
            let e = $(q);
            if (e) {
              e.classList.add("targetable");
              e.onclick = () => playerChooseTarget(tar);
            }
          }
        }
        renderLog();
        renderActions();
        playVisuals();
        refreshCardTooltip();
        if (game.winner) showEnd();
      }

      function syncGrandFieldBackground(g) {
        let arena = document.querySelector(".arena");
        if (!arena) return;
        arena.classList.remove("grand-P", "grand-A");
        document.querySelector(".player-side")?.classList.remove("grand-benefit", "grand-penalty");
        document.querySelector(".ai-side")?.classList.remove("grand-benefit", "grand-penalty");
        arena.classList.toggle("no-field-motion", false);
        if (g?.fieldEffect?.type === "grand") {
          let visualOwner = grandVisualOwner(g);
          arena.classList.add(`grand-${visualOwner}`);
          let good = visualOwner === "P" ? ".player-side" : ".ai-side",
            bad = visualOwner === "P" ? ".ai-side" : ".player-side";
          document.querySelector(good)?.classList.add("grand-benefit");
          document.querySelector(bad)?.classList.add("grand-penalty");
        }
      }

      function renderLog() {
        let box = $("#log");
        const distanceFromBottom = box.scrollHeight - box.scrollTop - box.clientHeight;
        const followLatest = lastLog === 0 || distanceFromBottom <= 28;
        const previousTop = box.scrollTop;
        let mobileClose = box.querySelector(".mobile-log-close");
        if (!mobileClose) {
          mobileClose = document.createElement("button");
          mobileClose.type = "button";
          mobileClose.className = "mobile-log-close";
          mobileClose.textContent = "로그 닫기";
          mobileClose.setAttribute("aria-label", "전투 로그 닫기");
          mobileClose.onclick = (event) => {
            event.preventDefault();
            event.stopPropagation();
            setBattleLogOpen(false);
          };
          box.prepend(mobileClose);
        }
        for (; lastLog < game.logs.length; lastLog++) {
          const l = game.logs[lastLog] || {};
          const message = normalizeGameNumberText(l.msg);
          if (!message) continue;
          const d = document.createElement("div");
          d.className = l.cls || "";
          d.textContent = message;
          box.appendChild(d);
        }
        if (followLatest) box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
        else box.scrollTop = previousTop;
      }
      function actionButton(box, label, fn, cls = "") {

      function paymentCostForAction(u, a) {
        let sk = card(u).skills.find((x) => x.id === a.skill);
        return effectiveSkillCost(game, game?.order?.[game.turn]?.[0] || "P", u, sk, a.costOverride ?? (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "hurricaneDive") ? "W*" : sk?.cost || ""));
      }
      function residualEnergyEntries(u, actor, plan) {
        let left = energyStore(u, actor).map((e, index) => ({ ...e, index }));
        for (const c of "RWB") {
          let need = plan?.[c] || 0;
          for (let i = left.length - 1; i >= 0 && need > 0.001; i--) {
            if (left[i].c !== c) continue;
            let take = Math.min(left[i].a, need);
            left[i].a -= take; need -= take;
          }
        }
        return left.filter((e) => e.a > 0.001);
      }
      function residualDualEnergyEntries(u, baseActor, plan) {
        let out = [];
        for (const who of ["beast", "rider"]) {
          let left = energyStore(u, who).map((e, index) => ({ ...e, index, actor: who }));
          if (who === baseActor) {
            for (const c of "RWB") {
              let need = plan?.[c] || 0;
              for (let i = left.length - 1; i >= 0 && need > 0.001; i--) {
                if (left[i].c !== c) continue;
                let take = Math.min(left[i].a, need);
                left[i].a -= take;
                need -= take;
              }
            }
          }
          out.push(...left.filter((e) => e.a > 0.001));
        }
        return out;
      }
      function openManualPayment(actions, done, title = "기술 코스트 선택") {
        if (!game || !Array.isArray(actions) || !actions.length) return;
        const [t, s] = game.order[game.turn], u = unitAt(game, t, s), a0 = actions[0], actor = a0.actor || null;
        if (!u) return;
        const store = energyStore(u, actor), picked = new Set();
        const requirements = actions.map(a => {
          const cost = paymentCostForAction(u, a);
          const transformed = transformedSkillReq(game, t, u, cost);
          const need = Object.values(transformed.req).reduce((n, x) => n + Number(x || 0), 0);
          return { action:a, cost, transformed, need };
        });
        const uniqueLabels = [...new Set(requirements.map(x => x.transformed.tokens.join(" ") || "없음"))];
        $("#modalContent").innerHTML = `<h2>${title}</h2><p>필요 코스트: <b>${uniqueLabels.join(" 또는 ")}</b>. 실제로 지불할 에너지를 선택하세요.</p><div class="energy manual-payment-energy"></div><div id="manualPaymentPreview" class="effect-box"></div><div class="choice-list"><button id="manualPaymentCancel">취소</button><button id="manualPaymentConfirm" class="primary" disabled>이 에너지로 지불</button></div>`;
        const box = $("#modalContent .manual-payment-energy"), preview = $("#manualPaymentPreview"), confirm = $("#manualPaymentConfirm");
        const counts = () => {

          for (const i of picked) {

            if (e) c[e.c] += Number(e.a || 0);
          }
          return c;
        };
        const planMatches = (reqInfo, c) => {
          const total = c.R + c.W + c.B;
          if (Math.abs(total - reqInfo.need) > .001) return false;
          if (!reqAcceptsCounts(reqInfo.transformed.req, c)) return false;
          const p = reqInfo.action.paymentPlan;
          if (p && (Math.abs(Number(p.R||0)-c.R)>.001 || Math.abs(Number(p.W||0)-c.W)>.001 || Math.abs(Number(p.B||0)-c.B)>.001)) return false;
          return true;
        };
        const refresh = () => {
          const c = counts(), total = c.R + c.W + c.B;
          const matchingInfo = requirements.filter(x => planMatches(x,c));
          const matching = matchingInfo.map(x => x.action);
          const targetNeeds = [...new Set(requirements.map(x => energyAmountText(x.need)))].join(" 또는 ");
          const abyssInfo = actionSkillHasEffect(a0, "abyssCannon")
            ? ` · 예상 기본 피해 ${Math.max(0, 100 - 40 * (4 - (Number(a0.baseCostCount) || 0)))}D · 독 조건 ${c.R > 0 && c.W > 0 && c.B > 0 ? "충족" : "불충족"}`
            : "";
          preview.textContent = `선택 ${c.R ? `R${energyAmountText(c.R)} ` : ""}${c.W ? `W${energyAmountText(c.W)} ` : ""}${c.B ? `B${energyAmountText(c.B)}` : ""} · ${matching.length ? "지불 가능" : `총 ${energyAmountText(total)}/${targetNeeds}, 코스트 조건 불충족`}${abyssInfo}`;
          confirm.disabled = !matching.length;
          return {c, matching};
        };
        store.forEach((e,i) => {
          const b = document.createElement("bu
